### [1] [Go Fishing with Alice: Compatible Carrollian and Poisson Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05573)
*Andrew James Bruce*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文受非交换Carroll几何启发，提出Carrollian–Poisson丛的概念：一种兼容Poisson结构的Carroll流形，其中兼容性表现为Carroll分布在Poisson锚映象中。作者给出若干实例（Kerr视界、BTZ视界、未来零无穷），并证明每个Carrollian–Poisson丛都可配备度量兼容且时钟兼容的Vaisman（逆变）联络，从而可定义和研究几何运动学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受非交换Carrollian几何的启发，旨在将Poisson结构与Carrollian几何相结合，建立Carrollian–Poisson丛的理论框架，为理解此类流形的几何运动学提供基础工具。

Method: 提出Carrollian–Poisson丛的定义，将兼容性表达为Carroll分布在Poisson锚映象中的包含关系；通过分析该兼容性的几何后果，并构造多个具体例子（外Kerr视界、BTZ视界、未来零无穷）加以验证；进一步证明每个此类丛均存在Vaisman（逆变）联络，同时满足度量兼容与时钟兼容条件。

Result: 建立了Carrollian–Poisson丛的完整几何框架，展示了多个物理相关实例，并证明了存在同时兼容度量和时钟的Vaisman联络，从而为在该几何背景下定义和研究运动学铺平了道路。

Conclusion: Carrollian–Poisson丛为融合Carroll几何与Poisson结构的理论提供了统一框架；Vaisman联络的存在保证了此类流形上的几何运动学可被精确定义。该工作对引力视界及渐近无穷等物理场景的研究具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We introduce, motivated by noncommutative Carrollian geometry, the notion of a Carrollian--Poisson bundle understood as a Carrollian manifold with a compatible Poisson structure. Compatibility is expressed as the inclusion of the Carroll distribution in the image of the Poisson anchor. We explore the geometric consequences of this compatibility and present several simple examples of Carrollian--Poisson bundles, including the (outer) Kerr horizon, the BTZ horizon, and future null infinity. Furthermore, we establish that every Carrollian--Poisson bundle can be equipped with a Vaisman (contravariant) connection that is both metric-compatible and clock-compatible, and thus geometric kinematics can be defined and studied.

</details>


### [2] [Interface foliation near a minimal isoparametric hypersurface for the Allen-Cahn equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05715)
*Guillermo Henry,Jimmy Petean*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在正Ricci曲率的闭Riemann流形上，利用等参函数构造了具有任意多个节点分量的Allen-Cahn方程解，并证明这些节点分量在λ趋于无穷时收敛到最小等参超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Allen-Cahn方程的解与极小超曲面之间的几何联系，特别是探索如何通过等参结构构造具有多个节点分量的解，并证明其能量有界及收敛行为。

Method: 利用等参函数的水平集结构，在最小等参超曲面的唯一水平集附近构造依赖λ的Allen-Cahn方程解，解沿等参函数的水平集为常数，并具有指定数目的节点分量。

Result: 证明了对任意正整数k，当λ足够大时存在恰好k个节点分量的解；节点分量在λ→∞时收敛到最小等参超曲面Γ，且所有此类解的泛函能量一致有界。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了Allen-Cahn方程与极小超曲面收敛理论中已有的构造方法，利用等参结构的对称性，实现了具有多节点分量的解的精确定性与能量估计，丰富了Allen-Cahn几何分析的理论结果。

Abstract: Let $(M,g)$ be a closed Riemannian manifold of positive Ricci curvature. Let $f$ be a proper isoparametric function on $M$, and $Γ$ the unique level set of $f$ which is a minimal hypersurface. For any positive integer $k$, and $λ>0$ large enough, we construct a solution of the Allen-Cahn equation $ Δu + λ(u-u^3 ) =0$ which is constant along the level sets of $f$ and has exactly $k$ nodal components. We prove that the nodal components approach the minimal isoparametric hypersurface $Γ$ as $λ\rightarrow \infty$ and the energy is uniformly bounded for all such solutions.

</details>


### [3] [The topology of tridiagonal isospectral sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05733)
*David Martínez Torres*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定非简单谱的三对角对称实矩阵集合的拓扑结构。发现该集合不是正交共轭类的子流形，而是干净相交的子流形集合。证明了该等谱集合是aspherical空间，且其Betti数为组合数，推广了简单谱情形下的已知结果，并将结果扩展到分裂实半单Lie代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于三对角等谱集的拓扑研究主要针对简单谱（即特征值互不相同）的情形，而非简单谱（存在退化特征值）的具体结构尚不清楚，需要揭示其拓扑本质并推广至更一般的Lie代数框架。

Method: 将三对角对称实矩阵的等谱集合视为正交共轭类中若干子流形的并，这些子流形以干净相交（clean intersection）的方式汇聚。利用拓扑方法（如aspherical性检验和Betti数计算）分析该集合的结构，并将技术推广到分裂实半单Lie代数的相应设定中。

Result: 证明了三对角等谱集合（针对固定非简单谱）为aspherical空间，其Betti数是组合数，成功将简单谱的结果推广到任意谱；同时将相关结论延伸至分裂实半单Lie代数。

Conclusion: 三对角等谱集在非简单谱下虽非流形，但具有清晰可描述的拓扑结构；本文结果统一了简单谱与非简单谱情形，并在一般Lie代数层面拓展了适用范围。

Abstract: We describe the topology of the set of tridiagonal symmetric real matrices with fixed non-simple spectrum. The tridiagonal isospectral set is not a submanifold of the corresponding orthogonal conjugacy class, but a collection of submanifolds which intersect cleanly. We show that the tridiagonal isospectral set is an aspherical space and that its Betti numbers are combinatorial numbers. This generalizes to arbitrary spectrum constructions and results known for simple spectrum. We also extend these results to split real semisimple Lie algebras.

</details>


### [4] [Cheeger constant rigidity for cocompact negatively curved manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05798)
*Kuntao Jin,Xiaodong Wang,Bo Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一个刚性结果：如果闭连通黎曼流形(M,g)（m≥2）的截面曲率≤-1，其万有覆盖空间(X,g_X)的等距变换群维数达到Yau下界m-1时，则(X,g_X)必等距于双曲空间H^m(-1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau已经证明了满足sec≤-1的闭流形的万有覆盖空间X的等距变换群维数至少为m-1。自然的问题是：当下界被达到时，空间结构是否被刚性刻画？本论文旨在回答这一刚性问题，即等距变换群维数取到最小值的空间是否必然为双曲空间。

Method: 论文采用刚性和对称性分析的方法：利用等距变换群维数达到最小的假设条件，结合截面曲率≤-1的几何约束，通过等距群的代数结构分析与黎曼流形的几何分类，证明空间必须等距于双曲空间H^m(-1)。

Result: 证明了刚性结论：若自由等距变换群的维数恰好等于m-1（即Yau下界被达到），则万有覆盖空间(X,g_X)与常数截面曲率为-1的双曲空间H^m(-1)等距。

Conclusion: 该结果将Yau的下界估计强化为刚性分类定理：在负曲率条件下，等距变换群的最小可能维数只能由双曲空间实现，这表明双曲空间是该几何条件下对称性最"贫乏"的唯一极端情形。

Abstract: Let $(M^m,g)$, $m\geq2$, be a closed connected Riemannian manifold with $\secg_g\leq-1$, and let $(X,g_X)$ be its universal cover. Yau proved that $\hiso(X)\geq m-1$. We prove that equality is rigid: if $\hiso(X)=m-1$, then $(X,g_X)$ is isometric to $\Hh^m(-1)$.

</details>


### [5] [Constant $k$th-mixed curvature on locally conformal Kähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05852)
*Kai Tang,Zuocai Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致局部共形Kähler（LCK）流形上具有常数第k次混合曲率的情形。利用Huang-Wan关于常数Chern全纯截面曲率的最新方法，作者证明：若紧致LCK流形具有非零常数第k次混合曲率，则其Hermitian度量必为Kähler度量；对于第二混合曲率，当曲率常数为零时同一结论也成立。文章还研究了与一般常数混合曲率猜想相关的特殊参数，获得了一个挠率-能量恒等式和紧致Hermitian流形上的符号障碍，并通过Bochner-Kähler几何刻画了一个例外的Kähler情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常数曲率问题是微分几何中的经典课题。混合曲率作为Chern曲率张量的重要推广，其常数情形与Kähler性之间的关系鲜有研究。作者希望探究紧致LCK流形上常数混合曲率对度量的强约束，以推进一般的常数混合曲率猜想。

Method: 采用Huang-Wan针对常数Chern全纯截面曲率发展出的分析方法，将其推广到kth混合曲率的设定中。通过建立紧凑性和参数化条件，利用LCK流形的特殊结构推导度量必须为Kähler的结论；同时结合挠率-能量恒等式与Bochner-Kähler几何进行情形刻画。

Result: 证明了：紧致LCK流形若有非零常数kth混合曲率，则其Hermitian度量是Kähler度量；第二混合曲率情形常数取零时结论依然成立。此外，在紧致Hermitian流形上获得了挠率-能量恒等式和符号障碍，并通过Bochner-Kähler几何刻画了特定的例外Kähler情形。

Conclusion: 不同常数混合曲率情形下，紧致LCK流形的Hermitian度量会被迫退化到Kähler情形，这体现了常数曲率条件的强刚性。相关工作为一般常数混合曲率猜想提供了新的证据与部分参数情况的解答。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider compact locally conformal Kähler manifolds with constant $k$th-mixed curvature. By using a recent method of Huang-Wan for constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature, we prove that if a compact LCK manifold has nonzero constant $k$th-mixed curvature, then its Hermitian metric is Kähler. For the second mixed curvature, the same conclusion also holds when the curvature constant is zero. We also study some special parameters related to the general constant mixed curvature conjecture. In particular, we obtain a torsion-energy identity and a sign obstruction for compact Hermitian manifolds, and characterize an exceptional Kähler case by Bochner--Kähler geometry.

</details>


### [6] [Single Lie Brackets on Closed Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05958)
*Zeyu Zeng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意闭光滑流形上，每个光滑向量场都可以表示为两个光滑向量场的李括号。通过有限输运构造、精确扩展、时间剪切与逆移位等组合技术实现，并推广到余切丛上的Poisson括号结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明闭光滑流形上每个光滑向量场均为两个光滑向量场的李括号，且不需要可定向性假设，适用于任意维度。该结果具有基础性意义，并可作为推论导出余切丛上线性函数关于Poisson括号的生成性质。

Method: 证明结合了多种几何分析技术：1) 有限输运构造，允许在一个水平集的紧子集附近规定数据；2) 精确扩展方法，实现跨越横向图圆盘的光滑延拓；3) 利用紧支横向微分同胚的环、时间剪切和逆移位来校正任意标量过渡缺陷至任意指定的正宽度；4) 在流盒中赤道附近安排正则对，随后在两组不相交的图帽上完成全局扩展。

Result: 证明了闭光滑流形上任意光滑向量场均可写成两个光滑向量场的李括号，所有辅助扩展命题均获证。该证明适用于所有维度且不需要可定向性条件。作为推论，余切丛上每个纤维线性的光滑函数均可表示为两个此类函数的Poisson括号。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了李括号满射性的完整构造性证明，消除了可定向性假设，并自然导出了辛几何中Fibrewise线性函数的Poisson括号生成结果，为哈密顿力学与辛拓扑提供了新的基础工具。

Abstract: We prove that every smooth vector field on a closed smooth manifold is the Lie bracket of two smooth vector fields. The proof combines a finite transport construction, which allows prescribed data near a compact subset of one level set, with an exact extension across transverse graph disks. A loop of compactly supported transverse diffeomorphisms, followed by a time shear and an inverse shift, corrects an arbitrary scalar transition defect in any prescribed positive width. After arranging a canonical pair near an equator in a flow box, the global extension is completed on two disjoint graph caps. The argument applies in every dimension and requires no orientability hypothesis. All auxiliary extension statements are proved. As a consequence, every smooth function on the cotangent bundle that is linear on each fiber is a Poisson bracket of two such functions.

</details>


### [7] [On products of locally conformally Kähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05964)
*Miron Stanciu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意两个或多个正维数紧致复流形的乘积空间上不存在严格的局部共形Kähler（lcK）度量，解决了一个长期存在的lcK几何猜想，该猜想此前仅在特定条件下被证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: lcK几何中长期存在一个悬而未决的猜想：正维数紧致复流形的乘积是否允许严格的lcK度量。此前该猜想仅在特定额外条件下得到验证，需要一般情形的证明来彻底解决。

Method: 文中采用几何分析的方法对乘积流形上的lcK度量结构进行深入研究，通过一般性的论证消除了先前证明中所需的特定条件限制，给出完整的一般性证明。

Result: 成功证明了任何正维数紧致复流形（包括两个及以上）的乘积都不存在严格的lcK度量，将此前仅在特定条件下成立的结论推广到了一般情形。

Conclusion: 该结果彻底解决了lcK几何中的这一长期猜想，为理解lcK流形的整体结构提供了重要的几何约束，即紧致正维数复流形的乘积空间上不会出现严格的lcK结构。

Abstract: We prove that no product of positive-dimensional compact complex manifolds admits a strict locally conformally Kähler (lcK) metric. This solves a long-standing conjecture in lcK geometry, previously established only under certain conditions.

</details>


### [8] [A Cheng-Yau type estimate for positive biharmonic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05965)
*Guosheng Jiang,Mingxiang Li,Zhehui Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立了完整黎曼流形上正双调和函数的Cheng-Yau型梯度估计。当Ricci曲率满足$Ric_g \\ge -(n-1)K g$时，在球$B_{2R}(p)$上的正双调和函数$u$满足局部估计$-\frac{\Delta_g u}{u} +\frac{1}{8n}\frac{|\nabla u|_g^2}{u^2} \\le C_n(R^{-2}+K)$。进一步，若Ricci曲率非负，则每个全局正双调和函数满足$\Delta_g u\\equiv c$及最优估计$|\\nabla u|_g^2\\le2cu$（$c\\ge0$）。此外还证明每个正$k$-重调和函数具有非负常数$(k-1)$次Laplacian，且增长阶至多为$2k-2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Cheng-Yau估计是调和函数理论的核心工具，而双调和函数作为调和函数的自然推广，其估计在几何分析中具有重要价值。此前对于正双调和函数在一般曲率条件下的梯度估计尚缺乏系统性结果，本文旨在填补这一空白，建立相应于调和函数情形的Cheng-Yau型上界估计，并探索其全局推论。

Method: 论文采用几何分析中经典的极大值原理与Hessian/Bochner型恒等式相结合的技术路线。通过对双调和方程结构的精细分析，构造合适的辅助测试函数并应用Ricci曲率下界的曲率条件，推导出局部梯度—Laplacian比的统一上界估计。对于非负Ricci曲率及$k$-重调和函数的全局情形，利用迭代估计与单调性方法获得刚性结论（如$\Delta_g u\\equiv c$）与最优增长阶。

Result: 主要结果包括三方面：（1）在$Ric_g\\ge-(n-1)Kg$条件下，正双调和函数满足局部Cheng-Yau型估计$-\frac{\Delta_g u}{u}+\\frac{1}{8n}\\frac{|\\nabla u|_g^2}{u^2}\\le C_n(R^{-2}+K)$；（2）非负Ricci曲率下，全局正双调和函数满足$\Delta_g u\\equiv c$（$c\\ge0$常数）及尖锐的梯度估计$|\\nabla u|_g^2\\le2cu$；（3）正$k$-重调和函数的$(k-1)$次Laplacian为非负常数，且函数增长阶不超过$2k-2$。

Conclusion: 本文成功将经典Cheng-Yau估计推广至双调和及更一般的$k$-重调和情形，获得了在负曲率下界与完整流形框架下的局部梯度估计，并在非负Ricci曲率下导出了刚性定理和最优增长界。这些结果不仅深化了对双调和函数几何性质的理解，也为研究更一般高阶椭圆算子的梯度估计提供了方法论的启示。

Abstract: We establish a Cheng-Yau type estimate for positive biharmonic functions on complete Riemannian manifolds with Ricci curvature satisfies $Ric_g \ge -(n-1)K g$. If $u$ is a positive biharmonic function in $B_{2R}(p)$, then $$ -\frac{Δ_g u}{u} +\frac{1}{8n}\frac{|\nabla u|_g^2}{u^2} \le C_n\left(R^{-2}+K\right) \quad\text{on }B_R(p). $$ Further, if the Ricci curvature is nonnegative, every global positive biharmonic function satisfies $Δ_g u\equiv c$ and the sharp estimate $|\nabla u|_g^2\le2cu$ for some nonnegative constant $c$. We also show that every positive $k$-polyharmonic function has nonnegative constant $(k-1)$-st Laplacian and growth of order at most $2k-2$.

</details>


### [9] [A sub-Riemannian endpoint map with no regular values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05991)
*Antonio Lerario,Luca Rizzi,Daniele Tiberio*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个没有正则值的端点映射，从而反驳了亚黎曼Sard猜想。值得注意的是，在该构造中极小化Sard性质仍然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚黎曼Sard猜想是亚黎曼几何中的一个重要猜想，该猜想认为在亚黎曼流形上，端点映射的临界值集合的测度特性应当满足类似Sard定理的性质。构造一个反例来验证或推翻这一猜想，是亚黎曼几何分析中的核心问题之一。

Method: 通过构造一个具体的端点映射，使其不具备任何正则值（即每个值都是临界值），同时确保在该构造中极小化Sard性质（minimizing Sard property）仍然成立，从而在反驳Sard猜想的同时保留了部分重要性质。

Result: 成功构造出一个没有正则值的端点映射，直接否证了亚黎曼Sard猜想；同时证明了该构造仍然满足极小化Sard性质，表明即使Sard猜想不成立，极小化情形下的相关性质仍然有效。

Conclusion: 亚黎曼Sard猜想被反例否定，但极小化Sard性质可能仍然成立，这意味着该领域的研究需要重新审视Sard猜想与极小化性质之间的微妙关系。

Abstract: We construct an endpoint map with no regular values, disproving the sub-Riemannian Sard conjecture. Remarkably, in our construction the minimizing Sard property remains valid.

</details>


### [10] [Rigidity of Ricci-Pinched Complete Self-Shrinkers in Arbitrary Codimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06001)
*Juan Li,Zhiyuan Xu*

Abstract: We complete the classification of compact simply connected homogeneous Einstein manifolds in dimension six by resolving the remaining cases for left-invariant Einstein metrics on $G=\mathrm{SU}(2)\times\mathrm{SU}(2)\cong S^3\times S^3$. Then we show that any $\mathbb Z_2$ symmetry generated by an inner involution $σ$ with $\trσ=-2$ necessarily extends to a $\mathbb Z_2\times\mathbb Z_2$ symmetry. Together with the previously known classification results, these theorems imply that every left-invariant Einstein metric on $G$ is, up to homothety and isometry, either the standard product metric $g_{\rm can}$ or the Jensen nearly Kähler metric $g_{\rm NK}$.

Abstract: We use the Kähler-Ricci flow to give alternate proofs of several known Miyaoka-Yau inequalities and slope semi-stabilities, in particular, for the case of compact Kähler manifolds with semi-ample canonical line bundles and K-semistable Fano manifolds. Moreover, when the canonical line bundle is semi-ample, and the Kodaira dimension is $n-1$, we prove the Miyaoka-Yau quantity is equal to the intersection of the Weil-Petersson metric and the Fubini-Study metric on the canonical model. *Giovanni Catino,Carlo Mantegazza*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类闭光滑黎曼流形上临界"Ricci–Bourguignon流"（即Schouten流）的短时间存在唯一性。由于临界参数处线性化主符号仍具有零特征值，作者通过引入独立标量未知量r构造了一个扩展系统（抛物型度量方程耦合输运-反应型标量曲率方程），利用Vol'pert–Khudyaev的非线性复合抛物-双曲系统理论得到光滑解，并通过误差传播恢复原流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界参数ρ=1/(2(n-1))处的Ricci–Bourguignon流（Schouten流）方程在通常的DeTurck修改后，线性化算子的主符号仍存在零特征值，导致先前Catino等人的理论无法处理该临界情形，其短时间存在唯一性问题悬而未决。

Method: 首先将原方程改写为Schouten张量形式。针对DeTurck修改后线性化主符号的退化性，引入独立的标量未知量r（表示标量曲率），构造扩展系统：包含度量Ricc型严格抛物方程，以及耦合的r输运-反应方程（带有曲率平方强迫项）。该扩展系统属于Vol'pert–Khudyaev研究的非线性复合抛物-双曲系统族，采用紧致流形上的标准调整后的相应定理获得唯一光滑解。最后证明R_g-r满足齐次线性抛物方程，从而标量曲率约束得以传播，恢复原始临界Ricci–Bourguignon流。

Result: 成功建立了临界Schouten值ρ=1/(2(n-1))处Ricci–Bourguignon流的短时间存在唯一性，填补了先前Catino等人提出的开放问题。方法关键是通过扩展系统消解了DeTurck算子主符号的零特征值退化。

Conclusion: 通过扩展系统技巧解决了临界参数下Ricci–Bourguignon流的短时间存在唯一性问题，该技术有望推广到其他具有类似退化主符号的几何流。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g_0)$ be a closed smooth Riemannian manifold, with $n\geqslant 3$. We prove short--time existence and uniqueness for the "critical" {\em Ricci--Bourguignon flow} \begin{equation} \partial_t g=-2\operatorname{Ric}_g+\frac{R_g}{n-1}g\,. \end{equation} Up to a constant rescaling of time, this is the {\em Schouten flow}, since the right--hand side of the equation is $-2(n-2)$ times the Schouten tensor. At the critical parameter, however, the principal symbol of the linearization of the operator that we obtain after the usual "DeTurck modification" still has a zero eigenvalue. We resolve this degeneracy by adjoining an independent scalar unknown $r$, intended to represent the scalar curvature, and considering an "extended" system consisting of a strictly parabolic equation for the metric coupled to a transport--reaction equation for $r$ with a curvature--square forcing. The extended system belongs to the class of nonlinear composite parabolic--hyperbolic systems studied by Vol'pert and Khudyaev~\cite{VolpertKhudyaev1972}. Then, a standard compact--manifold adaptation of their theorem yields a unique smooth solution of the extended system. Finally, the difference $R_g-r$ satisfies a homogeneous linear parabolic equation, so the scalar--curvature constraint "propagates" and the original critical Ricci--Bourguignon flow is recovered. This settles the short--time existence and uniqueness problem at the critical ``Schouten'' value $ρ=1/(2(n-1))$, which was left open by the previous Ricci--Bourguignon theory in~\cite{CatinoEtAl2017}.

</details>

### [19] [Uniform doubling on compact homogeneous spaces with cyclic Lie brackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06765)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We establish the uniform doubling property for $G$-invariant metrics on a wide class of compact Riemannian homogeneous spaces $(G/K,g)$, describing explicitly the volume growth of the metric balls $B_g(p,r)$. This property was previously known only for specific cases, including abelian Lie groups, the Lie group $SU(2)$ and quotients of $SU(2)\times \mathbb R^n$. Abstract: In this paper we study the global geometry and classification of complete minimal surfaces with embedded ends and finite total curvature in the product space $\mathbb{H}^2 \times \mathbb{R}$. In the $-4π$ case, we prove the surface is either a horizontal catenoid or simply-connected, with asymptotic boundary belonging to one of four explicit configurations. In the $-6π$ case, we show that any embedded example must be simply-connected and classify its possible ideal boundaries. We further construct, via an asymptotic Plateau problem solved by area-minimizing surfaces and successive Schwarz reflections, new 1- and 2-parameter families of (possibly non-embedded) simply-connected minimal surfaces with total curvature $-6π$ (and, more generally, $-2(2k-1)π$) whose ends are embedded and realize asymptotic configurations not previously known to occur.

Conclusion: 本文完整建立了neckpinch奇点从形成到稳定性的理论链条：有限时间形成（动力学刚性）、非退化极限轮廓（尖锐渐近）以及结构稳定性（C^2开集），并与三维mean curvature flow中的density定理相结合，确认非退化neckpinch是一个generic且稳定的现象。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the formation, precise asymptotics, and structural stability of neckpinch singularities in mean curvature flow. Motivated by the static rigidity of cylindrical self-shrinkers established by Colding, Ilmanen and Minicozzi, we first prove a dynamical rigidity result: mean curvature flow of hypersurfaces that are initially graphically close to a generalized cylinder on a sufficiently large scale, subject to a localized quadratic upward bending, inevitably develop a neckpinch singularity in finite time. We also establish sharp asymptotic expansions for the profile functions of these locally evolving graphs. We show that the rescaled graphical radius converges to a specific polynomial profile with quadratic bending, proving that the resulting singularities are nondegenerate. Finally, we establish an openness theorem showing that nondegenerate neckpinches are structurally stable under C^2 perturbations of the initial data. Combined with recent density theorems for the 3-dimensional mean curvature flow by Szekelyhidi, our results confirm that nondegenerate neckpinches constitute a generic and stable phenomenon in 3-dimensional mean curvature flow.

Abstract: The Dinew--Popovici functional is an energy functional for Hermitian symplectic metrics in a fixed Aeppli cohomology class. Dinew and Popovici proved that every critical point is Kähler in complex dimension three. We give a negative answer to Erfan Soheil's question about higher dimensions by constructing non-Kähler critical metrics on products of two Kähler surfaces $(S_1,η_1)$ and $(S_2,η_2)$. These metrics are critical under every variation on the product 4-fold. We characterize the critical product metrics and prove that non-Kähler critical products exist in the Aeppli class $[η_1+η_2]_A$ precisely when the canonical bundles of the two surface factors are smoothly trivial. As a by-product, we develop a geometric flow driven by the torsion tensor and converging to the fixed Kähler background when this background metric has nonnegative holomorphic bisectional curvature.

If the two one-sided tangential velocities of the reference sheet do not vanish simultaneously, the derivative of the unnormalized residual is Fredholm of index two. Fixing the mean normal displacement and the total circulation gives an index-zero problem; when the normalization differential restricted to the unnormalized kernel is onto $\mathbb{R}^2$, we obtain a parameter-dependent Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction. This yields a locally unique normalized branch in the nondegenerate case and explicit first- and second-order necessary conditions in the degenerate case. For a rotationally symmetric reference metric and a concentric circular sheet, the linearized problem separates into Fourier blocks and only finitely many modes can be resonant. In the constant-curvature disk family, every mode $k\geq2$ is nonresonant, whereas the first mode produces a two-dimensional reduced problem. The two first-mode kernel profiles are normal traces of ambient Killing fields, but they do not generate fixed-boundary symmetries or, by themselves, a nonconcentric branch.

This is the noncommutative integral of $D_{g_1}^2$ relative to the reference operator $D_{g_2}$. Its local density involves the curvatures of the two metrics and the difference of their Levi--Civita connections. Integration by parts gives a closed-manifold formula without explicit derivatives of the connection difference. For compact manifolds with boundary and $n\ge4$, we assume that both metrics are Riemannian and satisfy $g_r=h_r(x_n)^{-1}g^{\partial M}+dx_n^2$ near the boundary, with $h_r(0)=1$. We compute the noncommutative residues of two factorizations involving even and odd powers of $D_{g_2}$. Their interior contributions coincide, whereas their boundary terms are explicit multiples of the integral of $K_{g_1}-K_{g_2}$, with the coefficient for the even factorization twice that for the odd one. Here $K_{g_r}$ is the trace of the second fundamental form with respect to the inward unit normal. The calculation uses a second-order residue formula and direct boundary symbol expansions. When the metrics coincide, the formulas reduce to the Hodge--de Rham Kastler--Kalau--Walze identity.

Abstract: In its minimal realization, the Froggatt-Nielsen (FN) mechanism addresses the Standard Model (SM) flavor puzzle via the spontaneous breaking of a horizontal $U(1)_H$ symmetry. If $U(1)_H$ is global and broken before the end of inflation, it gives rise to a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson (axion-like particle) with a homogeneous initial misalignment, whose subsequent coherent evolution sources spontaneous $CPT$ violation in the SM sector. We show that in the presence of $B-L$-violating processes $-$ parametrized here model-independently via the dimension-five Weinberg operator responsible for light neutrino masses $-$ the minimal FN setup can generate the baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU), linking the origin of flavor directly to the BAU and neutrino masses. Solving a complete set of evolution equations for SM asymmetries, we demonstrate that this mechanism can readily yield a baryon asymmetry comparable to the observed value, or even exceeding it by several orders of magnitude. Accounting for baryonic isocurvature and entropy dilution from axion decays, we find that the observed BAU is successfully reproduced for axion oscillation temperatures above $10^{11}$ GeV and axion decay constants ranging from $10^{13\cdots 14}$ GeV to above $10^{17}$ GeV. Finally, while we focus on a global $U(1)_H$ symmetry and axion motion from standard misalignment, our framework is readily extendable to other global horizontal symmetries and arbitrary axion motion.

One event then requires σ_p\simeq2\times10^{-42} cm^2 and a kinetic mixing ε\simeq1\times10^{-6} at m_{A'}=1\GeV, with beam-dump coverage below 0.6\GeV pushing the mediator above that value. The ground state upscatters endothermically through the same operator, which excludes |δ|\lesssim300 keV and makes the published endothermic reading the small-splitting limit of one model. The decisive test is argon, whose first form-factor zero lies at 695 keV against 94.6 keV for xenon; argon yields 4 to 6 events per tonne-year near 250 keV, where the endothermic reading is forbidden at any exposure.

Our results indicate that the accumulated phase drift provides a particularly promising observable for probing Planck-scale mass corrections and for distinguishing a possible quantum-gravity contribution from conventional matter-induced effects.

Abstract: We propose a novel framework that simultaneously addresses the origin of Dirac neutrino masses and the nature of self-interacting dark matter (SIDM). This same scalar couples to a vector-like fermion DM candidate, inducing a tiny Majorana mass splitting that renders the DM pseudo-Dirac and inelastic. Crucially, the scalar also acts as a light mediator for DM self-interactions, potentially solving the small-scale structure problems of Cold DM. Furthermore, the spontaneous breaking of the dark parity required for this inelasticity produces a network of cosmological domain walls. We show that the explicit symmetry breaking needed to safely annihilate these walls generates a stochastic gravitational wave background. The non-holomorphic modular symmetry reduces the free parameters, correlating neutrino observables, addressing DM phenomenology, $ΔN_{\rm eff}$, and gravitational-wave signatures.

In the conventional thermal Higgsino limit of the MSSM, the observed relic abundance selects a mass near 1.1 TeV, while a neutralino splitting of a few hundred keV typically requires gaugino masses of order $10^7$ GeV. In the GNMSSM, Higgsino-Singlino mixing introduces an additional contribution to the splitting that can cancel the gaugino-induced contribution, allowing sub-MeV splitting with multi-TeV gauginos. This mixing also modifies the inelastic scattering coupling and annihilation rates, while coannihilation with sleptons provides freedom in obtaining the observed relic abundance. These points reproduce the observed relic abundance and satisfy direct-detection, Higgs, flavor, and collider constraints. Within the Standard Halo Model and extended-likelihood analysis, all six points yield $Δχ^2<1$ relative to the best fit. Our results show how the GNMSSM can accommodate the LZ high-recoil event without an ultraheavy gaugino sector. A quantitative assessment of solar-capture and neutrino-telescope constraints remains necessary for establishing viability.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 建立了扩展的k_perp-因子化公式，其每一阶都是作用于相同未积分分布的局域算符，揭示出初始态中已存在的动量空间各向异性，这些各向异性源于横向密度分布的梯度且随系统稀薄化（如轻离子碰撞）而增强。该框架还补充了实时Glasma计算带来的相干项，产生更完整的初始能量-动量张量。修正项可直接集成到现有的基于饱和的初始态模型中。

Conclusion: 梯度展开将k_perp-因子化从零阶近似提升为系统性的逐阶框架，在保持可处理的因子化形式的同时，动态生成初始动量各向异性并给出更精确的早期能量-动量张量。该修正随源的梯度增大，在更稀薄、涨落主导的系统中（如轻离子碰撞）变得更加重要，为唯象学研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We revisit single-inclusive gluon production in the dilute--dilute limit of the Color Glass Condensate and show that $k_\perp$-factorization is only the zeroth order of a systematic gradient expansion in the transverse positions of the two colliding sources, organized in powers of $1/(Q_sR)$, with $Q_s$ the saturation scale and $R$ the size over which the sources vary. Collecting terms order by order builds a ladder of scalar and tensor structures from the local distributions and their transverse derivatives, yielding an extended $k_\perp$-factorized formula that retains the spatial dependence of the sources and, through kinetic moments of the spectrum, the energy-momentum tensor. Repeating the expansion from a real-time computation of the classical Glasma fields in the future light cone reproduces the momentum-space result in the eikonal limit, but additionally retains coherent interference between amplitude and conjugate. This adds terms to the ladder (flow responses, chromo-electric--magnetic interference, and a longitudinal energy flux) further suppressed by powers of the large outgoing momentum $|\pp|\gg Q_s$. The hierarchy thus reveals momentum-space anisotropies present in the initial state before any hydrodynamic evolution, which leading $k_\perp$-factorization misses by construction. Since each order is a local operator on the same unintegrated distributions, these corrections can be added directly to existing saturation-based initial-state models, giving a dynamically generated initial momentum anisotropy and a more faithful early-time energy-momentum tensor without abandoning the tractable factorized form. Set by gradients of the transverse density profiles, they grow towards more dilute, fluctuation-dominated systems, e.g. light-ion collisions. Phenomenological consequences will be developed in a companion paper.

</details>


### [95] [Recent applications of the gradient flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07940)
*R. Harlander,A. Shindler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了梯度流作为QCD中规范不变且O(4)不变的紫外正则化手段，阐述了其在连接非微扰格点计算与微扰结果方面的作用，特别是在算子乘积展开（OPE）框架中的应用，并回顾了其在部分子分布函数、重介子观测量、夸克质量及强耦合常数梯度流定义等方面的最新应用，同时简要讨论了Ricci流在量子引力中的微扰表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度流为QCD提供了一个兼具规范不变性和O(4)不变性的紫外正则化方案，能够将非微扰格点计算与微扰理论结果有效结合，尤其在算子乘积展开（OPE）框架中具有重要价值。作者旨在系统梳理这一领域的核心概念、理论进展及最新应用。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，首先阐述梯度流作为紫外正则化器的理论基础，论证在给定OPE算子基下，流时依赖的flowed系数与regular Wilson系数之间的转换在每一微扰阶都是精确的。随后系统回顾该方法在部分子分布函数、重介子观测量、夸克质量以及强耦合梯度流定义等方向的应用，最后延伸讨论Ricci流在量子引力微扰表述中的应用。

Result: 论文论证了在给定OPE算子基中，flowed与regular Wilson系数之间的流时依赖转换在各微扰阶的精确性，并展示了梯度流方法在多个QCD物理量计算中的成功应用，包括PDF、重介子观测量、夸克质量和强耦合常数。此外还综述了Ricci流在量子引力和非高斯不动点研究中的应用。

Conclusion: 梯度流是一种强大且灵活的紫外正则化工具，能够无缝衔接非微扰格点QCD与微扰QCD。未来该方法有望在更多QCD观测量及量子引力研究中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: The gradient flow provides a gauge- and O(4)-invariant ultraviolet regulator for QCD. This opens new opportunities for combining non-perturbative lattice calculations with perturbative results, in particular in the context of an operator-product expansion (OPE). We review the concepts behind this and argue that, within the operator basis of a given OPE, the flow-time dependence of the conversion between flowed and regular Wilson coefficients is exact at each perturbative order. Recent applications to parton distribution functions, heavy-meson observables, quark masses, and gradient-flow definitions of the strong coupling are discussed. Finally, the perturbative formulation of the Ricci flow in quantum gravity and its application to the study of non-Gaussian fixed points is reviewed.

</details>


### [96] [Revisiting two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $B$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08053)
*Chun-Khiang Chua*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了双粲反粲重子衰变 \bar B → B_c \bar B_c，在原有 b→c\bar c q 贡献的基础上纳入 b→u\bar u q 贡献，更新了各种拓扑振幅分解。虽然 b→u\bar u q 在 ΔS=-1 过程中受 CKM 压低，但在 ΔS=0 过程中其效应被 λ^{-2}（约20倍）放大，当前数据甚至略微偏好非零的 b→u\bar u q 贡献，该贡献可显著提高多个特定衰变道的速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 \bar B → B_c \bar B_c 双粲重子衰变的理论分析仅考虑 b→c\bar c q 贡献而忽略了 b→u\bar u q 的拓扑贡献，导致某些衰变道的速率预测可能被严重低估，需要重新审视完整拓扑结构以更准确地描述实验数据。

Method: 采用拓扑振幅方法，将 b→u\bar u q 贡献纳入四类通道（3_f\bar 3_f、6_f\bar 3_f、\bar 3_f\bar 6_f、6_f\bar 6_f）的拓扑分解中，并利用 CKM 因子的相对大小（λ^{-2}≈20的放大效应）和现有实验数据对贡献进行定量分析。

Result: 在 ΔS=0 跃迁中 b→u\bar u q 与 b→c\bar c q 贡献的 CKM 因子相对大小被放大约20倍；当前 3_f\bar 3_f 类衰变数据允许甚至略微偏好非可忽略的 b→u\bar u q 贡献；该贡献可使 B^- → Ξ_c^0 \bar Ξ_c^-、\bar B_s^0 → Λ_c^+ \bar Ξ_c^- 和 \bar B^0 → Ξ_c^+ \bar Ξ_c^- 等衰变道具有比仅含 b→c\bar c d 贡献时大得多的速率。

Conclusion: b→u\bar u q 贡献在 \bar B → B_c \bar B_c 双粲重子衰变中可能起重要作用，特别是在 ΔS=0 跃迁中不可忽略。实验测量上述三个衰变道将有助于厘清 b→u\bar u q 贡献的角色，深化对该类衰变的动力学理解。

Abstract: The study of two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c \overline {\bf B}_c$ decays using the topological amplitude approach is revisited. We include the $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions, in addition to the $b\to c\bar c q$ contributions, to these decays. The topological decomposition of $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$, ${\bf B}_c(\bf 6_f) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$, ${\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 6_f})$ and ${\bf B}_c(\bf 6_f) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 6_f})$ decay amplitudes are updated accordingly. Although the $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions are CKM suppressed in $ΔS=-1$ transitions, their effects are significantly amplified in $ΔS=0$ transitions, as the relative size of the CKM factors in $b\to u\bar u q$ and $b\to c\bar c q$ contributions in $ΔS=0$ transitions is enlarged by $λ^{-2}$, roughly a factor of 20, from the one in $ΔS=-1$ transitions. Present data in $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$ decay rates allow or even slightly prefer non-negligible $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions. These $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions can allow much larger rates for $B^-\to Ξ_c^0 \bar Ξ_c^{-}$, $\overline B{}_s^0\to Λ_c^+ \bar Ξ_c^{-}$ and $\overline B{}^0\to Ξ_c^+ \bar Ξ_c^{-}$ decays than those only have $b\to c\bar c d$ contributions. Measuring these decays will be interesting and useful in clarifying the role of $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions in two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c \overline {\bf B}_c$ decays.

</details>

Method: 采用有效程展开（ERE）与谐振子复合性关系，在双通道框架内利用实验质量和宽度提取散射长度与有效程程；通过N=50000个蒙特卡洛样本对输入不确定性进行传播；利用复合系数X₂量化分子成分占比；另外执行ERE+CDD极点分析以检验是否存在与观测极点相容的自然裸态背景贡献。

Conclusion: 研究结果为χ_{c1}(3872)和T_{cc}^+主要是强子分子结构提供了强有力的定量证据，同时验证了ERE方法在近阈值奇异强子研究中具有高度稳健性，能够经受多种模型不确定性考量的检验。

Abstract: We investigate the near-threshold structure of the exotic tetraquark candidates $χ_{c1}(3872)$ and $T_{cc}^+(3875)$ using the effective-range expansion (ERE) and resonance compositeness relations. From the experimental masses and widths, we extract scattering lengths, effective ranges, and molecular compositeness coefficients within a two-channel framework, with uncertainties propagated through $N=50000$ Monte Carlo samples. We find large negative scattering lengths, $a=-8.49^{+0.93}_{-1.05}$ fm for $χ_{c1}(3872)$ and $a=-14.57^{+1.70}_{-1.66}$ fm for $T_{cc}^+$, and dominant molecular components $X_2>0.95$. The results are robust against variations of compositeness, experimental correlations, and the loop-function subtraction constant. An explicit ERE+CDD-pole analysis finds no physically reasonable bare-state contribution consistent with the observed poles and a natural background. Our results provide strong evidence for predominantly molecular structures of both states and demonstrate the robustness of the ERE approach to near-threshold exotic hadrons.

</details>

Abstract: We perform a quantitative analysis of the $B^{0}$ meson spectroscopic parameters in a hot and dense medium. For various fixed values of the baryon chemical potential, our numerical results indicate that both the mass and decay constant of the $B^{0}$ meson initially increase with temperature, reach a maximum, and then gradually decrease before eventually vanishing at sufficiently high temperatures. The corresponding vanishing points are found to be in the temperature intervals $(0.182-0.273)~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $(0.118-0.173)~\mathrm{GeV}$, for the mass and decay constant, respectively. Moreover, these temperatures move toward lower values as the baryon chemical potential increases. On the other hand, for various fixed temperatures, both the mass and decay constant exhibit a slight initial increase with increasing baryon chemical potential, followed by a subsequent decrease, eventually vanishing at sufficiently high chemical potentials. The vanishing points for the decay constant is determined to be in baryon chemical potential interval $(0.23-0.83)~\mathrm{GeV}$. For the mass, the decrease at $T=0$ is much weaker than at finite temperatures; excluding this case, the vanishing points for the mass are determined to be in baryon chemical potential interval $(0.38-1.43)~\mathrm{GeV}$ for different fixed temperatures. It is further observed that the baryon chemical potential values corresponding to the vanishing of both the mass and decay constant move to lower values as the temperature increases. In the $T \to 0$ and $μ_B \to 0$ limit ...

### [99] [Top-quark flavor-changing neutral currents and charged-lepton flavor violation in a generational three-Higgs-doublet model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08350)
*Hao-Ran Ma,Ti-Bin Hou,Yu-Ju Peng,Jin-Lei Yang,Tai-Fu Feng*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在CP守恒的G3HDM框架下，同时计算树图和单圈级别的顶夸克味改变中性流衰变（t→c h1）、带电轻子味破坏过程（h1→μτ）以及相干μ-e转换。对4839个参数点施加多层面约束：真空稳定性充分条件、微扰幺正性预选截断、希格斯质量与信号强度约束、电弱精确观测数据、UTfit 2025中性介子混合范围、B→Xsγ及Bs→μ+μ-稀有衰变实验限制，进行系统扫描。

Conclusion: G3HDM模型中，树级味改变中性希格斯耦合是主导机制，决定各类味破坏过程的相对大小。μ-e转换率在铝靶上的预测上限（~10^-15）可通过下一代μ-e转换实验进行检验，为该模型提供了可验证的实验信号。

Abstract: In this work, we systematically investigate top-quark flavor-changing neutral-current decays, charged-lepton flavor-violating processes, and coherent $μ-e$ conversion in the CP-conserving generational three-Higgs-doublet model (G3HDM), including the relevant tree-level and one-loop contributions. The 4839-point sample passes conservative sufficient vacuum-stability conditions, necessary perturbative-unitarity preselection cuts, Higgs-mass and signal-strength constraints, electroweak precision data, the UTfit 2025 neutral-meson-mixing ranges, $\overline{B}\to X_sγ$, and $B_s\toμ^+μ^-$. In the scanned region, $\mathrm{Br}(t\to c h_1)\leq 1.04\times10^{-5}$ and $\mathrm{Br}(h_1\toμτ)\leq 4.83\times10^{-4}$, while the maximal conversion rates are $\mathrm{CR}(μ\mathrm{Al}\to e\mathrm{Al})\approx 4.95\times10^{-15}$ and $\mathrm{CR}(μ\mathrm{Au}\to e\mathrm{Au})\approx 1.05\times10^{-14}$. The dominant collider signals are mainly driven by tree-level flavor-changing neutral-Higgs couplings, which determine the relative strengths of these processes and induce the pronounced enhancement of $t\to c h_1$ and $h_1\toμτ$. Meanwhile, the upper range of the predicted $μ-e$ conversion rate in aluminum lies within the sensitivity reach of next-generation $μ-e$ conversion experiments.
