<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于对P波夸克偶素光锥分布的重新关注，作者希望揭示相对论效应对这些态分布振幅的精确结构影响，因为它们在硬排他过程中是关键的非微扰输入量。

Method: 采用基光前量子化（BLFQ）形式化方法，研究P波夸克偶素领先扭曲的分布振幅，提取其宏观形状并分析相对论效应引起的结构性特征，特别是相对论性S/D分波贡献。

Result: 提取的分布振幅宏观形状与非相对论极限预期一致，但相对论效应引入了关键结构特征：轴矢量介子的分布振幅中出现新颖的"W"形结构，该结构由相对论诱导的S/D分波产生。

Conclusion: 这些发现为分析硬排他过程提供了必要的非微扰输入，并强调了相对论框架对理解现代高能对撞机上重夸克偶素产生与结构的重要性。

Abstract: Motivated by renewed interest in the light-cone distributions of $P$-wave quarkonia, we investigate the leading-twist distribution amplitudes of these states within the basis light-front quantization (BLFQ) formalism. While our extracted distributions exhibit macroscopic shapes consistent with expectations from the non-relativistic limit, we find that relativistic effects introduce critical structural features. Most notably, we observe novel ``W"-shaped structures in the distribution amplitudes of the axial vector mesons, which arise from relativistically induced $S/D$ partial waves and cannot be explained by non-relativistic dynamics. These findings provide essential non-perturbative inputs for analyzing hard exclusive processes and highlight the importance of relativistic frameworks for understanding heavy quarkonium production and structure at modern high-energy colliders.

</details>


### [101] [Time evolution of scalar condensate decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08420)
*Ayuki Kamada,Kodai Sakurai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量场相干振荡并通过产生子粒子衰变的物理过程，比较了参数共振与费曼图方法给出的衰变率，以及它们随时间趋近一致的行为。通过数值跟踪子粒子相密度的时间演化，发现最低阶不稳定带在数十个振荡周期内趋近费曼图结果（与耦合无关），而高阶不稳定带趋近行为依赖于耦合。进一步通过可解析求解的博色和费米Rabi模型验证了参数共振与费曼图方法在玻色子情形下全时间一致，而在费米子情形下长时间后不一致，差异源于Bose增强与Pauli阻塞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明参数共振与费曼图方法在长时间极限下给出相同的衰变率，但这一结果的趋近过程尚不清楚。作者希望理解参数共振结果如何随时间渐近于费曼图结果，以及趋近速率对哪些参数（如不稳定带的阶数、耦合强度）敏感，从而深入理解两种方法之间的联系与适用性。

Method: 作者采用两种互补的方法：（1）对具有相干振荡标量场的宇宙学模型进行数值计算，跟踪子粒子相位密度的时间演化，比较不同不稳定带（窄共振区）趋近费曼图结果的速率；（2）构造更简单且可解析求解的量子力学模型——玻色Rabi模型与费米Rabi模型，在精确可解框架下分别检验参数共振与费曼图方法给出衰变率的一致性。

Result: 数值结果表明：窄共振区中最低阶不稳定带在数十个标量振荡周期内趋近费曼图结果，且趋近行为独立于耦合常数；高阶不稳定带的趋近行为依赖于标量与子粒子之间的耦合。总衰变宽度主要由最低阶不稳定带贡献，因此在微扰论框架下趋近费曼图结果也与耦合无关。在玻色Rabi模型中，参数共振与费曼图结果在所有时间都一致；而在费米Rabi模型中，两者在长时间后不一致。

Conclusion: 该研究澄清了参数共振方法与费曼图方法之间的一致性不仅存在于长时间极限下，还揭示了趋近过程的结构：最低阶不稳定带主导的总衰变宽度与耦合无关地快速趋近费曼图结果。玻色与费米Rabi模型的对比表明，两种方法的一致性依赖于粒子的量子统计性质——Bose增强导致玻色子情形两种方法严格一致，而Pauli阻塞导致费米子情形存在系统性差异。

Abstract: A scalar field, which oscillates coherently over the space and decays through production of daughter particles, plays an important role in cosmology. It was recently shown that the parametric-resonance and Feynman-diagrammatic approaches give the same decay rate for a Bosonic daughter particle at a large time duration. We study how the parametric-resonance result approaches to this asymptotic value, by numerically following the time evolution of the phase density of the daughter particle. We see a difference among different instability bands in the narrow-resonance regime. The lowest-order instability band approaches to the Feynman-diagrammatic result in tens of scalar oscillation periods, while the high-order instability bands approach dependently also on a coupling between the scalar and daughter particles. This would infer that the total decay width, to which the lowest-order instability band contributes most dominantly in the perturbation theory, approaches to the Feynman-diagrammatic result also independently of the coupling. To confirm it, we consider a simpler and analytically solvable quantum mechanical model: Rabi models. In the Bosonic Rabi model, the parametric-resonance and Feynman-diagrammatic results agree with each other for all the time. On the other hand, in the Fermionic Rabi model, they disagree at a large time duration. This difference would be attributed to Bose enhancement vs Pauli blocking.

</details>


### [102] [Inclusive electron-nucleus cross section models from domain adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08463)
*Krzysztof M. Graczyk,Beata E. Kowal,Rwik Dharmapal Banerjee,Jose Luis Bonilla,Hemant Prasad,Jan T. Sobczyk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用迁移学习（TL）构建电子-原子核非弹性散射截面的数据驱动模型。基于在¹²C数据上预训练的深度神经网络集成，分别对³He、⁶Li、¹⁶O、²⁷Al、⁴⁰Ca和⁵⁶Fe进行微调，所有目标均获得改善，并系统研究了微调层数、训练数据比例与选择、运动学域重叠度对模型性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多原子核目标的电子散射实验数据有限或覆盖不全，难以独立训练可靠的深度学习模型。迁移学习可通过在数据丰富的碳核上预训练，再适配到数据稀缺的其他原子核目标，从而提升建模效果。

Method: 采用深度神经网络集成预训练于¹²C数据的方案，对六个不同原子核目标（³He、⁶Li、¹⁶O、²⁷Al、⁴⁰Ca、⁵⁶Fe）分别进行微调。系统分析了微调层数深度、训练数据占比与选取策略、源域与目标运动学域重叠度对预测精度和鲁棒性的影响，并与唯象F1F2模型进行对比。

Result: 所有目标经迁移后均有改善，其中氧基线已足够故改善有限；氧仅需浅层适配，而氦、钙、铁需要更深的微调；锂因数据量有限鲁棒性最差；铝对少量高约束测量点高度敏感。对超出碳训练域的运动学配置，适配模型在不确定度内与测量一致。

Conclusion: 迁移学习是构建跨核大数据驱动散射模型的有效策略，但迁移成功取决于目标数据的质量与覆盖，以及合理的微调深度选择；模型预测与唯象F1F2模型的比较为核结构函数建模提供了补充视角。

Abstract: We apply transfer learning (TL) to construct data-driven models of inclusive electron-nucleus cross sections. Starting from an ensemble of deep neural networks pretrained on \(^{12}\)C data, we fine-tune the models separately for \(^{3}\)He, \(^{6}\)Li, \(^{16}\)O, \(^{27}\)Al, \(^{40}\)Ca, and \(^{56}\)Fe. The resulting models improve for all targets, marginally so for oxygen, where the carbon baseline is already adequate, although their predictive robustness depends on the amount, coverage, and precision of the available target data. We systematically study how model performance depends on the number of fine-tuned layers, on the fraction and selection of the training data, and on the overlap between the source and target kinematic domains. The layer-wise analysis shows that oxygen requires only shallow adaptation, whereas helium, calcium, and iron require substantially deeper fine-tuning. Lithium represents the least robust case because of its limited dataset, while aluminum demonstrates a strong sensitivity to a small subset of highly constraining measurements. For selected kinematic configurations outside the coverage of the carbon training data, the adapted models remain consistent with the measurements within their estimated uncertainties. Finally, we compare the resulting predictions with those of the phenomenological F1F2 model.

</details>


### [103] [Nonperturbative functional renormalization group for Higgs-singlet models with physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08470)
*Norimi Yokozaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的非微扰泛函重整化群框架，在LPA'级别求解Z2对称实单态扩展标准模型在零温和有限温度下的Wetterich流方程，避免了有效势的多项式展开，并展示了其在电弱相变研究中的概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的泛函重整化群求解通常依赖低阶场展开（多项式截断），这在小场区域外可能失效，且难以处理有限温度电弱相变所需的全局有效势信息。作者希望获得一个连续、无网格、覆盖完整场与标度范围的有效势描述，以更可靠地研究一阶电弱相变及其 bounce 作用量等非微扰物理。

Method: 采用混合 tree-level 加 PINN 的ansatz表示尺度依赖的有效势，在LPA'级别求解Wetterich方程；RG流在多域设置中实施（同时在标度和场空间分段），通过导数匹配条件保证光滑性和数值稳定性；规范与Yukawa部门通过独立计算的微扰两环跑动耦合纳入，反常维度在LPA'级别包含。引入软一致性约束，使解在场空间全域保持与微扰理论在符号和数量级上接近。

Result: 作为概念验证，作者计算了两个基准温度下的一维场空间切片以及T=100 GeV时的二维有效势重构，并从中提取了两步bounce作用量。结果与重整化求和微扰理论及基于网格的松弛求解器进行了基准对比。发现软一致性约束对选择物理合理的流方程解是必要的（不仅是帮助性的），但收敛解对该引导——通过手动调节权重和分析热目标——存在残余依赖。

Conclusion: 该PINN框架提供了一种无需多项式截断求解有限温度泛函重整化群方程的新途径，能够获得连续无网格的有效势。然而，解对人为一致性引导的残余依赖被明确指出为核心开放问题，需要进一步发展更自动化的参数选择或更自洽的约束机制。

Abstract: We develop a nonperturbative functional renormalization group framework within the LPA' to solve the Wetterich flow equation for the $Z_2$-symmetric real singlet extension of the Standard Model at zero and finite temperature, without a polynomial expansion of the scale-dependent effective potential, using a physics-informed neural network (PINN) representation. In contrast to conventional truncations based on low-order field expansions, our hybrid tree-level--plus--neural-network ansatz yields a continuous, mesh-free description of the effective potential over the full relevant field and scale range. As a proof of concept, we apply the framework to the finite-temperature effective potential relevant to the electroweak phase transition in this model: one-dimensional field-space slices at two benchmark temperatures, and a two-dimensional reconstruction at $T=100$ GeV from which a two-step bounce action is extracted. The renormalization group flow is implemented in a multi-domain setup in scale and field space with derivative matching conditions that ensure smoothness and numerical stability. Gauge and Yukawa sectors are incorporated via independently computed perturbative two-loop running couplings, and anomalous dimensions are included in the flow at the LPA' level. We benchmark the network against resummed perturbation theory and against a grid-based relaxation solver of the same equation. Finally, we introduce a soft consistency constraint that keeps the solution close, in sign and order of magnitude, to perturbation theory across the field-space domain; we find it necessary, rather than merely helpful, for selecting a physically sensible solution of the flow equation, and find that the converged result retains a residual dependence on this guidance -- through hand-tuned weights and an analytic thermal target -- which we identify as the central open problem.

</details>


### [104] [Gravitational transverse momentum dependent distributions for the gluons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08499)
*Kauship Saha,Dipansu Mishra,Dipankar Chakrabarti,Asmita Mukherjee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在光前形式下研究了自旋0和自旋1/2强子中规范不变胶子能量-动量张量（EMT）的动量空间结构，将非定域胶子EMT用胶子引力横向动量依赖分布（引力TMDs）参数化，并推导了其与扭曲2和扭曲3胶子TMDs的联系。此外，展示了胶子EMT的特定分量编码了胶子平均动能四动量和强子内部机械性质（如压力和剪切分布）的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子内部胶子场的能量-动量结构和力学性质是量子色动力学（QCD）的核心问题之一。胶子的动量空间分布和内部力学性质（压力、剪切力）对于解释质子自旋危机、质量起源等基本问题至关重要，需要系统化的理论框架来研究胶子EMT的结构。

Method: 在光前形式体系中，对自旋0和自旋1/2强子系统地参数化规范不变的、非定域的胶子能量-动量张量（EMT），将其分解为胶子引力横向动量依赖分布（引力TMDs），并通过与扭曲2和扭曲3胶子TMDs的对比建立对应关系。

Result: 建立了胶子引力TMDs与标准扭曲2及扭曲3胶子TMDs之间的明确联系，并证明胶子EMT的特定洛伦兹分量可以提取胶子的平均动能四动量信息以及强子内部的压力和剪切力等力学性质。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和提取胶子在强子内部能量-动量与力学性质分布提供了完整的理论框架，有望为后续在电子-离子对撞机等实验中对胶子引力TMDs的测量提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We investigate the momentum-space structure of the gauge-invariant gluon energy-momentum tensor (EMT) for spin-$0$ and spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ hadrons in the light-front formalism. We parameterize the nonlocal gluon EMT in terms of so-called gluon gravitational transverse-momentum-dependent distributions (gravitational TMDs) and derive their connections to twist-2 and twist-3 gluon TMDs. Moreover, we demonstrate that particular components of the gluon EMT encode information about the average kinetic four-momentum of gluons and the internal mechanical properties of hadrons-such as pressure and shear distributions.

</details>


### [105] [A distance-independent constraint on the axion-electron coupling from RGB stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08504)
*Thomas Levasseur,Oscar Straniero,Andrea Caputo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用红巨星支顶端（TRGB）与红巨星支隆起（RGBB）之间的热星等差值这一与距离无关的微分观测量，对轴子-电子耦合常数施加新约束。通过对三个中等至高金属丰度球状星团（NGC 104、NGC 362、NGC 5904）的联合分析，得到g_ae/10^-13=0.8的最大似然值和1.49的95%置信上限，并排除g_ae/10^-13>7.5的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子作为强CP问题解法和暗物质候选者重新引起关注。已有对轴子-电子耦合的约束常受距离、星际消光、热改正零点等系统不确定性影响，因此需要一种对这些系统误差不敏感的观测量来获得更稳健的约束。

Method: 采用TRGB与RGBB之间的热星等差值作为与距离无关的微分观测量；利用三个中高金属丰度球状星团（NGC 104、NGC 362、NGC 5904）的RGB恒星光测星表，确定V和I波段特征星等并转化为热星等；先用恒星模型验证RGBB光度可复现，再通过含蒙特卡洛模拟的最大似然分析传播不确定性并导出耦合约束。

Result: 三星系联合分析得到g_ae/10^-13=0.8的最大似然值和1.49的95%置信上限；在合理质量损失假设下排除g_ae/10^-13>7.5（对应HB和AGB相消失），该限制比XENONnT等直接实验约束强一个数量级以上。该约束虽不如大型多星团样本严格，但显著更稳健。

Conclusion: 验证了这种微分、与距离无关的方法在约束超出标准模型物理方面的有效性，并指出将该方法应用于更广泛的球状星团样本可进一步精化g_ae约束。

Abstract: Interest in axions and axion-like particles has resurged, driven by their role in solving the strong CP problem and their appeal as dark matter candidates. The luminosity of the tip of the red giant branch (TRGB) offers a key observable for probing these particle properties. This work aims to improve existing bounds on the axion-electron coupling by adopting a differential observable that is less sensitive to distance, interstellar extinction, the zero-point of bolometric corrections (BCs), and other systematic uncertainties. We use the bolometric magnitude difference between the TRGB and the RGB bump (RGBB) as a distance-independent constraint, applied to three globular clusters of intermediate-to-high metallicity: NGC 104 (47 Tuc), NGC 362, and NGC 5904 (M5). Using photometric catalogs of RGB stars, we determine V- and I-band magnitudes of both features and convert them to bolometric values. After validating that our stellar models reproduce the observed RGBB luminosity, we perform a maximum likelihood analysis with Monte Carlo simulations to propagate uncertainties and derive new bounds on the coupling. A combined analysis of the three clusters yields a maximum likelihood at g13 = gae/10^-13 = 0.8 and a 95% C.L. upper limit of 1.49. Although slightly less stringent than recent bounds from larger multi-cluster samples, this limit is substantially more robust. Under reasonable mass-loss assumptions, g13 > 7.5 is ruled out, as it predicts the disappearance of the HB and AGB phases routinely observed in globular clusters, a limit more than an order of magnitude stronger than current direct experimental bounds such as XENONnT. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this differential, distance-independent method for constraining physics beyond the Standard Model. Applying it to a wider sample of globular clusters would further refine the constraint on gae.

</details>


### [106] [Hot QCD: transport coefficients in strong and weak coupling regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08521)
*Gabriele Parisi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在准粒子模型（QPM）框架下，首次利用N_f=3+1的新型格点QCD数据，评估了夸克-胶子等离子体的输运系数，包括剪切粘度η和重夸克空间扩散系数D_s。通过Fokker-Planck近似求解相对论Boltzmann方程计算D_s，并发展了对偶组分Chapman-Enskog形式来描述η/s，最后与基于随机碰撞积分求解相对论Boltzmann方程的Green-Kubo方法结果进行了对比，并比较了强耦合与弱耦合区域中(2πT D_s)/(4πη/s)的比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）研究中，准确计算输运系数（剪切粘度和重夸克扩散系数）对于理解其物质性质至关重要。传统研究多基于N_f=2或N_f=3的格点QCD数据，而新型N_f=3+1数据提供了更接近物理现实的重味环境。论文旨在利用这些新数据，在准粒子模型框架内统一计算η和D_s，揭示强耦合与弱耦合极限下的差异。

Method: 采用准粒子模型（QPM），其中胶子具有有效质量；夸克-胶子间的相互作用由树级Feynman图导出的散射矩阵来描述。D_s的计算采用Fokker-Planck近似下的相对论Boltzmann方程，分别研究了粲夸克和底夸克在N_f=3与N_f=3+1介质中的扩散。对于η/s比值，发展了对偶组分（双组分）Chapman-Enskog形式来处理夸克与胶子混合体系；温度依赖的剪切粘度与基于Green-Kubo关系的随机Boltzmann求解方法（通过随机实现碰撞积分）的结果进行了交叉验证。最终利用所获得的输运系数，比较强耦合与弱耦合区域中组合量(2πT D_s)/(4πη/s)的大小。

Result: 论文得出了QPM框架下剪切粘度随温度变化的依赖关系，并与Green-Kubo方法的结果进行了系统对比；同时获得了粲、底夸克在不同味数体系（N_f=3和N_f=3+1）中的空间扩散系数。通过对(2πT D_s)/(4πη/s)比值的强、弱耦合比较，揭示了输运系数之间的关联作为探测QGP耦合强度的有效判据。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将N_f=3+1的lQCD数据应用于QPM中的输运系数计算，并通过双组分Chapman-Enskog与Green-Kubo两种互补方法验证了η/s的结果，建立了从微扰理论到强耦合区域输运系数的自洽描述框架，为重夸克扩散与流体粘性之间关系提供了定量理解。

Abstract: We evaluate the transport coefficients, namely the shear viscosity $η$ and the spatial diffusion coefficient of heavy quarks $D_s$, within a Quasi-Particle Model (QPM). In particular, for the first time this has been done by exploiting novel lQCD data at $N_f=3+1$. In such a framework, in which the gluons are massive, the interactions among quarks and gluons have been evaluated via the scattering matrices evaluated from tree level Feynman diagrams. As far as the evaluation of $D_s$ is concerned, the Fokker-Planck approximation for the relativistic Boltzmann equation has been employed for the diffusion of both charm and bottom quarks in either a $N_f=3$ and a $N_f=3+1$ bulk. We have developed a dual-component Chapman-Enskog formalism for the ratio $η/s$ (being $s$ the entropy density) of a mixture of quarks and gluons. The temperature dependence of the shear viscosity is then compared to the results from a Green-Kubo approach, based on the numerical resolution of the relativistic Boltzmann equation with a stochastic implementation of the collision integral. Finally, using the above results, we compare the strong and weak coupling regimes for the ratio $(2πT D_s)/(4πη/s)$.

</details>


### [107] [Free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ and bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production in relativistic heavy-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08575)
*Melek Yilmaz Şengül*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于第二阶微扰理论和蒙特卡洛积分方法，计算了相对论性Pb+Pb碰撞中（γ=1500和γ=3000），束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对与自由μ⁺μ⁻对同时产生的碰撞参数依赖截面，并与文献中其他理论方法进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强电磁场中检验量子电动力学（QED）的有效性，并提供可用于未来实验验证的理论预测。与以往研究不同，本文采用依赖于碰撞参数的概率函数来计算束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对产生截面。

Method: 基于第二阶（second-order）微扰理论，结合蒙特卡洛积分技术，采用由自由μ⁺μ⁻对产生形式推广而来的形式主义来计算束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对产生截面，并同时计算自由μ⁺μ⁻对与束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对的联合产生过程。计算针对LHC中Pb+Pb相对论性碰撞，Lorentz因子取γ=1500和γ=3000。

Result: 给出了自由μ⁺μ⁻和束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对产生过程依赖于碰撞参数的产生概率，并将自由μ⁺μ⁻与束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对产生截面的结果与文献中其他理论方法的结果进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可与未来实验数据对照的预言，可作为强电磁场下QED的有效性检验。

Abstract: In our previous studies, we calculated the cross-section for the production of free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ pairs using a probability function dependent on the impact parameter, and also, we computed bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production cross section. In this study, unlike the others, we obtained the cross section for the production of bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pairs using the probability function dependent on the impact parameter. Here also, we simultaneously calculated the cross sections for producing free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ with bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pairs in relativistic $Pb+Pb$ collisions at the LHC with Lorentz factors at $γ=1500$ and $γ=3000$ reported in the literature. Our calculation is based on second-order perturbation theory, implemented by using Monte Carlo integration techniques. We present impact parameter dependent pair production probabilities for free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ and bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production processes. Notably, the bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production was evaluated by using a formalism adapted from that of free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ pair production. Our results obtained here for the free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ with bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production cross sections are compared with those from other theoretical methods available in the literature. This study aims to provide predictions that can be tested against future experimental data, thereby offering a valuable check for quantum electrodynamics (QED) in strong electromagnetic fields.

</details>


### [108] [The transverse-spin asymmetry $A^{\sinφ_{S_Λ}}_{UUT}$ in $Λ$ production in SIDIS within the collinear framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08644)
*Yongliang Yang,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了半单举深度非弹性散射中横向极化Λ超子产生的横向自旋不对称性A^{sinφ_{S_Λ}}_{UUT}，该不对称性对twist-3 T-odd夸克-胶子-夸克碎裂函数D̃_T敏感。作者在二夸克模型框架内计算了Λ超子的D̃_T，通过拟合DSV参数化确定模型参数，并预测了在EIC和EicC运动学区域中Λ超子电产生的不对称性，同时考察了奇异夸克贡献的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解横向极化Λ超子产生的自旋不对称性，特别是其对twist-3 T-odd碎裂函数的敏感性，进而通过该碎裂函数揭示非极化夸克碎裂与产生的强子横向极化之间的关联机制，为研究QCD中的自旋效应提供新的观测量。

Method: 采用二夸克（diquark）模型计算Λ超子的twist-3 T-odd碎裂函数D̃_T；通过拟合D_1和G_1到DSV参数化来确定模型参数；结合非极化部分子分布函数f_1的数值参数化，在EIC和EicC的运动学区域预测不对称性；并在现象学分析中包含f_1和D̃_T的QCD演化以及奇异夸克贡献的考察。

Result: 结果显示该横向自旋不对称性是可观的，且能够被奇异夸克贡献显著修正。

Conclusion: 该研究表明横向极化Λ产生的自旋不对称性在EIC和EicC实验中可测且大小可观，奇异夸克的贡献对结果有重要影响，为实验验证twist-3 T-odd碎裂函数及相关的自旋-轨道关联机制提供了理论预言基础。

Abstract: We investigate the transverse-spin asymmetry $A^{\sinφ_{S_Λ}}_{UUT}$ for transversely polarized $Λ$ production in semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering with an unpolarized electron beam and an unpolarized nucleon target. The spin-dependent asymmetry is sensitive to the twist-3 T-odd quark-gluon-quark fragmentation function $\tilde{D}_{T}$, which describes the correlation between an unpolarized fragmenting quark and the transverse polarization of the produced hadron. We calculate $\tilde{D}_{T}$ of the $Λ$ hyperon within a diquark model and investigate its contribution to the asymmetry. We determine the model parameters by fitting the fragmentation functions $D_1$ and $G_1$ to the DSV parametrization. Using the numerical results of the model and the available parametrization of the unpolarized parton distribution function $f_1$, we predict the asymmetry in the electroproduction of the $Λ$ hyperon in the kinematic regions of the EIC and the EicC. We also examine the impact of including the strange-quark contribution on the transverse-spin asymmetry. In our phenomenological analysis, we include the QCD evolution of the parton distribution function $f_1$ and the fragmentation function $\tilde{D}_{T}$. The results show that the asymmetry is sizable and can be significantly modified by the strange-quark contribution.

</details>


### [109] [From LUX-ZEPLIN to Colliders: Probing Higgsino Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08712)
*Kingman Cheung,Sin Kyu Kang,Ranjeet Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨LZ实验观测到的约248 keV高能核反冲事件作为1.1 TeV Higgsino非弹性暗物质的可能信号，预测带电伴随态chargino与中性态质量劈裂约350 MeV，其衰变产生亚厘米尺度带电径迹，该场景不受现有LHC限制，可在未来高能对撞机上检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验观测到能量约248 keV的核反冲事件，为质量约1.1 TeV的Higgsino非弹性暗物质模型提供了诱人的线索。作者希望探究该暗物质模型是否与现有对撞机约束一致，并评估其在未来高能对撞机上的可探测性。

Method: 在MSSM框架下分析该暗物质模型的理论预言，计算chargino与中性态之间的质量劈裂（约350 MeV量级），进而推导chargino在对撞机产生后的衰变寿命（约0.025 ns）和径迹长度（亚厘米尺度），并与现有LHC数据对比检查约束。

Result: 模型预言的chargino质量劈裂约为O(350) MeV，对应寿命约0.025 ns，产生亚厘米尺度的带电径迹或tracklet。该场景不受当前LHC搜索限制，但可在HL-LHC、100 TeV pp对撞机及缪子对撞机等未来实验中检验，关键挑战在于对极短径迹的重建能力。

Conclusion: 该Higgsino非弹性暗物质模型未被现有LHC数据排除，且有望在未来高能对撞机上通过重建亚厘米尺度带电径迹得到检验，实验上对极短径迹的重建灵敏度是决定性因素。

Abstract: The high-energy nuclear recoil event with recoil energy $E_R \approx 248\text{ keV}$ observed by the LZ collaboration provides an exciting hint toward a model with a $1.1\text{ TeV}$ Higgsino inelastic dark matter. Such a recoil energy requires a mass splitting of order ($δ\approx 350\text{ keV}$) between the two nearly-degenerate neutral states. We show that within the framework of the MSSM, the model predicts a nearly-degenerate charged Higgsino state, chargino, whose mass splitting from the neutral states is of order $\mathcal{O}(350)$~MeV. The subsequent decays of such charginos once produced at colliders would lead to a sub-centimeter charged track or tracklet (with lifetime $τ\approx 0.025\text{ ns}$) at the detector. We show that such a scenario is not constrained by the current LHC bounds, and could be tested at future high-energy colliders, including HL-LHC, 100 TeV $pp$ colliders, and muon colliders. The most critical requirement is how short a track or tracklet can be reconstructed.

</details>


### [110] [Backward Compton scattering with three vortex particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08758)
*Yi Liao,Zhaolong Teng,Hao-Lin Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了"三涡旋"逆康普顿散射过程 γ_vx + e^-_pw → γ_vx + e^-_vx，即入射涡旋光子与平面波电子迎头碰撞后，两个末态粒子均投影到涡旋态。作者解析推导了散射振幅和微分截面，数值展示了能量-角度关联、拓扑荷相关的干涉条纹及主导峰的系统性偏移，证明了该过程可作为产生高能涡旋光子和电子的潜在手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将涡旋（携带轨道角动量）特性传递给高能散射产物的机制。传统康普顿散射产生的高能光子通常为平面波，而涡旋光子/电子在高能物理、材料科学等领域有重要应用前景。通过研究末态也投影为涡旋态的三重涡旋逆康普顿散射，评估该过程产生高能涡旋粒子的可行性。

Method: (1) 采用理想Bessel涡旋态的叠加来描述入射涡旋光子，末态粒子也投影到涡旋态；(2) 引入Bessel-Gaussian波包对入射光子进行物理归一化，以消除理想Bessel态的边界奇异性；(3) 通过旋转对称性推导总角动量沿碰撞轴守恒所对应的选择定则；(4) 解析推导散射振幅及两末态粒子在能量和锥角空间中的微分截面表达式；(5) 对10 MeV电子与中心能量为1 MeV或10 keV的涡旋光子碰撞进行数值计算与分析。

Result: 计算结果显示：末态光子分布呈现强烈的能量-角度相关性、拓扑荷相关的干涉条纹，以及主导峰的系统性偏移，可通过角度或能量后选择来增强特定拓扑荷扇区；对于纵向极化入射电子，占优通道倾向于产生匹配螺旋度的末态光子；在1 MeV光子基准下，末态电子可被制备为MeV量级的涡旋态，具有可观锥角和大的角动量投影。

Conclusion: 三重涡旋逆康普顿散射可作为产生和控制高能涡旋光子与涡旋电子的可行物理方案，为实验上拓展涡旋粒子束的能量范围提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate backward Compton scattering in which an incident vortex (vx) photon collides head-on with a plane-wave (pw) electron and both final-state particles are projected onto vortex states, $γ_{\rm vx}+e^-_{\rm pw}\toγ_{\rm vx}+e^-_{\rm vx}$. We derive analytically the scattering amplitude and differential cross sections in the energy and cone angle of either final particle. Rotational symmetry implies a selection rule associated with the conservation of the total angular momentum along the collision axis. A Bessel-Gaussian wave packet is adopted for the incident photon to provide a physical normalization and regularize the boundary singularities of ideal Bessel states. We present numerical results for a $10~{\rm MeV}$ electron colliding with a vortex photon of central energy $1~{\rm MeV}$ or $10~{\rm keV}$. The final-photon distributions exhibit strong energy--angle correlations, topological-charge-dependent interference fringes, and systematic shifts of their dominant peaks, allowing different topological charge sectors to be enhanced through angular or energy post-selection. For a longitudinally polarized incident electron, the dominant channels favor final photons of matching helicity. For the $1~{\rm MeV}$ photon benchmark, the final electron can likewise be produced as a MeV-scale vortex state with a sizable cone angle and a large angular-momentum projection. These results demonstrate that triple-vortex backward Compton scattering offers a potential means of generating and controlling high-energy vortex photons and electrons.

</details>


### [111] [Theory updates for parton distributions in Hessian formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08761)
*Petja Paakkinen,Hannu Paukkunen,Sami Yrjänheikki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种粒子分布（PDF）重加权工具箱的新扩展方法，将已知的重加权方法与新的"去重加权"变体相结合，允许用户近似地从原有全局分析中移除数据集，从而研究更新理论预测对已有PDF全局分析结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDF重加权方法只能影响现有分析中已有的数据集，但当某个观测的理论处理方式发生变化（如精度从N^nLO提升到N^{n+1}LO，或加入电弱效应、质量效应、重求和、高扭曲效应甚至BSM物理）时，需要一种方法在近似层次上移除受影响的数据集并重新评估其影响。

Method: 论文提出的方法将标准重加权(rewrighting)方法与新型去重加权(deweighting)变体相结合。去重加权在近似意义上从原始分析中移除某数据集。该方法在Hessian形式体系下实现，使得一般用户可以方便地研究更新的理论预测对已有PDF全局分析的影响。

Result: 作者通过一个案例研究验证了该方法：将微子诱导DIS中双子(muon)产生的处理从传统方式更新为新型半单举DIS(SIDIS)方法，展示了该方法在实际应用中的操作流程和效果。

Conclusion: 这种结合重加权与去重加权的扩展方法为PDF全局分析提供了更灵活的工具，使用户无需重新运行完整的全局拟合，即可测试理论改变（包括精度升级、新效应纳入乃至BSM影响）对已有结果的影响。

Abstract: We present a new extension to the toolbox of parton-distribution reweighting methods, which enables a general user to study the impact of an updated theory prediction on the results of a pre-existing parton-distribution global analysis. This new method is a combination of the well-known reweighting method with a new deweighting variant where a dataset is removed from the original analysis in an approximate way. Specific use cases of this method could include e.g. testing the impact of updating the treatment of an observable from N$^n$LO to N$^{n+1}$LO precision in the global analysis, or including previously ignored electroweak, mass, resummation, or higher-twist effects, or even testing the impact of some beyond-standard-model physics. We discuss the implementation of this method in the Hessian formalism, and show its use in a single case study, where we update the treatment of dimuon production in neutrino-induced DIS with the novel semi-inclusive DIS approach.

</details>


### [112] [How to choose a good rational basis for elliptic Feynman integrals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08875)
*Ekta Chaubey,Vasily Sotnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多圈散射振幅中，当通过代数基变换无法解耦微分方程块而需要引入完全椭圆积分变换时，是否存在类似的和通用的最小关系结构。作者扩展了arXiv:2504.20897中提出的基于椭圆推广的leading singularities构造方法，分析了其基所满足的有理微分方程的离对角块，发现这些块的ε依赖可以组织成一种在解耦变换下保持不变的通用结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多圈散射振幅表示为超越函数与过程依赖有理系数线性组合的形式是有利的。通常这些超越函数满足带有耦合齐次块的微分方程系统。当可以通过代数基变换消除耦合块时，有理基与代数基之间的关系是通用且最小的。作者希望探究当解耦需要涉及完全椭圆积分的变换时，是否也存在类似的通用最小关系。

Method: 基于arXiv:2504.20897中提出的方法，利用椭圆推广的leading singularities构造基，并将其推广到分析这些基所满足的有理微分方程的离对角块。研究这些离对角块在不同解耦变换下的变换性质，特别是ε依赖的组织结构。

Result: 发现这些未对角块的ε依赖可以被组织成一种通用结构，且这种结构在用于将解表示为迭代积分的解耦变换下保持不变。

Conclusion: 即使在需要涉及完全椭圆积分的解耦变换情形中，所构造的基的微分方程离对角块也遵循一种通用的、在解耦变换下保持不变的ε依赖结构，这为此类复杂情形的振幅表示提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Representing multi-loop scattering amplitudes as linear combinations of multivalued transcendental functions with process-dependent rational coefficients has long been understood to be advantageous. In general, these transcendental functions satisfy a system of differential equations with coupled homogeneous blocks. When these coupled blocks can be removed through algebraic basis transformations, the relation between the rational and algebraic bases is universal and minimal. Here, we ask whether an analogous universal and minimal relation exists when decoupling requires transformations involving complete elliptic integrals. We elaborate on the method proposed in ref. arXiv:2504.20897, in which we suggested that a basis constructed using an elliptic generalization of leading singularities may provide an answer to this question. We extend this analysis to the off-diagonal blocks of the rational differential equations satisfied by the bases obtained through this construction. We find that the $ε$ dependence of these blocks can be organized into a universal structure that is preserved under the decoupling transformation used to express the solutions in terms of iterated integrals.

</details>


### [113] [Chiral matching of dimension-7 baryon-number-violating operators and application to $ΔI=3/2$ nucleon decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08785)
*Xiao-Dong Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了低能有效场论中维度-7（dim-7）重子数破坏算符的手征匹配，将所有dim-7算符分类为两种洛伦兹结构及其手征翻转对应物，导出了它们在手征微扰论中的领头阶强子实现。以此为工具，系统地研究了ΔI=3/2核子衰变模式（包括n→ℓ⁻π⁺、p→ℓ⁻π⁺π⁺和n→ℓ⁻π⁺(π⁰,η)），利用现有实验上限对相应Wilson系数施加严格约束，并从两体衰变的关联性推出了六个三体衰变模式的改进的部分寿命下界。此外还展示了一个具体的紫外模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索dim-7重子数破坏算符在低能下的强子化表现，并将之应用于核子衰变的系统性研究，以从实验数据中提取对物理新物理参数的约束，并为未来的稀有衰变搜索提供理论指导。

Method: 采用手征微扰论框架，对dim-7算符进行洛伦兹结构分类（两类结构及其手征翻转伙伴），推导其领头阶手征拉氏量实现；结合实验界对两体衰变模式的限制，利用模式间的理论关联性推导三体衰变的部分寿命下界；并构建紫外完整模型以在领头阶产生相关算符。

Result: 完成了dim-7算符在手征微扰论中的系统匹配，对ΔI=3/2核子衰变相关的Wilson系数给出了新的严格约束，并将三个三体衰变模式（各含电子和缪子两个通道，共六个模式）的部分寿命下界显著改进。

Conclusion: 工作为在核子衰变领域探索dim-7算符贡献开辟了系统路径，对验证重子数不守恒的新物理场景具有明确的预言和实验可检验性，并激励未来对这些奇异衰变模式进行搜索。

Abstract: We study chiral matching of dimension-7 (dim-7) baryon-number-violating operators in low-energy effective field theory. We show that all dim-7 operators can be classified into two distinct Lorentz structures and their chirality-flipped counterparts, and we derive their leading-order hadronic realizations in chiral perturbation theory. As an application, we systematically investigate $ΔI=3/2$ nucleon decays, including $n\to \ell^-π^+$, $p\to \ell^- π^+π^+$, and $n\to \ell^- π^+ (π^0, η)$, with $\ell=e,~μ$. Using existing experimental bounds on $n\to \ell^-π^+$, we set stringent constraints on the relevant Wilson coefficients and derive improved lower limits on the partial lifetimes of the six three-body modes from their correlations with the two-body modes. We further present a concrete ultraviolet model that generates the dim-7 operators and the associated $ΔI=3/2$ nucleon decays at leading order. Our results pave the way for exploring dim-7 contributions to nucleon decays and motivate future searches for these exotic modes.

</details>


### [114] [Relativistic spatial distributions of transverse angular momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08902)
*Cédric Lorcé,Asmita Mukherjee,Ravi Singh,Ho-Yeon Won*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在先前工作的基础上，将横平面内总角动量（含轨道角动量和自旋）的空间分布研究扩展到两个方向：一是研究相对于正则中心的横向boost分布；二是分析当横向总角动量和boost相对于不同相对论中心（质心、能量中心、自旋中心）定义时，其空间分布如何变化。针对自旋1/2靶，推导了横平面内的二维空间分布、纵向洛伦兹boost下的空间模式演化，并提供了光前形式下的对应分布，建立了瞬态形式与光前形式描述之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子内部角动量和boost的空间分布依赖于参考点（pivot）的选取，而不同的相对论中心（质心、能量中心、自旋中心、正则中心）会给出不同的物理图像。此前工作仅考虑了相对于正则中心（或自旋中心）的横向总角动量分布，且未涉及boost分布，因此需要在更一般的框架下系统地分析这些分布及其对参考点选择的依赖关系。

Method: 选取自旋1/2靶，分别相对于正则中心、相对论质心、能量中心和自旋中心定义横向总角动量密度和boost密度。推导这些量在横平面内的二维空间分布，研究其在纵向洛伦兹boost下的演化行为，并进一步给出光前形式下的对应分布，以建立瞬态形式与光前形式之间的明确联系。

Result: 得到了相对于不同相对论中心的横向总角动量和boost的二维空间分布解析表达式；揭示了空间模式在纵向洛伦兹boost下如何演化；给出了光前形式下的横向分布，并建立了与瞬态形式描述的对应关系。

Conclusion: 横向角动量与boost的空间分布强烈依赖于参考中心的选择，不同相对论中心给出了不同的物理图像；通过瞬态形式与光前形式的对比分析，为理解核子内部角动量与boost的结构提供了更完整的图景。

Abstract: In our previous work [C. Lorcé et al., Phys. Lett. B 868 (2025) 139792], we investigated the 2D spatial distributions of transverse total angular momentum, including orbital angular momentum and intrinsic spin, relative to the canonical center (or center of spin). In the present work, we extend this analysis in two directions. First, we study the corresponding transverse boost distributions relative to the canonical center. Second, since the definition of generalized angular momentum density depends crucially on the choice of pivot, we analyze how the spatial distributions of transverse total angular momentum and boost are modified when they are defined relative to different relativistic centers, viz.~the relativistic centers of mass, energy, and spin. Considering spin-1/2 targets, we derive the corresponding 2D spatial distributions in the transverse plane, and further investigate how the spatial patterns evolve under longitudinal Lorentz boosts. Additionally, we provide the corresponding light-front distributions in the transverse plane, establishing a clear connection between the instant-form and light-front descriptions of transverse angular momentum and boost.

</details>


### [115] [Neutrino mass, scalar dark matter, and collider signatures in a radiative doublet-triplet model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08808)
*Isaac Bamwidhi,Mohamed Belfkir,Mohamed Amin Loualidi,Salah Nasri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于T4-3-i拓扑的一圈辐射中微子质量模型，具体探索其T4-3-i-C1实现——包含惰性标量二重态、实标量三重态、Majorana费米子三重态和一个Dirac单态费米子。精确的Z₂对称性禁止树级III型贡献并使最轻Z₂奇粒子稳定，使一圈拓扑成为中微子质量的主导来源。模型包含CP偶中性标量暗物质候选，并预测秩二中微子质量矩阵（一个中微子无质量）。综合分析显示两种中微子质量排序均可行，逆序给出更大观测值从而更容易被未来实验检验；暗物质遗迹密度可通过湮灭和共湮灭过程解释，电弱三重态产生不同的实验信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的中微子质量和暗物质问题，通过构造T4-3-i拓扑的一圈辐射中微子质量模型，寻找能够同时解释中微子质量和暗物质的新物理框架，并评估其可检验性。

Method: 采用T4-3-i-C1拓扑实现，引入惰性标量二重态、实标量三重态、Majorana费米子三重态及Dirac单态费米子，以精确Z₂对称性禁戒树级III型项。结合理论自洽性与实验约束进行联合分析，检验两种中微子质量排序（正序和逆序），并计算可观测物理量（m_ββ、m_β、Σm_i）及暗物质遗迹丰度。

Result: 模型在两种中微子质量排序下均存在可行解，其中逆序预测更大的m_ββ、m_β和Σm_i，增强其在未来β衰变、无中微子双β衰变及宇宙学探测中的可检验性。在有效参数区内，暗物质遗迹丰度可由湮灭与共湮灭过程解释，电弱三重态给出瞬发、位移或长寿命粒子信号。

Conclusion: 该T4-3-i-C1辐射模型是同时解释中微子质量与暗物质的可行理论框架，逆序质量排序更具实验可检验性，且电弱三重态提供与暗物质及低能搜索互补的对撞机探针。

Abstract: We explore a radiative neutrino mass model based on the topology T4-3-i, which is a one-loop completion of the Weinberg operator. We focus on its T4-3-i-C1 realization, containing an inert scalar doublet, a real scalar triplet, a Majorana fermion triplet, and a Dirac singlet fermion. An exact $Z_2$ symmetry forbids the tree-level type-III term and stabilizes the lightest $Z_2$-odd particle, leaving the one-loop topology as the leading source of neutrino mass. The model accomodate a neutral {\it CP}-even scalar dark matter candidate and predicts a rank two neutrino mass matrix, and thus one neutrino is massless. A combined analysis incorporating theoretical and experimental constraints reveals viable solutions to both neutrino mass orderings, with the inverted ordering predicting larger values of $m_{ββ}$, $m_β$, and $\sum_i m_i$, thereby enhancing its testability through future beta-decay, neutrinoless double beta decay, and cosmological probes. Within the same viable parameter region, the observed dark matter relic abundance is accounted for by annihilation and coannihilation processes, while the electroweak triplet sector gives rise to prompt, displaced, or long-lived particle signatures, offering complementary collider probes to dark matter and low-energy searches.

</details>


### [116] [Axion Hair and Pulsar Electrodynamics: modelling, discharge dynamics, and particle-in-cell simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08840)
*Samuel J. Witte,Andrea Caputo,Stefan Stelzl,Alexander Chernoglazov,Alexander A. Philippov,Surjeet Rajendran*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提供了先前随伴论文所展示的静态度子轴子场梯度（"轴子毛发"）对老年旋转脉冲星近场电动力学主导效应的完整理论与数值框架。文章详细描述了稠密核物质如何产生轴子毛发，并在解析和数值两个层面（1D粒子网格模拟）研究了轴子梯度对极隙区域放电参数和电子动力学的修正。结果表明轴子毛发可增强加速或增强屏蔽效应，具体取决于恒星半球以及流线和回流线的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随伴论文已展示了轴子毛发在老年脉冲星近场电动力学中的主导效应并产生新约束，但缺乏完整的理论与数值支撑框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为那些约束提供严谨的理论推导和数值验证基础。

Method: 采用解析与数值相结合的方法：(1) 详细描述稠密核物质产生轴子毛发的物理过程，在相关参数空间内为每种相互作用计算自洽的场分布；(2) 解析层面研究轴子梯度对极隙区域的有效放电参数的修正，基于分裂单极磁场的力自由极限以及偶极场构型的偏离； (3) 数值层面开发专用的1D粒子网格（PIC）模拟，捕获恒星附近的主要动力学行为。

Result: 结果表明轴子毛发会增强加速或增强屏蔽效应，具体效应类型在恒星北半部和南半部之间、在支持外流和回流电流的场线之间会发生切换。

Conclusion: 该工作为轴子毛发主导老年旋转脉冲星近场电动力学提供了完整严格的理论与数值框架，为轻QCD轴子及CP破坏的轴子-核子相互作用提供了新的约束基础。

Abstract: In a companion paper, we demonstrated that static axion field gradients sourced by dense nuclear matter (\emph{axion hair}) can dominate the near-field electrodynamics of old rotation-powered pulsars, leading to new constraints on light QCD axions and on CP-violating axion-nucleon interactions. This article provides the extended theoretical and numerical framework underlying those results. We begin by providing a detailed description of the sourcing of axion hair from dense nuclear matter, computing self-consistent field profiles for each interaction across the relevant parameter space. We then study the modification to the electrodynamics induced in the polar gap region by these axion gradients; this is done at the analytic level by studying the modification induced by axion field gradients on the effective discharge parameter (computed in the force-free limit of the split monopole magnetic field configuration, and looking at leading deviations from the force-free limit for dipolar field configurations), and numerically by developing dedicated 1D particle-in-cell simulations which capture the leading order dynamical behavior near the star. Our results demonstrate that axion hair serves to either enhance acceleration, or enhance screening, where the relevant effect changes between the northern and southern hemispheres of the star, and between the field lines which support out-flowing and return currents.

</details>


### [117] [Two-Loop Anomalous Dimension for Non-Global and Clustering Logarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09104)
*Thomas Becher,Jürg Haag,Nicolas Schalch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有效场论框架下推导了两圈反常维度，用于次领头非全局对数和聚类对数的重求和。研究发现修改的最小减除(M5bar)方案存在问题，因为它需要额外维度来设置部分子簇射以执行重求和。作者提出了一组修正的偶极子减除方案，避免了这一问题，可获得与参考系无关的重整化群演化结果，并在Marzili框架中实现了领头色极限，数值验证了喷注间间隙截面方案无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非全局和聚类对数在高能对撞机物理中影响喷注间间隙截面等观测量，需要在有效场论框架下推导两圈反常维度进行重求和。然而重归一化方案的选择至关重要——标准MSbar方案要求额外维度来构造部分子簇射，这带来了理论上的不便，因此需要探索不受该问题影响的替代方案。

Method: 在软-共线有效理论(SCET)框架中，推导次领头非全局对数和首次推导聚类对数的两圈反常维度。重点分析方案选择影响，指出MSbar方案的维度问题，转而讨论一组修正的偶极子减除(dipole subtraction)方案以获得参考系无关的重整化群演化。在Marzili框架中实现领头色极限结果，进行数值验证。

Result: 给出了两圈反常维度的推导结果，含次领头非全局对数及首次的聚类对数；证明MSbar方案在部分子簇射构造上需要额外维度，而修正偶极子减除方案可避免此问题；数值验证了喷注间间隙截面的方案无关性。

Conclusion: 修正偶极子减除方案是比MSbar更适合执行此类重求和的方案选择，能够在不需要额外维度的前提下给出参考系无关的重整化群演化结果，为高精度次领头级非全局和聚类对数重求和提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We derive the two-loop anomalous dimension relevant for the resummation of subleading non-global and, for the first time, also clustering logarithms within the framework of effective field theory. Special emphasis is put on the choice of the renormalization scheme. We show that the modified minimal subtraction scheme is problematic since it requires extra dimensions to set up the parton shower to perform the resummation. We discuss a set of modified dipole subtraction schemes which are free from this complication and can be used to obtain a frame-independent result for the renormalization-group evolution. We implement the leading-color limit of our result in the Marzili framework and numerically verify the scheme independence for gap-between-jets cross sections.

</details>


### [118] [ALP-mediated inelastic dark matter and the LUX-ZEPLIN high-recoil candidate event LZ230616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08893)
*Guan-Wen Yuan,Bo Zhang,Wen-Yu Cao,Lei Feng,Ruizhi Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究LZ暗物质实验报告的高能候选事件LZ230616（核反冲能量约248 keV），提出其可能源于两个Majorana暗物质态之间的非弹性散射，由与胶子耦合的轴子类粒子（ALP）介导。研究发现特定参数下（mχ≈0.35 TeV，δ≈330 keV）可产生接近候选能量的窄谱，但需要较大的耦合常数乘积，且对银河系晕速度截断高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ合作组报告了一个能量约248 keV的高能候选事件，其能量远高于标准WIMP预期的低能反冲谱，需要新的理论框架来解释这一反常信号。

Method: 提出两个Majorana暗物质态间通过轴子类粒子介导的非弹性散射机制；正质量劈裂抑制低能反冲，动量依赖相互作用重塑高能谱形；独立处理暗扇区与胶子耦合，保留动量依赖的核子形状因子和氙核响应；采用近似单事件似然分析扫描参数空间。

Result: 对于ma=0.3 GeV，在mχ≈0.35 TeV和δ≈330 keV时，可在候选事件能量附近产生窄谱，但需较大耦合常数乘积，且结果对银河系晕速度截断高度敏感。

Conclusion: 该分析确立了后续利用热遗迹丰度和实验室对介导子约束进行检验所需的运动学和耦合条件，表明此解释在耦合要求上极为严格，需进一步实验数据验证。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a high-energy candidate event LZ230616 with a reconstructed nuclear recoil energy $E_R=248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}~{\rm keV}$. We investigate a possible interpretation in terms of inelastic scattering between two Majorana dark matter states mediated by an axionlike particle coupled to gluons. The positive mass splitting suppresses low-energy recoils, while the momentum dependence of the interaction reshapes the high-energy spectrum. We treat the dark-sector and gluonic couplings independently and retain the momentum-dependent nucleon form factors and xenon nuclear responses. Using an approximate single-event likelihood, we find that, for $m_a=0.3~{\rm GeV}$, a narrow spectrum near the candidate energy arises at $m_χ\simeq0.35~{\rm TeV}$ and $δ\simeq330~{\rm keV}$, although this configuration requires a large coupling product and is highly sensitive to the Galactic halo speed cutoff. Our analysis establishes the kinematic and coupling requirements for subsequent tests using the thermal relic abundance and laboratory constraints on the mediator.

</details>


### [119] [Probing the Inert Scalar Sector of the Inert Doublet Model via Vector-Boson Fusion at a Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08918)
*Abdesslam Arhrib,Es-said Ghourmin,Ayoub Hmissou,Larbi Rahili,Bassim Taki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未来高能缪子对撞机（MuCs）通过电弱规范玻色子融合过程对惰性双希格斯模型（IDM）惰性标量扇区的探测灵敏度，涵盖2→4过程的带电希格斯对产生及相关联通道，并在3、10和20 TeV能级下进行探测器级蒙特卡洛分析，结果表明能量越高灵敏度越强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索未来高能缪子对撞机对IDM惰性标量扇区的探测潜力。IDM是暗物质的重要候选框架，其中性标量H作为最轻惰性态和暗物质候选者，但其产生与探测在高能对撞机中的灵敏度尚未被充分研究。

Method: 研究WW融合诱导的带电希格斯对产生（μ+μ-→H+H-νν¯）、WZ和Wγ融合诱导的关联通道（HH±μ∓ν和AH±μ∓ν）。首先分析2→2子过程WW→H+H-和WV→H+S_i中的相消干涉，施加理论自洽性约束和现有实验限制（暗物质和对撞机搜索），然后在√s=3、10和20 TeV下进行探测器级蒙特卡洛分析并结合基于剪切的信号-本底筛选。

Result: 结果表明对惰性标量产生的灵敏度随碰撞能量显著增加，确立10和20 TeV的MuCs阶段作为探测IDM惰性标量扇区的有力工具。

Conclusion: 高能缪子对撞机（特别是10和20 TeV阶段）凭借VBF过程的能量增强特性，能够对IDM惰性标量产生提供强有力的探测灵敏度，是研究惰性标量与暗物质的理想实验平台。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of a future high-energy muon collider (MuCs) to the inert scalar sector of the Inert Doublet Model (IDM) through a comprehensive set of $2\to4$ electroweak gauge-boson fusion processes. The scalar spectrum consists of two CP-even neutral scalars: $h$ and $H$, one CP-odd neutral scalar $A$, and a pair of charged scalars $H^\pm$. We assume the neutral scalar $H$ to be the lightest inert state and the dark matter (DM) candidate, while the observed $125$~GeV Higgs boson is identified with the scalar of the Standard Model-like doublet. We consider charged Higgs pair production: $μ^+μ^-\to H^+H^-ν\barν$ induced by WW fusion, as well as the associated channels: $μ^+μ^-\to HH^\pmμ^\mpν$ and $μ^+μ^-\to AH^\pmμ^\mpν$, induced by $WZ$ and $Wγ$ fusion. Owing to their Vector Boson Fusion (VBF) origin, the corresponding cross sections increase significantly with collider energy, making them particularly well suited for exploration at a high-energy MuCs. We first study the $2\to 2$ subprocesses $WW\to H^+ H^-$ and $WV\to H^+ S_i$, $V=Z, γ$ and
  $S_i=H$ or $A$ and establish the destructive interference among the contributing diagrams. After imposing theoretical consistency requirements and current experimental constraints, including those from DM and collider searches, we evaluate the corresponding signal rates at $\sqrt{s}=3$, $10$, and $20$~TeV. We further perform a detector-level Monte Carlo analysis combined with a cut-based signal-background selection. Our results demonstrate that the sensitivity to inert scalar production increases significantly with collision energy, establishing the 10 and 20~TeV MuCs stages as powerful probes of the IDM inert scalar sector.

</details>


### [120] [A Vector-Like Lepton Interpretation of the High-Energy Nuclear Recoil Candidate in LUX-ZEPLIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08993)
*Fatemeh Elahi,Pedro Schwaller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验报告了一个在高反冲能量(248 keV)下的核反冲候选事件，由于该能量下自旋无关(SI)响应被强烈抑制，作者提出通过非相对论算符O4产生的弹性自旋相关(SD)散射来解释，使用singlet-doublet Majorana暗物质模型，在Higgs盲点区域同时预测O(1)事件率和正确的热遗迹丰度，并可通过太阳中微子搜索进行独立检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验在高反冲能量窗口报告了一个核反冲候选事件，而标准自旋无关(SI)暗物质散射在该能量下被强烈抑制，需要一个能产生更硬反冲谱的散射机制来解释这一事件。

Method: 作者考虑通过非相对论算符O4（SD散射）实现该反冲谱，采用singlet-doublet Majorana暗物质模型，利用Z玻色子对角耦合产生SD相互作用；同时沿Higgs盲点抑制SI相互作用但保留非零Z耦合，并计算遗迹丰度和太阳俘获率来约束参数空间。

Result: 找到了Higgs盲点中同时预测LZ高反冲窗口O(1)事件率与正确热遗迹丰度的参数区域；当前IceCube太阳中微子限制已排除该热参数空间的一部分，但仍有可行区域存在，额外的氙曝光和更灵敏的太阳中微子搜索可提供互补检验。

Conclusion: LZ高能事件可通过SD散射（O4算符）的singlet-doublet Majorana暗物质解释，该参数区域同时满足热遗迹丰度要求，且可通过太阳中微子观测进行独立验证。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R=248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}\,{\rm keV}$ in an exposure of $2.84\,{\rm tonne\,yr}$. At this unusually high recoil energy, the spin-independent (SI) xenon response is strongly suppressed, motivating dark matter interactions with a harder recoil spectrum. We show that elastic spin-dependent (SD) scattering through the nonrelativistic operator $\mathcal O_4=\mathbf S_{\rm DM}\cdot\mathbf S_N$ provides a simple realization of such a spectrum. We consider singlet-doublet Majorana dark matter, for which a diagonal $Z$ coupling generates the required SD interaction. The accompanying Higgs-mediated SI interaction would generically produce too many low-energy recoils, but can be suppressed along a Higgs blind spot while retaining a nonzero $Z$ coupling. We find a region of the Higgs blind spot that simultaneously predicts an $\mathcal O(1)$ event rate in the LZ high-recoil window and reproduces the observed thermal relic abundance. The same SD interaction leads to solar capture, allowing this region to be tested independently with solar-neutrino searches. Current IceCube limits already probe part of the LZ-compatible thermal parameter space, while a viable region remains. Additional xenon exposure and improved solar-neutrino searches can therefore provide complementary tests of this interpretation.

</details>

### [121] [Coherent multipath wave response on Reissner-Nordström analogue surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05534)
*Peng-Yu Chen,Sen Guo,Qing-Quan Jiang,Yu Liang,Kai Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了将Reissner-Nordström黑洞电荷依赖的空间几何映射到模拟弯曲表面对波场相干响应的理论框架，揭示稳态空间条纹、频谱共振梳和瞬态时间回波梯级是同一电荷控制路径谱的不同物理投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示相对论致密天体的内禀度量如何主导宏观波动现象，建立强场引力透镜与桌面变换光学之间的理论桥梁，为多领域模拟引力实验提供蓝图。

Method: 通过在同构嵌入的赤道切片上求解精确的空间测地线边值问题，提取离散多回路路径长度谱；采用有限路径表面惠更斯-菲涅尔构造将几何骨架转化为物理波场；解析计算任意阶缠绕轨迹及其高缠绕累积极限。

Result: 证明模拟电荷作为精确物理旋钮可重构视界喉部并从根本上重组离散路径序列；几何形变唯一决定宏观干涉，揭示空间条纹、频谱梳状结构和时间回波梯级三者统一于电荷控制的路径谱。

Conclusion: 建立了从系统参数到响应的系统性方法论，为强场相对论现象与桌面实验室模拟之间提供了严谨的理论衔接，有望推动多领域模拟引力实验的发展。

Abstract: To uncover how the intrinsic metric of a relativistic compact object governs macroscopic wave phenomena, we establish a theoretical framework mapping the charge dependent spatial geometry of a Reissner-Nordström (RN) black hole onto the coherent response of an analogue curved surface. By solving an exact spatial geodesic boundary value problem on an isometrically embedded equatorial slice, we extract the discrete multi-loop path-length spectrum and convert this geometric backbone into a physical wave field via a finite-path surface Huygens-Fresnel construction. We analytically compute the arbitrary order winding trajectories alongside their high winding accumulation limits, demonstrating that the analogue charge acts as a precise physical dial that reconfigures the event horizon throat and fundamentally reorganizes the discrete path sequence. Furthermore, we find that this underlying geometric deformation uniquely dictates the macroscopic interference, revealing that steady state spatial fringes, spectral resonance combs, and transient temporal echo ladders are intrinsically unified physical projections of a single charge controlled path spectrum. This systematic parameter to response methodology establishes a rigorous theoretical bridge between strong field gravitational lensing and tabletop transformation optics, providing a highly tunable blueprint for future multi domain analogue gravity experiments.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum-gravity-inspired Alcubierre warp-drive geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05554)
*Kimet Jusufi,Francisco S. N. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于弦论T-对偶性导致的零-point长度l₀，构建了一种T-对偶启发的Alcubierre几何（翘曲驱动度量），通过将翘曲轮廓与涂抹源的互补累积质量分数对应，获得了度规在气泡中心为C²光滑、能量密度和York膨胀具有闭式解的一族模型。该模型在固定速度下消除了轮廓收缩极限R→0的发散，但明确声明这是有效ansatz而非从弦修正场方程推导的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论的T-对偶性通过路径积分对偶赋予低能传播子一个零-point长度l₀，软化引力场短距离行为。作者希望将此思想应用于Alcubierre翘曲驱动几何，以解决常规Alcubierre几何中轮廓收缩极限的发散问题。

Method: 将翘曲轮廓识别为涂抹源的互补累积质量分数，选取有效单尺度轮廓f(r_s)=1-r_s³/(r_s²+l²)^(3/2)，其中l²=R²+l₀²。分析该度规的光滑性（C²在气泡中心）、能量密度的闭式表达式、能量密度的体积积分以及York膨胀。

Result: 度规在气泡中心为C²且在其他处光滑；能量密度体积积分在几何单位下为E=-(15π/1024)v_s²l，|ρ_E|受常数×v_s²/l²限制。由于l不能低于l₀，该单尺度族消除了固定速度下轮廓收缩极限R→0的发散。但作者强调这是有效ansatz并非严格推导，且不能与常规薄壁极限的正则化混淆。

Conclusion: 该T-对偶启发的Alcubierre几何消除了轮廓收缩发散，但仅是有效构造，需要进一步进行重整化应力张量的显式计算才能讨论半经典稳定性或修正量子能量不等式。奇异物质仍不可避免。

Abstract: String T-duality, through its correspondence with path-integral duality, endows the low-energy propagator with a zero-point length $l_0=2π\sqrt{α'}$ that softens the short-distance behavior of gravitational fields. Motivated by the regular black-hole construction obtained from the corresponding smeared source, we formulate a T-duality-inspired Alcubierre geometry by identifying the warp profile with the complementary cumulative mass fraction of that source. For the effective one-scale choice $f(r_s)=1-r_s^3/(r_s^2+l^2)^{3/2}$, with $l^2=R^2+l_0^2$, the metric is $C^2$ at the bubble center and smooth elsewhere, while the energy density, its volume integral, and the York expansion are available in closed form. In particular, $E=-(15π/1024)v_s^2 l$ in geometric units and $|ρ_E|$ is bounded by a constant times $v_s^2/l^2$. Within this one-scale family, the model therefore removes the divergence associated with the profile-contraction limit $R\to 0$ at fixed velocity, because $l$ cannot fall below $l_0$. This should not be confused with a regularization of the conventional Alcubierre thin-wall limit at fixed macroscopic bubble radius, in which an independent wall thickness is taken to zero. We emphasize, however, that the construction is an effective ansatz rather than a derivation from string-corrected field equations: $R$ is a macroscopic profile scale, the choice $l^2=R^2+l_0^2$ is an interpolation, and the classical $l_0\to 0$ limit remains a smooth thick-walled profile rather than the distributional Alcubierre top hat. Exotic matter remains necessary, and neither semiclassical stability nor a modified quantum energy inequality is claimed without an explicit renormalized stress-tensor calculation.

</details>

Abstract: It is known that the spacetime metric can be recovered from quantum-field correlators. We extend this programme by showing that the Einstein tensor can be directly written in terms of UV-regulated field correlations. The same correlation structure also directly determines the Ricci tensor, while higher coincidence derivatives recover the connection and Riemann tensor. We further show that the renormalized stress-energy tensor can be formulated within this framework, up to the standard finite renormalization freedom. These results provide a correlation-based organization of the semiclassical Einstein equations and advance the programme of formulating spacetime geometry in terms of quantum field correlations.

</details>

Result: 成功实现了低频共振模式的指数增长，随后不稳定性被非线性效应抑制并进入长寿命非平衡稳态。高分辨率测量识别出非线性频率偏移、谐波产生以及相干的三波和四波混频现象，这些非线性相互作用将能量在多个模式间重新分配，最终淬灭了线性理论所预测的无界增长。

Abstract: Rotating black holes can amplify incident waves through superradiant scattering. When these waves are confined, repeated amplification gives rise to the black-hole bomb instability, whose nonlinear evolution remains poorly understood despite its central role in models of bosonic clouds around astrophysical black holes. Here, we reproduce the black-hole bomb mechanism in a laboratory setting using a gravity simulator based on a draining vortex in superfluid helium. Surface waves propagating on the superfluid interface experience an effective rotating spacetime and undergo repeated superradiant amplification within a cylindrical cavity. By tuning the temperature and flow parameters, we achieve exponential growth of a low-frequency resonant mode, followed by the arrest of the instability and the formation of a long-lived non-equilibrium steady state. Using spatially and temporally resolved measurements, we identify nonlinear frequency shifts, harmonic generation, and coherent three- and four-wave mixing that redistribute energy among interacting modes. This novel end state of the experimental black-hole bomb highlights the role of nonlinear wave interactions in quenching the runaway growth expected from linear theory and governing the system's late-time dynamics. Our results establish a laboratory framework for investigating the nonlinear evolution of black-hole bombs, with implications for analogous phenomena involving ultralight bosonic fields around rotating black holes.

</details>


### [125] [A compact time-frequency representation for gravitational-wave data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05601)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出用多个相移高斯函数的求和来构造"高斯"窗函数，使其在时频域上具有更好的对称性。通过解析推导计算该窗函数的时间-频率方差积，并用数值方法进行验证；同时比较其与海森堡-伽博不确定极限的接近程度。

Result: 解析计算结果与数值验证一致。在对称情形下（窗函数在时间和频率上形式相同），该高斯窗函数的时间-频率面积仅比海森堡-伽博不确定极限（最低可达面积）高约2.4%。

Conclusion: 作者推测该高斯窗函数可能达到任何威尔逊-多贝西小波窗函数所能取得的最小时间-频率面积，表明这种窗函数在引力波时频分析中具有潜在优势。

Abstract: Time-frequency (wavelet) domain analyses are seeing greater use for gravitational-wave data analysis due to the advantages they have in handling non-stationary noise. A popular choice is the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) wavelet transform, which uses a window function that is very compact in frequency, but more spread out in time. In this work, we consider an alternative window function that is built from a sum of phase-shifted Gaussians. This "Gaussian" window is more symmetric in time-frequency, and consequently more compact. We examine the properties of this window function and the implications it has for gravitational-wave analyses. We calculate the window's time-frequency variance product analytically and verify it numerically. For the symmetric case, where the window has the same form in time and frequency, the construction comes within $\sim2.4\%$ of saturating the Heisenberg-Gabor uncertainty limit. We conjecture that this Gaussian window achieves the minimum time-frequency area of any WD wavelet window.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的介质引力有效场论框架，证明不同介质并非不同的理论而是同一理论中不同的关联函数插入。这一框架不仅修正了介质中的标准引力过程（如1PN动力学、记忆效应），还发现了真空中不存在的现象（Love数共振、引力子分裂与双折射不透明度），并指出了具体的观测前景，为将环境效应纳入引力波天文学提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: We develop an effective field theory of gravity in relativistic media. Integrating out the medium leaves vacuum gravity with updated Feynman rules: a graviton propagator dressed by the stress-energy two-point function of the environment, medium-induced bulk graviton vertices from higher correlators, and generalized worldline couplings encoded in matched Wilson coefficients. The diagram topologies are unchanged from vacuum, so different media (perfect fluids, collisionless matter, coherent scalar fields such as wave dark matter) are not different theories but different correlators inserted into the same diagrams. We derive the in-medium rules for a relativistic fluid and obtain the full 1PN Einstein-Infeld-Hoffmann potential, the 1PN Stokes drag, the gravitational self-energy and a generalized Christodoulou memory whose new tensor structure records the direction of the surrounding flow, turning the permanent strain into an $\textit{astrophysical weathervane}$. The same rules activate phenomena forbidden in vacuum: the sound pole converts the symmetry-protected, non-running black-hole Love number into a resonant, running one, and opens the channel $h\to hh$ in a moving medium, yielding a closed-form decay rate and a birefringent $\textit{gravitational opacity}$ set by the local flow geometry. We assess observational prospects, from dephasing and tidal resonances within reach of the Einstein Telescope and LISA to proof-of-principle memory and opacity signatures.

</details>


### [127] [Fermionic Casimir memory from an anisotropic gravitational pulse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05666)

Result: 发现脉冲产生的费米子对沉积能量包含有限的边界依赖贡献，其符号由脉冲持续时间和MIT间隙谱时标相对大小决定：绝热区域为负，脉冲极限从上方趋零。对应的压力修正非单调，出现两次符号反转，可交替增强/削弱卡西米尔吸引，形成费米子耗散记忆。

Conclusion: 受限费米子场在引力脉冲后留下与玻色子截然不同的耗散性量子记忆，其后果表现为对静态卡西米尔力的非单调、可反转的压力修正，揭示了费米统计与边界条件共同塑造的引力量子效应。

Abstract: We investigate the late-time energy deposited by a weak, spatially homogeneous anisotropic gravitational pulse in a massless Dirac field confined between two static parallel plates. The field satisfies MIT bag boundary conditions, while the prescribed geometry approaches Minkowski spacetime in both asymptotic time regions. Using a tetrad formulation, we show that the linear spin-connection contribution cancels for the traceless anisotropy, leaving a coupling to the shear component of the Dirac stress tensor. The pulse produces real fermion--antifermion pairs, whose deposited energy is evaluated through the corresponding Bogoliubov coefficients. Subtracting the production in unbounded space isolates a finite boundary-dependent contribution governed by the pulse duration relative to the characteristic spectral timescale set by the MIT gap. This contribution is negative in the adiabatic regime, where confinement suppresses excitations relative to the continuous free spectrum, and approaches zero from above in the impulsive limit. The resulting pressure correction is nonmonotonic and exhibits two sign changes, alternately reinforcing and reducing the ordinary attractive Casimir force. Unlike the bosonic vacuum-polarization memory previously obtained for the same class of backgrounds, the present effect is a dissipative fermionic memory carried by gravitationally created pairs. Here, memory denotes the persistent late-time modification of the quantum state after the pulse has vanished.

</details>


### [128] [Self-supervised reconstruction of transients in data from space-borne gravitational-wave detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05890)
*Yuxiang Xu,Minghui Du,Bo Liang,Zihao Xiao,Tianyu Zhao,Peng Xu*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Noise2Noise思想的自监督框架，用于在无干净训练目标和未知瞬变形态的情况下，从空间引力波数据中提取瞬态信号（如大质量黑洞双星合并、仪器毛刺和宇宙弦爆发等），并支持对仪器异常信号的后续条件性相减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间引力波数据中可能存在波形形态未知的瞬态信号，提取这类信号对表征新源和缓解仪器异常具有重要意义。然而现有的基于深度神经网络（DNN）的提取方法依赖干净的训练目标和特定波形类别的样本，这在瞬变形态事先未知时限制了其适用性。

Method: 开发了一种受Noise2Noise（N2N）启发的自监督学习框架，直接从含噪观测中学习，无需干净训练目标，且在推理阶段不需要瞬变特定的波形模板。使用仅由含噪的大质量黑洞双星（MBHB）观测训练的单一模型进行信号提取。

Result: 该模型在源混淆的测试数据中能够高重叠度地恢复MBHB信号，并能恢复仪器毛刺和宇宙弦爆发等其他引力波瞬态信号的主导形态。在模拟连续数据流中，当独立信息将候选瞬变识别为仪器异常时，提取的波形可在无需异常特定模板的情况下从数据中相减，显著抑制了毛刺主导频带内的注入异常功率。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持将自监督提取用于未知形态候选瞬变信号的初始波形估计，从而支持后续的表征分析，并在适当时对仪器异常进行条件性相减。

Abstract: Space-based gravitational-wave (GW) data may contain transient signals whose waveform morphologies are not known in advance. Extracting these signals is important for characterizing new sources and mitigating instrumental anomalies. However, existing deep neural network (DNN)-based extraction approaches rely on clean training targets and waveform-class-specific examples, which may limit their applicability when the transient morphology is not specified in advance. This work develops a Noise2Noise (N2N)-inspired self-supervised framework that learns from noisy observations without clean training targets and requires no transient-specific waveform templates at inference. A single model trained on noisy massive black-hole binary (MBHB) observations provides high-overlap MBHB recovery and recovers the dominant morphologies of instrumental glitches and other GW transient signals such as cosmic string bursts in source-confused test data. Beyond waveform recovery, when independent information identifies a candidate transient as instrumental, its extracted waveform can be subtracted from the data without an anomaly-specific template. In a simulated continuous data stream, this procedure substantially suppresses the injected-anomaly power within the glitch-dominated frequency band. These results support the use of self-supervised extraction for initial waveform estimation of candidate transients with unknown morphologies, enabling subsequent characterization and, where appropriate, conditional subtraction of instrumental anomalies.

</details>


### [129] [Thin accretion disk and gravitational capture cross sections of a quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder black hole immersed in an external magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05906)
*Anuar Idrissov,Hernando Quevedo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个处于外部均匀磁场中的量子Oppenheimer-Snyder黑洞的Novikov-Thorne吸积盘及引力俘获截面。发现量子参数对盘可观测量的影响较弱，而磁场耦合则大幅提升效率、峰值通量和谱峰高度并将其移至高频；两者的吸收效应相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力效应与外部磁场共同作用下致密天体（黑洞及无视界的紧凑天体）吸积盘的可观测特征及粒子俘获行为，以期为区分量子黑洞与经典黑洞提供理论观测预言。

Method: 在静态时空下，利用Wald纯轴向矢量势描述均匀磁场，通过洛伦兹力作用于弱带电吸积物质。计算带电圆轨道、ISCO、辐射通量、有效温度、红移因子、微分/光谱光度及辐射效率，同时计算边缘束缚轨道及无质量、有质量和带电粒子的俘获截面，并对照Schwarzschild极限及热光度-效率恒等式进行验证。

Result: 量子参数对盘观测量仅产生微弱改变；磁场耦合大幅提高效率、峰值通量与谱峰高度并将谱峰移至更高频。量子参数使各类俘获截面缩小，磁场耦合增大有质量粒子截面但光子截面不变，即影观测量仅响应量子参数。均匀场使带电粒子运动受限，超出磁屏蔽半径的粒子无法到达黑洞。

Conclusion: 在该测试场模型中，量子参数与磁场耦合对吸积盘和俘获行为具有方向相反的效应，影观测可作为探测量子参数的独立渠道，本文结果可为未来对量子引力修正致密天体的天文观测提供参考。

Abstract: We study a geometrically thin, optically thick Novikov--Thorne accretion disk and the gravitational capture cross sections of a quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder black hole immersed in an external, asymptotically uniform magnetic field. The exterior geometry carries a single quantum parameter, and we work over its entire admissible range, which contains a two-horizon black hole, an extremal configuration and a horizonless compact object, and which ends where the photon sphere disappears. Since the spacetime is static, the Wald potential is purely axial, so the field acts on the disk only through the Lorentz force on weakly charged accreting matter and all of its effects are controlled by a single magnetic coupling. We compute the charged circular orbits, the innermost stable circular orbit, the radiative flux, the effective temperature, the redshift factor, the differential and spectral luminosity and the radiative efficiency, together with the marginally bound orbit and the capture cross sections of massless, massive and charged particles. The construction is checked against the exact Schwarzschild limit and against the identity that relates the bolometric luminosity to the efficiency. The quantum parameter changes the disk observables only weakly, whereas the magnetic coupling raises the efficiency, the peak flux and the height of the spectral peak by large factors and shifts that peak to higher frequency. The two parameters act with opposite signs on absorption. The quantum parameter shrinks every capture cross section, while the magnetic coupling enlarges the one for massive particles and leaves the photon cross section unchanged, so that within this test-field model shadow observables respond to the quantum parameter alone. For charged particles the uniform field confines the motion, and no particle reaches the hole from beyond a magnetic shielding radius.

</details>


### [130] [Slow rotation of a localized Kalb-Ramond wormhole: Wald charges and fractional quadrupolar hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05921)
*Sardor Murodov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个由幽灵标量、标量局域径向弦扇区和与Ricci标量及Ricci张量非最小耦合的Kalb-Ramond二形式场支撑的可穿越虫洞的自洽慢转分支（至二阶）。研究表明该分支ADM质量为零但具有有限的Iyer-Wald角动量，且在四极扇区出现了由长程极向Kalb-Ramond响应产生的分数阶物质支撑度规尾迹，使标准Geroch-Hansen和质心四极构造在光滑形式下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有非平凡物质场（幽灵标量、弦扇区和Kalb-Ramond二形式场）支撑的可穿越虫洞的旋转扩展，特别是探究高阶旋转效应对其渐近结构、荷定义及多极展开的影响，考察电荷级渐近平坦性与光滑多极渐近性之间的分离。

Method: 采用标准基准分支上构造慢转展开至高阶（二阶），通过协变渐近分析检验ADM质量和Iyer-Wald角动量；比较协变与度规角动量定义的相容性；在四极扇区分析长程Kalb-Ramond极向响应并追踪其对度规尾迹和曲率的影响，评估共形完备化在空间无穷远处的可微性。

Result: 基准分支ADM质量消失而一阶轴模特具有限Iyer-Wald角动量；非最小耦合对角荷的贡献在无穷远衰减，使协变与度规角动量定义一致。二阶无质量修正，旋转增大了喉部面积并使其呈扁球形。四极扇区出现长程极向Kalb-Ramond响应产生非整数阶度规尾迹，比真空四极衰减更慢，且相同非整数层次出现在曲率中，降低了空间无穷远处共形完备化的可微性。Geroch-Hansen和质心四极构造在此分支不适用，尽管全局质量和角动量荷仍明确定义。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了电荷级渐近平坦性与光滑多极渐近性之间的分离，并将分数Kalb-Ramond毛认定为此基准解的旋转四极特征标志。标准多极构造方法（Geroch-Hansen及质心四极方案）在此类含有长程Kalb-Ramond尾迹的虫洞背景下需要推广或替代。

Abstract: We construct an action-consistent slow-rotation branch, through second order, for a traversable wormhole supported by a phantom scalar, a scalar-localized radial string sector, and a Kalb-Ramond two-form coupled nonminimally to both the Ricci scalar and Ricci tensor. For the regular benchmark branch, covariant asymptotic analysis shows a vanishing ADM mass, while the first-order axial mode carries finite Iyer-Wald angular momentum. The nonminimal contributions to the angular charge decay at infinity, so the covariant and metric definitions of angular momentum agree. At second order, no nonzero mass correction is resolved; at fixed asymptotic scalar charge, rotation increases the throat area and makes the throat oblate. The most distinctive result arises in the quadrupolar sector. A long-range polar Kalb-Ramond response couples to the localized background and generates a matter-supported metric tail that decays more slowly than the ordinary vacuum quadrupole. The same noninteger hierarchy appears in curvature and reduces the differentiability of the conformal completion at spatial infinity. Consequently, the Geroch-Hansen and asymptotically Cartesian mass-centered quadrupole constructions are not applicable in their standard smooth form to this branch, even though its global mass and angular-momentum charges remain well defined. This separates charge-level asymptotic flatness from smooth multipolar asymptotics and identifies fractional Kalb-Ramond hair as the leading rotational quadrupolar signature of the benchmark solution.

</details>


### [131] [Physics-based phenomenological modeling of binary black hole hierarchical formation 1: Synthetic universes from globular cluster simulations for GWTC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05996)
*R. O'Shaughnessy,R. Mechum,M. Qazalbash,Z. Rosenberg,M. Zeeshan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文对GWTC-5.0双黑洞观测数据进行三组分迭代建模：球状星团物理归一化群体、隔离双星现象学通道以及中等质量同位素组分，预测了质量谱断裂、质量比特征和有效自旋分布等关联性可检验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力波双黑洞事件的不同天体物理形成通道（球状星团与场通道）的相对贡献，并解释观测到的高质量事件数量过多及其有效自旋分布展宽等现象。

Method: 采用三组分迭代建模方法：(1) 基于Rapster球状星团模拟的物理归一化群体，参数涵盖星团质量、金属丰度、形成红移、致密度和原生黑洞自旋；利用层次并合再现高质量事件及更大的有效自旋弥散，要求黑洞以零原生自旋诞生。(2) 现象学场（孤立双星）通道提供低质量、优先对齐的群体，其引入使星团致密度似然变平。(3) 额外加入一个中间质量各向同性组分来描述在场双星残骸于星团中再处理的通道。

Result: 物理归一化将高质量事件率转化为球状星团贡献率约0.39%，局部速率分别为星团通道约9.1和场通道约16.0 Gpc^-3 yr^-1。模型预测质量谱在约35和70倍太阳质量处出现断裂，约0.5质量比特征（对应第一代+第二代配对），以及有效自旋分布对称且在45倍太阳质量以上急剧展宽。

Conclusion: 提出的中间质量组分仅为假设而非确定性探测，现有数值支持不足以进行可靠的证据比较。但三个组分模型产生的关联性、质量分辨预言可随着引力波普查增长而进行检验。

Abstract: We iteratively model the GWTC-5.0 binary-black-hole census with three components. First, a physically normalized population from Rapster globular-cluster simulations spans cluster mass, metallicity, formation redshift, compactness, and natal black-hole spin. Hierarchical mergers reproduce higher-mass events and their larger effective-spin dispersion, provided black holes are born with zero natal spin. Second, a phenomenological field (isolated-binary) channel supplies the low-mass, preferentially aligned population. Its inclusion flattens the cluster compactness likelihood, permitting ordinary dense globular-cluster birth radii without requiring nuclear-cluster-like conditions. Finally, residual tension at $15$--$30 M_\odot$ motivates an intermediate-mass isotropic component representing field remnants reprocessed in clusters. This is a hypothesis, not a detection: current numerical support precludes a reliable evidence comparison. Physical normalization converts the high-mass rate into $\hat{f}_{\rm GC}\simeq0.39\%$, with local rates $R_{\rm cl}\simeq9.1$ and $R_{\rm field}\simeq16.0$ Gpc$^{-3}$ yr$^{-1}$. The model predicts linked mass-spectrum breaks near $35$ and $70 M_\odot$, a $q\simeq0.5$ feature from first-plus-second-generation pairings, and a symmetric effective-spin distribution that broadens sharply above $45 M_\odot$. These correlated, mass-resolved predictions can be tested as the gravitational-wave census grows.

</details>


### [132] [A principal-symbol obstruction in metric-affine Gauss--Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06084)
*Hamed Bouzari Nezhad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析了四维黎曼Gauss–Bonnet组合在具有独立仿射联络的几何中的多种延拓方式。通过比较两种自然候选（metric-contracted表示与double-epsilon表示）在Levi-Civita极限之外的二阶导数主形式，发现前者在扭free子空间上具有非零且不定的符号，阻碍了联络的纯代数消去；而后者在该截面上主符号恒为零，不构成此类阻碍。由此论证独立联络的领头微分结构依赖于Gauss–Bonnet组合的仿射延拓选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在度规-仿射引力理论中，Gauss–Bonnet组合从黎曼几何延拓到具有独立仿射联络的几何时存在多种可能性。不同延拓虽然共享相同的Levi-Civita极限，但可能赋予独立联络不同的动力学结构，因此需要澄清它们是否在领头微分阶上保持一致，以判断联络是否可被代数消去及理论的约化性质。

Method: 作者在具有标量耦合f(φ)到Gauss–Bonnet项的度规-仿射模型中，比较两种候选延拓的联络二次二阶导数主形式。对于metric-contracted表示，在时刻主余矢量的24维hook-free无挠子空间上进行显式计算，得到秩18、零度6且不定的主形式；对于double-epsilon表示，则对联络的任意微扰在该截面上验证对应的二次形式恒为零。

Result: metric-contracted延拓在无挠witness子空间上的主形式秩为18、零度为6且不定；全无挠形式在相同余矢量处不定、秩至少18。在f(φ)≠0的区域，线性化联络方程的二阶主符号非零，阻碍了激活方向的纯代数局部消去。相反，double-epsilon延拓的对应二次二阶形式在联络任意微扰下恒为零，主符号随之消失，从而不存在此类阻碍。两种延拓的领头微分结构在Levi-Civita极限之外发生分歧。

Conclusion: 独立仿射联络的领头微分结构取决于Riemannian Gauss–Bonnet组合的仿射延拓选择：metric-contracted延拓中的联络在局部不可被纯代数消去（至少沿激活方向），而double-epsilon延拓中该特定障碍缺失。此结果对联络的代数可消去性及相应有效理论的推导具有直接含义，但double-epsilon情形下障碍的缺失本身并不自动保证代数可消去性成立。

Abstract: The four-dimensional Riemannian Gauss--Bonnet combination admits more than one continuation to a geometry with an independent affine connection. Two natural candidates, the metric-contracted representative and the double-epsilon representative, agree in their Levi-Civita limit, and we ask whether they also give the independent connection the same leading differential structure. In a metric-affine model with a scalar coupling $f(φ)$ to the Gauss--Bonnet term, we compare their quadratic two-derivative connection principal forms about the Levi-Civita locus. For the metric-contracted continuation, the principal form, evaluated on a twenty-four-dimensional hook-free torsion-free witness subspace at a timelike principal covector, has rank $18$ and nullity $6$ and is indefinite; the full torsion-free form at the same covector is therefore indefinite with rank at least $18$. Locally where $f(φ)\neq0$, the two-derivative principal symbol of the linearized connection equations is nonzero, which obstructs purely algebraic local elimination of the connection in the activated directions. For the double-epsilon continuation, the corresponding quadratic two-derivative form vanishes identically for an arbitrary perturbation of the connection about that locus, so its principal symbol vanishes with it, and that particular obstruction is absent, though its absence does not by itself establish algebraic eliminability. Thus the leading differential structure of the independent connection depends on the affine continuation of the Riemannian Gauss--Bonnet combination.

</details>


### [133] [Regular black holes from dynamical-tension string hedgehogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06097)
*Sebastian Bahamonde,Eduardo I. Guendelman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了修改测度弦论中的径向弦云结构，证明它能产生正则的渐近平坦黑洞，克服了传统等张力径向弦云只能产生奇异锥形几何的障碍。作者提出复合体张力标量场生成的连续径向弦世界面，在受约束的SO(3) hedgehog分支上简化为爱因斯坦引力与非线性函数K(Y)及辅助三形式的耦合，并给出模型无关的条件：正则中心的曲率有限要求张力T~O(r²)，且正的正则中心必为de Sitter型；有限ADM质量要求张力在无穷远处被屏蔽。最后通过一个三次方张力剖面给出渐近平坦几何正则黑洞的连续族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统径向弦云模型由于非零常数张力产生能量密度正比于r^{-2}，只能形成奇异锥形几何而非渐近平坦的正则黑洞。作者旨在通过修改测度公式化方法克服这一结构障碍，构建具有正则中心且渐近平坦的黑洞解。

Method: 采用修改测度公式下的弦论框架，构建一个连续径向世界面族，其张力由复合体张力标量场生成。在受约束SO(3) hedgehog分支上，系统等价于爱因斯垣引力耦合到非线性函数K(Y)和辅助三形式的理论。通过推导模型无关条件下，直接以径向张力T(r)表达正则中心和渐近平坦性所需条件，再以具体三次方张力剖面构造显式黑洞解。

Result: 得到了模型无关的判据：中心曲率有限要求张力T=O(r²)，正能量条件禁止更快的主阶幂次，因此满足正能量条件的正正则中心必须是de Sitter型；有限ADM质量要求张力在无穷远处被屏蔽，但在非平庸正截面以快于r^{-2}被屏蔽的情况下全域正能量条件无法满足。一个精确的三次方张力剖面实现了这些性质，给出连续族的渐近平坦几何正则黑洞，其对Schwarzschild解的首次修正出现在r^{-4}阶。

Conclusion: 修改测度公式化弦论能够克服传统径向弦云产生奇异锥形几何的限制，构造出曲率正则、渐近平坦的黑洞解。这些解具有de Sitter型的负弯曲正则中心，保持了正能量条件（尽管能量条件不能全域成立），其对Schwarzschild几何的偏差量级为r^{-4}。

Abstract: A spherical cloud of radial strings with non-zero constant tension has an energy density proportional to $r^{-2}$ and therefore produces a singular conical geometry rather than an asymptotically flat regular black hole. We show that this obstruction can be removed within the modified-measure formulation of strings. A continuum of radial worldsheets whose tensions are generated by a composite bulk tension scalar reduces, on a constrained $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ hedgehog branch, to Einstein gravity coupled to a nonlinear function $K(Y)$ and an auxiliary three-form. This construction gives model-independent conditions directly in terms of the radial tension $\mathcal{T}(r)$. Finite curvature at the centre requires $\mathcal{T}=\mathcal{O}(r^2)$, while the null energy condition forbids a faster leading power. Thus a positive regular centre satisfying the null energy condition is necessarily de Sitter. Finite ADM mass requires the tension to be screened at infinity, whereas the dominant energy condition cannot hold globally for a non-trivial positive profile screened faster than $r^{-2}$. An exact cubic profile realises these properties and gives a continuous family of asymptotically flat geometrically regular black holes whose first correction to Schwarzschild appears at order $r^{-4}$.

</details>


### [134] [Heat distribution of quantum fields interacting with Unruh-DeWitt detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06185)
*Marcos L. W. Basso,Alberto Saa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在闵可夫斯基时空中量子场与Unruh-DeWitt探测器之间热交换统计的运算框架，利用干涉协议定义了无需投影测量的热特征函数，并推导了微扰表达式。研究发现真空态下热统计符合单向泊松分布的低强度展开，而KMS态下具有双向泊松结构。文章推导了熵的Landauer不等式并验证了其数值表现，还建立了带开关的探测器-场动力学中热力学第一、第二定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相对论量子场论中定义和测量热交换统计存在根本性困难：传统的投影测量可能破坏相对论因果性，而量子场与探测器之间的热力学状态需要自洽的表述。作者希望构建一个不依赖破坏性测量的框架来研究热统计及其涨落关系。

Method: 作者采用干涉测量协议（interferometric protocol）定义热交换的特征函数，避免了投影测量的因果性悖论。在微扰论框架下推导特征函数与热分布的表达式；随后利用代数熵平衡定理（Jakšić-Pillet定理）推导Landauer不等式，并与基于全计数统计的互补Landauer界进行数值比较。在无隙和δ-开关极限下，动力学允许非微扰处理，特征函数化为双向Poisson过程。

Result: 真空态下热统计与单向Poisson分布的低强度展开一致；KMS态下至二阶微扰出现双向Poisson结构。涨落关系从探测器性质与场关联中自然涌现，并在特定极限下退化为已知的热交换涨落关系。非微扰极限下精确的涨落定理由KMS条件决定，特征函数与双向Poisson过程精确一致。

Conclusion: 该研究为相对论量子场论中的热统计提供了自洽的运算框架，澄清了涨落关系的涌现机制，并确认了Landauer不等式的适用性与紧致度取决于探测器初始态和相互作用时间。

Abstract: We introduce an operational framework for heat-exchange statistics between a quantum field and an Unruh-DeWitt detector in Minkowski spacetime. Using an interferometric protocol, we define the characteristic function of heat without projective measurements, consistently with relativistic causality. We derive perturbative expressions for this function and the heat distribution. In the vacuum, the statistics coincides with the low-intensity expansion of a unidirectional Poisson law, while for Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) states it has a bidirectional Poisson structure up to second order in perturbation theory. We study how fluctuation relations emerge from detector properties and field correlations, identifying regimes where they reduce to known heat-exchange fluctuation relations. We derive the entropic Landauer inequality as a specialization of the algebraic entropy-balance theorem of Jakšić and Pillet and verify it numerically within the perturbative detector-field interaction. We compare its tightness with a complementary Landauer bound derived from the full counting statistics of heat, analyzing their dependence on the detector's initial state and interaction time. We also formulate the first and second laws for the explicitly switched detector-field dynamics, identifying the external switching work that reconciles the field-energy gain with the detector-energy change. In the gapless and delta-switching regimes, where the dynamics admits a non-perturbative treatment, the characteristic function coincides with that of a bidirectional Poisson process, yielding an exact fluctuation theorem determined by the KMS condition. These results provide a consistent framework for heat statistics in relativistic quantum field theory and clarify the emergence of fluctuation relations.

</details>


### [135] [Alternative neural-network follow-up for all-sky FrequencyHough in continuous gravitational-wave searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06211)
*Martina Di Cesare,Pia Astone,Rosario De Rosa,David Keitel,Cristiano Palomba,Marco Serra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将神经网络（NN）分类器集成到全天空连续引力波搜索策略中的新方法。该方法基于FrequencyHough管道生成的候选信号，在不增加计算成本的前提下提升探测灵敏度，并可在多个探测器间并行化以增强探测概率和降低误报率。作者分析了LIGO/Virgo第三次观测运行（O3）的数据，频率范围[129,229] Hz，自旋下降率范围[-2.5×10⁻⁹, 1.0×10⁻⁹] Hz/s，训练基于与真实O3数据统计一致的噪声，并成功验证了该方法在低应变下区分信号与噪声的能力，以及识别训练自旋下降率范围之外的硬件注入信号的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中预计存在约10⁸-10⁹颗中子星，但目前仅有约10³颗通过电磁观测被确认，巨大的观测缺口促使研究者寻求基于替代信使（如引力波）的探测方法。全天空连续引力波搜索无需任何先验源信息即可探测电磁静默的中子星，但由于参数空间广阔，传统方法在灵敏度和计算成本之间存在矛盾，亟需新的方法提升搜索性能。

Method: 本文提出将神经网络分类器集成到基于FrequencyHough管道的全天空搜索策略中。方法从FrequencyHough管道生成候选信号，利用NN分类器进行信号与噪声的判别，在不增加计算开销的前提下提高灵敏度。该架构支持跨多个探测器并行化处理。模型在统计上与真实O3数据一致的合成噪声上训练，并在真实O3数据及硬件注入信号上进行验证，测试范围包括训练自旋下降率范围之外的注入信号。

Result: 结果显示该方法在区分信号与噪声方面表现稳健，即使在低应变条件下也能保持良好判别性能。此外，该方法成功识别了位于训练自旋下降率范围之外的硬件注入信号，证明了模型的泛化能力和实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了神经网络分类器对全天空连续引力波搜索的有效增强作用，在不增加计算成本的前提下提升了灵敏度，并通过多探测器并行化增强了检测概率、降低了误报。在O3数据上的验证表明该方法对真实噪声环境具有鲁棒性，且具备超出训练参数范围的泛化能力，为未来引力波观测运行中更高效的隐藏中子星群探测提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Continuous gravitational waves are long-lived signals emitted by spinning neutron stars (NSs) or boson clouds around black holes. While the Milky Way is expected to host $\mathrm{O}(10^{8}-10^{9})$ NSs, only $\mathrm{O}(10^{3})$ are currently identified through electromagnetic observations. This large observational gap motivates searches based on alternative messengers. In particular, all-sky searches for continuous gravitational waves (GWs) offer a unique opportunity to detect NSs that are electromagnetically silent or otherwise undiscovered. By exploring a broad region of parameter space without any a priori source information, these searches can probe the vast hidden NS population of our Galaxy.
  In this work, we present a novel way to include a Neural Network (NN) classifier into an all-sky search strategy for isolated NSs. Starting from candidates produced by the FrequencyHough pipeline running on data from ground-based detectors such as LIGO and Virgo, the proposed method improves sensitivity without increasing computational cost and can be parallelized across multiple detectors to enhance detection probability and reduce false alarms.
  We analyze data from the third observing run (O3) --- April 1, 2019 to March 27, 2020 --- and focus on the frequency range [129, 229] Hz and spin-down interval [$-2.5\cdot10^{-9}, 1.0\cdot10^{-9}$] Hz/s. The model is trained on noise constructed to be statistically consistent with real O3 data and tested on real O3 data. Results show robust performance in distinguishing signal from noise, even at low strain, and successful identification of hardware injections outside the training spin-down range.

</details>


### [136] [Distinguishing lensing and precessional modulation in binary black-hole inspiral waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06347)
*Tien N. Nguyen-Vo,Tamanjyot Singh,Benjamin McKallip,Michael Kesden,Lindsay King*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个L形引力波探测器区分双黑洞（BBH）致密双星合并过程中两种信号调制来源的能力：一种是引力波被大尺度结构透镜化产生的多像干涉调制，另一种是由自旋与轨道角动量不对齐引起的进动调制。研究发现，当透镜导致的图像间时间延迟足够短（少于三个干涉条纹出现在探测器灵敏度带内）时，进动调制可以模拟两幅等放大倍数图像之间的透镜调制信号。由于中等红移下强透镜事件罕见，而BBH形成通道中不对齐自旋很常见，因此排除进动调制对于识别真正的透镜化系统至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBH引力波信号可能受到两种物理效应的调制：引力透镜产生的多像干涉，以及自旋不对齐引起的进动调制。这两种效应在波形上可能难以区分，因此需要研究单探测器是否能有效甄别这两种调制的来源，以避免将进动系统误判为透镜化系统。

Method: 通过理论分析和信号模拟，研究单L形探测器对两种调制来源（透镜干涉调制与进动调制）的可区分性。分析了干涉条纹数量与时间延迟的关系，并考察进动调制在何种参数条件下可能模仿透镜调制特征。
