The construction relies on a pullback theorem showing that perfectoid profinite étale towers are preserved under universally injective morphisms of rigid analytic spaces.

</details>


### [221] [The contraherent version of the theorem of Slavik and Stovicek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08491)
*Leonid Positselski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在非半分离的拟紧拟分离概形上，存在一个局部cotorsion的contraherent余层，它不拥有到任何局部内射的局部contraherent余层的容许单态射。构造与证明沿用了Slavik和Stovicek在arXiv:1902.05740中的论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数几何中，contraherent余层是对偶于拟凝聚层的一类对象。此前已知在非半分离概形上，局部内射quasi-coherent层可能不满足某些内射性性质。本文旨在通过构造反例，说明在非半分离概形上，局部cotorsion contraherent余层缺乏到局部内射对象的容许嵌入，从而揭示该范畴中内射对象性质的局限性。

Method: 作者参照Slavik和Stovicek在arXiv:1902.05740中的论证思路，在非半分离的拟紧拟分离概形X上具体构造一个局部cotorsion的contraherent余层，然后证明该对象不存在到任何局部内射局部contraherent余层的容许单态射，从而给出反例。

Result: 成功构造了一个局部cotorsion contraherent余层，它无法容许单态射地嵌入到任何局部内射的局部contraherent余层中。同时指出了互补的正结果（见arXiv:2603.27732v5第4.7节）：在有限Krull维数的Noether概形上，每个W-局部contraherent余层都可容许单态射地嵌入到某个局部cotorsion的W-局部contraherent余层中。

Conclusion: 该反例表明在非半分离概形上，局部cotorsion contraherent余层类不能作为局部内射类下的覆盖/包络结构的基础，说明"局部内射性"这一概念在半分离条件缺失时并不充分。互补的正结果则说明在有限维Noether概形上，通过W-局部化的框架可以恢复较好的容许嵌入性质。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a quasi-compact, quasi-separated scheme that is not semi-separated. We present a locally cotorsion contraherent cosheaf on $X$ that does not have an admissible monomorphism into any locally injective locally contraherent cosheaf. The construction and proof follow the arguments of Slavik and Stovicek in arXiv:1902.05740. A complementary positive result, claiming that every $\mathbf W$-locally contraherent cosheaf on a Noetherian scheme $X$ of finite Krull dimension has an admissible monomorphism into a locally cotorsion $\mathbf W$-locally contraherent cosheaf, can be found in the preprint arXiv:2603.27732v5, Section 4.7.

</details>


### [222] [q-Opers and Quantum/Classical Duality Beyond Type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04739)
*Peter Koroteev,Myungbo Shim,Rahul Singh*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过q-oper的语言，提出了量子XXZ自旋链的Bethe Ansatz方程与经典群上的多体三角Ruijsenaars-Schneider/Macdonald系统之间对偶性的代数几何描述。该对偶将B/C/D型经典哈密顿量的能级集与具有开边界条件的A型XXZ Bethe Ansatz方程解集一一对应，推广了之前GL(N) XXZ链与N体Ruijsenaars-Schneider系统对偶的结果，并在(q-oper)数据和N体模型两个层面实现了Z/2Z折叠，同时处理了非约化的BC型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将先前建立的GL(N) XXZ自旋链（带扭曲周期边界条件）与N体Ruijsenaars-Schneider系统之间的著名对偶性推广到经典群（B/C/D型）的情形，并为此提供严格的代数几何（q-oper）框架。

Method: 利用q-opers的代数几何语言，在GL(N) q-oper数据和N体三角Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型两个层面上执行Z/2Z折叠（folding）构造，从而建立开边界XXZ Bethe Ansatz方程与B/C/D型Ruijsenaars-Schneider/Macdonald哈密顿量能级集之间的双射关系。

Result: 建立了B/C/D型经典哈密顿量能量级集与开边界XXZ Bethe Ansatz方程解集之间的双射，将BC型非约化系统也纳入分析框架，系统地推广了GL(N)情形的已有对偶结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为XXZ自旋链与经典群Ruijsenaars-Schneider系统的对偶性提供了统一的代数几何描述，表明Z/2Z折叠机制是对偶关系在经典群情形下的核心结构。

Abstract: Using the language of q-opers, we propose an algebro-geometric description of the duality between Bethe Ansatz equations for quantum XXZ spin chains and many-body trigonometric Ruijsenaars-Schneider/Macdonald systems for classical groups. Upon this duality, the energy level sets of the classical B/C/D-type Hamiltonians are found to be in bijection with the set of solutions of the XXZ Bethe Ansatz equations of type A, albeit with open boundary conditions. This dictionary generalizes previous results in which a GL(N) XXZ spin chain with twisted periodic boundary conditions was dual to an N-body Ruijsenaars-Schneider system. Our construction implements a $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ folding both at the level of the (GL(N),q)-oper data as well as at the level of the N-body trigonometric Ruijsenaars-Schneider model. The nonreduced BC-type systems appear in our analysis as well.

</details>


### [223] [On the $a$-number of Fermat type function fields and some of their subfields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08565)
*Marie Frank vom Braucke*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文计算了Cartier算子作用在Hermitian函数域、Fermat型函数域以及Hurwitz函数域的全纯微分上的作用，并由此获得了这些函数域a-数的各种结果，包括对Kodama和Washio关于Fermat函数域结果的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数域F的a-数是Cartier算子作用于其全纯微分上的核的维数，这是一个重要的不变量。此前关于Fermat函数域的a-数已有Kodama和Washio的结果，作者希望将其推广到更广泛的函数域类型（如Hermitian、Fermat型和Hurwitz函数域），并系统计算Cartier算子的作用。

Method: 作者显式地构造并计算了Cartier算子作用于Hermitian函数域H、Fermat型函数域F_{n,m}以及Hurwitz函数域H_n的全纯微分上的具体作用形式，通过分析该算子的核维数来推导各函数域的a-数，并利用此方法推广了Kodama和Washio关于Fermat函数域的经典结论。

Result: 获得了Hermitian函数域、Fermat型函数域和Hurwitz函数域的a-数的具体结果，并将Kodama和Washio关于Fermat函数域的结论推广到了更一般的情形。

Conclusion: 通过显式计算Cartier算子在几类重要函数域上的作用，系统性地获得了这些函数域的a-数，推广了已有结果，为研究带较大p-秩亏空的函数域提供了新的工具和结论。

Abstract: The $a$-number of a function field $F$ is the dimension of the kernel of the Cartier operator acting on the holomorphic differentials of $F$. We compute the action of the Cartier operator on the holomorphic differentials on the Hermitian function field $\mathcal{H}$, function fields of Fermat type $\mathcal{F}_{n,m}$ and the Hurwitz function field $\mathcal{H}_n$. From this, we obtain various results about the $a$-number of these function fields, including a generalisation of the results on the Fermat function field from Kodama and Washio.

</details>


### [224] [Singularities of totally invariant hypersurfaces of endomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08633)
*Amaël Broustet,Andreas Höring*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究复射影流形上极化自同态的完全不变超曲面D，证明了D的奇异轨迹的每个不可约分量在D中余维数为1；特别地，若D是正规的，则D光滑。作为应用，证明了P^4上自同态的完全不变因子的线性性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全不变超曲面在极化自同态研究中是重要对象，其奇异性结构直接关系到不变量理论的分析。理解D的奇异轨迹的余维数是刻画D几何性质的关键，然而此前这一问题尚未被完整解决。

Method: 通过分析极化自同态在完全不变超曲面上的拉回结构，利用代数几何中关于奇异轨迹维数估计的工具，结合正规性的定义，证明了奇异轨迹每个不可约分量在D中余维数为1的结论；随后将该结论应用至P^4中完全不变因子的线性性证明。

Result: 证明了完全不变超曲面D的奇异轨迹的每个不可约分量在D中余维数恰为1，进而若D正规则D光滑；并在此框架下推导出P^4上完全不变因子的线性性。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了极化自同态完全不变超曲面的奇异性结构受到高度约束：奇异轨迹不能具有余维数大于1的分量。这一结构定理为后续在高维射影空间中完全不变因子的分类问题提供了关键工具。

Abstract: Let $f\colon X\to X$ be a polarised endomorphism of a complex projective manifold, and let $D\subset X$ be a reduced totally invariant hypersurface. We prove that every irreducible component of the singular locus of $D$ has codimension one in $D$. In particular, if $D$ is normal, then it is smooth. As an application we prove the linearity of totally invariant divisors for endomorphisms of $\mathbb P^4$.

</details>


### [225] [The derived length of subgroups of the Cremona group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08876)
*Jean-Philippe Furter,Isac Hedén*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明复数域上平面 Cremona 群中可解子群的最大导列长度为 5，其中有限可解子群的最大导列长度为 4，有界可解子群的紧界 5 也被给出简洁证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定平面 Cremona 群中可解子群的最大导列长度是一个基础而重要的问题，此前存在部分结果但未完整解决。

Method: 核心工具是一个中心化子定理：导列长度为 4 的有限可解子群的中心化子不含 loxodromic 元素。证明依赖于 Blanc 对极大代数子群的分类，并结合等变 Sarkisov 理论、轨道估计、超刚性以及亏格至少 2 的固定曲线等工具。

Result: 证明了任意可解子群的最大导列长度为 5，有限可解子群的最大导列长度为 4，并为已知的有界可解子群紧界 5 提供了简短证明。

Conclusion: 完整解决了平面 Cremona 群各类可解子群的导列长度上界问题。

Abstract: We prove that the maximal derived length of a solvable subgroup of the plane Cremona group over $\mathbb C$ is equal to $5$. We also show that the maximal derived length of a finite solvable subgroup is $4$, and give a short proof of the known sharp bound $5$ for bounded solvable subgroups. A major ingredient is a centraliser theorem: the centraliser of a finite solvable subgroup of derived length $4$ contains no loxodromic element. The proof relies on Blanc's classification of maximal algebraic subgroups and then uses equivariant Sarkisov theory, orbit estimates, superrigidity, and fixed curves of genus at least two.

</details>


### [226] [Derived invariance of Hodge numbers for smooth projective complex fivefolds with $K_X = 0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08917)
*Roland Abuaf*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有平凡典范丛的导出等价光滑射影复五维流形具有相同的霍奇数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导出等价流形是否具有相同霍奇数是一个基本问题。对一般维数或一般型流形这并不成立，但在特殊情形（如平凡典范丛）下需要探明其霍奇数的导出不变性。

Method: 将Hochschild同调的导出不变性与Mukai配对在个别Hochschild对角上的细化相结合。在复共轭复合后，该配对在奇数Hochschild度上给出被Fourier-Mukai等价保持的Hermite形式。利用Lefschetz分解与Hodge-Riemann双线性关系计算其符号，并结合适用于具有平凡第一Chern类的五维流形的Hirzebruch-Riemann-Roch关系。

Result: 证明了具有平凡典范丛的导出等价光滑射影复五维流形具有相同的霍奇数。

Conclusion: 通过Hochschild同调、Mukai配对的细化、Hermite形式的符号计算以及HRR关系，确立了五维平凡典范丛情形下霍奇数的导出不变性。

Abstract: We prove that derived-equivalent smooth projective complex fivefolds with trivial canonical bundle have the same Hodge numbers. The proof combines the derived invariance of Hochschild homology with a refinement of the Mukai pairing on the individual Hochschild diagonals. After composition with complex conjugation, the pairing gives rise, in odd Hochschild degree, to Hermitian forms preserved by Fourier-Mukai equivalences. Their signatures are computed using the Lefschetz decomposition and the Hodge-Riemann bilinear relations. Together with a Hirzebruch-Riemann-Roch relation specific to fivefolds with trivial first Chern class, this determines all Hodge numbers.

</details>


### [227] [Quasi-algebraic quantization for the B-twist Langlands TQFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09098)
*Eric Yen-Yo Chen,Emilio Franco*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是构造Kapustin-Witten B-扭变Langlands QFT的超全纯边界条件（BBB-branes）系列工作的第一部分。作者定义了Deligne模栈上的拟代数层范畴，作为Hitchin模栈的twistor空间的类比，并以此构造了Moore-Tachikawa范畴简化版本的表示，该构造受Ben-Zvi-Sakellaridis-Venkatesh的相对Langlands纲领启发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在系统性地构造Langlands量子场论（QFT）的超全纯边界条件族，即(BBB)-branes。这一构造需要将几何表示论、数学物理与相对Langlands纲领相结合，为理解Langlands对偶的几何本质提供新的工具。

Method: 作者首先在Deligne模栈上定义了拟代数层的范畴，将其作为Hitchin模栈的twistor空间的类比；随后利用该范畴构造了简化版Moore-Tachikawa范畴的表示，将边界条件的构造问题转化为代数几何范畴的表示论问题。

Result: 本文作为系列工作的第一部分，完成了基础性构造：定义了准代数层范畴并建立了它与简化Moore-Tachikawa范畴表示之间的联系，为后续构造完整的超全纯(BBB)-brane边界条件奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该工作为将相对Langlands纲领（Ben-Zvi-Sakellaridis-Venkatesh）的框架性与物理中的边界条件构造联系起来提供了新的路径，是构建几何Langlands对应中超全纯边界条件的系统化方案的第一步。

Abstract: This is the first part of a program to construct hyperholomorphic families of boundary conditions for the Kapustin--Witten B-twist of the Langlands QFT, otherwise known as \textit{(BBB)-branes}. We define the category of quasi-algebraic sheaves over the Deligne moduli stack, which serves as an analog of the twistor space of Hitchin's moduli stack. This allow us to construct a representation of a simplified version of the Moore--Tachikawa category which is motivated by the relative Langlands program in the sense of Ben-Zvi--Sakellaridis--Venkatesh.

</details>


### [228] [Automorphisms of toric varieties and Gale duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09051)
*Ivan Arzhantsev,Kirill Shakhmatov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对完备的环面三维流形X进行了分类，满足条件：自同构群Aut(X)在X上以开轨道作用，且该开轨道的补集中不含除子（即扇Σ_X中每个射线ρ都关联一个Demazure根）。文章还进一步从中找出Aut(X)在光滑点集X^reg上传递的那些流形，分类基于这些性质的Gale对偶解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究环面流形的自同构群作用性质，特别是自同构群是否具有大规模轨道和传递性，是代数几何中一个重要的结构性问题。本文关注开轨道补集不含除子的情形（即"几乎齐性"的加强版本），以及自同构群在光滑点集上的传递性，这些性质与流形的扇结构及Demazure根相关。

Method: 利用Gale对偶（Gale duality）这一组合工具，将环面流形扇的几何与组合性质转化为对偶层面的条件，从而对满足开轨道补集无除子的完备环面三维流形进行完整分类；在此基础上进一步筛选出自同构群在光滑点集上传递的例子。

Result: 给出了所有满足条件（对每条射线存在关联的Demazure根，即开轨道补集不含除子）的完备环面三维流形的完整分类，并从中确定了Aut(X)在光滑点集X^reg上传递的那些流形类别。

Conclusion: 通过Gale对偶的方法成功地实现了对这类环面三维流形的完全分类，揭示了自同构群作用性质与扇结构之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We classify complete toric threefolds $X$ such that the automorphism group $\text{Aut}(X)$ acts on $X$ with an open orbit whose complement does not contain a divisor. The latter condition means that for any ray $ρ$ of the fan $Σ_X$ there is a Demazure root of $Σ_X$ associated with $ρ$. We also find among these varieties those $X$ for which the group $\text{Aut}(X)$ is transitive on the smooth locus $X^{\text{reg}}$. The classifications are based on Gale-dual interpretations of these properties.

</details>


### [229] [Unramified cohomology and Brauer--Manin pairing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09127)
*Amalendu Krishna,Jitendra Rathore*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在适当的条件下，局部域或严格局部域上的光滑射影曲面X的ℓ-adic非分歧上同调H³_nr(X, Qℓ/Zℓ)的Tate模消失，并将其应用于回答X的开子簇上的Brauer-Manin配对左右核的相关问题。为了证明消失定理，作者在半稳定模型上建立了余维数二循环的Chow群的Bloch公式，并推导出该循环到模型闭纤维的限制映射的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在研究算术代数几何中的核心问题：光滑射影曲面在局部域或严格局部域上的非分歧上同调的结构，特别是其Tate模的消失性。这一结果与Brauer-Manin配对的性质密切相关，有助于理解开子簇上的局部全局原理（local-global principle）的障碍。

Method: 本文方法分为两步：（1）在半稳定模型上建立余维数二循环的Chow群的Bloch公式，这推广了经典Bloch公式到半稳定（非光滑）模型的情形；（2）利用该Bloch公式推导余维数二循环到模型闭纤维限制映射的性质，进而证明H³_nr(X, Qℓ/Zℓ)的Tate模在适当条件下消失。

Result: 在适当的条件下（局部或严格局部域上光滑射影曲面，剩余特征与ℓ互素），H³_nr(X, Qℓ/Zℓ)的Tate模消失。这一结果被应用于回答Brauer-Manin配对的开子簇情形的左右核问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立半稳定模型上的Bloch公式这一关键技术工具，成功证明了非分歧上同调Tate模的消失定理，并将其应用于Brauer-Manin配对核的研究，深化了对算术曲面算术性质的理解。

Abstract: We show that the Tate module of the $\ell$-adic unramified cohomology
  $H^3_{\nr}(X, {\Q_\ell}/{\Z_\ell})$ of
  a smooth projective surface $X$ over a local or strictly local field $k$ of
  residue characteristic prime to $\ell$ vanishes under suitable conditions.
  We apply this to answer some questions concerning the left and
  right kernels of the Brauer--Manin pairing for open subvarieties of $X$.
  To prove the vanishing theorem, we establish Bloch's formula for the Chow group of
  codimension-two cycles on a semistable model of $X$, and derive applications to
  the restriction map for such cycles to the closed
  fiber of the model.

</details>


### [230] [On the size of the fibers of a morphism of algebraic varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09128)
*Abdelkader Mokkadem*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于代数簇之间的态射，具有给定纤维大小（整数或无穷）的所有点构成的集合是可构造集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数簇态射中纤维的基数性质在Zariski拓扑下的可构造性，这是代数几何中关于纤维结构和层的标准性质问题。

Method: 对代数簇态射的纤维大小进行分层，利用代数几何中构造性集合的性质（可通过有限并、补和闭包操作生成），证明每个保持固定纤维基数m的点集均可构造。

Result: 对于任何整数m或m为无穷的情况，所有纤维大小恰为m的点所组成的集合都是可构造集。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了代数簇态射纤维基数可作为可构造层看待，为后续几何与应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Let u be a morphism of algebraic varieties. Given m integer or infinite, we prove that the set of points whose fiber has size m is a constructible set.

</details>


### [231] [The border Waring rank of $x_1\cdots x_n$ is $2^{n-1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09141)
*Jong In Han*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特征为零的域上，单项式$x_1x_2\cdots x_n$的边界Waring秩恰好为$2^{n-1}$。由此可知经典的极化恒等式即使容许取极限，也是最优的Waring分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定特定张量（尤其是单项式张量）的边界Waring秩，以验证是否存在比经典极化恒等式更优（在容许极限意义下）的分解。

Method: 将单项式视为对称张量，对应到$n\times n$永久张量；但下界证明基于$n\times n$行列式张量的高阶Koszulflattening，尽管行列式张量并非对称张量。

Result: 边界Waring秩下界和上界匹配，精确值为$2^{n-1}$，极化恒等式为最优分解。

Conclusion: 用非对称张量（行列式张量）的flattening方法成功证明了对称张量（永久张量/单项式对应张量）的边界Waring秩下界，说明该技术手段具有跨类别的适用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the border Waring rank of $x_1x_2\cdots x_n$ over fields of characteristic zero is exactly $2^{n-1}$. As a consequence, the classical polarization identity is an optimal Waring decomposition even if we allow limits. As a symmetric tensor, this monomial is identified with the $n\times n$ permanent tensor. However, the lower bound is proved via the higher-order Koszul flattening of the $n\times n$ determinant tensor, which is not symmetric.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Cosmology, Cluster Algebras, and $\boldsymbol{u}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05606)
*Daniel Glazer,Austin Joyce*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探索了德西特空间中共形耦合标量场的波函数与关联函数中蕴含的簇代数结构，通过u-变量将宇宙学运动学与簇变量联系起来，揭示了链图与圈图分别对应A型和B型簇代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将宇宙学中的散射振幅/波函数结构与数学中的簇代数联系起来，旨在建立运动学变量与簇变量之间的映射，并利用簇相容性条件来引导(bootstrap)符号，从而深入理解宇宙学关联函数的代数结构。

Method: 利用在宇宙学图和簇代数两侧都出现的u-变量作为桥梁。由于u-变量参数化刚性空间，自然地将宇宙学图中产生的u-变量与簇代数中出现的u-变量等同起来，从而建立运动学变量到簇变量的映射。对于n-site链图得到A_{2n-2}型簇代数，对于n-site圈图动量积分得到B_{2n-1}型簇代数。利用簇相容性条件约束符号的可能条目并用于bootstrap符号。

Result: 链图情形下，宇宙学波函数和低点函数的乘积都满足簇相容性，且关联函数是特定组合，因此波函数与关联函数共享相同的簇结构。圈图情形下，波函数被簇结构唯一选取，而关联函数不呈现与波函数相同的相容性。

Conclusion: 链图与圈图的波函数和关联函数展现出不同的簇结构行为：链图情形两者共有簇结构，而圈图情形波函数由簇结构唯一确定但关联函数不满足相同的相容性，表明宇宙学波函数的代数结构在圈图中更为深刻地被簇代数所约束。

Abstract: We explore the cluster algebra structures present in the wavefunction and correlators of conformally coupled scalar fields in de Sitter space. To connect cosmological kinematics to cluster variables, we utilize the so-called $u$-variables that appear on both sides. Since $u$-variables parameterize a rigid space, it is natural to identify the ones that appear from cosmological graphs and those that arise from cluster algebras. This provides a mapping between kinematic variables and cluster variables. For $n$-site chain Feynman graphs, this gives a coordinate-invariant relation to $A_{2n-2}$-type cluster algebras, while in the case of $n$-site cycle graph momentum integrands, the relevant cluster algebra is $B_{2n-1}$, as found previously. Since kinematic variables are mapped to nonlinear combinations of cluster variables, cluster compatibility imposes strong conditions on possible symbol entries. We use this notion of compatibility to bootstrap the symbol. In the chain case, both the cosmological wavefunction and products of lower-point functions satisfy cluster compatibility, with the correlator a particular combination, so that the wavefunction and correlator share the same cluster structure. In the cycle case, however, the wavefunction appears to be uniquely selected by the cluster structure, and the correlator does not display the same compatibility as the wavefunction.

</details>


### [233] [Matrix Holography on an Optical Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05613)
*Robin Löwenberg,Julian Sonner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原子系综晶格与时间平均Floquet动力学的模拟量子模拟协议，用于构造玻色型BFSS类矩阵量子力学中的四体势能项。与需要深电路的基于门的数字实现不同，该方法通过每个Floquet循环中固定的控制阶段序列生成目标模型，使连续酉操作数量不依赖于矩阵尺寸N。主要缩放成本在于控制增长晶格所需的频率范围，该成本至多以O(N³)增长，为大规模N提供了多项式缩放的实验路径。此外，耦合参数可调至O(1)以上，从而支持全息相所需强耦合物理的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于门的数字量子模拟需要深电路来实现BFSS类矩阵量子力学中的四体相互作用，随着矩阵尺寸N增大会面临严重的实现困难。作者旨在开发一种模拟量子模拟方案，能够在保持固定电路深度的同时有效构造四体势，并能够达到全息对应所需的强耦合参数区域。

Method: 利用原子系综组成的晶格结构，通过时间平均的Floquet动力学协议来合成四体相互作用势。每个Floquet周期内使用固定数量的控制阶段，不随矩阵大小N增长。通过控制晶格所需的频率范围来管理主要资源开销，其增长不超过O(N³)，并通过调整参数实现超过O(1)的耦合强度。

Result: 证明了该模拟协议能够构造BFSS型矩阵量子力学中的四体势项，其电路深度（连续酉操作数）对N保持恒定，频率控制的资源开销以O(N³)多项式增长，且耦合参数可以调节到O(1)以上以进入强耦合区域。

Conclusion: 提出的模拟量子模拟方案为在大规模N下实现BFSS类矩阵量子力学提供了一条可扩展的多项式路径，并具备进入强耦合/全息相的能力，相较数字量子模拟具有显著的实验可行性优势。

Abstract: We propose an analog quantum-simulation protocol based on a lattice of atomic ensembles and time-averaged Floquet dynamics to construct the four-body potentials that typically appear in bosonic BFSS-like matrix quantum mechanics. In contrast to gate-based digital implementations requiring deep circuits, our approach generates the target model through a fixed set of control stages per Floquet cycle, keeping the number of consecutive unitaries constant with respect to the matrix size $N$. The principal scaling cost in our construction is given by the frequency range required to control the growing lattice. We show that this cost grows at most as $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$, giving a polynomial scaling route to large$-N$, attractive to experimental implementation. Additionally, we demonstrate that the coupling parameter can be tuned beyond $\mathcal{O}(1)$, enabling the simulation of strong-coupling physics necessary for holographic phases.

</details>


### [234] [Equation of State of a Strongly Coupled Perfect Fluid with Spin from Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05630)
*Andres Anabalon,Horatiu Nastase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用全息原理推导了2+1维强耦合相对论完美流体的有限自旋状态方程，通过AdS4中的旋转黑洞描述其热力学性质，发现存在一个临界旋转速度，超过该速度流体将失去热力学稳定性，给出了强耦合夸克胶子等离子体的旋转界限。此外，同一引力解的第二个边界对应一个非旋转黑洞，但其对偶流体旋转并具有"奇特"状态方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立强耦合物质含有限自旋（内禀角动量）时的全息状态方程，并厘清黑洞旋转、内禀角动量与相对论流体热力学稳定性之间的直接联系，为有限旋转夸克胶子等离子体提供理论约束。

Method: 采用全息对偶方法：利用AdS4中的旋转黑洞作为引力背景，由该黑洞的霍金温度、角速度和贝肯斯坦-霍金熵确定边界流体（2+1维强耦合相对论完美流体）的热力学性质，进而推导状态方程并分析局部热力学稳定性；同时考察同一引力解中的第二个（非旋转）黑洞对偶的"奇特"状态方程。

Result: 获得有限自旋流体的状态方程；发现存在临界旋转速度，当4πT/ω>2.77239时流体保持稳定，超过该界限流体变得热力学不稳定。第二种（非旋转）黑洞所对偶的旋转流体具有不同的"奇特"状态方程。

Conclusion: 全息方法成功给出了强耦合有限旋转物质的状态方程，建立了黑洞旋转与边界流体有限自旋的热力学联系，并为强耦合夸克胶子等离子体的旋转幅度提供了明确的稳定性上界。

Abstract: We derive the equation of state of a strongly coupled relativistic perfect fluid with finite spin in $2+1$ dimensions using holography. The dual gravitational description is provided by a spinning black hole in $AdS_4$, whose Hawking temperature, angular velocity, and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy determine the thermodynamic properties of the boundary fluid. We obtain the equation of state and analyze its local thermodynamic stability, finding a critical rotation above which the fluid becomes thermodynamically unstable providing a bound for the rotation of a strongly coupled quark gluon plasma at temperature $T$ and angular velocity $ω$ given by $\frac{4πT}ω> 2.77239$. Remarkably, the same gravitational solution contains a second black hole associated with a second boundary. Although this black hole is non-spinning, its dual fluid rotates and possesses a distinct, ``exotic'' equation of state. Our results provide a holographic equation of state for strongly coupled matter with finite spin and establish a direct connection between black-hole rotation, intrinsic angular momentum, and the thermodynamic stability of relativistic fluids.

</details>


### [235] [Birth and Death in Two-color ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05653)
*Marco S. Bianchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了有限秩U(2)×U(2) ABJM理论中half-BPS算符的两点度规在领头阶量子变形下的形式。在自然的耦合无关Schur框架下，其树级归一化的两圈修正是一个作用于双行杨图链上的Jacobi算子。通过基态变换可将其映射为可逆生灭过程，其平稳分布是限定在至多两行图上的Plancherel测度。该算子的本征模为Racah多项式，其大n极限趋近于径向Ornstein-Uhlenbeck动力学，等价的二自旋描述将该Jacobi算子识别为受限SU(2) Casimir算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABJM理论中half-BPS算子空间上的量子度规（即关联函数）是理解该理论在有限秩下可积结构与量子几何的重要对象。作者的目标是超越已有的平面极限/大N近似，解析求解包含量子修正的精确两点度量结构。

Method: 论文采用Schur框架（耦合无关的基），将两圈树级归一化修正精确表示为作用于双行杨图链上的Jacobi算子。通过基态变换（ground-state transform）将问题转化为可逆生灭过程，利用Plancherel测度作为平稳分布，并通过Racah多项式对角化该算子。还借助二自旋表示将Jacobi算子联系到受限SU(2) Casimir，从而从群论角度给出等价描述。

Result: 得到了精确的量子变形两圈修正闭合表达式；确立了本征谱（Racah多项式）与平稳分布（条件Plancherel测度）；展示了与径向Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的联系及其大n极限行为；建立了与受限SU(2) Casimir的等价性。

Conclusion: 本文的工作为ABJM理论在有限秩下的精确量子度量提供了完整的解析框架，揭示了杨图组合学、概率论（生灭过程/Plancherel测度）、特殊函数（Racah多项式）与随机过程（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck）之间的深刻联系，为后续研究非平面修正的精确可解结构奠定了基础。

Abstract: We solve exactly the leading quantum deformation of the two-point metric of half-BPS operators in $U(2)\times U(2)$ ABJM theory at finite rank. In the natural coupling-independent Schur frame, its tree-normalized two-loop correction is a Jacobi operator on the chain of two-row Young diagrams. A ground-state transform maps it to a reversible birth--death process whose stationary distribution is the Plancherel measure conditioned on diagrams with at most two rows. Its eigenmodes are Racah polynomials, its large-$n$ limit approaches radial Ornstein--Uhlenbeck dynamics, and an equivalent two-spin description identifies the Jacobi operator with a restricted $SU(2)$ Casimir.

</details>


### [236] [Searching for Bell-CHSH-Violating Bose Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05685)
*T. Newton,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在相对论自由玻色场中，通过精确谱纤维方法显式构造了满足Bell不等式所需的有限楔局域可观测量，实现了从经典正则对到可测纤维对合的转化，并给出算子代数层面的严格证明（楔局域性、自伴性、酉性与精确反对易性）。平移胞可观测量产生显式Bell违背（带受控无穷尾误差），而引入反射分支后违背量显著增大，表明简单的有界局域可观测量可在相对论QFT中构造性揭示Bell非局域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论量子场论中是否存在简单的有界局域可观测量能够构造性地展现Bell非局域性，是一个长期悬而未决的基础问题。此前相关工作往往依赖非构造性或无穷维的可观测量，作者希望提供一种显式、可验证的构造方案，同时消除对平移胞结果是否为数值假象的疑虑。

Method: 采用精确谱纤维（exact spectral-fibre）方法：将自由玻色场中的正则对（canonical pair）转化为可测的纤维对合（fibre involutions），从而构造有界的楔局域可观测量。通过算子代数框架严格证明楔局域性、自伴性、酉性及精确反对易关系。进一步构造平移胞（translation-cell）型可观测量并给出含无穷尾误差的Bell违背估计，再引入反射分支（reflection branches）以增强违背幅度。

Result: 平移胞可观测量给出了显式的Bell违背，且误差由可控的无穷尾项支配；引入反射分支后Bell违背显著增大。所有构造均满足楔局域性、自伴性、酉性与精确反对易性，提供了算子代数层面的严格证明。

Conclusion: 该工作表明简单有界的局域可观测量即可在相对论量子场论中构造性地暴露Bell非局域性，并且平移胞结果并非数值假象，而是一个严格有效的下界。

Abstract: We construct explicit bounded wedge-local observables for Bell inequalities in a relativistic free Bose field using an exact spectral-fibre approach. The construction turns the relevant canonical pair into measurable fibre involutions and provides an operator-algebraic proof of wedge locality, self-adjointness, unitarity, and exact anticommutation. Translation-cell observables yield an explicit Bell violation with a controlled infinite-tail error, while inclusion of reflection branches produces a substantially larger violation. These results demonstrate that simple bounded local observables can expose Bell nonlocality constructively in relativistic quantum field theory and that the translation-cell result is not artifact but a strictly valid lower bound.

</details>


### [237] [Landau and cluster structures of one-loop amplitudes in ${\mathcal N}=4$ SYM in dimensional regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05716)
*Athanasia-Konstantina Angelopoulou,Ruth Britto,Matteo Parisi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究N=4超对称Yang-Mills（SYM）理论中一圈平面振幅的广义幺正性、Landau分析与簇邻接之间的深层联系。通过任意重数和螺旋度下的一圈振幅，揭示了权重二符号的分解结构，证明了具有标志簇代数形式的簇邻接性质，并提出代数四质量字母的Sklyanin括号模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射振幅的三个重要框架——广义幺正性、Landau分析和簇邻接各自独立地描述了振幅的不同方面，但它们之间的接口关系此前并不清楚。作者希望通过具体计算（一环路N=4 SYM振幅）来显式建立这些框架之间的关联，从而深化对振幅结构本质的理解。

Method: 作者对N=4 SYM中任意多重性和螺旋度的一圈平面振幅进行维度正则化分析。具体方法包括：(1) 将权重二符号分解为LS部分（由最大割主导奇异性提供系数、嵌套割的Landau轨迹组织符号条目）、代数四质量扇区和残余项；(2) 利用BCFW类似方式的整体留数定理证明两质量三角形关系（box系数间的恒等式），并将其几何理解为树级amplituhedron中同一区域的分解；(3) 证明完整有理符号满足F_{2,4;n}标志簇代数中的簇邻接；(4) 在仅依赖动量扭量四括号的扇区内猜想更强的簇性质并对其NMHV振幅证明。

Result: 作者证明了两质量三角形关系的成立，给出了它们在amplituhedron中的几何解释；证明了完整有理符号（包括红外发散部分）对所有重数和螺旋度均满足标志簇代数Fl_{2,4;n}的簇邻接；对NMHV振幅证明了Gr(4,n)中更强的簇性质（每个系数的极点与符号条目兼容）；并观察到代数四质量字母呈现Sklyanin括号模式。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统性地将广义幺正性、Landau分析和簇邻接三种框架统一到N=4 SYM的一圈平面振幅中，建立了这些看似独立概念之间的显式联系。两质量三角形关系的几何解释以及簇邻接对红外发散部分的推广具有重要意义，同时代数四质量字母中的Sklyanin括号结构暗示了更深层的数学结构。

Abstract: Generalized unitarity, Landau analysis, and cluster adjacency encode complementary aspects of scattering amplitudes. We use one-loop planar $\mathcal N=4$ SYM amplitudes in dimensional regularization, at arbitrary multiplicity and helicity, to make their interface explicit. The weight-two symbol decomposes into an LS part, in which maximal-cut leading singularities furnish the coefficients and Landau loci associated with nested cuts organize the ordered symbol entries, an algebraic four-mass sector, and residual terms. Cancellations of certain letters contributed by individual box integrals, as well as further simplifications, are explained by the two-mass triangle relations among box coefficients. We prove these relations using a BCFW-like application of the global residue theorem and show that they can be understood geometrically as different dissections of the same region in the tree amplituhedron obtained by projecting the loop geometry of a triple cut. We then prove that the full rational symbol, including its infrared-divergent part, obeys cluster adjacency in the flag cluster algebra $\mathrm{Fl}_{2,4;n}$ for all multiplicities and helicities. Within the sector depending only on momentum-twistor four-brackets, we conjecture a stronger cluster property in $\operatorname{Gr}(4,n)$ for all helicities and prove it for NMHV amplitudes: the amplitude admits a representation in which every pole of each coefficient is compatible with both symbol entries. Finally, we observe that the algebraic four-mass letters, although non-rational, exhibit a suggestive Sklyanin-bracket pattern.

</details>


### [238] [Non-critical M-Theory and the Resolved Conifold: Refinement and Nonperturbative Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06075)
*Fengjun Xu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Hořava–Keeler对应（有限温度非临界M理论与resolved-conifold A模型的对应）推广到refined情形。通过静态旋转的非临界M理论真空的grand potential精确重现refined Gopakumar–Vafa展开，并建立了与Hattab–Palti及Chuang提出的非微扰conifold完备化的精确匹配，为该完备化提供了微观谱实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将已有的Hořava–Keeler对应从非refined推广到refined理论，以连接弦理论中的拓扑弦A模型与M理论更丰富的物理内容，并通过非临界M理论的谱和resolvent为conifold的非微扰完备化提供微观解释。

Method: 作者引入静态旋转非临界M理论真空，其角向化学势将Ω背景从自对偶点(ε1=-ε2)变形。通过双层descent推导grand potential的refined展开，并将其与Hattab–Palti和Chuang各自的非微扰完备化进行逐项比较，同时利用单粒子Schwinger积分的integration cycle作为桥梁，从M理论谱和resolvent实现完备化的微观对应。

Result: 成功建立refined Hořava–Keeler对应：非临界M理论的grand potential重现refined Gopakumar–Vafa展开；在有限热半径下与非微扰conifold完备化精确匹配。作者确定了非恒定BPS扇区和三次局部贡献，指出剩余的多项式ambiguity与模无关的constant-map扇区需单独规范化，并阐明resolved conifold单一primitive spinless多重态使得该识别成为可能的机制。

Conclusion: 该工作为非临界M理论与拓扑弦A模型之间的对应提供了refined推广并给出了微观谱实现，展示了resolved conifold的特殊结构（单一自旋less多重态）是该对应成功的关键。未来要推广到更一般的局部Calabi–Yau几何，需要补充额外的电荷和自旋依赖的微观数据。

Abstract: We extend the Hořava--Keeler correspondence between finite-temperature non-critical M-theory and the resolved-conifold A-model to the refined theory. The grand potential of the stationary rotating non-critical M-theory vacuum reproduces the refined Gopakumar--Vafa expansion, with the angular chemical potential deforming the $Ω$-background away from the self-dual locus $\eps_1=-\eps_2$. At finite thermal radius, we establish an exact match with the nonperturbative conifold completions proposed by Hattab--Palti and by Chuang in the unrefined and refined cases, respectively. Both the one-particle Schwinger integrand and its integration cycle arise from the non-critical M-theory spectrum and resolvent, providing a microscopic spectral realization of these completions. The correspondence fixes the nonconstant BPS sector and the cubic local contribution, while the remaining polynomial ambiguity and the modulus-independent constant-map sector require separate normalization. We explain why the single primitive spinless multiplet of the resolved conifold makes this identification possible, and discuss what additional charge- and spin-dependent microscopic data would be needed for an extension to more general local Calabi--Yau geometries.

</details>


### [239] [Spherical DAHA as an algebra of framed BPS states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07911)
*Kunal Gupta,Pietro Longhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将4d N=2类S理论（A型）中半Omega背景下的线算符与SL_N skein代数的对应扩展至GL_N skein代数，并证明刺穿环面的GL_2 skein代数同构于GL_2球面双重仿射Hecke代数SḢ^{q,t}_2。构造基于SU(2) N=2^*规范理论的q-非阿贝尔化，将skein代数实现为与Seiberg-Witten曲线相关的量子环面代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有工作建立了类S理论线算符与SL_N skein代数的联系，本文旨在将该对应推广到GL_N情形，并建立GL_2 skein代数与球面双重仿射Hecke代数(DAHA)之间的严格同构，从而统一不同的DAHA表示。

Method: 采用q-非阿贝尔化方法，基于SU(2) N=2^*规范理论的Seiberg-Witten几何构造量子环面代数。通过分析Coulomb branch的不同区域（Fenchel-Nielsen弱耦合图表与Fock-Goncharov强耦合图表）获得SḢ^{q,t}_2的不同表示，并利用framed wall-crossing与vanilla BPS谱来连接这些不同描述。

Result: 证明了刺穿环面的GL_2 skein代数与GL_2球面双重仿射Hecke代数SḢ^{q,t}_2之间的同构关系，并展示了该代数在Coulomb branch不同区域呈现出从Macdonald q-差分模到cluster型实现等多种表示形式。

Conclusion: N=2^*理论的BPS谱与framed wall-crossing为球面DAHA的多种表示提供了一个统一的物理框架，建立了物理理论与代数表示论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Line operators of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ theories of class $\mathcal{S}$ of type $A_{N-1}$ in a half Omega background provide a physical model for $SL_N$ skein algebras. In this paper we extend this correspondence to $GL_N$ skein algebras and prove that the $GL_2$ skein algebra of the punctured torus is isomorphic to the $GL_2$ spherical double affine Hecke algebra ${{\mathbf{S}\ddot{\mathbf{H}}}}^{q,t}_2$. The construction is based on $\mathfrak{q}$-nonabelianization for the $SU(2)$ $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ gauge theory, which realizes the skein algebra as a quantum torus algebra associated with the Seiberg-Witten curve. Different regions of the Coulomb branch yield distinct presentations of ${{\mathbf{S}\ddot{\mathbf{H}}}}^{q,t}_2$. These range from the Macdonald $q$-difference module in Fenchel-Nielsen (weak coupling) charts to cluster-type realizations in Fock-Goncharov (strong coupling) charts. Transitions between these descriptions are governed by the vanilla BPS spectrum of the $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ theory via framed wall-crossing, which provides a unified physical framework for several representations of spherical DAHA.

</details>


### [240] [The Wilson-line-dressed charged sector of scalar QED: superselection and the infraparticle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06224)
*Gordon W. Semenoff,Conor Waterfield*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种显式规范与洛伦兹不变的微扰计算框架，研究标量量子电动力学中Wilson线修饰标量场算子所创造的带电态在有限时间与距离下的演化。通过Osterwalder-Schrader反射与欧几里得粘合，将两个修饰态的重叠表示为欧几里得二点函数并在重整化微扰论中评价，其中以Stueckelberg光子质量作为规范、BRST与洛伦兹不变的红外截断。主要发现：非平行Wilson线修饰的态呈“云正交”，重叠度随红外调节器幂次消失，指数仅依赖于两条修饰线的夹角，是尖点反常维数的红外类比且当带电物质有质量隙时一圈精确；带电态因此按修饰取向被超选择。在超选择扇区内二点函数红外有限但表现出infraparticle，质壳极点被支割边缘取代；带电修饰的渐近扩展在固有时上呈幂律，指数一圈精确且依赖于修饰与运动方向。云正交性与infraparticle对基本红外截断都极其敏感，但infraparticle标度律在较宽的固有时窗口内仍然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电态的量子电动力学（QED）红外结构长期以来主要依赖非微扰或代数论证，缺乏显式且可计算的方法来研究带电态的时间/距离演化、红外奇异性与超选择结构。作者旨在建立一个规范与洛伦兹不变的微扰框架，以封闭形式揭示带电态的结构特征。

Method: 采用Wilson线修饰的标量场算子构造带电态；通过Osterwalder-Schrader反射与欧几里得粘合将态重叠转化为欧几里得二点函数；引入Stueckelberg光子质量作为规范、BRST与洛伦兹不变的红外截断；在重整化微扰论中闭环计算。

Result: （1）非平行Wilson线修饰的态呈云正交，重叠度以红外调节器幂次衰减，指数仅取决于两线夹角，一圈精确（当物质有质量隙时）；（2）带电态按修饰方向被超选择；（3）超选择扇区内二点函数红外有限但呈infraparticle，质壳极点被支割起点替代；（4）带电电荷在渐近区域的扩展呈固有时幂律，指数一圈精确且依赖于修饰与运动方向；（5）infraparticle标度律在宽固有时窗口内稳健成立。

Conclusion: 本文提供了量子电动力学带电态红外结构的首个显式、闭式的微扰计算，弥合了此前代数/非微扰论证与可计算框架之间的鸿沟，并揭示了云正交性、超选择与infraparticle之间的精确数学关系，为理解QED中带电粒子物理提供了新的定量视角。

Abstract: We develop a manifestly gauge and Lorentz invariant perturbative calculus to study the finite time and distance evolution of the electrically charged states created by Wilson-line-dressed scalar field operators in scalar quantum electrodynamics. By an Osterwalder-Schrader reflection and Euclidean gluing, the overlap of two dressed states is expressed as a Euclidean 2 point function of dressed operators, which is then evaluated in renormalized perturbation theory with a Stueckelberg photon mass as a gauge, BRST and Lorentz invariant infrared cutoff. This renders explicit and computable in closed form several structural features of the charged sector that have so far been accessible mainly through non-perturbative or algebraic arguments. We find that states dressed by non-parallel Wilson lines are ``cloud orthogonal". Their overlap vanishes as a power of the infrared regulator, with an exponent that depends only on the angle between the two dressings and is an infrared analogue of the cusp anomalous dimension with the difference that it is one-loop exact whenever the charged matter is mass-gapped. Charged states are thereby superselected by the orientation of their dressing. Within a superselection sector the 2 point function is infrared finite but exhibits the infraparticle. The mass shell pole is replaced by the edge of a branch cut. As a result, the asymptotic spread of a dressed charge is a power law in proper time with a one-loop-exact computable exponent depending on the dressing and on the direction of motion. We show that although cloud orthogonality and the infraparticle are each extremely sensitive to fundamental infrared cutoffs, the infraparticle scaling law persists across a wide window of proper time.

</details>


### [241] [Finite stress-tensor moment tomography of boundary-graviton coherences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06363)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究2+1维爱因斯坦引力中，位于渐近边界的观察者如何通过边界应力张量的有限阶矩（tomography）来探测边界引力子态（Virasoro后裔）。结果表明：对于能级N≥2的后裔态，N阶应力矩断层扫描信息不完整，而N+1阶则信息完整；在完整左右理论以及可半经典解释的重扇区中同样存在精确的阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2+1维引力中不存在局部体引力子，但AdS_3引力在Brown-Henneaux边界条件下存在由Virasoro后裔描述的边界引力子。理解边界观察者如何通过探测这些态来获取信息是一个基本问题，特别是确定用有限阶次应力张量矩进行断层扫描时信息完整性的精确阈值。

Method: 作者固定一个最高权重模，假设Shapovalov形式非退化，并假设被探测的未知态具有已知的有限后裔支撑且层间相干性为未知数据。通过分析计算不同阶次（N阶与N+1阶）应力张量插入对应的断层扫描是否信息完整（IC），并使用密度矩阵分块重构的方法给出精确阈值。

Result: 对于能级N≥2的后裔态，N阶应力矩扫描在信息上不完整（正则幺正曲线上，正交纯态可具有相同的完整N阶记录），而N+1阶扫描信息完整。对能级至L≥2的精确有限窗口结果，L阶不完整而L+1阶可恢复每个密度矩阵分块。左右全理论和重扇区（半经典体解释成立时）也有对应精确阈值，后者给出了有限支撑边界引力子态首次可区分的阶次。

Conclusion: 研究确立了AdS_3边界引力子态断层扫描中信息完整性的精确临界阶次，即需要比目标后裔能级高一阶的应力矩才能实现信息的完整恢复，并在半经典重扇区中给出了态可区分性的明确边界。

Abstract: Einstein gravity in 2+1 dimensions is a topological theory that does not admit the existence of local bulk gravitons. However, $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ gravity with Brown-Henneaux boundary conditions does admit the existence of boundary gravitons, which are described by Virasoro descendants. An observer living on the asymptotic boundary can use finite order moments of the boundary stress tensor as a probe of these states. We fix one highest-weight module and assume that the Shapovalov forms are nondegenerate. We also assume that the unknown state the observer is probing has a known finite descendant support and that interlevel coherence is part of the unknown data. We find that, given a descendant at level $N\geq2$, stress moment tomography at order $N$ (meaning $N$ stress moment insertions) is informationally incomplete, while tomography with stress moments of order $N+1$ is informationally complete. This is to say that, on the unitary regular locus, orthogonal pure states can have the same complete order-$N$ record. Likewise, for exact finite-window result for levels through $L\geq2$, tomography of order $L$ is incomplete while tomography of order $L+1$ recovers every density-matrix block. Corresponding exact thresholds hold in the full left-right theory. In heavy sectors for which a semiclassical bulk interpretation exists, these thresholds say when finite-support boundary-graviton states first become distinguishable.

</details>


### [242] [Fermionic hidden zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06552)
*Yong Zhang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现并证明了树级振幅中一类普遍的"隐藏零点"：在D=4,6,10维中，具有任意偶数个无质量实伴随费米子和胶子的颜色序规范理论振幅在特定运动学形态（矩形运动学位形+物种依赖的桥条件）下 vanish。该结果通过世界sheet分析在任意多重度上成立，其普适性源于局域融合道 ff→g、fg/gf→f、gg→g，并推广到含引力子/引力微子的十维振幅及单迹 Einstein-Yang-Mills 振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知的隐藏零点主要针对纯胶子（玻色子）振幅，本文旨在揭示并严格证明包含无质量费米子的树级振幅中是否存在类似的结构化零点，从而深化对规范与引力振幅隐藏零点普适性的理解。

Method: 采用世界sheet（弦论）分析，在任意多重度下系统性地构造一般矩形运动学形态（rectangular kinematic loci）附加物种依赖的桥条件，分别处理费米子-费米子桥（由矢量流χγ^μ χ控制）、混合桥及胶子桥（由规范协变收缩决定），并推广至含引力子/引力微子及伴随胶微子的情形。

Result: 证明了对任意偶数费米子数及任意排列，颜色序规范振幅在D=4,6,10中于上述运动学形态处 vanish；零点的普适性追溯到三种局域融合道 ff→g、fg/gf→f、gg→g，且更高费米子多重度不产生新的原始桥结构。同样机制延拓至十维振幅（含引力子及任意偶数引力微子，在因式化γ-迹零极化下）及单迹 EYM 振幅。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了规范与引力振幅中费米子隐藏零点背后存在有限的局域结构，表明隐藏零点并非玻色子振幅的偶然性质，而是一普适现象，可由局域融合道统一理解。

Abstract: We uncover and prove a general class of hidden zeros in tree-level amplitudes with massless fermions. For arbitrary even numbers and arbitrary arrangements of massless real adjoint fermions and gluons, color-ordered gauge-theory amplitudes in $D=4,6,10$ vanish on general rectangular kinematic loci supplemented by species-dependent bridge conditions. The fermion--fermion bridge is governed simply by the vector current $χ_iγ^μχ_j$, while mixed and gluonic bridges are fixed by the corresponding gauge-covariant contractions. A worldsheet analysis proves the result at arbitrary multiplicity and traces its universality to the local fusion channels $ff\to g$, $fg/gf\to f$, and $gg\to g$, with no new primitive bridge structures appearing at higher fermion multiplicity. The same mechanism extends to ten-dimensional amplitudes with gravitons and arbitrary even numbers of gravitinos in factorized gamma-traceless polarizations, and to single-trace Einstein-Yang-Mills amplitudes with adjoint gluinos and gravitons. These results expose a finite local structure underlying fermionic hidden zeros across gauge and gravitational amplitudes.

</details>


### [243] [Constructing the Monopole Formula for $A$-Type Good Quivers via Quiver Yangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06711)
*Tiantai Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文基于推测的（并在树型quiver上验证的）quiver Yangian与Coulomb分支代数对应关系，给出了3D N=4优A型quiver规范理论（即T_ρ(SU(N))理论）的单极公式的quiver Yangian解释。作者利用代数在1/2-BPS涡旋上的作用，将截断的移位quiver Yangian的生成元重新组织为边界适配生成元，并在Slodowy切片上以U(N) Casimir不变量形式获得经典关系。论文进一步证明了这些边界适配生成元和Casimir关系具有与T_ρ(SU(N))单极公式的Hall-Littlewood表达式所预测一致的正确fugacity，从而将其重构为截断移位quiver Yangian的Hilbert级数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为T_ρ(SU(N))理论（3D N=4优A型quiver规范理论）的单极公式提供代数层面的深刻解释，通过quiver Yangian与Coulomb分支代数对应关系建立数学物理学的桥梁，将单极公式重构为更基本的代数结构的Hilbert级数。

Method: 论文方法包括：(1)利用quiver Yangian在1/2-BPS涡旋上的代数作用；(2)将截断移位quiver Yangian的生成元重新组织为边界适配生成元；(3)在Slodowy切片S^{gl_N}_ρ上以U(N) Casimir不变量形式推导经典关系；(4)通过fugacity比较验证边界适配生成元和Casimir关系与Hall-Littlewood单极公式表达式的一致性。

Result: 论文成功给出了T_ρ(SU(N))理论单极公式的quiver Yangian解释，建立了边界适配生成元与Slodowy切片坐标之间的对应关系，并以U(N) Casimir关系的形式导出了经典代数结构，验证了这些结构与Hall-Littlewood预测的fugacity完全一致。

Conclusion: 作者重构了T_ρ(SU(N))的单极公式，将其作为截断移位quiver Yangian的Hilbert级数，从而将单极公式纳入更统一的quiver Yangian/Coulomb分支代数对应框架中，为该对应关系提供了新的非平凡支持证据。

Abstract: Based on the conjectured (and checked for tree-type quivers) quiver Yangian/Coulomb branch algebra correspondence, we give a quiver Yangian interpretation of the monopole formulas for 3D $\mathcal N=4$ good $A$-type quiver gauge theories, also known as the $T_ρ(SU(N))$ theories. Using the algebra action on the $\frac12$-BPS vortices, we reorganize the generators of the truncated shifted quiver Yangian into boundary-adapted generators, and obtain the classical relations in terms of $U(N)$ Casimirs on the Slodowy slice $\mathcal S^{\mathfrak{gl}_N}_ρ$, whose coordinates are given by these boundary-adapted generators. We also show that the boundary-adapted generators and the Casimir relations have the correct fugacities as predicted by the Hall-Littlewood expression of the monopole formula for $T_ρ(SU(N))$, hence reconstructing it as the Hilbert series of the truncated shifted quiver Yangian.

</details>


### [244] [Dual N=1 Lagrangians for Argyres-Douglas theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06995)
*Minseok Cho,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现了一组新的四维N=1拉格朗日规范理论，它们在红外流动到N=2 Argyres-Douglas理论的(A1,A2n)、(A1,A2n+1)、(A1,D2n+1)和(A1,D2n+2)类型。新对偶理论由带有若干规范单态翻转场的SU(2)^n箭图规范理论给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为已有的N=1拉格朗日实现（由Sp(n)或SU(n+1)规范理论给出）提供新的对偶描述，交换了箭图中的秩与节点数，从而更深入地理解Argyres-Douglas理论的红外实现与对偶性结构。

Method: 构造SU(2)^n箭图规范理论，加入若干规范单态翻转场（flip fields），并验证这些理论在红外流动到特定类型的N=2 Argyres-Douglas理论。

Result: 成功找到了覆盖四类Argyres-Douglas理论的新的N=1拉格朗日对偶描述，即SU(2)^n类型的（带翻转场）箭图规范理论，与先前基于Sp(n)或SU(n+1)的描述形成对偶关系。

Conclusion: 这些新的对偶理论丰富了Argyres-Douglas理论的可拉格朗日描述集合，展示了在箭图表示中通过交换秩和节点数来生成新的N=1实现的可能性。

Abstract: We find a new set of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ Lagrangian gauge theories that flow in the infrared to $\mathcal{N}=2$ Argyres-Douglas theories of type $(A_1, A_{2n})$, $(A_1, A_{2n+1})$, $(A_1, D_{2n+1})$, and $(A_1, D_{2n+2})$. The new dual theories are given by $SU(2)^n$ quiver gauge theories with a number of gauge-singlet flip fields. This description provides dual theories for the previous $\mathcal{N}=1$ Lagrangian realizations given by $Sp(n)$ or $SU(n+1)$ gauge theories, exchanging the rank and the number of nodes in a quiver diagram.

</details>


### [245] [Tachyon-massless couplings at order $α'$ in bosonic string theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07221)
*Mohammad R. Garousi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过T-对偶方法推导了玻色弦理论在α'阶（四导数阶）下包含快子场和质量场的有效作用量中的耦合项。研究发现，通过高阶导数场重定义，可以选取一个规范方案，使得除快子势能本身外，所有剩余耦合仅涉及快子的协变导数和质量场。构建的最小基包含14个独立项，施加T-对偶约束后缩减为6个非零耦合，由两个未定参数表示。通过零快子极限匹配和四快子顶点算符的球面S-矩阵比较，固定了这两个参数。最终确定的快子势能形式为(2/α')(-1+cos T)，暗示闭合弦快子凝聚到T=π处的最小值，产生宇宙学常数为Λ=-4/α'的反德西特时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有提议认为玻色弦理论的经典有效作用量应仅包含偶数个快子场的耦合。本文旨在通过T-对偶程序系统地推导这类耦合在α'阶的具体形式，并验证与快子凝聚相关的物理行为。

Method: 本文采用T-对偶程序推导耦合。首先通过高阶导数场重定义选定一个规范方案，使除快子势外的耦合仅涉快子协变导数和质量场；然后构建四导数阶的14个独立最小耦合基；施加T-对偶将其缩减为6个非零耦合（含两个未定参数）；通过零快子极限与已知有效作用量匹配确定一个参数，通过与四快子顶点算符的球面S-矩阵比较确定另一个参数。

Result: 得到6个非零耦合项，由两个参数确定。参数比较不仅固定了系数，还确立了快子势T^4项系数为正。快子质量项与四次方项一致地给出势能形式(2/α')(-1+cos T)，表明闭合弦快子在T=π处凝聚，产生Λ=-4/α'的AdS时空。

Conclusion: 本文通过T-对偶和S-矩阵匹配系统地确定了α'阶快子-质量场耦合，所得的余弦型快子势能结构暗示快子凝聚导致反德西特时空的生成，为理解闭合弦快子凝聚提供了坚实的有效场论基础。

Abstract: Recently, it has been proposed that the classical effective action of bosonic string theory should contain only couplings with an even number of tachyon fields. We employ the T-duality procedure to derive such couplings at order \(α'\) (four-derivative order) for the tachyon and massless fields. We first observe that, through higher-derivative field redefinitions of the tachyon potential term, one can choose a scheme in which, apart from the tachyon potential itself, all remaining couplings involve only covariant derivatives of the tachyon and the massless fields. We then construct a minimal basis of such couplings at four-derivative order, consisting of 14 independent terms. Imposing T-duality reduces this set to 6 nonzero couplings, expressed in terms of two unfixed parameters. One of these parameters is determined by matching to the known effective action in the zero-tachyon limit, while the other is fixed by comparison with the sphere-level S-matrix element of four tachyon vertex operators. This latter comparison also establishes that the coefficient of the \(T^4\) term in the tachyon potential is positive. Remarkably, the tachyon mass term and this quartic term are consistent with a potential of the form \((2/α')(-1+\cos T)\), which implies that the closed string tachyon condenses to the minimum of the potential at \(T=π\), thereby generating an Anti-de Sitter spacetime with cosmological constant \(Λ=-4/α'\).

</details>


### [246] [Can one hear the shape of a black hole singularity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07514)
*Sean A. Hartnoll,Alexander Zhiboedov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了渐近AdS黑洞（其内部具有近奇点Kasner时期）的大过振数准正则频率ωn。研究发现Kasner指数可以从色散∂ω_n/∂k²在k=0处的大n行为中被干净地提取出来，且Kasner跃迁（如BKL近奇点混沌中出现的）在准正则频率中是“可听见的”，并揭示了过振数n与到奇点的固有时之间的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞内部近奇点几何（Kasner时期）是否以及如何通过准正则模式谱被外部观测者“听到”，即建立内部奇点结构与外部可观测的准正则频率之间的深层联系。

Method: 使用WKB分析，将由近奇点时空决定的非解析指数与诸如从边界到奇点的光学距离等非普适数据分离开来，并在多个数值模型中验证公式。

Result: 表明Kasner指数可从准正则频率的色散关系大n行为中提取；Kasner跃迁在准正则频率中可被观测到；过振数n与到奇点的固有时之间建立了映射关系。

Conclusion: 黑洞内部近奇点动力学的信息可以通过准正则模式谱传递到外部，为探测黑洞内部结构（包括BKL混沌）提供了理论上的声学/谱学途径。

Abstract: We compute the large overtone quasinormal frequencies $ω_n$ of asymptotically AdS black holes with interior near-singularity Kasner epochs. We show that the Kasner exponents can be extracted cleanly from the large $n$ behaviour of the dispersion $\partial ω_n/\partial k^2$ at $k=0$, where $k$ is a boundary momentum. We show that Kasner transitions, as occur in BKL near-singularity chaos, are audible in the quasinormal frequencies and reveal a map between overtone number $n$ and proper time to the singularity. Our results are obtained using a WKB analysis that isolates non-analytic exponents, determined by the near-singularity spacetime, from non-universal data such as the optical distance from the boundary to the singularity. We check our formulae in several numerical models.

</details>


### [247] [Exact spin form factors and correlations at a massive Kramers-Wannier interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07612)
*Zoltan Bajnok,Yizhuang Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了标度Ising模型中大规模Kramer-Wannier界面的有限体积自旋形状因子和两点关联函数，该界面分隔有序相与无序相。通过两种互补的格点实现（自对偶非均匀Ising链与混合热平均的Toeplitz/Fredholm方法）相结合，获得了精确的Neveu-Schwarz-Ramond自旋矩阵元和界面关联子的因式化Fredholm行列式，并用缺陷TCSA和独立费米子自旋链计算进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标度Ising模型中，分隔有序相与无序相的Kramer-Wannier界面（具有符号变化的Majorana质量剖面）的精确有限体积性质（尤其是自旋形状因子与关联函数）尚未被彻底求解，需要发展精确的解析方法来刻画此界面的自旋物理。

Method: 作者采用双重互补的格点描述：(1) 自对偶非均匀Ising链，其连续极限具有变号Majorana质量剖面；(2) 交换欧氏时空后的混合热平均，可借助Toeplitz/Fredholm行列式方法处理。两种路径结合导出精确的Neveu-Schwarz/Ramond自旋矩阵元以及界面关联子的因式化Fredholm行列式表达式。

Result: 获得了质量为有限值的Kramer-Wannier界面的精确自旋形状因子（NS/Ramond扇区矩阵元）以及两点关联函数的因式化Fredholm行列式。相关结果通过缺陷TCSA（截断共形空间方法）和独立的费米子自旋链数值计算得到了验证，确认了谱与矩阵元的正确性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了大规模Kramer-Wannier界面的完整精确解，为标度Ising模型中有序/无序界面上的可积量子场论计算提供了统一的解析框架，并展示了格点组合方法与解析场论方法互补的有效性。

Abstract: We determine exact finite-volume spin form factors and two-point correlations for a massive Kramer--Wannier interface in the scaling Ising model, separating ordered and disordered phases. The interface admits two complementary lattice realizations: a self-dual inhomogeneous Ising chain whose continuum limit has a sign-changing Majorana mass profile, and, after exchanging Euclidean space and time, a mixed thermal average amenable to Toeplitz/Fredholm methods. Combining these descriptions, we obtain the exact Neveu-Schwarz-Ramond spin matrix elements and a factorized Fredholm determinant for the interface correlator. Defect TCSA and independent fermionic spin-chain calculations confirm the predicted spectrum and matrix elements.

</details>


### [248] [(Anti-)De Sitter null strings and Carroll-Weyl symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07765)
*Ulf Lindström,Bo Sundborg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将标准无张力弦提升到R^{1,d}并规范掉世界sheet尺度对称性，以新颖方式构造了dS_d无张力弦。作者证明了所得作用量正是Carrol-Weyl对称弦作用量，并澄清了该模型应被解释为d维dS空间中的弦而非d+1维平坦闵可夫斯基空间中的弦，同时该构造可推广到AdS无张力弦的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无张力弦（tensionless string）在dS_d背景中的形式化构造一直缺乏清晰的描述。此前关于Carrol-Weyl对称弦作用量的解释存在争议——它被怀疑代表d+1维平坦闵可夫斯基空间中的d+1维弦，但该解读存在明显问题。作者旨在通过代数方法构造d维dS空间的无张力弦，并澄清Carrol-Weyl弦模型的真实目标空间含义。

Method: 作者采用"提升-规范"策略：(1) 将标准无张力弦提升到R^{1,d}空间；(2) 规范世界sheet尺度对称性，引入破坏平移但保持dS_d对称性SO(1,d)的作用量项；(3) 选择dS对称的规范固定条件，将理论约化到d维，得到具有Carrol-Weyl对称性的作用量。该构造是纯代数的，可自然推广到AdS无张力弦的构造。

Result: 作者得到了d维dS_d无张力弦的作用量，证明其具有所有预期性质。该作用量与Carrol-Weyl对称弦作用量一致，从而确认其正确的目标空间解释是d维dS空间而非d+1维平坦闵可夫斯基空间，解决了先前文献中的争议。同时，该方法被扩展用于构造AdS背景的无张力弦。构造中使用的提升方法生成的曲率几何是完全代数化的，可能有更广泛的应用。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种从平坦空间无张力弦出发、通过提升和规范固定尺度对称性来构造dS/AdS背景无张力弦的系统方法，澄清了Carrol-Weyl弦模型的维度诠释问题，并为无张力弦在弯曲时空中的形式化提供了新的代数途径，且该方法在张力弦对(A)dS背景的构造中面临明确障碍。

Abstract: We construct a $dS_d$ null string in a novel way by lifting the standard tensionless string to $R^{1,d}$. Gauging worldsheet scale symmetry introduces terms in the action breaking translation invariance, but preserving the $dS_d$ symmetry $SO(1,d)$. Choosing a De Sitter symmetric gauge fixing of the scale symmetry yields a $d$ dimensional theory which we show has all the expected properties of $dS_d$ tensionless strings. The action happens to be the intriguing Carrol-Weyl symmetric string action in $R^{1,d}$, now with a clear target space interpretation in $d$ dimensions. That it does not, as recently claimed, represent a ($d+1$)-dimensional string in flat ($d+1$)-dimensional Minkowski space settles an apparent issue with the Carrol-Weyl string model. Furthermore, while there are clear obstacles for similar formulations of tensile strings in (A)dS backgrounds, our new insights are easily modified to the formulation of Anti-De Sitter tensionless strings. That our construction generates a curved geometry purely algebraically may have even wider applications.

</details>


### [249] [Eikonal Scattering of Two D0-branes with Graviton Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07788)
*Paolo Di Vecchia,Dawid Maskalaniec*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用边界态形式研究两个D0-膜散射过程中无质量闭弦态的发射，计算了玻色弦理论和II型超弦理论中引力子与膨胀子发射的五点振幅，并分析了其场论极限。对于非零碰撞参数，结果可由相应低能理论的世界线公式独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究D0-膜散射中的闭弦态发射过程，以深化对弦理论中开放弦-闭弦耦合及低能有效场论行为的理解。

Method: 采用边界态形式（boundary-state formalism）计算D0-膜散射中质量为零的闭弦态（引力子和膨胀子）发射的五点振幅，涵盖玻色弦理论与II型超弦理论两种情况，并对振幅进行场论极限分析。

Result: 在非零碰撞参数条件下，弦理论五点振幅的场论极限与低能理论世界线公式的独立计算结果完全一致，验证了计算的正确性。

Conclusion: 边界态形式能够有效描述D0-膜散射中的闭弦态发射过程，且弦理论结果在场论极限下与低能有效理论的世界线计算方法相互印证，为理解D0-膜动力学及其引力子/膨胀子辐射提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study the emission of a massless closed-string state in the scattering of two D0-branes using the boundary-state formalism. We compute the corresponding five-point amplitudes for graviton and dilaton emission in bosonic string theory and in type II superstring theory, and analyze their field-theory limit. For nonzero impact parameter, the resulting amplitudes are reproduced by an independent calculation in the worldline formulation of the corresponding low-energy theory.

</details>


### [250] [Logarithmic correction to the entropy of near-extremal higher-curvature black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07801)
*Pablo Bueno,Pablo A. Cano,Robie A. Hennigar,Javier Moreno,Ángel J. Murcia,Guido van der Velde*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类广泛的D维引力理论（可通过二维Horndeski理论有效描述）中近极端黑洞的低温动力学。作者推导出了一个普适的近视界有效描述（即Jackiw-Teitelboim引力），并证明了熵的对数量子修正S_JT=(3/2)log(T/T_breakdown)在所有允许二维Horndeski描述的模型中都是普适的，同时给出了T_breakdown的通用表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端黑洞的低温动力学及其量子修正具有重要理论意义。此前在爱因斯坦引力耦合物质的情形下，已从单圈精确JT配分函数推导出熵的对数修正，但需要验证这种修正是否对更广泛类别的引力理论（如Lovelock和Quasitopological引力）也普适成立。

Method: 作者利用球对称约化将广泛的D维引力理论映射为二维Horndeski理论，在此基础上推导近视界极限下统一的JT引力有效描述，并计算近极端黑洞的低温熵修正及其主导尺度T_breakdown。

Result: 证明了JT引力的熵对数修正形式（系数3/2）在所有具有二维Horndeski描述的引力模型中均普适，确定了T_breakdown的一般形式可由D维理论的数据表示，并用Lovelock引力中的带电黑洞和Quasitopological引力中的规则黑洞进行了具体验证。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了二维Horndeski理论框架下近极端黑洞量子修正的普适性，表明对数熵修正在广泛的引力理论族中不依赖于具体模型细节，仅需要JT引力描述存在，这深化了对近极端黑洞量子性质的理解。

Abstract: The spherically symmetric sector of broad classes of $D$-dimensional gravitational theories can be effectively described by general two-dimensional Horndeski theories. Exploiting this correspondence, we study the low-temperature dynamics of near-extremal black hole solutions of the parent theories by deriving a universal near-horizon effective description in terms of Jackiw--Teitelboim (JT) gravity. We explicitly show that the logarithmic quantum correction to the entropy, $S_{\rm JT}=\frac{3}{2}\log \left(T/T_{\rm breakdown} \right)$, previously derived for near-extremal black holes in Einstein gravity coupled to matter from the one-loop exact JT partition function, is universal across all models admitting an effective two-dimensional Horndeski description. We determine the general form of the scale at which this correction becomes dominant, $T_{\rm breakdown}$, in terms of the data of the $D$-dimensional theories. We illustrate our general results with charged black holes in Lovelock gravities and regular black holes in Quasitopological gravities.

</details>


### [251] [SolutionsX: supergravity theories/solutions as code, with an agentic workflow for machine-verifiable physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07826)
*Vasil Dimitrov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: SolutionsX是一个Mathematica包，它将xAct环境转化为可存储、机器可验证的记录，使超引力解等物理计算能够在新内核中重放、自带验证并供后续复用或扩展。论文通过重现文献中的超引力解、性能基准测试及三个AI智能体盲测（能在三小时内正确编码近期论文）展示其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中的超引力解难以验证：约定可能未明确说明、论文存在笔误、代数推导繁琐耗时。研究人员需要重新推导才能确认结果，这一过程效率低下且容易出错。

Method: 提出SolutionsX Mathematica包，将任意xAct环境转化为存储的机器可验证记录：代码可在全新内核中自动重放运行、携带自身的验证逻辑、可被提取以复用或扩展。适用范围涵盖抽象张量、微分形式、任意维度的旋量等，并演示了从文献出发的完整工作流及AI智能体辅助编码流程。

Result: 论文包含三方面结果：1) 完整重现了一个已发表的超引力解工作流；2) 将包的分量计算引擎与xAct进行基准对比；3) 三次AI智能体盲测中，智能体在给定包和小工具集的情况下，能在三小时内正确编码一篇近期论文。

Conclusion: 这类机器可验证的记录数据库对当前研究人员有用，同时为AI智能体提供可学习的工作示例。该包还能在智能体的思维过程中充当内部验证器——任何声明在输出前都先在内核中得到验证。

Abstract: A supergravity solution in the literature is an artifact that cannot be verified without re-deriving it: conventions might not be clearly stated, papers have typos, algebra is tedious. SolutionsX is a Mathematica package that turns an xAct environment, whatever physics it may describe, into a stored, machine-verifiable record: code that replays in a fresh kernel, carries its own verification, and can later be fetched for re-use or extension. The shipped examples are supergravity theories and their solutions, but the scope is anything xAct reaches: abstract tensors, differential forms, spinors in any dimension. We follow one published solution through the entire workflow, benchmark the package's component engine against xAct's, and report on three blind runs of an agentic workflow in which an AI coding agent, given the package and a small toolkit, correctly codified a recent paper in under three hours. A database of such records is useful to researchers today and gives agents worked examples to learn from. The package acts as an internal verifier during the thought process of an agent: a claim is checked in the kernel before it is made.

</details>


### [252] [Unitary quantum conformal Bondi-Metzner-Sachs field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07828)
*Daniel Grumiller,Iva Lovrekovic*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文对具有量子共形Bondi-Metzner-Sachs（BMS）代数对称性的二维场论施加幺正性约束。正定条件将中心荷限制在0<c<2.053，而顶点代数的正性进一步将中心荷限定为仅四个允许值：c=1, 3/2, 13/7和2。研究者计算了这些理论的初级场谱，并验证其与BPS型和全息不等式相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近平坦时空的渐近对称性（BMS代数）与共形对称性的结合（量子共形BMS代数）提供了研究量子引力与场论对称结构的重要框架。然而，在二维场论中，并非所有具有该对称性的理论都是幺正的。作者旨在通过幺正性和正性约束来系统筛选出真正自洽的幺正场论候选者。

Method: 本文采用两种互补的正性约束方法：(1) 对共形BMS代数的表示施加幺正性条件（Positivity constraints），将中心荷约束到区间0<c<2.053；(2) 利用关联的顶点代数（vertex algebra）的正性（即幺正顶点算子代数的性质），进一步筛选出仅有的四个允许中心荷c=1, 3/2, 13/7, 2。随后对每个理论计算其初级场（primaries）谱，并检验谱与BPS类不等式及全息不等式的相容性。

Result: 研究得出以下主要结果：中心荷的取值范围被限制在0<c<2.053；通过顶点代数正性约束后，仅剩下四个幺正场论候选者，对应中心荷c=1, 3/2, 13/7和2；这四个理论的初级场谱均与BPS型不等式和全息不等式相容，证实了这些理论的物理自洽性。

Conclusion: 通过幺正性和正性约束，量子共形BMS对称的二维幺正场论被严格筛选为仅四种候选理论（c=1, 3/2, 13/7, 2），其谱性质与BPS及全息约束相容，为研究渐近平坦时空背景中的量子场论和全息对应提供了重要的理论候选集合。

Abstract: We impose unitarity constraints on two-dimensional field theories whose symmetries form the quantum conformal Bondi-Metzner-Sachs algebra, which combines conformal symmetries with asymptotic symmetries of asymptotically flat spacetimes. Positivity constraints put bounds on the central charge, 0<c<2.053. Positivity of the associated vertex algebra allows only four central charges, c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2. Thus, only four candidates for unitary field theories remain. We calculate their spectra of primaries and show that they are compatible with BPS-like and holographic inequalities.

</details>


### [253] [Diffeomorphism-invariant Approach to Asymptotically Safe Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07829)
*Friederike Ihssen,Benjamin Knorr,Silas Mezger,Jan M. Pawlowski,Paul P. Sprenger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的微分同胚不变方法用于渐近安全的度规量子引力，基于物理信息重整化群流与保证每步微分同胚不变性的RG核，首次以显式微分同胚不变方式计算Reuter不动点，并系统讨论了正则化依赖与近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近安全的度规量子引力中，保持微分同胚不变性是一个核心挑战。传统方法在重整化群（RG）流程中可能破坏严格不变性，且难以控制算子的相关次数计数。作者希望开发一种内在不变的形式化方法，使有效作用量在每一步RG演化中保持量子微分同胚不变与背景无关性，从而更可靠地计算不动点结构。

Method: 基于物理信息（physics-informed）的RG流框架，引入一个特殊的RG核，该核在每个RG步骤中保证微分同胚不变性。该方法允许对度规量子引力中的算子相关次数进行控制与维护，从而实现背景无关的量子有效作用量。作为首个应用，以显式微分同胚不变的方式计算Reuter不动点，并辅以正则化依赖的详细讨论及近似误差的系统估算。

Result: 成功以显式微分同胚不变的方式计算了Reuter不动点，并通过对正则化方案依赖性与近似误差的系统分析，验证了该方法的可靠性和结果稳定性。

Conclusion: 该微分同胚不变方法为渐近安全量子引力的计算提供了更为原则性且可靠的工具箱，能够在保持量子微分同胚不变性和背景无关性的同时，实现对不动点及可重正化量子引力行为的精确验证。

Abstract: We provide a novel diffeomorphism-invariant approach to asymptotically safe metric quantum gravity. It is based on physics-informed renormalisation group flows with a renormalisation group kernel that guarantees diffeomorphism invariance at each renormalisation group step. Importantly, it also allows us to control and maintain the relevance counting of operators in metric quantum gravity. The resulting effective action is quantum diffeomorphism-invariant and background-independent. As a first non-trivial application, we compute the Reuter fixed point in a manifestly diffeomorphism-invariant way. This computation is augmented with a detailed discussion of regularisation dependence and a systematic estimate of the errors arising from approximations.

</details>


### [254] [Truncated Polyakov bootstrap for BCFTs: Neumann-Dirichlet flow and Ising special transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07841)
*Kaushik Kangsabanik,Apratim Kaviraj,Astha Tiwari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将截断Polyakov bootstrap方法扩展到边界共形场论（BCFT），利用解析泛函框架求解BCFT crossing方程，通过追踪广义自由场（GFF）从Neumann边界条件到Dirichlet边界条件的形变流来识别非微扰解，并将该方法应用于3d Ising特殊转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将近期提出的截断Polyakov bootstrap形式主义推广到边界共形场论（BCFT），以寻找BCFT crossing方程的近似解，并建立非微扰解与广义自由场边界条件形变之间的联系。

Method: 采用解析泛函框架（analytic functional framework）求解BCFT crossing方程。关键思路是将非微扰BCFT解与围绕广义自由场（GFF）的形变族联系起来——通过追踪BCFT数据从Neumann边界条件的GFF流向Dirichlet边界条件的流。对于3d Ising特殊转变，则以微扰Wilson-Fisher描述作为形变来识别解。

Result: 获得的BCFT数据与现有格点计算结果高度吻合（在可用之处），其余则为新的预测结果。

Conclusion: 截断Polyakov bootstrap可以成功地扩展到BCFT，并通过GFF边界条件之间的形变参数化来系统识别非微扰解，为无法从格点计算获得的BCFT数据提供了新的理论预测。

Abstract: We extend the recently proposed truncated Polyakov bootstrap to boundary conformal field theories (BCFTs). The formalism uses the analytic functional framework to find approximate solutions of BCFT crossing. We show that a generic nonperturbative solution can be identified by relating it to a family of deformations around generalized free fields (GFF) with a fixed boundary condition. We show this by tracking the flow of BCFT data from GFF with Neumann boundary condition to that with Dirichlet. The same method is then used for the 3d Ising special transition, using the perturbative Wilson-Fisher description as the deformation to identify it. The BCFT data obtained are in remarkable agreement with existing lattice results where available, and are otherwise new.

</details>


### [255] [Scattering of Null strings - Flipped Vacuum & CHY](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07845)
*Arjun Bagchi,Sachin Grover,Sharang Rajesh Iyer,Amartya Saha,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究零张力弦（null tensionless strings）的散射。基于ILST作用的经典零弦衍生出三种基于不同真空和2d共形Carroll代数（CCA）表示的量子理论。作者聚焦于基于"翻转"真空构建的量子理论（实现了2d CCA的最高权表示），通过引入与紧致化零弦密切相关的新型顶点算子，构建了该理论的散射振幅，并证明当考虑零"翻转"弦中无质量态的散射时，自然得到Cachazo-He-Yuan (CHY)公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索零张力弦理论中基于不同真空和CCA表示构建的量子理论的性质，特别是理解"翻转"真空对应理论的散射行为，并建立其与CHY公式等高效散射振幅计算公式之间的联系。

Method: 在ILST经典零弦框架下，选取"翻转"真空实现2d CCA的最高权表示；引入与紧致化零弦相关的新的顶点算子类来构建散射振幅，通过分析无质量态散射来推导散射公式。

Result: 研究表明，在零"翻转"弦中考虑无质量态散射时，自然导出了CHY公式，揭示了该量子零弦理论与已知高效散射振幅计算框架之间的深层联系。

Conclusion: 零张力"翻转"弦的散射振幅自然重现CHY公式，这可能为理解弦理论不同量子实现之间以及与散射振幅现代方法之间的联系提供新的视角。

Abstract: We investigate the scattering of null tensionless strings. Classical null strings, given by the ILST action, give rise to three distinct quantum theories built on different vacua and representations of the underlying 2d Conformal Carroll algebra (CCA) which form the residual gauge symmetries on the null worldsheet. In this paper, we are interested in the quantum theory built out of the so-called ``flipped'' vacuum which realises the highest weight representation of the 2d CCA. By considering a novel class of vertex operators, intimately connected to the compactified null string, we build scattering amplitudes of the theory. We show that one naturally obtains the Cachazo-He-Yuan (CHY) formulae when one considers scattering of massless states in the null ``flipped'' string.

</details>


### [256] [Double Copy from the Flipped Null String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07846)
*Arjun Bagchi,Sachin Grover,Sharang Rajesh Iyer,Amartya Saha,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了翻转真空下玻色子零弦树级振幅的双拷贝结构，证明其level-one矢量关联函数提供高阶导数(DF)²规范理论的CHY运动学半被积函数，而特殊的紧致动量-绕数扇区产生互补的Parke-Taylor因子。对于可分解的level-two态，同一Carroll世界面产生两个运动学半被积函数，从而对称双拷贝到六导数Weyl三次引力扇区。外部动量共同的格子扇区重现了BCJ排序关系和相应的场论KLT表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双拷贝结构在弦论/Carroll世界面框架下的深层起源，特别是寻找一个直接提供双拷贝(double copy)的弦论实现，并建立与紧致格子的O(d,d)对称性相容的涌现色扇区描述。

Method: 在翻转真空下的玻色子零弦(Carrollian world-sheet)框架中系统分析树级振幅的关联函数结构：通过分解level-one矢量关联函数与CHY积分核，识别(DF)²规范理论运动学被积函数；利用紧致动量-绕数扇区构造Parke-Taylor因子；对level-two可分解态提取两个独立运动学半被积函数实现对称双拷贝；最后建立BCJ/KLT关系与格点扇区的对应。

Result: 证明了零弦的level-one矢量关联函数精确给出(DF)²理论的CHY运动学半被积函数；紧致格点扇区产生互补的Parke-Taylor因子；可分解level-two态通过对称双拷贝生成六导数Weyl引力振幅；格点扇区重现BCJ排序关系与KLT表示，确证了Carroll世界面作为双拷贝的弦论起源。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了零弦/Carroll世界面与双拷贝结构之间的直接联系，揭示了涌现色扇区可由电流代数实现并与紧致格子的O(d,d)结构相容，为理解双拷贝的弦论起源及高阶导数振幅提供了新的统一框架。

Abstract: We study the double-copy structure of tree amplitudes of the bosonic null string in the flipped vacuum. We show that its level-one vector correlator supplies the Cachazo-He-Yuan (CHY) kinematic half-integrand of the higher-derivative $(DF)^2$ gauge theory, while distinguished compact momentum-winding sectors generate the complementary Parke-Taylor factor. For factorized level-two states, the same Carrollian world-sheet produces two kinematic half-integrands and hence the symmetric double copy to the six-derivative Weyl-cubed graviton sector. Lattice sectors with common external momenta reproduce the Bern-Carrasco-Johansson (BCJ) ordering relation and the corresponding field-theory Kawai-Lewellen-Tye (KLT) representation. These results provide a direct Carrollian world-sheet origin of the double copy and point toward a current-algebra realization of the emergent color sector compatible with the $O(d,d)$ structure of the compact lattice.

</details>


### [257] [Entanglement Wedge Reconstruction Beyond the Large N Limit via the Twirled Petz Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07866)
*Arash Alipour Shahmiri,Maryam Sharifian,Niloofar Vardian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用量子纠错框架系统地处理AdS/CFT中纠缠楔重构问题，在大N展开的次领头阶通过扭曲Petz映射获得了一阶1/N修正的显式双局域形式，为由半经典区域走向全量子引力区域的体重构提供了受控路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠楔重构是AdS/CFT中体局域性最精确的表述，但此前其理解仅停留在领头阶大N展开（通过模流和Petz映射重构），超出半经典区域后的推广尚不清楚，需要对超越大N极限的次领头修正给出系统性的处理方法。

Method: 作者将量子纠错作为自然框架来处理体重构问题：在领头阶重现已知的重构公式；通过引入扭曲Petz映射（twirled Petz map），系统地纳入1/N的次领头修正，得到由次领头Petz核控制的显式双局域（bi-local）修正项。

Result: 在领头阶精确复现已知重构公式；超越领头近似，获得了由次领头Petz核控制的显式双局域1/N修正；建立了超越大N的受控体重构方法，并明确了量子纠错在全息中的角色。

Conclusion: 该框架为在全量子引力设定下理解体重构提供了一条受控路径，使纠缠楔重构从半经典区域向全量子引力区域的外推有了系统且明晰的方法论基础。

Abstract: Entanglement wedge reconstruction provides the sharpest formulation of bulk locality in AdS/CFT, identifying the entanglement wedge as the largest bulk region reconstructible from a boundary subregion. While this statement is well understood at leading order in the large N expansion through both modular flow and Petz map reconstruction, its extension beyond the semiclassical regime has remained unclear. In this work, we show that quantum error correction provides the natural framework for addressing this problem. At leading order, we reproduce the known leading-order reconstruction formulas, and by employing the twirled Petz map, we obtain a systematic and explicit method for incorporating subleading 1/N corrections. Beyond the leading approximation, we have obtained an explicit bi-local correction controlled by a subleading Petz kernel. Our results establish a controlled approach to bulk reconstruction beyond large N and sharpening the role of quantum error correction in holography. This framework provides a controlled path toward understanding bulk reconstruction in fully quantum gravitational settings.

</details>


### [258] [ABJM/BMN Index Matching in the Plane-Wave Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07872)
*Chi-Ming Chang,Kangning Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在系数层面建立了ABJM理论与BMN矩阵模型在平面波极限下的超对称指标等式，并扩展到多膜情形，建立了标量修饰的等通量ABJM单极子与模糊球膜BMN真空之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前仅有单膜情形的指标关系，需要建立多膜、多重复性下的系数化扩展，以更全面地理解ABJM理论在平面波极限下与BMN矩阵模型的对应关系。

Method: 对每个固定的正Chern-Simons能级k，考虑固定重数（即固定有限ABJM规范秩）的多膜族，在纵向动量趋于无穷且两两差固定时建立指标等式。通过将标量修饰的等通量ABJM单极子映射为BMN模糊球膜真空（重数对应重合膜数，通量大小对应沿M理论圆的每膜动量），对相关的Higgs留数进行对角完整度积分，得到ABJM平面波贡献，其单极子-谐波核与BMN矩阵谐波核在有限BMN上自旋截断内一致。在有限动量下，完整指标差分解为非平面波余项与该截断以上的谐波尾部。

Result: 在任意固定正Chern-Simons能级k下，系数层面建立多膜ABJM与BMN矩阵模型的平面波极限指标等式；增加k将非平面波余项推向更高逃逸度阶数，而不改变存留核或截断贡献。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了此前单膜指标关系的系数化多膜扩展，深化了ABJM理论平面波极限与BMN矩阵模型的对应理解。

Abstract: We establish coefficientwise equality between the refined supersymmetric indices of ABJM theory and the BMN matrix model in the plane-wave limit at every fixed positive Chern-Simons level k. For multi-membrane families with fixed multiplicities, and hence fixed finite ABJM gauge rank, the equality holds as all longitudinal momenta tend to infinity with their pairwise differences fixed. Scalar-dressed equal-flux ABJM monopoles correspond to BMN vacua of fuzzy-sphere membranes: multiplicities count coincident membranes, whereas flux magnitudes give the momentum per membrane along the M-theory circle. At the index level, the associated Higgs residues yield a diagonal-holonomy integral for the ABJM plane-wave contribution, whose monopole-harmonic kernel agrees with the BMN matrix-harmonic kernel up to the finite BMN upper-spin cutoff. At finite momentum, the full index difference splits into the non-plane-wave remainder and the harmonic tail above this cutoff. Increasing k shifts the former to higher fugacity grade without changing the surviving kernel or the cutoff contribution. This gives a coefficientwise, multi-membrane extension of the earlier single-membrane index relation.

</details>


### [259] [Wilson loops, M2-branes and strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07928)
*Alexia Nix*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通用方法，用于在渐近AdS4背景中研究探针M2-膜和基本弦的半经典量子化。在场论侧，这些配分函数对偶于一类三维N≥2 Chern-Simons-物质理论的半BPS Wilson圈真空期望值。作者利用该方法对Wilson圈的半经典行为给出了新预测，并在可行时与超对称局域化得到的已知结果进行了匹配，同时表明正确的全息系综选择意味着探针M2-膜的半经典量子化可给出对偶Wilson圈在规范群秩N下的完整微扰答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种普适方法来研究渐近AdS4背景中探针M2-膜与基本弦的半经典量子化，从而对三维Chern-Simons-物质理论中半BPS Wilson圈的行为进行系统性的全息预测，并弥合全息对偶两侧的计算间隙。

Method: 通过建立通用的半经典量子化框架，同时处理探针M2-膜和基本弦在渐近AdS4背景中的涨落；在场论侧将其与三维N≥2 Chern-Simons-物质理论中半BPS Wilson圈的真空期望值建立对偶关系；利用正确的全息系综选择（正则系综）以获得N的完整微扰展开。

Result: 得到了Wilson圈半经典行为的新的全息预测；在候选理论中与超对称局域化导出的已知解析结果进行了对比验证；证明了探针M2-膜半经典量子化配合恰当的全息系综选择可获得N的全阶（精确）微扰展开，而不仅仅是一阶大N结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为渐近AdS4背景中M2-膜与弦的涨落计算提供了统一的处理框架，明确了全息系综选择在半经典量子化中的关键作用，并建立了与场论侧超对称局域化结果之间的系统对偶关系。作为会议论文集贡献，该文总结了相关研究的最新进展。

Abstract: We establish a universal approach for studying the semiclassical quantization of both probe M2-branes and fundamental strings in asymptotically AdS$_{4}$ backgrounds. On the field theory side, their partition functions are dual to the vacuum expectation value of half-BPS Wilson loops of a class of three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}\geq 2$ Chern-Simons-matter theories. Utilizing this universal approach we arrive at novel predictions for the semiclassical behaviour of the Wilson loop or upon availability match our results with the previously obtained expressions via supersymmetric localization. We also show that the correct choice of holographic ensemble suggests that the semiclassical quantization of probe M2-branes yields the full perturbative answer in the rank of the gauge group $N$ for the dual Wilson loop. This is a proceedings contribution to the ''Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary'', held at the National and Kapodistrian University of Athens on December 17-19 2025.

</details>


### [260] [Fortuitous ABJM Operators as Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08052)
*Connor Behan,Leonardo Pipolo de Gioia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在ABJM理论的U(N)_k × U(N)_-k规范理论中，以N作为形式参数对异常维度矩阵进行对角化，实现了对BPS态的系统研究。作者发现16条"偶然BPS"算子轨迹及其后裔，这些算子在有限个N值下才具有BPS性质，在大N极限下与可积性预测一致。研究还表明许多此类算子只能被特定类型的引力子"着装"，且ABJM理论中第一非BPS态的能隙与N=4超杨-Mills定性不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超共形规范理论中的BPS态可以通过枚举幂零超电荷的上同调类或对角化异常维度矩阵两种方式研究。后者能更深入地理解全息理论如何根据算子对偶于无视界几何还是黑洞来区分它们。在N=4超杨-Mills中，由于数值上的限制，难以研究多条"偶然BPS"轨迹，因此在ABJM理论中利用N作为形式参数进行对角化可以突破这一瓶颈，获得更多此类轨迹。

Method: 作者在ABJM理论的U(N)_k × U(N)_-k规范理论中(将N视为形式参数)对异常维度矩阵进行精确对角化。通过对角化结果定义算子轨迹，特别关注"偶然BPS"算子——即仅在有限个N值下为BPS的算子。作者系统遍历了这些轨迹，分析了它们在后裔结构、可积性极限下的行为，以及被引力子"着装"的方式，并与N=4超杨-Mills的结果进行了对比。

Result: 在ABJM理论中获得了16条"偶然BPS"算子轨迹及其后裔（对比N=4超杨-Mills中数值上难以研究超过一条此类轨迹）；发现这些算子在大N极限下呈现简单形式并与可积性预测一致；揭示了多数此类算子只能被特定类型引力子所着装，与黑洞情形相似；发现ABJM理论中第一非BPS态能隙的性质与N=4超杨-Mills的定性行为不同（与预期一致）。

Conclusion: ABJM理论提供了一个比N=4超杨-Mills更适合研究偶然BPS算子的平台，通过以N为形式参数进行对角化，可以系统地追踪多条算子轨迹。这些轨迹在大N下与可积性一致，其着装模式反映与黑洞对偶的深层结构。作者还指出这种上同调层面的分析对完整算子的性质也有启示，且ABJM与N=4超杨-Mills在谱隙上的定性差异与预期相一致。

Abstract: Given a superconformal gauge theory at a fixed loop order, BPS states can be studied in two ways --- by enumerating cohomology classes of a nilpotent supercharge or by diagonalizing the anomalous dimension matrix. The second one allows for a deeper look at how holographic theories distinguish between operators based on whether they are dual to horizonless geometries or black holes. To this end, we carry out the diagonalization in $\mathrm{U}(N)_k \times \mathrm{U}(N)_{-k}$ ABJM theory with $N$ as a formal parameter. This makes it possible to define trajectories of operators and focus on the fortuitous ones which are characterized by being BPS at only finitely many values of $N$. In contrast to $\mathcal{N} = 4$ Super Yang-Mills, where it has been numerically prohibitive to study more than one of these, ABJM gives us access to 16 such trajectories along with their descendants. We show that they take an especially simple form at large $N$ where they agree with the predictions of integrability. We also show that many of them, as with black holes, can only be dressed by certain types of gravitons and comment on how this exercise in cohomology also has implications for properties of the full operators. Finally, we show that the gap to the first non-BPS state in ABJM theory qualitatively differs from that of $\mathcal{N} = 4$ Super Yang-Mills in the expected way.

</details>


### [261] [Bogomol'nyi equations for Kruglov strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08111)
*I. Praseyto,U. Ubaydillah,H. S. Ramadhan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了以Kruglov非线性电动力学替代Maxwell规范扇区的Abelian gauge-Higgs涡旋的Bogomol'nyi方程。通过无应力方法推导出第一序方程，引入本构映射Φ(Y;σ)分析其单调性与值域，确定了不同σ取值下光滑可容许Bogomol'nyi分支的存在条件，并给出了六个代表性σ值的解析形式及数值涡旋解，最终BPS弦张量保持纯拓扑性μ_BPS=2πn。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性电动力学框架下推广Bogomol'nyi涡旋方程。Kruglov非线性电动力学作为介于Maxwell、Born-Infeld与指数电动力学之间的幂律插值理论，其涡旋解的BPS结构尚未被系统研究。现有方法通常事先假定Higgs势形式，而本文希望从无应力条件直接推导，同时需要处理规范扇区与Higgs扇区之间的隐式耦合。

Method: (1) 以Kruglov非线性电动力学替代Maxwell项构造涡旋模型；(2) 采用无应力方法（stressless method），从空间应力张量消失条件直接推导一对第一序Bogomol'nyi方程，无需先验假设Higgs势；(3) 引入本构映射Φ(Y;σ)处理规范与Higgs扇区的隐式代数耦合，分析其单调性、值域及有界性，从而确定Bogomol'nyi分支的可容许条件及非线性参数β的约束；(4) 对六个代表性σ值推导闭式本构关系、BPS势及规范场方程（涵盖线性、二次、三次代数结构）；(5) 数值求解涡旋剖面并验证BPS张量性质。

Result: 本构映射的性质取决于σ：σ>1/2时严格单调且无界；0<σ<1/2时存在有限最大值；σ=1/2为有界边缘情形。后两种情形下非线性参数β受到显式约束。对六个代表性σ值获得了闭式解，并通过数值计算得到涡旋剖面。BPS弦张量恒为μ_BPS=2πn，与σ和β均无关，纯拓扑。

Conclusion: 本文在Kruglov非线性电动力学框架下完整构建了可积涡旋的BPS结构，揭示了本构映射的单调性/值域特征与Bogomol'nyi分支可容许性之间的直接联系。不同σ对应的代数结构（线性至立方）展示了理论丰富的层次性。BPS张量的拓扑不变性验证了该非线性推广保持了涡旋的拓扑量子化本质。

Abstract: We construct the Bogomol'nyi equations for Abelian gauge--Higgs vortices in which the Maxwell gauge sector is replaced by Kruglov nonlinear electrodynamics, a power-law family that interpolates between Maxwell theory, Born--Infeld electrodynamics, and exponential electrodynamics, characterized by a dimensionless exponent $σ$. Using the stressless method, we derive a pair of first-order equations directly from the vanishing of the spatial stress tensor, without assuming the Higgs potential \textit{a priori}. For generic $σ$, the gauge and Higgs sectors are coupled through an implicit algebraic relation. We therefore introduce a constitutive map $Φ(Y;σ)$ and analyze its monotonicity and range to determine the conditions for a smooth admissible Bogomol'nyi branch. For $σ>1/2$, the constitutive map is strictly monotonic and unbounded, whereas for $0<σ<1/2$ it possesses a finite maximum; the marginal case $σ=1/2$ is bounded. These properties yield explicit bounds on the nonlinear parameter $β$ for the latter cases. We further obtain closed-form constitutive relations, BPS potentials, and gauge-field equations for six representative values of $σ$, spanning linear, quadratic, and cubic algebraic structures. The corresponding vortex profiles are then computed numerically. The resulting BPS string tension is purely topological, $μ_{\rm BPS}=2πn$, independent of both $σ$ and $β$.

</details>


### [262] [Scalar Casimir Effect on a Two-Dimensional Sphere with a Wu--Yang Magnetic Monopole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08378)
*Jun-Cheng Zhang,Xiao-Yin Pan,Juhao Wu,Qing-hai Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个复标量场在由中心Wu-Yang磁单极子贯穿的二维球面上的Casimir效应。研究发现，当单极子强度足够大时，可以逆转Casimir压力的符号，将吸引力变为排斥力，且该行为在广泛的曲率耦合范围内持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Wu-Yang磁单极子的非平凡拓扑结构如何影响真空Casimir能量，特别是能否提供排斥性的真空应力，这可能对引力与宇宙学场景具有重要意义。

Method: 在该背景下，带电场被视为S^2上非平凡复线丛的截面（体现单极子的非平凡拓扑）。作者解析求解Klein-Gordon方程，并利用适用于多值函数的广义Abel-Plana公式计算Casimir能量。

Result: 结果表明，当磁单极子强度足够大时，可以反转Casimir压力的符号，将吸引力变为排斥力；这一现象在广泛的曲率耦合参数范围内均成立。

Conclusion: Wu-Yang磁单极子可能作为排斥性真空应力的来源，对引力与宇宙学可能有潜在影响。

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect of a complex scalar field on a two-dimensional sphere threaded by a fixed Wu--Yang magnetic monopole at the center. In this background the charged scalar field is a section of a nontrivial complex line bundle over $S^2$, reflecting the monopole's nontrivial topology. We solve the Klein--Gordon equation analytically and compute the Casimir energy using a generalized Abel--Plana formula for multivalued functions. We find that the monopole can reverse the sign of the Casimir pressure, turning an attractive force into a repulsive one when the monopole strength is sufficiently large. This behavior persists across a broad range of curvature couplings and suggests that Wu--Yang monopoles may provide a source of repulsive vacuum stress, with possible implications for gravitational and cosmological settings.

</details>


### [263] [Gravitational decoherence from soft-graviton $S$-matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08584)
*Hiroki Matsui*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过引力子S矩阵的软极限扇区重新审视物质叠加态的引力退相干，利用Weinberg软定理和相干辐射态的重叠计算，推导出规范不变、非负的领头软退相干指数，并将其扩展到单模和双模压缩引力子态（包括受暴胀启发的双模情形），发现压缩可增强或抑制退相干，而相位平均则给出由引力子占据数控制的增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力退相干是量子引力与量子信息交叉领域的重要问题，但以往处理中红外奇异性、规范依赖和软引力子贡献的处理不够系统。作者希望利用领头软扇区的S矩阵方法，从Weinberg软定理出发为引力退相干提供第一性原理且规范不变的描述，并进一步探讨非真空（压缩态）引力背景下的退相干行为，从而连接软引力子散射、红外量子信息与退相干。

Method: 从Weinberg软定理出发，计算两个物质叠加分支间相干辐射态的重叠积分；针对具有相同总四动量的分支构型，推导出领头软阶的规范不变且非负的退相干指数，并精确计算偏振求和与角度积分。在非相对论极限下展开得到四极主导的表达式。随后将框架扩展到单模和双模压缩引力子态，考虑相对相位对退相干的影响及相位平均的情形；对相位平均的遗迹引力波背景采用近似平的能谱密度，并用红外截断与观测时间的倒数相关联，计算退相干贡献。

Result: 真空态下，软辐射可区分的叠加分支间的相干性随红外截断的移除而对数或幂律消失（退相干完全化）。领头非相对论项纯粹是四极的（源于能量-动量守恒约束）。压缩态情况下，相对相位决定压缩对退相干是增强还是抑制；相位平均后退相干被引力子占据数控制的因子增强。对于近似平坦能谱的相位平均遗迹背景，退相干贡献随引力波能量密度成正比并随观测时间的四次方增长。

Conclusion: 工作为引力退相干建立了基于软引力子S矩阵的系统理论框架，统一处理了真空与非真空（压缩态）情形，揭示了红外软辐射在量子退相干中的核心作用，并为未来利用遗迹引力波背景的退相干观测量提供了理论依据。这些发现将软引力子散射、红外量子信息和引力背景中的退相干联系起来，具有重要的理论意义与潜在观测相关性。

Abstract: We revisit gravitational decoherence of matter superpositions using the leading soft sector of the graviton $S$-matrix. Starting from Weinberg's soft theorem and the overlap of coherent radiation states, we derive, for branch configurations with the same total four-momentum, a gauge-invariant, nonnegative leading-soft decoherence exponent and evaluate the polarization sum and angular integrals exactly at this order. In the vacuum, coherence between branches with distinguishable soft radiation vanishes as the infrared cutoff is removed. The leading nonrelativistic term is purely quadrupolar as a consequence of energy--momentum conservation. We extend the analysis to single- and two-mode squeezed graviton states, including the two-mode case motivated by inflation. Squeezing can enhance or suppress decoherence depending on the relative phase, while phase averaging gives an enhancement governed by the graviton occupation. For a phase-averaged relic background with an approximately flat energy-density spectrum over the relevant soft frequency band, the sharp-cutoff identification of the infrared scale with the inverse observation time gives a contribution that grows with the gravitational-wave energy density and with the fourth power of the observation time. These results connect soft-graviton scattering, infrared quantum information, and decoherence in nonvacuum gravitational backgrounds.

</details>


### [264] [Symplectic Chern-Simons theories dual to Abelian rank-$0$ theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08648)
*Tadashi Okazaki,Douglas J. Smith*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出三维辛Chern-Simons理论USp(2n)在水平k=n+1/2+ℓ处（带一个基础表示的手性多重态）与Creutzig-Garner-Kim的Abel秩-0理论T_{ℓ,n}对偶，适用于所有正整数对(n,ℓ)。由于基础表示是伪实的，水平取半整数，无非Abel味对称性，超对称增强至N=4。两侧的Higgs和Coulomb分支均消失。作者在所有(n,ℓ)情况下匹配了实质量变形下的超对称真空计数，并在若干情况下匹配了超对称指标；随后详细研究了ℓ=1即水平n+3/2的最小USp(2n) Chern-Simons理论情形，其对偶为带tadpole水平矩阵和裸单极超势的U(1)^n理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三维对称Chern-Simons物质理论的新对偶关系，特别是针对具有伪实表示（半整数Chern-Simons水平）和非Abel味对称性缺失的系统，将之与Abel秩-0理论家族建立联系，以加深对三维N=4超对称场论间对偶的理解。

Method: 通过具体构造对偶对的两侧理论，利用实质量变形下的超对称真空计数进行匹配，并计算若干情形下的超对称指标作为验证；针对ℓ=1的特殊情形展开细致分析，揭示U(1)^n理论的具体结构（tadpole水平矩阵与裸单极超势）。

Result: 完成了USp(2n)辛Chern-Simons理论（水平k=n+1/2+ℓ）与Abel秩-0理论T_{ℓ,n}之间对偶的建立；在所有(n,ℓ)配对下匹配了超对称真空计数，在多个案例中验证了超对称指标；并对ℓ=1情形的对偶结构进行了详细刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一类新的、含半整数Chern-Simons水平的非Abel三维理论与其Abel对偶之间的精确对偶关系，进一步丰富了三维N=4超对称场论的对偶图景。
