Abstract: We propose that the 3d symplectic Chern-Simons theory $USp(2n)$ at level $k=n+\frac12+\ell$ with a single chiral multiplet in the fundamental representation is dual, for every pair of positive integers $(n,\ell)$, to the Abelian rank-$0$ theory $\mathcal{T}_{\ell,n}$ of Creutzig, Garner and Kim. The fundamental is pseudo-real, so the level is half-integral, there is no non-Abelian flavor symmetry, and the supersymmetry is enhanced to $\mathcal{N}=4$. Both sides have vanishing Higgs and Coulomb branches. We match the counting of supersymmetric vacua under real mass deformations for all $(n,\ell)$, and the supersymmetric indices in a number of cases. We then study in detail the case $\ell=1$, the minimal $USp(2n)$ Chern-Simons theory at level $n+\frac32$, whose dual is a $U(1)^{n}$ theory with a tadpole level matrix and a bare monopole superpotential.

</details>


### [265] [Mass for Particles from Extra Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08697)
*F. E. A. de Souza,M. O. Tahim,R. I. de Oliveira Junior,I. M. Macêdo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究codimension-two hedgehog膜世界中的几何质量产生机制，表明无质量粒子通过沿额外紧致角维度的运动获得有效四维质量。在规范量子化后可得到动力学决定的Kaluza-Klein谱。玻色子扇区无径向约束，但扩展到N=1,2自旋粒子模型后，自旋-曲率耦合会改变有效势并引入依赖于能级的KK模局域化模式。本文建立了通量支撑的codimension-two膜世界中几何质量产生的粒子力学实现，并突显了体几何、自旋与有效四维质量谱之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种不依赖传统Higgs机制的几何质量产生方案，在hedgehog膜世界构型中通过粒子的几何运动（沿紧致额外维），从粒子力学层面理解质量如何从体空间的拓扑/几何结构中涌现，并考察自旋自由度对质量谱和局域化模式的影响。

Method: 在codimension-two的hedgehog膜世界模型中，将无质量体粒子沿额外紧致角维的运动进行规范量子化，推导出有效四维质量对应的Kaluza-Klein谱。进一步将该分析扩展至N=1和N=2的自旋粒子模型，引入自旋-曲率耦合项以考察其对有效势的修正，并分析由此产生的能级依赖的KK模局域化模式。

Result: 玻色子扇区径向无约束，粒子在径向上不产生限制；而在引入自旋-曲率耦合后，自旋粒子的有效势被改变，导致KK模式出现依赖于能级的局域化分布。整个机制在通量支撑的codimension-two膜世界中成立，给出了粒子力学层面的几何质量产生谱。

Conclusion: 建立了自旋粒子力学框架下几何质量产生的具体实现，证实自旋-曲率耦合与膜世界体几何共同决定了有效四维质量谱及KK模的局域化特性，为理解额外维模型中的质量层级问题提供了新的粒子力学途径。

Abstract: We investigate a geometric mechanism for mass generation in a codimension-two hedgehog braneworld. A massless particle propagating in the bulk acquires an effective four-dimensional mass through its motion along the compact angular extra dimension, yielding a dynamically determined Kaluza-Klein spectrum after canonical quantization. While the bosonic sector exhibits no radial confinement, extending the analysis to N = 1, 2 spinning-particle models shows that the spin-curvature coupling modifies the effective potential and introduces a level-dependent localization pattern for the Kaluza-Klein modes. The results establish a particle-mechanics realization of geometric mass generation in flux-supported codimension-two braneworlds and highlight the interplay between bulk geometry, spin, and the effective four-dimensional mass spectrum.

</details>


### [266] [Splitting Dynamics of Multiply Quantized Vortices in Holographic Superfluid of Finite Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08831)
*Shanquan Lan,Xin Li,Jiexiong Mo,Yu Tian,Yu-Kun Yan,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过拟正规模线性扰动分析和全非线性实时数值模拟，研究了二维全息超流体中多重量子化涡旋（绕数n=5-8）的劈裂动力学，揭示了三个新物理现象：不稳定模数量不再严格服从2n-3公式（n=8时缺失p=2(n-1)模）、主导不稳定模随温度的转换出现跳变行为（n≥7时从p=2直接跳跃到更高阶模）、高绕数涡旋的单一劈裂图案可包含多个具有不同拓扑结构的子劈裂图案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多重量子化涡旋的劈裂不稳定性是涡旋动力学的核心问题，此前研究主要针对较低绕数涡旋，而对高绕数(n≥5)涡旋的不稳定性结构、主导模转换规律及劈裂图案的复杂度缺乏系统认识，特别是在全息超流体框架下的温度相关行为尚待探索。

Method: 将拟正规模的线性扰动分析与完全非线性的实时数值模拟相结合：线性分析用于确定各不稳定模的存在性和增长率，非线性模拟用于验证线性预测并揭示劈裂动力学的实际演化图案。研究对象为二维全息超流体中绕数n=5到8的涡旋，覆盖有限温度范围。

Result: 发现三个新现象：(1) n=8涡旋中p=2(n-1)不稳定模在整个温度范围内缺失，仅存在2n-4个不稳定模，违反经典的2n-3公式；(2) 主导不稳定模随温度的转换模式随绕数变化——n≤6时依次从p=2过渡到p=n，而n≥7时出现跳跃式转换（n=7在T=0.325Tc从p=2跳到p=4，在T=0.359Tc直接跳到p=7；n=8在T=0.302Tc直接从p=2跳到p=8）；(3) n=8涡旋的l=4劈裂图案在低、中、高温度下展示三种具有不同拓扑结构的子图案。非线性模拟确认了线性稳定性分析的预测。

Conclusion: 高绕数涡旋的劈裂不稳定性远比之前认识到的更为丰富，经典公式和规律在高绕数范围内不再严格成立，这为冷原子实验提供了新的可观测预测和理论预期。

Abstract: We study the splitting dynamics of multiply quantized vortices with winding numbers $n=5,6,7$ and $8$ in a two-dimensional holographic superfluid at finite temperature, by combining linear perturbation analysis of quasinormal modes with fully nonlinear real-time numerical simulations. Three new physical phenomena are revealed. First, the number of unstable modes no longer strictly follows the $2n-3$ formula as $n$ increases. For the vortex with $n=8$, the unstable mode with $p=2(n-1)$ is absent throughout the entire temperature range, so that only $2n-4$ unstable modes exist. Second, the transition of the dominant unstable mode with increasing temperature exhibits new characteristics. For vortices with $n\le 6$, the dominant mode changes sequentially as $p=2,3,\dots,n$, whereas for $n\ge 7$ jump-like transitions occur-for instance, for $n=7$ the dominant mode jumps from $p=2$ to $p=4$ at $T=0.325T_c$ and then directly to $p=7$ at $T=0.359T_c$, and for $n=8$ it jumps directly from $p=2$ to $p=8$ at $T=0.302T_c$. Third, a single splitting pattern of high-winding-number vortices can contain multiple sub-splitting patterns with distinct topological structures, as exemplified by the $l=4$ pattern of the $n=8$ vortex, which exhibits three sub-patterns at low, intermediate and high temperatures. The nonlinear simulations confirm the predictions of the linear stability analysis, and the implications of our results for cold-atom experiments are discussed.

</details>


### [267] [Covariant Phase Space and Carroll-Weyl $χ$ Symmetry of Carroll Non-BPS D$_p$-branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08874)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了Klusoň的规范Carroll非BPS D_p-膜作用的协变相空间。Carroll极限通过共轭对的收缩表述，规范一形式保持不变，而领头哈密顿约束在电场类和磁场类扇区中不同。文章分离了携带D-1个局域相空间自由度的非BPS通用扇区和需要二阶背景条件才携带D-2个自由度的速子真空扇区，并证明Carroll-Weyl χ变换保持辛形式并承认荷，但受约束阻碍，除高度受限的全局真空扇区外无法提升为第一类规范生成元，这与弦论中受限χ对称性可完成的情况形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Carroll极限下非BPS D_p-膜的规范结构和平移不变性。与null弦理论形成对比，后者中的受限χ-对称性可被提升为额外的第一类约束。需要通过协变相空间分析阐明Carroll非BPS D_p-膜约束代数的闭合性。

Method: 采用协变相空间方法，将Carroll极限表述为规范相空间的收缩。系统分析两类扇区（电场类与磁场类）的领头哈密顿约束代数的弱闭合与强闭合。分别考察非BPS通用扇区与速子真空扇区，利用秩条件刻画约束结构。最后通过χ变换对辛形式的作用及约束阻碍分析其可否作为第一类规范生成元。

Result: 非BPS通用扇区携带D-1个局域自由度，而速子真空扇区仅在施加二阶背景条件后携带D-2个自由度；电场类通用扇区存在特定秩条件，真空扇区中该条件被强化。Carroll-Weyl χ变换保持辛形式并存在对应荷，但受约束阻碍，仅在高度受限的全局真空扇区才可提升为第一类规范生成元，这与null弦理论的情况不同。

Conclusion: KluSonův非BPS D_p-膜的Carroll极限具有不同于null弦理论的约束结构，χ对称性无法一般性地提升为第一类约束，凸显了p-膜背景中Carroll对称性的特殊性。

Abstract: We analyze the covariant phase space of Klusoň's canonical Carroll non-BPS D\(_p\)-brane actions. The Carroll limit is formulated as a contraction of the canonical phase space: the canonical one-form is invariant under the scaling of conjugate pairs, while the leading Hamiltonian constraint differs between the electric-like and magnetic-like sectors. This difference controls the weak closure of the electric-like constraint algebra and the stronger closure of the magnetic-like Hamiltonian brackets. We separate the generic non-BPS sector, which carries \(D-1\) local phase-space degrees of freedom, from the tachyon-vacuum sector, which carries \(D-2\) only after imposing second-class background conditions. We also identify the rank condition for the generic electric-like sector and its strengthening in the vacuum sector. Finally, we show that the Carroll--Weyl \(χ\) transformation preserves the symplectic form and admits a charge, but is obstructed by the constraints and cannot be promoted to a first-class gauge generator except in a highly restricted global vacuum sector. This contrasts with null string theory, where a restricted \(χ\)-symmetry can be completed to an additional first-class constraint.

</details>


### [268] [Quantum spacetime and gravity from the IKKT matrix model: an invitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08955)
*Harold C. Steinacker*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于IKKT矩阵模型构建了一个时空、引力及基础物理的统一理论框架，该框架以非平凡矩阵真空（作为3+1维非交换时空）为核心，其背景解能够描述膨胀的均匀各向同性FLRW时空，并满足单圈有效作用或有限自由度每体积的要求。背景扰动产生传播场与弱耦合规范理论，同时给出非标准的量子引力描述并引入红外修正，而模型的最大超对称性对保证有效局域性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提供一个基于IKKT矩阵模型来统一描述时空、引力和基础物理的简洁且结构化的理论框架，克服传统量子引力方案的困难，并概念性地阐明非交换几何背景下如何自然涌现出3+1维时空与有效局域物理。

Method: 从IKKT矩阵模型出发，研究其非平凡真空（矩阵背景）作为非交换时空生成机制；通过构造满足均匀各向同性FLRW膨胀特性的特定背景类，并验证其在单圈有效作用层面的解性（保持每体积有限自由度）；进而分析背景扰动在该框架中如何涌现规范场传播与量子引力描述，并利用模型的最大超对称性来保证有效局域性。

Result: 构建了一类描述膨胀均匀各向同性FLRW时空的矩阵背景，它们是单圈有效作用的解且具有每体积有限自由度；背景扰动的有效低能理论表现为弱耦合规范理论，同时引力的描述是非标准的并伴随红外修正；确认了最大超对称性对于维持有效局域性的关键作用。

Conclusion: IKKT矩阵模型为时空引力与基础物理提供了一个概念上自洽且结构上统一的替代性描述框架，其中时空几何从矩阵背景的非平凡结构中自然涌现，量子引力呈现出与标准方法不同的红外修正特征，为未来深入探索该框架的动力学与可观测预言奠定了基础。

Abstract: This is a concise and compact introduction to a framework for spacetime, gravity, and fundamental physics based on the IKKT matrix model, focusing on conceptual and structural aspects. The approach rests on nontrivial vacua or matrix backgrounds, which serve as 3+1-dimensional noncommutative spacetime. We discuss a specific class of backgrounds describing expanding homogeneous and isotropic FLRW spacetime, which provide a solution of the one-loop effective action with finitely many dof per volume. Perturbations of the background give rise to fields propagating on spacetime governed by a weakly-coupled gauge theory, as well as a nonstandard description of (quantum) gravity leading to some IR modifications. Maximal supersymmetry of the model is essential to ensure effective locality.

</details>


### [269] [5d SCFT fixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09007)
*Michele Del Zotto,Andrea Sangiovanni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的5d SCFT "fixtures"（支架），填补了具有非单李型全局对称性的5d SCFT构建模块的空白。这些fixtures物理上是希格斯分支RG流的结果，通过M理论在奇异三维流形上的几何实现来明确描述，从而使得构造具有非单李型味对称性的5d共形物质SCFT成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在5d SCFT的原子分类框架中，具有非单李型（non-simply laced）全局对称性的构建模块此前是缺失的，本文旨在通过引入新的5d UV固定点类别来填补这一空白。

Method: 引入"5d SCFT fixtures"这一新类别，它们由希格斯分支RG流产生，并通过M理论在奇异三维流形（singular threefolds）上的紧化给出明确几何实现。研究者进一步探讨了其圆约化与4d class-S型理论之间的联系。

Result: 成功明确了5d SCFT fixtures的几何构造，使得能够构建具有非单李型味对称性的5d共形物质SCFT，并建立了这类理论与4d class-S型理论通过圆约化的关联。

Conclusion: 本文完成了5d SCFT原子分类中缺失的非单李型全局对称性构建模块，为完整理解5d SCFT分类以及其与4d理论的维度约化关系提供了新的框架。

Abstract: In the context of the atomic classification of 5d SCFTs building blocks with non-simply laced global symmetries are lacking. In this work we fill this gap by introducing a new class of 5d UV fixed points (the 5d SCFT "fixtures"). Physically, 5d SCFTs fixtures are the result of a Higgs branch RG flow, realized geometrically by M-theory on singular threefolds which we identify explicitly. In particular, this enables the construction of 5d conformal matter SCFTs with non-simply laced flavor symmetry. Along the way, we discuss their circle reduction in relation to 4d theories of class-S type.

</details>


### [270] [M5 Instantons in ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09041)
*Jieming Lin,Jesse van Muiden,Zihan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究M理论中渐近AdS₄×S⁷/Z_k几何内的超对称M5-膜瞬子，其超对称嵌入由M5-膜κ-对称性决定，迫使瞬子局域化到等变不动点并缠绕(S³×S³)/Z_k。量子层面发现这些瞬子的单圈配分函数为零，提示体配分函数应改用超引力模和M2-膜瞬子展开，与ABJM理论S³配分函数的已知结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究M理论中渐近AdS₄×S⁷/Z_k几何的M5-膜瞬子存在性及其量子贡献，以理解其对偶ABJM理论的非微扰结构。

Method: 利用M5-膜κ-对称性确定瞬子的超对称嵌入条件，将瞬子局域化限制到渐近AdS₄几何中的等变不动点，并计算缠绕(S³×S³)/Z_k的瞬子配分函数。

Result: M5-膜瞬子的单圈配分函数为零，表明此类瞬子不贡献体配分函数，而超引力模与M2-膜瞬子才是展开的合适基。

Conclusion: M5-膜瞬子在量子层面上对ABJM对偶的描述不产生贡献，体配分函数应按超引力模和M2-膜瞬子展开，这与ABJM理论S³配分函数的已知结果一致，强化了全息对偶的非微扰一致性。

Abstract: We study the existence of supersymmetric M5-brane instantons in asymptotically AdS$_4 \times S^7/\mathbf{Z}_k$ geometries of M-theory, dual to the ABJM theory. The supersymmetric embeddings of these instantons are determined by the M5-brane $κ$-symmetry, which forces them to localise to the equivariant fixed points in the asymptotically AdS$_4$ geometry, and wrap $(S^3\times S^3)/\mathbf{Z}_k $. At the quantum level we find that the one-loop partition function of these instantons vanishes, suggesting that the bulk partition function is instead to be expanded in terms of supergravity modes and M2-brane instantons, in line with known results for the $S^3$ partition function of the dual ABJM theory.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Thick subcategories over weakly symmetric algebras with radical cube zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05781)
*Kaito Kimura*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限维弱对称代数（立方零根）的（稳定）模范畴中的厚子范畴。当Ext矩阵的谱半径为2时，包含该代数的厚子范畴可由某个阿贝尔范畴的Serre子范畴分类；当谱半径不为2时，仅存在平凡厚子范畴。作为应用，文章首次构造了非完全交的交换Noetherian Gorenstein dominant局部环的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限维代数的模范畴结构是表示论的核心问题之一，厚子范畴的分类有助于深入理解模范畴的几何与同调性质。弱对称代数因其与Gorenstein环的同调代数密切相关而备受关注，立方零根情形具有相对简洁但非平凡的结构，可作为研究厚子范畴分类的合适切入点。

Method: 作者利用Ext矩阵的谱半径作为关键不变量，将弱对称立方零根代数划分为两类：谱半径为2（即谱半径达到最大值的情况）与谱半径不等于2的情况。在前者中，通过建立与某个阿贝尔范畴的Serre子范畴之间的等价对应来完成厚子范畴的分类；在后者中证明仅存在平凡厚子范畴。

Result: 获得了弱对称立方零根代数厚子范畴的完整分类：当Ext矩阵谱半径为2时，包含代数的厚子范畴（等价于稳定范畴的厚子范畴）完全由对应阿贝尔范畴的Serre子范畴刻画；当谱半径不等于2时，无非平凡厚子范畴。作为推论，构造了首个非完全交的交换Noetherian Gorenstein dominant局部环的实例。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了模范畴厚子范畴分类与谱论之间的联系，同时为Gorenstein dominant局部环理论提供了重要的反例性构造，表明"非完全交"的Gorenstein dominant环确实存在，推进了相关领域的认识。

Abstract: In this paper, we study thick subcategories of the (stable) module categories of finite-dimensional weakly symmetric algebras with radical cube zero. When the spectral radius of the Ext matrix is two, thick subcategories containing the algebra, equivalently thick subcategories of the stable category, are classified by Serre subcategories of a certain abelian category; when it is not two, only the trivial thick subcategories occur. As an application, this yields, to the best of our knowledge, the first examples of commutative Noetherian Gorenstein dominant local rings that are not complete intersections.

</details>


### [272] [A Note on Additive Diameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06023)
*Ernesto Ingrosso*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出关于群表示加法直径的两个结果：一是修正arXiv:2504.07573第一版中Proposition 5.3的证明（关于SL_n(C)在sl_n(C)上的加法直径上界），二是证明了该文Question 6.7中右边不等式diam^G_+(V,U) ≤ diam^𝔤_+(V,U)，该结果不需不可约性或连通性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前arXiv:2504.07573中的Proposition 5.3可能存在问题需要修正；同时其中提出的Question 6.7右边不等式需要证明。这两个结果对于理解群与李代数框架下加法直径的关系具有重要意义。

Method: 第一个结果的证明使用对称群在非对角矩阵位置上的作用进行平均化论证。第二个结果直接证明群作用下的加法直径不超过其李代数作用下的加法直径，无需不可约性或连通性假设。

Result: 证明了当ε∈(0,1/3)、n>9/ε²且dim U>εn²时，diam^{SL_n(C)}_+(sl_n(C),U)≤32/ε+8；并证明了diam^G_+(V,U)≤diam^𝔤_+(V,U)对所有复代数群G及其有限维模V成立。

Conclusion: 本文修正了前述论文中的命题证明，并回答了一个开放问题中的不等式方向，且该不等式在非常一般的条件下成立。所用平均化方法已进一步发展为arXiv:2609.03882中的内容。

Abstract: We record two results on additive diameters of group representations. First we give a corrected version of Proposition 5.3 from the first arXiv version of arXiv:2504.07573. Let $0<\varepsilon<1/3$, let $n>9/\varepsilon^2$, and let $U\leq\mathfrak{sl}_n(\mathbb{C})$ with $\dim U>\varepsilon n^2$. Then $\operatorname{diam}^{\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbb{C})}_{+}(\mathfrak{sl}_n(\mathbb{C}),U)\leq 32/\varepsilon+8$. The proof uses an averaging argument for the action of the symmetric group on the off-diagonal matrix positions. This argument was subsequently developed further in arXiv:2609.03882. We also prove the right-hand inequality in Question 6.7 of arXiv:2504.07573. If $G$ is a complex algebraic group, $V$ is a finite-dimensional $G$-module, $U\leq V$, and $\mathfrak g=\operatorname{Lie}(G)$, then $\operatorname{diam}^{G}_{+}(V,U)\leq\operatorname{diam}^{\mathfrak g}_{+}(V,U)$. No irreducibility or connectedness assumption is needed.

</details>


### [273] [PAGR: Proof-Carrying Algebraic-Geometric Retrieval: A Quiver-, Provenance-, and Sheaf-Theoretic Framework for Grounded LLM Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06127)
*Xingting Wang,Min Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出PAGR（Proof-Carrying Algebraic-Geometric Retrieval）框架，将检索增强生成中三个被混淆的问题——知识认证、潜在表示和组合语义——在数学上严格分离，通过符号层（typed quiver、路径方程、Horn包含）与几何层（quiver表示、cellular sheaf）的交互，实现"学习几何可以排序证据但不能认证知识"的认识论分离原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成通常被表述为统计信息检索问题，基于图的变体引入了关系结构，但该结构的数学地位常常未被充分规定。三个不同的问题经常被混淆：哪些陈述被认证为知识、哪些潜在表示对检索有用、哪些多跳组合在语义上可接受。这种混淆阻碍了对系统可靠性的形式化保证。

Method: PAGR框架包含：符号层是一个由typed quiver、路径方程和正Horn包含生成的多种类关系理论；quiver表示将内积空间分配给实体类型、线性算子分配给关系；cellular sheaf度量局部到全局的一致性；semiring provenance记录推导并支持机器可检查的证书。核心是认识论分离原则：学习到的几何可能排序和组织证据，但不能将假设提升为认证的真值。证明了认证标准在学习组件任意替换下的不变性。

Result: 主要结果包括：认证标准的替换不变性证明、条件完备性界、将等距群识别为基于残差检索的对称性、一个上同调一致性诊断工具，以及用于可行路径扩展的有界双模拟索引。

Conclusion: PAGR是一种数学架构，用于区分系统应在何处寻找证据与被允许视作知识的内容，从而为RAG系统提供可证的安全性和可靠性边界。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation is usually formulated as a statistical information-retrieval problem. Graph-based variants add relational structure, but the mathematical status of that structure is often left underspecified. Three distinct questions tend to be conflated: which statements are certified as knowledge, which latent representations are useful for retrieval, and which multi-hop compositions are semantically admissible.
  We propose Proof-Carrying Algebraic-Geometric Retrieval (PAGR), a framework that separates these questions mathematically. Its symbolic layer is a many-sorted relational theory generated by a typed quiver, path equations, and positive Horn inclusions. A quiver representation assigns inner-product spaces to entity types and linear operators to relations. A cellular sheaf measures local-to-global consistency. Semiring provenance records derivations and supports machine-checkable certificates.
  The central principle is epistemic separation: learned geometry may rank and organize evidence, but cannot promote a hypothesis to certified ground truth. We show the certification criterion is invariant under arbitrary replacement of learned components. Further results include a conditional completeness bound, identification of the isometry group as the relevant symmetry for residual-based retrieval, a cohomological consistency diagnostic, and a bounded-bisimulation index for admissible-path expansion.
  PAGR is a mathematical architecture for separating where a system should look from what it is allowed to treat as knowledge.

</details>


### [274] [Criteria for discrete decomposability and admissibility in the branching problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06458)
*Masatoshi Kitagawa*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了约化李群G的不可约表示限制到约化子群H时离散可分解的充分条件。作者在之前工作中证明了对对称对(G,H)该条件充分，本文在G满足一定假设（如G是线性的）下，将充分性推广到任意约化子群H。作为应用，证明了当h的紧致因子在某种意义下是极大的时候，离散可分解性与h-可容许性是等价的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于不可约表示限制到子群时的离散可分解性，T. Kobayashi给出了基于相伴簇的必要条件。此前作者已证明该条件对对称对也是充分的，自然的问题是：这一充分性是否对更一般的约化子群也成立？本文旨在回答这一开放问题，建立更广泛的离散可分解性判别准则。

Method: 本文采用表示论与几何分析相结合的方法。在G满足适当假设（如G为线性约化群）的框架下，利用相伴簇技术与分支定律的分析工具，将之前针对对称对建立的充分性证明推广至任意约化子群H的情形。证明中可能涉及对不可约表示限制分解中连续谱的排除技术以及约化子群结构性质的分析。

Result: 证明了在G满足一定假设（例如G是线性的）下，Kobayashi的相伴簇必要条件对于任意约化子群H也是离散可分解的充分条件。并由此推出：当h的紧致因子在某种极大意义下时，离散可分解性与h-可容许性（即表示作为h-模分裂为有限重数的不可约表示直和）等价。

Conclusion: 本文将对离散可分解性的判别从对称对情形成功推广到一般约化子群，给出了在G满足线性等假设下的完整充分必要条件。这一结果深化了对约化李群表示限制分解结构（离散部分与连续谱）的理解，并建立了离散可分解性与h-可容许性之间的重要等价关系。

Abstract: In this paper, we study when the restriction of an irreducible representation of a reductive Lie group $G$ to a reductive subgroup $H$ is discretely decomposable. T.\ Kobayashi gave a necessary condition for discrete decomposability in terms of associated varieties. In a previous paper, we proved that the condition is also sufficient if $(G, H)$ is a symmetric pair. The purpose of this paper is to prove the sufficiency for any reductive subgroup $H$ under some hypothesis on $G$ (e.g., $G$ is linear). As an application, we show that discrete decomposability is equivalent to $\mathfrak{h}$-admissibility when the compact factor of $\mathfrak{h}$ is maximal in some sense.

</details>


### [275] [A hereditary theorem for rigid and pseudorigid components of Lusztig's nilpotent varieties in type $A$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06601)
*Erez Lapid,Mark Shusterman*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究A型Lusztig幂零簇的不可约分量（由S_n中排列σ对应的C_σ）的几何性质：刻画出C_σ⊕C_σ仍为不可约分量的排列σ的组合条件。这类排列称为"伪光滑"（pseudosmooth），可通过直接和递归及删除合适角落来描述，其终态为8个具体排列的"条目膨胀为连续递减块"形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Lusztig幂零簇不可约分量的结构是表示论与代数几何中的重要问题。本文关注C_σ与其自身的直和是否仍为不可约分量这一几何性质，旨在用排列的组合语言完全刻画该性质，从而揭示不可约分量的加法（直和）封闭性规律。

Method: 采用组合方法刻画"伪光滑"排列。核心工具是一个关于具有不相交极值点的多段（multisegment）分解的遗传性质（hereditary property），并利用直接和递归与删除合适角落的递归描述来构造所有满足条件的排列。

Result: 给出C_σ⊕C_σ是不可约分量的充要组合条件：σ为"伪光滑"排列，由递归（直接和与删除角落）生成，终态为将8个固定排列（3412, 4231, 35142, 42513, 45312, 426153, 463152, 526413）的条目膨胀为连续递减块所得的全部排列。

Conclusion: 通过引入多段分解的遗传性质作为新工具，将C_σ⊕C_σ的不可约分量性质完全转化为排列σ的纯组合刻画（伪光滑性），给出了穷尽性描述。

Abstract: For irreducible components $C_σ$ of Lusztig's nilpotent varieties of type $A$ with graded dimension $(1,2,\dots,n,n-1,\dots,1)$ arising from permutations $σ\in S_n$, we characterize the property that $C_σ\oplus C_σ$ is an irreducible component combinatorially in terms of $σ$. The permutations that occur, which we call \emph{pseudosmooth}, are described by a recursion on direct sums and deleting suitable corners, whose terminal cases are the permutations obtained from \[ 3412,\quad 4231,\quad 35142,\quad 42513,\quad 45312,\quad 426153,\quad 463152,\quad 526413 \] by inflating the entries into consecutive decreasing blocks. The main new input is a hereditary property valid for decompositions of multisegments with disjoint extreme points.

</details>


### [276] [Invariant Jacobian and the Center of a Homogeneous Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06719)
*Yue Hu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在群作用下具有不变Jacobian的齐次形式。对于有限维不可约复表示，证明了由形式的中心定义的典范分解要么是平凡的，要么给出一个不可约系统，这决定了Jacobian J(f)的模结构。该理论应用于对称群，并分类了自然置换模上具有不变Jacobian的齐次形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解齐次形式在群作用下其Jacobian保持不变的结构，特别是在有限维不可约复表示框架下，通过形式的中心定义的典范分解来刻画Jacobian的模结构，并将此理论应用于对称群的自然置换模分类问题。

Method: 利用有限维不可约复表示理论，分析形式的中心所定义的典范分解；证明该分解要么平凡要么构成不可约性系统（imprimitivity system）；通过该系统确定Jacobian J(f)的模结构，并将理论推广应用到对称群上对自然置换模进行分类。

Result: 证明了有限维不可约复表示中由形式的中心定义的典范分解的性质，并利用该结果完成了对称群自然置换模上具有不变Jacobian的齐次形式的分类。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了群作用下齐次形式的典范分解与其Jacobian模结构之间的密切联系，并在对称群的框架下给出了完整的分类结果。

Abstract: We study homogeneous forms with invariant Jacobian under group actions. For finite-dimensional irreducible complex representations, we show that the canonical decomposition defined by the center of the form is either trivial or gives a system of imprimitivity, which determines the module structure of Jacobian $J(f)$. We apply the theory to symmetric groups and classify homogeneous forms with invariant Jacobian on the natural permutation module.

</details>


### [277] [Derived Reconstruction of Semisimple Tensor and Module Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06753)
*Sheng Tan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究半单张量范畴及其半单模范畴的有界导出范畴，通过万有分次群的整值特征和整数偏差分类了有界幺半t-结构，并利用模分量的稳定子子群分类了相容模t-结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半单张量范畴及其模范畴可能具有无限多个简单同构类，这为研究其有界导出范畴中的t-结构带来了复杂性。此前对有限型或特定条件下的分类不再适用，需要一个更一般性的框架来理解和分类这类导出范畴中的幺半t-结构和模t-结构。

Method: 本文利用万有分次群（universal grading group）的整值特征（integer-valued characters）以及一个整数偏差（integer deviation）来分类有界幺半t-结构；对于相容的模t-结构，则利用模分量的稳定子子群（stabilizer subgroups）进行分类。文中证明了相应t-结构的心与原范畴之间具有张量等价和模等价关系。

Result: 证明了有界幺半t-结构与万有分次群的整值特征及整数偏差之间存在一一对应关系；兼容模t-结构由模分量的稳定子子群分类。对应的核（heart）与原张量范畴及模范畴张量/模等价。由此推出幺半导出等价和相干导出模等价分别可以恢复张量范畴和其模范畴。

Conclusion: 本文建立了半单张量范畴（允许无穷多简单对象）有界导出范畴中幺半t-结构与模t-结构的完整分类理论，并表明该分类具有重建性——幺半导出等价可以完全恢复原张量范畴，相干导出模等价可以完全恢复原模范畴。

Abstract: We study bounded derived categories of semisimple tensor categories and their semisimple module categories. These categories may have infinitely many simple isomorphism classes. We classify bounded monoidal $t$-structures by integer-valued characters of the universal grading group and an integer deviation. We also classify compatible module $t$-structures using stabilizer subgroups of the module components. The corresponding hearts are tensor and module equivalent to the original categories. It follows that a monoidal derived equivalence and a coherent derived module equivalence recover both the tensor category and its module category.

</details>


### [278] [$q$-Oper Structures on a Formal Punctured Disc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07588)
*Rudrendra Kashyap,Rahul Singh*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在形式穿孔圆盘上，每个$(G,q)$-联络都允许一个$(G,q)$-oper结构，其中$G$是连通的约化复代数群，$q$不是单位根。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立$(G,q)$-联络与$(G,q)$-oper结构之间的对应关系，这是量子化几何与模空间理论中的重要问题。证明其存在性是理解这类几何结构的基石。

Method: 作者运用复代数群的表示论与形式穿孔圆盘上的微分模理论，通过构造适当的方式为任意$(G,q)$-联络赋予$(G,q)$-oper结构，从而证明其存在性。

Result: 成功证明了在给定的形式穿孔圆盘上与约化复代数群$G$及非单位根$q$相关联的每一个$(G,q)$-联络均具备$(G,q)$-oper结构。

Conclusion: 这一结果表明$(G,q)$-oper结构在$(G,q)$-联络的模空间中普遍存在，为后续研究相关模空间的结构与几何性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected reductive complex algebraic group and let $q\in\mathbb{C}^{\times}$ be not a root of unity. We prove that every $(G,q)$-connection on a formal punctured disc admits a $(G,q)$-oper structure.

</details>


### [279] [Koszul duality for finite-dimensional absolutely Koszul algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06768)
*A. M. Bouhada*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文通过Koszul对偶描述极小投射分解的线性部分，证明了有限维Koszul代数Λ是绝对Koszul的当且仅当其Koszul对偶代数Λ^{!}是分次左余凝聚的。由此推出每个有限维分次模在绝对Koszul代数上具有有理Poincaré级数，并证明了有限整体线性缺陷的绝对Koszul代数具有有限分次有限维数，最后细化了绝对Koszul代数的Koszul对偶理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画绝对Koszul代数的本质特征，并回应Green等人提出的关于Koszul对偶代数的问题。绝对Koszul代数作为Koszul代数的推广，其结构性质（如模的Poincaré级数、有限维数等）尚需深入探索，作者希望借助Koszul对偶这一工具获得统一的刻画。

Method: 利用Koszul对偶将极小投射分辨的线性部分用对偶代数描述，建立绝对Koszul性与对偶代数的分级左余凝聚性之间的等价关系。结合线性缺陷（linearity defect）与分次有限维数等工具，推广并精化Koszul对偶定理。

Result: 获得了绝对Koszul代数等价于其对偶代数为分次左余凝聚这一充要条件；表明绝对Koszul代数上所有有限维分次模的Poincaré级数均为有理函数；有限全局线性缺陷的绝对Koszul代数具有有限分次有限维数；并通过Koszul对偶代数回答了Green等人的问题。

Conclusion: 论文建立了一个闭合的理论框架，将绝对Koszul性、对偶代数的余凝聚性、Poincaré级数的有理性与有限维数的有限性联系起来，深化了对绝对Koszul代数结构及其Koszul对偶的理解。

Abstract: Let \(Λ\) be a finite-dimensional Koszul algebra. Using a description of the linear part of minimal projective resolutions in terms of Koszul duality, we prove that \(Λ\) is absolutely Koszul if and only if its Koszul dual \(Λ^{!}\) is graded left co-coherent. As a consequence, every finite-dimensional graded module over a finite-dimensional absolutely Koszul algebra has a rational Poincaré series.We further show that an absolutely Koszul algebra of finite global linearity defect has finite graded finitistic dimension. Finally, we refine Koszul duality for finite-dimensional absolutely Koszul algebras. As a further application, we answer a question of Green et al.~\cite{12} by means of the Koszul dual algebra.

</details>


### [280] [A short note on Arthur parameters with singular orbit closures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06793)
*Kristaps John Balodis*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Balodis、Cunningham、Fiori和Zhang先前工作中提出的关于GL_n/F（F为p-adic域）的Arthur参数分解问题，证明每个Arthur参数ψ可分解为ψ_1⊕...⊕ψ_r，使得相关Vogan簇中的轨道C_ψ有光滑闭包当且仅当每个C_{ψ_i}光滑，且C_{ψ_i}有光滑闭包当且仅当它是开轨道或闭轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决先前Balodis、Cunningham、Fiori和Zhang工作中留下的关于Arthur参数的一个开放性问题，具体是在GL_n/F（p-adic域）情形下的轨道闭包光滑性问题。

Method: 利用Vogan簇理论，将Arthur参数ψ分解为不可约子参数ψ_i的直和，并通过分析每个分量对应的轨道C_{ψ_i}在Vogan簇中的闭包光滑性来建立判别准则。关键论证是证明光滑闭包的性质在每个分量上是独立的，并且仅当轨道为开或闭时其闭包才光滑。

Result: 证明了每个Arthur参数ψ都可分解为ψ≅ψ_1⊕...⊕ψ_r，使得Vogan簇中C_ψ=C_{ψ_1}×...×C_{ψ_r}的闭包光滑当且仅当每个C_{ψ_i}的闭包光滑；此外，C_{ψ_i}的闭包光滑当且仅当C_{ψ_i}是开轨道或闭轨道。

Conclusion: 完整解决了先前工作中关于Arthur参数在GL_n/p-adic域情形下轨道闭包光滑性的判定问题，给出了清晰的结构分解准则。

Abstract: This short article resolves a question about Arthur parameters highlighted in previous work of Balodis, Cunningham, Fiori and Zhang, in the case of $\mathrm{GL}_n/F$ where $F/\mathbb{Q}_p$ is a $p$-adic field. Namely, we prove that every Arthur parameter $ψ$ of $\mathrm{GL}_n/F$, can be factored into Arthur parameters $ψ\cong ψ_1\oplus\cdots \oplusψ_r$ such that the orbit $C_ψ=C_{ψ_1}\times\cdots\times C_{ψ_r}\subseteq V_{λ_{ψ_1}}\times \cdots \times V_{λ_{ψ_r}}$ in the associated Vogan variety has smooth closure if and only if each $\bar{C}_{ψ_i}$ is smooth for $i=1, \ldots, r$, and that $C_{ψ_i}$ has smooth closure if and only if it is open or closed.

</details>


### [281] [Quasi-split iYangians: minimalistic presentations and coideal structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08269)
*Binhe Huang,Kang Lu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了与ADE型拟分裂对称对（具有非平凡图对合）相关的iYangian（即Drinfeld current展现下的扭曲Yangian），建立了由零次和一次生成元构成的最小化展现，分离处理了A_{2n}型的孤立秩二情形，并构建了拟分裂iYangian到Yangian的显式单射同态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: iYangian（扭曲Yangian的Drinfeld current展现）是量子群与I对称相关的重要代数对象，其最小化展现和与Yangian的关系是该领域的核心问题。此前工作（如arxiv:2511.07136）在分裂情形下已有部分结果，但拟分裂对称对情形（尤其涉及非平凡图对合的ADE型与A_{2n}型的孤立秩二情形）尚未被系统处理。

Method: 作者针对ADE型中具有非平凡图对合的拟分裂对称对，建立最小化呈现；对A_{2n}型将孤立秩二情形（需要额外两条关系）与高秩情形分开处理；通过构造显式单射同态嵌入Yangian并识别像为右余理想子代数，建立Drinfeld展现与J展现之间的同构；最后推导Drinfeld currents及其余积的三角估计，应用于有限维Yangian模限制得到的^imath-ell-权。

Result: 建立了最小化生成元体系，证明了在秩至少为2时分裂iYangian最小化展现中的额外关系是冗余的（强化了arxiv:2511.07136的定理3.1）；构造了从拟分裂iYangian到Yangian的显式单射同态，给出了Drinfeld与J两种展现的同构，并获得了Drinfeld currents三角估计及其对^imath-ell-权的应用。

Conclusion: 本文完整处理了ADE型拟分裂对称对的iYangian的最小化展现问题，解决了A_{2n}型孤立秩二情形的特殊情况，揭示了高秩情形下相邻非中心轨道迫使额外关系成立的机制，并通过嵌入Yangian建立了不同展现间的桥梁，为^imath-ell-权理论提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We study iYangians, namely twisted Yangians in the Drinfeld current presentation, associated with quasi-split symmetric pairs of type $\mathsf{ADE}$ with nontrivial diagram involution. We establish minimalistic presentations in terms of degree-zero and degree-one generators. Type $\mathsf A_{2n}$ is treated separately: the isolated rank-two case requires two additional relations, whereas in higher rank these relations are forced by the neighboring noncentral orbit. As a by-product, we strengthen Theorem 3.1 in arxiv:2511.07136 by proving that the extra relation in the minimalistic presentation of split iYangian is redundant whenever $\mathfrak g$ has rank at least two. We also construct explicit injective homomorphisms from quasi-split iYangians into Yangians, identify their images as right coideal subalgebras, and obtain isomorphisms between the Drinfeld and $J$ presentations. Finally, we derive triangular estimates for the Drinfeld currents and their coproducts and apply them to the ${}^\imath\ell$-weights arising by restriction from finite-dimensional Yangian modules.

</details>


### [282] [Affine and cyclotomic Brauer categorification via $\imath$-Kac--Moody $2$-categories: the half-integral type $\operatorname{AIII}$ case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07018)
*Mengmeng Gao,Hebing Rui,Linliang Song*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在仿射Brauer范畴（循环Brauer/循环Nazarov--Wenzl代数）与半整数拟分裂AIII型imath-Kac--Moody 2-范畴之间建立了范畴桥梁，构造了两种方向的对应关系，并证明了相应循环Brauer范畴的局部幺代数与主2-表示的前导环商局部幺代数同构，从而给出循环Brauer/Nazarov--Wenzl代数的自然Z-分次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶Schur--Weyl对偶中涉及B,C,D型李代数的抛物范畴O时会出现循环Brauer/循环Nazarov--Wenzl代数，但这类代数与Kac--Moody型范畴化的联系远不如A型理论（循环Hecke代数对应范畴化）那样成熟。作者希望填补这一空白，为循环Brauer代数建立imath-Kac--Moody范畴化框架。

Method: (1) 构造仿射Brauer范畴在局部Schurian范畴上的作用，其点谱恰为1/2+Z，由此得到广义幂零的U^imath_+偶分量2-表示；(2) 反之证明U^imath_+的任何幂零2-子表示携带兼容的仿射Brauer作用，点谱包含于1/2+Z；(3) 将上述构造应用于环商，证明u-可容许循环Brauer范畴所附的局部幺代数与U^imath_+主2-表示的相应环商的局部幺代数同构，从而导出Z-分次。

Result: 建立了仿射Brauer范畴与Bao--Shan--Wang--Webster的AIII型imath-Kac--Moody 2-范畴之间双向往返对应；证明u-可容许半整数循环Brauer代数的局部幺代数同构于对应imath-2-表示环商的局部幺代数，首次给出该类代数的imath-Kac--Moody 2-范畴化实现及自然Z-分次。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了迈向Brauer型Brundan--Kleshchev--Ariki理论的范畴化与分次框架，其中余理想代数和imath-典范基有望取代普通量子群和典范基。

Abstract: Cyclotomic Brauer or cyclotomic Nazarov--Wenzl algebras arise in higher Schur--Weyl dualities involving parabolic categories $\mathcal O$ for Lie algebras of types $B,C$ and $D$. Their connection with Kac--Moody-type categorification is substantially less developed than the corresponding type $A$ theory for cyclotomic Hecke algebras.
  We construct a categorical bridge between affine Brauer-type representation theory and the half-integral quasi-split type $\operatorname{AIII}$ $\imath$-Kac--Moody $2$-category of Bao--Shan--Wang--Webster. More precisely, an action of the affine Brauer category on a locally Schurian category, with dot spectrum exactly $\frac{1}{2}+\mathbb Z$, determines a generalized nilpotent $2$-representation of the even component $\mathfrak U^{\imath}_{+}$. Conversely, every nilpotent $2$-subrepresentation of an ambient locally Schurian $2$-representation of $\mathfrak U^{\imath}_{+}$ carries a compatible affine Brauer action whose dot spectrum is contained in $\frac{1}{2}+\mathbb Z$. Applying these constructions to cyclotomic quotients, we prove that the locally unital algebra attached to a $\mathbf u$-admissible cyclotomic Brauer category is isomorphic to the locally unital algebra attached to the corresponding cyclotomic quotient of the principal $2$-representation of $\mathfrak U^{\imath}_{+}$. Consequently, the associated cyclotomic Brauer (or cyclotomic Nazarov--Wenzl) algebras acquire natural $\mathbb Z$-gradings. To our knowledge, this is the first such categorical realization of $\mathbf u$-admissible half-integral cyclotomic Brauer algebras by means of an $\imath$-Kac--Moody $2$-category. It provides the categorical and graded framework toward a Brauer-type extension of the Brundan--Kleshchev--Ariki theory in which coideal algebras and $\imath$-canonical bases are expected to replace ordinary quantum groups and canonical bases.

</details>


### [283] [Representation and Extending structure of a Multiplicative Lie algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07359)
*Neeraj Kumar Maurya,Sumit Kumar Upadhyay*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为乘法李代数建立了广义表示理论，研究了等价表示、不可约表示和完全可约表示，引入了乘法李代数在向量空间上的作用概念，并证明了作用与表示之间存在一一对应关系，还通过群研究了乘法李代数的扩张结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法李代数是一种推广李代数结构的代数体系，但目前缺乏系统的表示理论。作者旨在填补这一空白，为乘法李代数建立一般的表示理论框架。

Method: 首先定义乘法李代数的表示概念，然后研究等价表示、不可约表示和完全可约表示的性质；接着定义乘法李代数在向量空间上的作用，并证明该作用与表示之间存在双射对应；最后利用群来研究乘法李代数的扩张结构。

Result: 建立了乘法李代数的表示理论基本框架，证明了乘法李代数在向量空间上的作用与表示一一对应，并给出了通过群构造乘法李代数扩张结构的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为乘法李代数提供了系统的表示理论基础，其表示与作用的一一对应关系以及扩张结构的刻画为进一步研究乘法李代数的结构理论和表示论铺平了道路。

Abstract: In this paper, we attempt to develop a general notion of representation theory for multiplicative Lie algebras. Consequently, we study equivalent, irreducible, and completely reducible representations. We also introduce the notion of the action of a multiplicative Lie algebra on a vector space and show that there is a one-to-one correspondence between the action and the representation. We also studied the extended structure of a multiplicative Lie algebra through a group.

</details>


### [284] [Presenting mirabolic quantum Schur algebras $\mathcal{MS}_v(2,d)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08353)
*Hongjia Chen,Jian Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了幻向量量子Schur代数MS_v(2,d)的生成元和定义关系表示，证明了它同构于Rosso幻向量量子sl_2除以由P_d(k)和(ℓ-1)Q_d(k)生成的双边理想所得的商代数，并进一步确定了该商代数的分裂Wedderburn分解及基于权幂等元的等价表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻向量量子Schur代数MS_v(2,d)在量子群的表示理论中具有重要地位，但其精确的代数呈现（即生成元与关系）此前尚不明确，研究者希望给出其完整结构描述。

Method: 作者以自然满态射为出发点，从Rosso幻向量量子sl_2出发，通过确定该态射的核来确定定义关系。具体地，引入两类关键多项式P_d(k)和Q_d(k)，证明商代数同构于幻向量量子Schur代数，并利用Wedderburn理论（结合分裂分裂分解）及幂等元方法进一步刻画代数结构。

Result: 证明了MS_v(2,d) ≅ U_v^{mir}(2)/⟨P_d(k),(ℓ−1)Q_d(k)⟩，即给出了一组精确的定义关系（P_d(k)=0及(ℓ−1)Q_d(k)=0），并在此基础上确定了该商代数的分裂Wedderburn分解，同时提供了以权幂等元表达的等价呈现。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了幻向量量子Schur代数MS_v(2,d)作为幻向量量子sl_2商的代数结构，为后续研究该代数的表示论及模结构奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $v$ be an indeterminate. We give a presentation of the mirabolic quantum Schur algebra $\mathcal{MS}_v(2,d)$ in terms of generators and defining relations. More precisely, we determine the kernel of the natural epimorphism from Rosso's mirabolic quantum $\mathfrak{sl}_2$, $\mathbf U^{\mathrm{mir}}_v(2)=\langle e,f,k^{\pm 1},\ell\rangle$, onto $\mathcal{MS}_v(2,d)$. Setting $P_d(k)=\prod_{r=0}^{d}(k-v^{2r-d})$ and $Q_d(k)=\prod_{r=1}^{d}(k-v^{2r-d})$, we prove $\mathcal{MS}_v(2,d)\cong \mathbf U^{\mathrm{mir}}_v(2)/ \left\langle P_d(k),(\ell-1)Q_d(k)\right\rangle$. We further determine the split Wedderburn decomposition of this quotient and provide an equivalent presentation in terms of weight idempotents.

</details>


### [285] [Partial $F$-invariants and cluster categorifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08781)
*Peigen Cao,Ryo Fujita,Kota Murakami*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究簇代数中F-不变量的细化——偏F-不变量，建立了其在初始种子变化下的突变公式，证明了Reading猜想（非相容簇变量可通过g-向量的符号相干性加以分离），并证明偏F-不变量与偏E-不变量、极化R-矩阵积序（偏𝔡-不变量）在相应背景下的一致性，从而得到可达简单模极化积序的组合公式，并验证了Kirillov–Reshetikhin模之间极化积序的猜想显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F-不变量统一了加法分类中的E-不变量与幺半分类中的𝔡-不变量，是簇代数中重要的组合不变量。作者研究其细化版本（偏F-不变量），旨在建立突变公式并解决Reading猜想，同时在不同数学框架（表示论、量子仿射代数）之间建立对应统一关系。

Method: 通过定义偏F-不变量并在初始种子变化下推导其突变公式，利用g-向量的符号相干性来证明Reading猜想；进而通过比较偏F-不变量与偏E-不变量（对带位势箭图的可达装饰表示）以及偏𝔡-不变量（量子仿射代数中有限维可达单模的规范化R-矩阵积序）的一致性，实现分类框架的统一。

Result: 建立了偏F-不变量的突变公式；证明了Reading猜想；证明了在簇单项式情形下偏F-不变量与偏E-不变量和偏𝔡-不变量完全一致；由此得到可达单模积序的q-字符组合公式，并验证了KR模之间积序的猜想显式公式。

Conclusion: 该工作进一步巩固了簇代数理论中组合、表示论与量子群三个层面的内在统一性，为理解F-不变量的结构性质及其跨领域应用提供了新工具，并解决了一个重要的公开猜想。

Abstract: The $F$-invariant in cluster algebras is a combinatorial invariant that unifies the $E$-invariant from additive categorification and the $\mathfrak{d}$-invariant from monoidal categorification. In this paper, we study its refinement, the partial $F$-invariant, and establish its mutation formula under changes of the initial seed. As an application, we prove a conjecture of Reading, which asserts that the non-compatible cluster variables can be separated by sign-coherence of $g$-vectors upon varying the initial seed. We further show that, for cluster monomials, the partial $F$-invariants coincide with both the partial $E$-invariants for reachable decorated representations of quivers with potentials and the pole orders of normalized $R$-matrices (or partial $\mathfrak{d}$-invariants) for finite-dimensional reachable simple modules over quantum affine algebras. As consequences, we obtain a combinatorial formula for the pole orders for reachable simple modules in terms of $q$-characters; we verify the conjectural explicit formula for the pole orders between Kirillov--Reshetikhin modules.

</details>


### [286] [Quasi-projective dimension and Gorenstein projective dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07700)
*Yongyun Qin,Lanlan Yang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文从Gorenstein同调代数的角度研究了拟投射维数这一同调不变量，证明了在某些条件下拟投射维数与Gorenstein投射维数一致，并引入和研究了拟Gorenstein投射维数作为拟投射维数的Gorenstein类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gheibi、Jorgensen和Takahashi近期引入了拟投射维数作为经典投射维数的推广，本文旨在从Gorenstein同调代数的视角深入探究这一不变量的性质，并建立其与Gorenstein投射维数之间的联系。

Method: 首先证明拟投射维数在特定条件下与Gorenstein投射维数相等；其次引入拟Gorenstein投射维数的概念，将其作为拟投射维数的Gorenstein类比进行系统研究与讨论。

Result: 确立了拟投射维数与Gorenstein投射维数在特定条件下的等价性，并成功引入了拟Gorenstein投射维数这一新概念，初步建立了其基本理论框架。

Conclusion: 本文深化了对拟投射维数的理解，揭示了其与Gorenstein投射维数的内在联系，并通过引入拟Gorenstein投射维数扩展了Gorenstein同调代数的研究工具。

Abstract: Gheibi, Jorgensen and Takahashi recently introduced the quasi-projective dimension, a homological invariant that extends the classical projective dimension. In this paper, we investigate this invariant from the perspective of Gorenstein homological algebra. First, we show that the quasi-projective dimension coincides with the Gorenstein projective dimension under certain conditions. Second, we introduce and study the quasi-Gorenstein projective dimension as a Gorenstein analogue of the quasi-projective dimension.

</details>


### [287] [Splitting torsion classes in higher homological algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07953)
*Erlend D. Børve,Peter Jorgensen,Mads Hustad Sandøy*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在d-abelian范畴中推广了Hoshino经典定理，给出了d-扭转类为分裂扭转类的判据（特别是关于τ_d^-稳定性），并证明了在Dynkin A型等额外假设下，d-扭转类格子的"脊柱"元素恰为分裂d-扭转类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典表示论中，Hoshino定理刻画了扭转类何时是分裂的。作者希望将这一经典结果推广到更高的同调维数（d-abelian范畴），并进一步理解d-扭转类格子的结构——特别是哪些扭转类位于最大长度链上（即脊柱元素）。

Method: 作者在d-abelian范畴中引入了d-扭转类的概念，然后给出分裂性的判据，核心是d-扭转类对逆d-Auslander-Reiten平移τ_d^-的稳定性。接着将这些判据应用于满足额外假设的情形（如Dynkin A型），证明脊柱元素恰好对应分裂扭转类。

Result: 获得了分裂d-扭转类的判据（推广了Hoshino定理），并在Dynkin A型等满足额外假设的情形下证明了格子的脊柱元素（属于某条最大长度链的元素）恰为分裂d-扭转类。

Conclusion: 分裂d-扭转类与d-扭转类格子的脊柱之间存在精确对应关系，这推广了经典表示论中的结果并揭示了更高维表示论中扭转类格子的组合结构。

Abstract: Let $d \geqslant 1$ be an integer, $\mathscr{M}$ a suitable $d$-abelian category. There is a notion of higher torsion classes in $\mathscr{M}$, also known as $d$-torsion classes.
  We generalise a classic theorem of Hoshino by providing criteria for a $d$-torsion class $\mathscr{U}$ to be splitting, notably that $\mathscr{U}$ is stable under $τ_d^-$, the inverse $d$-Auslander--Reiten translation.
  We apply the criteria to show that under additional assumptions, which are satisfied in Dynkin type $A$, the elements of the spine of the lattice of $d$-torsion classes are precisely the splitting $d$-torsion classes. The spine consists of the elements which belong to a chain of maximal length.

</details>


### [288] [Atomic Decompositions of Lie Characters and the Dominant Weight Poset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08024)
*Felipe de Mattos Chafik Hindi,Waldeck Schutzer*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究复半单李代数不可约模V(λ)在当前优势权偏序集上的原子数a(μ,λ)的非负性问题。作者证明当Dynkin图的所有连通分支均为路径（即类型A_n, B_n, C_n, F_4, G_2）时原子数非负，并将其与显式权重空间的维数等同；通过D_4及三叉节点的限制构造说明该条件必要，证明原子数非负当且仅当Dynkin图为路径。此外，在优势室深处的一个显式范围内，a(μ,λ)等于λ-μ关于非单纯正根的Kostant剖分数，从而负原子数被限制在边界带状区域内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望回答原子数a(μ,λ)何时非负的问题。这些原子数是将特征标ch V(λ)在权多重度函数的Moebius变换意义下用W-不变基Θ_μ展开时的系数，其符号结构对理解李代数表示在不同优势权之间的"原子分解"以及支配序区间的组合本质至关重要。

Method: 利用crosscut定理将原子数转化为一个交错和，它由μ在[μ,λ]中覆盖上的子集所索引，每一项是至多2^{rankΦ}个权多重度。当Dynkin图为路径时，通过按路径顺序交替取提升算子（raising operators）的核与降低算子（lowering operators）的余核，构造出显式权重空间并证明a(μ,λ)等于其维数。对非路径情形（如D_4），构造反例族并利用限制到支撑集的方法推广到所有含三叉节点的不可约类型。

Result: 证明了：若Dynkin图每个连通分支均为路径，则所有a(μ,λ)≥0，且a(μ,λ)等于某个由提升/降低算子交替作用切出的显式权重空间的维数；D_4给出反例，说明条件是必要的；若不限制在优势权上，通过限制到λ-μ的支撑集，反例族可携带到每个含三叉节点的不可约类型，故一个不可约有限根系的所有原子数非负当且仅当其Dynkin图为路径（即A_n, B_n, C_n, F_4, G_2）。在优势室的显式深处范围内，a(μ,λ)等于λ-μ关于非单纯正根的Kostant剖分数，负原子数仅出现在边界带状区域。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了原子数非负的根系类型：恰好是Dynkin图为路径的类型。由负值仅可能在边界带出现以及深处区域与Kostant剖分数的联系可以看出，原子数的符号结构受Dynkin图形状深层约束，而Moebius变换与覆盖树的结构提供了将原子数与显式权重空间维数（Kostant剖分数）联系的组合框架。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{g}$ be a complex semisimple Lie algebra and, for a dominant integral weight $λ$, let $Θ_λ$ be the multiplicity-free sum of the weights of the irreducible module $V(λ)$. The $Θ_μ$ form a $\mathbb{Z}$-basis of the $W$-invariants, so there are unique integers $a(μ,λ)$, the atomic numbers, with $\mathrm{ch}\,V(λ) = \sum_μa(μ,λ)Θ_μ$ over dominant $μ$. We ask when they are nonnegative. They are the Moebius transform of the weight-multiplicity function on the dominant-weight poset, which the crosscut theorem turns into an alternating sum of at most $2^{\mathrm{rank}\,Φ}$ multiplicities indexed by subsets of the covers of $μ$ in $[μ,λ]$.
  We prove that $a(μ,λ)\ge 0$ whenever every connected component of the Dynkin diagram is a path, and identify the coefficient with the dimension of an explicit weight space: it is cut out of $V(λ)$ by alternately taking kernels of raising operators and cokernels of lowering operators, one per cover, in order along the path. Type $D_4$ shows the hypothesis is necessary. Put $β= α_1 + 2α_2 + 2α_3 + 2α_4$; for every dominant $μ$ with $\langleμ,α_2^\vee\rangle = 1$ and $μ+β$ dominant, $a(μ,μ+β)$ is $-2$ if $\langleμ,α_1^\vee\rangle = 0$ and $-1$ otherwise. Restriction to the support of $λ-μ$ carries this family into every irreducible type with a trivalent node. Thus an irreducible finite root system has all atomic numbers nonnegative exactly when its Dynkin diagram is a path, namely in types $A_n$, $B_n$, $C_n$, $F_4$, $G_2$.
  Deep in the dominant chamber, in an explicit range, $a(μ,λ)$ is the Kostant partition number of $λ-μ$ for the nonsimple positive roots; negative atomic numbers are therefore confined to boundary slabs.

</details>


### [289] [Essential algebra of the shifted Burnside biset functor with abelian shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08616)
*Olcay Coşkun,Ruslan Muslumov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限群G上的移位Burnside双函子kB_T的本质代数，在G的平凡商群与T的截断非同构时给出本质代数的完整描述，并在T为阿贝尔群且|T|在k中可逆时证明该代数可分解为一族矩阵代数的直和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Romero已处理G与T阶互素的情形，本文旨在将该结果推广到更一般的条件，并进一步在T为阿贝尔群的情形获得更完整的结构描述。

Method: 首先建立判定G×G×T的覆盖子群在星积意义下是否可通过更小阶群因子的准则（其中第一个准则对T不加任何假设），利用这些准则刻画本质代数，并通过链路类与约化子群直积分解代数结构。

Result: 当G的平凡商群不与T的截断同构时，完整描述本质代数kB_T(G)；当T为阿贝尔群且|T|在k中可逆时，证明kB_T(G)同构于群代数k[Out_T(A)]上矩阵代数的直和（A遍历G×T的约化子群的链路类），并给出其单模的参数化。

Conclusion: 本文推广了Romero的互素情形结果，建立了移位Burnside双函子本质代数的结构定理，为奇点等价与表示论的研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $T$ be a finite group and let $kB_T$ denote the shifted Burnside biset functor over a commutative ring $k$. We study the essential algebra $\widehat{kB_T}(G)$ of $kB_T$ at an arbitrary finite group $G$. We first establish criteria determining when a covering subgroup of $G\times G\times T$ factors, with respect to the star product, through a group of order strictly smaller than $|G|$; the first criterion places no hypothesis on $T$. As an application, we describe $\widehat{kB_T}(G)$ completely whenever no nontrivial quotient of $G$ is isomorphic to a section of $T$, extending the coprime case treated by Romero. When $T$ is abelian and $|T|$ is invertible in $k$, we prove that $\widehat{kB_T}(G)$ decomposes as a direct sum of matrix algebras over the group algebras of the groups $Out_T(A)$, where $A$ runs over the linkage classes of reduced subgroups of $G\times T$, and we parametrize the simple modules of $\widehat{kB_T}(G)$.

</details>


### [290] [The Auslander-Reiten conjecture for algebras with radical cube zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08679)
*Xiaojin Zhang,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于分裂的有限维代数A（其根J满足J^3=0），任意非投射模M若满足对所有i>0有Ext^i_A(M,A)=0，则在1到3s+1之间存在非零自扩张（s为单模同构类数）。由此推出A满足Auslander-Reiten猜想，进而代数闭域上所有根立方为零的有限维代数都满足该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Auslander-Reiten猜想是表示论中的经典开放问题，断言每个自正交生成模都是投射模。该猜想在一般情形下尚未解决，因此需要验证其在特定代数类（如根立方为零的代数）上是否成立。本文旨在针对根满足J^3=0的一类代数建立该猜想。

Method: 设A为分裂有限维代数，其根J满足J^3=0，s为单A-模的同构类数目。作者通过分析与自扩张相关的同调性质，证明任何满足Ext^i_A(M,A)=0（所有i>0）的非投射模M必然在1到3s+1之间某个次数上具有非零自扩张。这一结果直接蕴含Auslander-Reiten猜想成立。

Result: 证明了非投射模若对所有正次数满足Ext^i_A(M,A)=0，则必在1到3s+1之间存在非零自扩张；特别地，该类代数满足Auslander-Reiten猜想。作为推论，代数闭域上所有根立方为零的有限维代数均满足Auslander-Reiten猜想。

Conclusion: 本文成功验证了根立方为零的有限维代数类上Auslander-Reiten猜想成立，通过给出自扩张存在性的具体界（3s+1），为该猜想在更广泛代数类上的研究提供了新的方法和视角。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a split finite-dimensional algebra over a field whose radical $J$ satisfies $J^3=0$, and let $s$ be the number of isomorphism classes of simple $A$-modules. We prove that a non-projective module $M$ with ${\rm Ext}_A^i(M,A)=0$ for all $i>0$ has a non-zero self-extension in some degree between $1$ and $3s+1$. In particular, $A$ satisfies the Auslander--Reiten conjecture, which asserts that every self-orthogonal generator is projective. As a consequence, every finite-dimensional algebra over an algebraically closed field with radical cube zero satisfies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture.

</details>


### [291] [From Pre-triangulation to Triangulation: Obstructions and Exact Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08684)
*Ping He,Bo Le*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了Verdier八面体公理的一个新的等价表述：将预三角范畴中的初始方格与普通Hom群的某个商群中的障碍相关联，障碍消失等价于存在好的完备化，而TR4等价于可复合态射对应的方格中该障碍消失。这一重新表述揭示了预三角化之间的相对逆Heller比较与精确提升之间的关系，并产生了多个应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Verdier八面体公理（TR4）是三角范畴理论的核心公理之一，但其传统表述较为复杂且难以验证。作者希望找到TR4的一个更简洁、更可操作的等价形式，从而能够更方便地判断一个预三角范畴是否满足三角范畴公理。

Method: 作者将TR4重述为关于初始方格（initial square）的条件：每个初始方格在预三角范畴中定义一个普通Hom群的商群中的障碍类，障碍消失等价于该方格有好的完备化。进而，TR4等价于所有可复合态射伴随的方格障碍消失。这一框架还被转化为关于Freyd范畴中短正合序列的精确提升条件，即通过相对逆Heller比较来描述。

Result: 作为应用，作者证明了Díaz Cabrera--Muro的奇异预三角范畴在任意代数闭域上实际上是三角范畴。此外，构造了任意域上A_{3a-1}型预投射代数的标量族，以及特征二的若干Dynkin预投射代数的标量族；在这些族中，唯一三角的成员是零参数对应的对象。局部方程还产生了可分下降结果，包括非模等变化和Markman--Mehrotra K3形变范畴的典范三角化。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入障碍类给出了TR4的一个全新而实用的等价刻画，为判断预三角范畴是否三角化提供了有效工具，并通过多个具体应用（包括奇异预三角范畴的三角化、标量族构造以及可分下降）验证了该理论框架的广泛适用性和推进力。

Abstract: We give a new equivalent formulation of Verdier's octahedral axiom. An initial square in a pre-triangulated category determines an obstruction in a quotient of ordinary Hom groups. Its vanishing is equivalent to the existence of a good completion, and $\mathrm{TR4}$ is equivalent to this vanishing for the squares associated with composable morphisms. As a consequence, if two pre-triangulations have the same underlying additive category and one is triangulated, then the other satisfies $\mathrm{TR4}$ precisely when their relative inverse Heller comparison admits an exact lift along every short exact sequence in the Freyd category.
  As applications, we prove that the exotic pre-triangulated category of Díaz Cabrera--Muro is in fact a triangulated category over every algebraically closed field. We construct scalar families for type-$A_{3a-1}$ preprojective algebras over arbitrary fields and for a range of Dynkin preprojective algebras in characteristic two; in each family the zero parameter is the unique triangulated member. The local equations also yield separable descent, including canonical triangulations for non-modular equivariantizations and for the Markman--Mehrotra K3 deformation categories.

</details>


### [292] [Symplectic Dirac operators on homogeneous spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09111)
*Dan Ciubotaru,Gang Liu,Salah Mehdi,Nicolas Prudhon*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了齐次空间上的辛Dirac算子及其表示论作用。作者定义了辛Dolbeault算子并计算了其交换子作为经典Dirac算子平方的辛类比，给出了其满足Parthasarathy型公式的充要条件，并证明了条件不满足时三次扰动无法弥补该缺陷（与正交情形中Kostant三次Dirac算子形成对比）。此外还建立了由辛Dolbeault算子生成的sl₂结构，并导出了酉表示的Dirac型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将正交情形中经典的Dirac算子理论（如Parthasarathy和Kostant的Dirac算子）推广到辛几何/辛表示论的框架中，研究辛Dirac算子在表示论中的角色，并探索其能否产生类似Parthasarathy型公式和Dirac不等式的强大工具。

Method: 在齐次空间上定义辛Dirac算子，通过不变极化将其分解为两个辛Dolbeault算子；计算其交换子并与经典Dirac算子平方类比；对Parthasarathy型公式的存在性给出充要条件，并检验三次扰动项的可行性；利用sl₂结构推导各级Dirac型不等式。

Result: 给出了交换子满足Parthasarathy型公式的充要条件；证明条件不满足时不存在三次扰动能恢复该公式（区别于Kostant的立方Dirac算子）；建立了sl₂结构并推导出各级不等式，其中零级不等式恢复标准Parthasarathy-Dirac不等式，高级不等式提供新约束；对SU(1,n)中特定Kraljević角的表示，证明了级一不等式加强了某些满足显式最高权条件的K-类型的基本Parthasarathy不等式。

Conclusion: 辛Dirac算子理论为辛流形上的表示论提供了新的框架；虽然Parthasarathy型公式仅在特定条件下成立，但通过sl₂结构和Dirac型不等式仍然获得了对酉表示的有力约束，并发现了对SU(1,n)等特殊情形的新加强不等式。

Abstract: We define symplectic Dirac operators on homogeneous spaces and study their representation-theoretic role. For an invariant polarization, the symplectic Dirac operator decomposes into two symplectic Dolbeault operators. We compute their commutator as the natural symplectic analogue of the square of the classical Dirac operator. Our first main result gives a necessary and sufficient condition for this commutator to satisfy a Parthasarathy-type formula. We further prove that, whenever this condition fails, no cubic perturbation of the symplectic Dolbeault operators can yield such a formula, in contrast with Kostant's cubic Dirac operator in the orthogonal setting. As applications, we establish an ${\mathfrak s}{\mathfrak l}_2$-structure generated by the symplectic Dolbeault operators and derive Dirac-type inequalities for unitary representations of Hermitian symmetric spaces labelled by the levels of the symmetric powers of the antiholomorphic tangent space at the identity. The level-zero inequality recovers the standard Parthasarathy-Dirac inequality, while the higher levels inequalities yield new constraints. For $SU(1,n)$, we show that, for representations with a specific Kraljević corner, the level-one inequality strengthens all basic Parthasarathy inequalities of the first kind for particular $K$-types, precisely those satisfying an explicit highest-weight condition.

</details>


<div id='math.SG'></div>

# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [A contact-geometric variant of Smale's contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06431)
*Florian Buck,Christopher Schmidt,Kai Zehmisch*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文研究开旋转对称Darboux球上"垂直凸"接触形式（在紧集外与标准接触形式一致且Reeb流无俘获轨道）的空间拓扑。在三维和五维情形下，将该空间按紧支撑微分同胚商与可缩的幺正（monodromy）空间等同，并证明等变乘积分裂，进而得到标准接触形式的轨道是强形变收缩核。由此，三维中垂直凸接触形式空间可缩，五维中其同伦等价于紧支撑微分同胚群（因而连通）。定义标准接触结构的子空间在五维中具有相应紧支撑接触同胚群的同伦型，三维中可缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Smale关于二维圆盘紧支撑微分同胚群收缩定理的启发，作者希望将类似的拓扑刚性结果推广到接触几何设定中，研究接触形式空间中标准形式轨道的拓扑结构以及无俘获Reeb流这类动力学条件的拓扑刻画。

Method: 通过将垂直凸接触形式空间的模空间与幺正空间建立同胚对应，在维数3和5中利用低维接触拓扑技巧证明等变乘积分裂定理，从而将问题归约为对幺正空间可缩性的分析，并借助Smale定理式的思想完成形变收缩。

Result: 建立了几何空间与拓扑空间（幺正空间）之间的同胚/同伦对应；标准接触形式的轨道是强形变收缩核；三维垂直凸接触形式空间可缩，五维中同伦等价于紧支撑微分同胚群（连通）；标准接触结构子空间在五维中具有紧支撑接触同胚群的同伦型，三维中可缩。

Conclusion: 垂直凸接触形式空间（及其标准接触结构子空间）的拓扑结构在维数3和5下被完全确定，推广了Smale收缩定理到接触几何框架，揭示了动力学条件（无俘获轨道）对接触形式空间拓扑的强约束作用。

Abstract: Motivated by Smale's contraction of the compactly supported diffeomorphism group of the two-disc, we study contact forms on an open rotationally symmetric Darboux ball that agree with the standard contact form outside a compact set and whose Reeb flows have no trapped orbits. Such forms are called vertically convex.
  In dimensions three and five, we identify the quotient of their space by compactly supported diffeomorphisms with a contractible monodromy space and prove an equivariant product splitting. Consequently, the orbit of the standard contact form is a strong deformation retract. It follows that the space of vertically convex contact forms is contractible in dimension three and homotopy equivalent to the compactly supported diffeomorphism group, hence connected, in dimension five.
  The analogous subspace of forms defining the standard contact structure has the homotopy type of the corresponding compactly supported contactomorphism group in dimension five and is contractible in dimension three.

</details>


### [294] [Lusternik-Schnirelmann category of Lee Forms on locally conformally symplectic manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06709)
*Kenji Fukushi*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文将Farber关于闭1-形式的Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴理论引入局部共形辛（LCS）几何中，通过将其应用于Lee类来研究LCS结构。特别地，这一方法为第二类LCS结构提供了新的拓扑研究途径，并获得了该不变量在LCS爆破下的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭辛流形的Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴已有多种估计方法。LCS几何作为辛几何的推广涉及闭1-形式（Lee形式）。作者希望通过将Farber的闭1-形式范畴理论应用于Lee类，为LCS结构（尤其是第二类LCS结构）提供新的拓扑不变量和估计工具。

Method: 将Farber引入的关于闭1-形式的Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴概念推广到LCS几何中，具体做法是将该范畴理论作用于Lee类。通过分析该不变量在LCS爆破（blow-up）过程中的行为来建立下界估计。

Result: 成功引入了LCS几何中基于Lee类的Farber范畴理论，为第二类LCS结构提供了新的拓扑方法，并建立了一个在LCS爆破下不变量的下界估计。

Conclusion: Farber的闭1-形式范畴理论可以自然地融入LCS几何框架，作用于Lee类后形成了新的拓扑不变量，能够区分或刻画第二类LCS结构，并可用于LCS爆破下的几何约束分析。

Abstract: The Lusternik--Schnirelmann category of a closed symplectic manifold admits various estimates. Locally conformally symplectic (LCS) geometry is a generalization of symplectic geometry involving a closed one-form, called the Lee form. In this paper, we introduce Farber's Lusternik--Schnirelmann category for closed one-forms into LCS geometry by applying it to the Lee class. In particular, this provides a new topological approach to LCS structures of the second kind. We then obtain lower bounds for this invariant under LCS blow-ups.

</details>


### [295] [Mirror Counts of Spectral Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06821)
*Tom Graber,Mingyuan Hu,Eric Zaslow*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于凸格多边形相关的有理结点曲线计数与勒让德链环的rulings之间的对偶关系。作者用构造性层（constructible sheaves）的语言对相对Jacobian可积系统进行镜像对偶，并证明rulings计数与原问题$N_\Delta$等价。文中通过多种方法（构造性方法、热带曲线计数、对数Gromov-Witten局部化）验证了不同计数途径的一致性，并猜想ruling分解可以恢复Block-Göttsche的细化热带不变量，从而编码更高亏格的对数Gromov-Witten不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数几何与辛几何交汇处，理解曲线计数的不变性及其镜像对偶是核心问题。作者希望通过可积系统与构造性层的语言为经典的有理结点曲线计数问题提供新的视角和计算工具，并建立其与勒让德链环理论、热带几何及对数Gromov-Witten理论之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者将相对Jacobian的线性系统定义为可积系统，利用构造性层在二维环面（微支撑在勒让德链环上）的语言建立镜像对偶。随后定义类比三维空间中勒让德链环rulings的计数问题，并证明其与原计数$N_\Delta$等价。通过具体例子，作者用构造性方法、热带曲线计数及对数Gromov-Witten理论中的局部化方法进行多重验证。rulings还产生了构造性层模空间的stratification，并推测该分解可恢复Block-Göttsche的细化热带不变量。

Result: 作者证明了该dual counting问题与原计数问题$N_\Delta$的等价性，并在多个具体例子中用构造性方法、热带曲线计数和对数Gromov-Witten局部化等独立方法进行数值验证，确证了各不同计数途径的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了凸格多边形曲线计数与勒让德链环rulings之间新的镜像对偶框架，多种独立计算方法的相互印证为该对偶提供了有力证据。作者提出的ruling分解与Block-Göttsche细化热带不变量之间关系的猜想，为未来通过Bousseau定理获取更高亏格对数Gromov-Witten不变量指明了方向。

Abstract: Given a convex lattice polygon $Δ\subset \mathbb R^2$, let $N_Δ$ be the count of rational, nodal curves in the linear system of the ample line bundle $L_Δ$ on the toric variety $\mathbb P_Δ$ defined by $Δ$, having fixed intersection with the toric boundary. We show that the relative Jacobian of the linear system defines an integrable system that has a mirror dual in the language of constructible sheaves on a two-torus microsupported on a Legendrian link. In this setting, we define the dual counting problem using an analogue of rulings of Legendrian links in three-space, then prove equivalence with $N_Δ$. We perform calculations of $N_Δ$ in several examples using constructible methods, tropical curve counting, and localization in logarithmic Gromov-Witten theory to demonstrate the equality of the different approaches.
  Moreover, the rulings give rise to a stratification of the moduli of constructible sheaves. We conjecture that this ruling decomposition recovers the refined tropical invariants of Block-Göttsche, and hence encodes higher-genus logarithmic Gromov--Witten invariants, by a theorem of Bousseau.

</details>


### [296] [Legendrian Reidemeister moves for the convex surface projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07123)
*Zijian Rong*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文证明了加厚凸曲面中Legendre纽结的Legendre Reidemeister定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该定理为Legendre纽结在加厚凸曲面中的等价性提供了完整的移动刻画，是理解低维切触拓扑中纽结分类的基础工具。

Method: 通过系统性地分析加厚凸曲面中Legendre纽结在投影图下的所有可能的初等变形，建立与标准Reidemeister移动对应的Legendre版本移动集合并证明其完备性。

Result: 成功证明了Legendre Reidemeister定理在加厚凸曲面这一更广泛背景下的成立，扩展了经典三维空间中该定理的适用范围。

Conclusion: 该定理为后续研究加厚凸曲面中的Legendre纽结分类及不变量的建立提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove a Legendrian Reidemeister theorem for Legendrian knots in thickened convex surfaces.

</details>


### [297] [Classification of Legendrian doubles and suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08557)
*Yasemin Yildirim*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一类称为"Legendrian doubles"的Legendrian子流形，其位于辛流形切触边界中，通过对开书分解页面中的精确Lagrangian填充进行加倍操作获得。这些Legendrian double被证明具有正则柔性精确Lagrangian填充，因此可仅由经典数据在Legendrian各向同性意义下分类。此外，作者证明此前提出的Legendrian suspension构造（基于Seidel的Lefschetz纤维化悬垂构造）实际上可以视为一种Legendrian double。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在推广Courte和Ekholm之前的构造到任意开书分解，建立Legendrian double的更一般框架，并揭示不同构造（Legendrian suspension与Legendrian double）之间的内在联系。同时通过证明其具有柔性填充，获得这类Legendrian的完整分类。

Method: 作者通过在任意开书分解的页面中，对精确Lagrangian填充进行"加倍"（doubling）操作，构造出位于相应切触流形中的Legendrian子流形。利用柔性/刚性辛几何技术，证明这些Legendrian admits regular flexible exact Lagrangian fillings，进而借助柔性分类定理完成Legendrian各向同性分类。最后通过对比Legendrian suspension的几何定义，证明其本质上是Lagrangian doubling的产物。

Result: 建立了Legendrian double的一般构造框架并推广了Courte-Ekholm结果；证明了所得Legendrian具备正则柔性精确Lagrangian填充，从而由经典数据完全分类；证明了Legendrian suspension构造恰好是Legendrian double的一种特例。

Conclusion: Legendrian double为理解开书分解页面中Lagrangian几何与切触流形中Legendrian几何之间的联系提供了一个统一框架，同时将此前独立的构造联系起来，并为这类几何对象给出完整的Legendrian分类。

Abstract: We define a construction of Legendrians inside contact manifolds that arise by doubling an exact Lagrangian filling in the page of an open book decomposition. This can be seen as a generalization of a previous construction by Courte and Ekholm to arbitrary open books. These Legendrians, called Legendrian doubles, are shown to admit regular flexible exact Lagrangian fillings, and they are thus classified up to Legendrian isotopy by classical data. Finally, we show that the Legendrian suspension construction, as defined by Arikan and the author in previous work,-this is a Legendrian contained inside a page of an open book that is obtained by using Seidel's suspension of Lefschetz fibrations- is a Legendrian double.

</details>


### [298] [Shifted Contact Structures on Exact Symplectic Fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08617)
*Mehmet Fırat Arıkan,Kadri İlker Berktav,Efe İzbudak*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文提出了派生接触Thurston定理：在派生代数几何框架下，若态射π: X→S具有移位精确辛纤维化结构且目标栈S具有移位接触结构，则在适当条件下可在源栈X上构造与π兼容的移位接触结构，将经典接触几何中的接触Thurston定理推广到派生范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典接触几何中，第一作者引入了接触辛结构的概念（具有接触底的精确辛纤维化），并在温和条件下证明了纤维化全空间继承接触结构，即接触Thurston定理。本文动机旨在将这一结果推广到派生几何（derived algebraic geometry）框架下，结合此前关于派生辛Thurston定理的工作，建立派生版本的接触Thurston定理。

Method: 本文方法依赖于相对移位结构（relative shifted structures）理论。具体而言，作者证明了在适当条件下，若派生栈间的态射π: X→S具有移位精确辛纤维化结构（shifted exact symplectic fibration structure），且目标栈S具有移位接触结构（shifted contact structure），则可以构造源栈X上的移位接触结构，且该结构与π相容。这一结果本质上建立了从相对构造到绝对构造（relative-to-absolute construction）的传程序。

Result: 作者成功证明了派生接触Thurston定理，即从目标的移位接触结构和态射的移位精确辛纤维化结构出发，可在源上构造移位接触结构。作为应用，作者给出了派生接触框架下相对到绝对构造形式主义的具体例子，涵盖法向栈（conormal stacks）、商映射栈（quotient mapping stacks）以及仿射精确辛纤维化（affine exact symplectic fibrations）等类型。

Conclusion: 本文建立了经典的接触Thurston定理在派生几何中的对应物——派生接触Thurston定理，该方法本质上是一种相对到绝对类型的构造，并通过若干具体例子（法向栈、商映射栈、仿射精确辛纤维化）展示了该形式主义的适用性与有效性。

Abstract: Within the framework of classical contact geometry, the first author introduced the notion of contact symplectic structure --the data of an exact symplectic fibration with a contact base-- and proved, under mild conditions, that the total space of such a fibration inherits a contact structure compatible with the fibration map, thereby establishing the contact Thurston theorem. This paper provides a derived contact version of that result by incorporating our prior work on the derived symplectic Thurston theorem. In this paper, we prove, under certain conditions, that if a morphism $π: X \rightarrow S$ of derived stacks has a shifted exact symplectic fibration structure and the target stack $S$ admits a shifted contact structure, then one can construct a shifted contact structure on the source stack $X$, compatible with $π$ in a sense similar to the smooth case. Our framework relies on the theory of relative shifted structures; hence our result, called the derived contact Thurston theorem, in fact establishes a relative-to-absolute type construction. As an application, we present examples of our relative-to-absolute construction formalism in the derived contact setting, including conormal stacks, quotient mapping stacks, and affine exact symplectic fibrations.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [299] [Iterative construction of the R-matrices in arbitrary dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06274)
*Petr Pivovarov,Pavel Pyatov*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种允许迭代求解常数Yang-Baxter方程的特殊ansatz，构造了四组作用于维度线性增长向量空间张量积的常数R矩阵序列（每组的R矩阵依赖单个复参数）。通过谱分析，第一和第三系列分别与量子群U_q(sl(2))和U_q(sl(2|1))的对称张量表示相关联，第二和第四系列与q为单位根情形下的表示有关。应用Reshetikhin-Turaev过程，前三序列分别产生彩色Jones多项式、ADO不变量和Alexander多项式，但第三序列对应的链环不变量退化为平凡结果（恒等于1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在寻找一种特殊的ansatz形式，使得常数Yang-Baxter方程可以迭代求解，从而构造新的R矩阵序列，并探讨这些R矩阵与量子群表示理论及链环不变量之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者提出一种特殊的ansatz，允许对常数Yang-Baxter方程进行迭代求解；通过该ansatz构造四系列常数R矩阵；利用谱分析确定各系列对应的量子群表示类型（包括U_q(sl(2))、U_q(sl(2|1))对称张量表示及q为单位根时的幂零表示）；并采用Reshetikhin-Turaev构造将R矩阵转化为链环不变量（彩色Jones多项式、ADO不变量、Alexander多项式）以验证其对应关系。

Result: 构造了四组R矩阵序列。谱分析表明第一、三系列分别对应U_q(sl(2))和U_q(sl(2|1))的对称张量表示；第二系列对应U_q(sl(2))在q为单位根时的幂零表示。Reshetikhin-Turaev程序分别从第一、二、四系列导出彩色Jones多项式、ADO不变量和Alexander多项式；但第三系列得到了出乎意料的平凡结果（恒等于1）。

Conclusion: 该ansatz能系统生成与不同量子群表示类型相关的R矩阵序列，并与多种经典链环不变量建立对应关系。第三系列虽然对应U_q(sl(2|1))的对称张量表示，但在Reshetikhin-Turaev构造下产生平凡链环不变量，表明超量子群对称张量表示在此框架下不能产生非平凡的拓扑不变量，这一发现值得进一步研究。

Abstract: We investigate a special ansats that allows for an iterative solution of the constant Yang-Baxter equation. Testing this ansatz, we construct four sequences of the constant R-matrices. In each sequence the R-matrices act on the tensor squares of vector spaces of linearly growing dimensions. Each R-matrix also depends on a single complex parameter.
  By analyzing the spectra of the R-matrices, we conclude that the first and the third series are associated with the symmetric tensor representations of the quantum groups U_q(sl(2)) and U_q(sl(2|1)), respectively. The other two series appear to be related to representations of quantum groups in the case where q is a root of unity. The elements of the second series are associated with nilpotent representations of U_q(sl(2)).
  We also check the correspondence between these R-matrices and link invariants. Applying the Reshetikhin-Turaev procedure to the first, second, and fourth sequences yields, respectively, colored Jones polynomials, ADO invariants, and Alexander polynomials. To our surprise, a similar calculation for the third series yields a trivial result, identically equal to 1.

</details>


### [300] [Thickening realization and positivity properties of canonical bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06676)
*Jiepeng Fang,Xuhua He*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文引入"加厚实现"（thickening realization）方法，将量子群的修正形式U的点上的典范基B的结构常数与一个更大的量子群的负部分典范基的结构常数联系起来，从而证明了Lusztig关于余乘法、双线性形式以及部分乘法（含球抛物因子）的猜想，并在简单连通有限型情况下完全证明了Lusztig猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lusztig曾猜想：对于对称Cartan数据关联的量子群U及其修正形式U的点，典范基B在乘法、余乘法和双线性形式下的结构常数都属于N[v,v^{-1}]（即具有正性）。这一猜想此前并未在任意对称Cartan数据下得到完全证明，需要新的技术工具来建立正性结果。

Method: 作者引入了"加厚实现"（thickening realization），将修正形式量子群U的典范基B与一个更大的量子群U~的负部分典范基建立联系。通过此实现，U中的相关结构常数被等同于U~^-中的结构常数，从而可以利用已知或可证的U~^-中的正性结果来推导U中的结构常数正性。

Result: 对于任意对称Cartan数据，证明了：(1) B关于余乘法的正性；(2) B关于双线性形式的正性；(3) 当乘法中一个因子为球抛物型（spherical parabolic）时乘法的正性。特别地，在简单连通有限型情形下完全证明了Lusztig猜想。此外，还证明了一类广泛的张量积（可积模的张量积）的典范基具有转移矩阵和B作用的正性。

Conclusion: 加厚实现方法为典范基的结构常数正性提供了统一的证明框架，在任意对称Cartan数据下解决了大部分Lusztig猜想，并在有限型情形下完全解决。该方法还可推广到张量积范畴中典范基的正性问题，显示了广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Let $\mathbf{U}$ be a quantum group associated with a symmetric Cartan datum, let $\dot{\mathbf{U}}$ be its modified form, and let $\dot{\mathbf{B}}$ be the canonical basis of $\dot{\mathbf{U}}$. Lusztig conjectured that the structure constants of the multiplication, comultiplication, and bilinear form in $\dot{\mathbf{U}}$ with respect to $\dot{\mathbf{B}}$ belong to $\mathbb{N}[v,v^{-1}]$.
  We introduce the \emph{thickening realization}, which relates $\dot{\mathbf{B}}$ to the canonical basis of the negative part of a larger quantum group $\tilde{\mathbf{U}}^-$. More precisely, it identifies the relevant structure constants in $\dot{\mathbf{U}}$ with the structure constants in $\tilde{\mathbf{U}}^-$. As consequences, we prove, for arbitrary symmetric Cartan datum, $\dot{\mathbf{B}}$ has the positivity properties for the comultiplication and bilinear form, as well as the positivity for the multiplication whenever one factor is spherical parabolic. In particular, Lusztig's conjecture holds for simply-laced finite type. We also prove the canonical bases of a broad class of tensor products of integrable modules have the positivity properties for the transition matrices and actions by $\dot{\mathbf{B}}$.

</details>


### [301] [Quantum Michelson Contrast: Logarithmic Parametrisation, Subadditivity, and Geometric Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06997)
*Irina Nikolaeva,Andrej Novikov*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究基于算子Michelson对比度的对数参数化——量子对数对比度（QLC），证明其在乘法下具有次可加性，建立正算子QLC与投影Thompson距离的精确几何等同，并揭示了次可加性等号成立的维度阈值：2×2与3×3正矩阵中等号迫使算子交换，而n=4是最小存在非交换等号例子的维度。此外还推导了收缩性质、和不等式、密度算子迹估计及Cesàro均值和无穷乘积的极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期工作中为正常算子引入了Michelson对比度算子，本文旨在系统地研究其对数千参数化的自然结构，认识到对数提升arctanh(Δ(A)) = ½ ln κ(A)具有更深刻的几何与代数意义，并期望建立其与Thompson几何的联系，探索次可加性何时取等号的维度条件。

Method: 通过极分解将量子对数对比度推广到所有可逆算子；利用投影Thompson距离对正可逆算子建立QLC的几何等同性；通过构造和分析不同维度（n=2,3,4）下的矩阵例子来证明次可加性等号的精确维度阈值，并辅以收缩性质、和不等式、迹估计及极限行为的研究。

Result: 证明了QLC的乘法次可加性；建立了正可逆算子QLC与投影Thompson距离的精确等同；证明对2×2和3×3正矩阵次可加等号迫使算子交换，而n=4为最小存在非交换等号例子的维度；推导了收缩性质（如与幺正变换相关的Lipschitz估计）、正算子的和不等式、密度算子的迹估计、以及Cesàro均值和无穷积分的极限公式。

Conclusion: 量子对数对比度QLC是从条件数κ(A)导出的自然算子不变量，具有次可加性，与Thompson几何有精确联系。其等号行为的维度阈值n=4揭示了算子代数的内在复杂度跃变，这一定性结论可能对量子信息中的可分辨性、纠缠度量或不平等传播有潜在意义，值得进一步探索高维情况下的复杂结构。

Abstract: Building on the operator Michelson contrast introduced in our previous work for positive operators, we investigate its natural logarithmic parametrisation. For a positive invertible operator $A$, the operator Michelson contrast is $Δ(A)=(κ(A)-1)/(κ(A)+1)$, where $κ(A)=\|A\|\|A^{-1}\|$ is the condition number. Its logarithmic lift is $\operatorname{arctanh}(Δ(A))=\frac12\lnκ(A)$. We call this quantity the \emph{Quantum Logarithmic Contrast} (QLC), and extend it to all invertible operators via the polar decomposition. We prove that QLC is subadditive under multiplication: $\operatorname{QLC}(AB)\le \operatorname{QLC}(A)+\operatorname{QLC}(B)$. For positive invertible operators, we establish an exact geometric identification: $\operatorname{QLC}(A)$ equals the projective Thompson distance from the identity ray to the ray generated by $A$. The main result is a sharp dimensional threshold for equality in the subadditivity law: for $2\times2$ and $3\times3$ positive matrices, equality forces commutativity, while $n=4$ is the smallest dimension in which noncommuting equality examples exist. We also derive contraction properties, sum inequalities, trace estimates for density operators, and limiting behaviour for Cesàro means and infinite products.

</details>


### [302] [A dual theory of Zhu's theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07179)
*Hao Wang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文建立了Zhu的A(V)理论的对偶理论：针对阶化顶点算子余代数V，研究了余结合余代数C(V)，证明了允许V-余模与C(V)-余模之间存在一一对应关系，并证明了V是余有理的（即所有允许V-余模完全可约）当且仅当其对偶顶点算子代数V'是有理的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu的A(V)理论是顶点算子代数研究中的核心工具，它将顶点算子代数的表示论与其模空间联系起来。本文旨在建立该理论的对偶版本，即对顶点算子余代数建立相应的结构理论，并探讨余有理性的判定条件。

Method: 针对阶化顶点算子余代数V，构造与其对偶的余结合余代数C(V)，建立允许V-余模与C(V)-余模之间的双射对应关系；在此基础上，通过对偶性质证明V的余有理性与对偶顶点算子代数V'的有理性等价。

Result: 证明了允许V-余模范畴与C(V)-余模范畴等价，即上述对应关系在两个方向上都成立；并给出了余有理性的刻画：V是余有理的当且仅当其线性对偶V'（作为顶点算子代数）是有理的。

Conclusion: 该工作将Zhu的A(V)理论系统地对偶化到顶点算子余代数情形，建立了余代数版本的核心结构定理，并通过余有理与有理的等价性揭示了两类对象表示论之间深层的对偶关系。

Abstract: We establish a dual theory of Zhu's $A(\mathcal {V})$-theory, i.e., for a graded vertex operator coalgebra $V$, the coassociative coalgebra $C(V)$ are investigated, any admissible $V$-comodule gives a $C(V)$-comodule, and vice versa. We also prove that $V$ is corational, which means every admissible $V$-comodule is completely reducible, if and only if its dual vertex operator algebra $V'$ is rational.

</details>


### [303] [A dual theory of twisted Zhu's theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07203)
*Hao Wang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究了分次顶点算子余代数$V$及其有限阶自同构$g$下的允许$g$-扭转$V$-余模理论。证明了$\mathcal{M}$是允许$g$-扭转$V$-余模当且仅当其分次对偶$\mathcal{M}'$是对偶顶点算子代数$V'$的允许$g^{-1}$-扭转模。建立了扭转Zhu理论的對偶理论，构造了余结合余代数$C_g(V)$，并证明了$g$-余有理当且仅当对偶代数$V'$是$g$-有理的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有顶点算子代数的扭模理论（包括扭Zhu理论）被广泛研究，但其对偶方向——顶点算子余代数的扭余模理论相对缺乏。本文旨在发展顶点算子余代数的允许$g$-扭转余模理论，建立与顶点算子代数扭模理论的对偶对应关系。

Method: 本文采用对偶性的方法：（1）利用分次对偶将允许$g$-扭转$V$-余模与允许$g^{-1}$-扭转$V'$-模建立一一对应；（2）构造余结合余代数$C_g(V)$，推广了扭Zhu理论到余代数情形；（3）通过$C_g(V)$-余模刻画允许$g$-扭转$V$-余模的结构。

Result: 证明了允许$g$-扭转$V$-余模的判定准则（等价于对偶模的条件）；构造了余结合余代数$C_g(V)$，建立了$C_g(V)$-余模与允许$g$-扭转$V$-余模之间的等价关系；证明了$V$的$g$-余有理性与$V'$的$g$-理性之间的等价性。

Conclusion: 本文完整建立了顶点算子余代数的扭转余模理论，包括扭转Zhu理论的對偶版本（$C_g(V)$理论），并揭示了顶点算子余代数的$g$-余有理性与顶点算子代数的$g$-理性之间的对偶关系。该工作填补了顶点算子余代数在扭模理论方面的空白。

Abstract: The notion of admissible $g$-twisted $V$-comodules is investigated for a graded vertex operator coalgebra $V$ and a finite order automorphism $g\in \Aut V$. We prove that $\mathcal {M}$ is an admissible $g$-twisted $V$-comodule if and only if its graded dual $\mathcal {M}'$ is an admissible $g^{-1}$-twisted $V'$-module for vertex operator algebra $V'$. Then we establish a dual theory of twisted Zhu's theory, i.e., there is a coassociative coalgebra $C_g(V)$ for a graded vertex operator coalgebra $V$, any admissible $g$-twisted $V$-comodule gives a $C_g(V)$-comodule, and vice versa. We also prove that $V$ is $g$-corational, which means every admissible $g$-twisted $V$-comodule is completely reducible, if and only if its dual vertex operator algebra $V'$ is $g$-rational.

</details>


### [304] [Homological properties of quantum groups governed by small quantum groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08205)
*Yimin Huang,Tiancheng Qi,Quanshui Wu,Ruipeng Zhu*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个无特征依赖的一般性框架，用于研究一大类模有限Hopf代数的同调性质，将许多量子群（在单位根处）及其多参数形变的同调性质研究归结到对应的小量子群。作者证明了左同调积分空间的同构性、对称Frobenius扩张的判据、幺模性的纤维代数判据，并将这些结果应用于证明所有Andruskiewitsch-Angiono-Yakimov大量子群是仿射Noether幺模Artin-Schelter Gorenstein Hopf代数，同时给出了其Calabi-Yau性质的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子群在单位根处的大量子群及其多参数形变的同调性质难以直接研究，而已有理论对这类对象缺乏统一的、不依赖特征的一般性框架。本文旨在建立这样一个框架，将大量子群的同调性质约化到更易处理的有限维小量子群上。

Method: 文中引入关于具有大中心Hopf子代数C的仿射Hopf代数H的框架，利用Lu-Wu-Zhang意义下的左同调积分空间与单位纤维代数的左积分空间的双模同构，建立对称Frobenius扩张与幺模性等性质的判据。证明依赖于对每个极大理想处的纤维代数作为单位纤维代数上的模范畴的幺模性分析，并将此抽象框架应用到位根处量子群及Andruskiewitsch-Angiono-Yakimov大量子群。

Result: 证明了H的左同调积分空间与单位纤维代数的左积分空间双模同构；H是C的对称Frobenius扩张当且仅当单位纤维代数幺模且H的antipode平方为内自同构；在代数闭域上，H幺模当且仅当每个极大理想处的纤维代数作为单位纤维代数表示范畴上的模范畴是幺模的。据此证明了所有AAY大量子群是仿射Noether幺模Artin-Schelter Gorenstein Hopf代数，并给出了其Calabi-Yau的充要条件。

Conclusion: 该框架为一大类量子群同调性质的研究提供了统一的约化工具，将"大"量子群的同调问题归结为"小"量子群的相应问题，并具体导出AAY大量子群的良好同调性质及其Calabi-Yau判据。

Abstract: We develop a general characteristic-free framework for studying the homological properties of a broad class of module-finite Hopf algebras. This framework makes it possible to reduce the study of the homological properties of many quantum groups at roots of unity and their multiparameter deformations to the study of the corresponding small quantum groups. For any affine Hopf algebra $H$ admitting a large central Hopf subalgebra $C$, we prove that its left homological integral space, in the sense of Lu-Wu-Zhang, is isomorphic as a bimodule to the left integral space of the identity fiber algebra, which is a finite-dimensional Hopf algebra. Consequently, $H$ is a symmetric Frobenius extension of $C$ if and only if the corresponding identity fiber algebra is unimodular and the square of the antipode of $H$ is inner, thus providing an effective criterion for the Calabi-Yau property of $H$. For a broad class of quantum groups at roots of unity, an appropriate large central Hopf subalgebra can be chosen such that the identity fiber algebra is the corresponding small quantum group. Therefore, some homological properties of these big quantum groups are governed by those of their corresponding small quantum groups. Assuming that the base field is algebraically closed, we prove that $H$ is unimodular if and only if, for some (equivalently, every) maximal ideal $\mathfrak{m}$ of $C$, the category of finite-dimensional representations of the fiber algebra at $\mathfrak{m}$ is unimodular in the sense of Yadav as a module category over the finite tensor category of finite-dimensional representations of the identity fiber algebra. As an application, we prove that all Andruskiewitsch-Angiono-Yakimov large quantum groups are affine noetherian unimodular Artin-Schelter Gorenstein Hopf algebras. We also give a necessary and sufficient condition for these large quantum groups to be Calabi-Yau.

</details>


### [305] [Double cross products with projections and relative Rota-Baxter operators on Hopf algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08400)
*Yunnan Li*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究了带投影的双交叉积Hopf代数结构，证明其等价于Sciandra引入的（Yetter-Drinfeld）相对Rota-Baxter算子，并探讨了其与braiding算子、Doi-Hopf模之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双交叉积是Hopf代数构造的重要工具，广义量子双是其典型例子。理解双交叉积与新兴的代数结构（如相对Rota-Baxter算子）之间的联系，有助于统一和发展Hopf代数理论及其在辫子方程等领域的应用。

Method: 利用Radford's biproduct理论作为框架，将带投影的双交叉积Hopf代数进行简化分析，建立其与（Yetter-Drinfeld）相对Rota-Baxter算子的等价性；通过余伴随余作用将相应的辫子Hopf代数置于Yetter-Drinfeld模范畴中；进一步考察该结构在单一Hopf代数上诱导匹配对作用的条件，并证明其与产生辫子方程解的braiding算子等价；最后将相对Rota-Baxter算子视为Doi-Hopf模之间的态射。

Result: 建立了带投影的双交叉积Hopf代数与(Yetter-Drinfeld)相对Rota-Baxter算子之间的等价关系；证明了在特定条件下该结构可诱导单一Hopf代数上的匹配对作用，等价于辫子方程解对应的braiding算子；揭示了相对Rota-Baxter算子作为Doi-Hopf模态射的结构性质。

Conclusion: 本文通过Radford's biproduct理论框架，将带投影的双交叉积与相对Rota-Baxter算子、braiding算子及Doi-Hopf模等概念建立了深层联系，为Hopf代数理论及相关量子代数结构的统一理解提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Given a matched pair of Hopf algebras, the double cross product construction yields a new Hopf algebra, the prototypical example of which is the generalized quantum double arising from a (Hopf) skew pairing. In this paper, we specifically investigate double cross products with projections in the framework of Radford's biproduct theory, and establish that such an algebraic structure is equivalent to a simplified version of (Yetter-Drinfeld) relative Rota-Baxter operators on Hopf algebras recently introduced by Sciandra, with the corresponding braided Hopf algebras lying in the Yetter-Drinfeld module category under coadjoint coactions. We further examine conditions under which this algebraic structure induces a matched pair of actions on a single Hopf algebra, which is in turn equivalent to the notion of braiding operators on Hopf algebras that yield solutions of the braid equation. In addition, we show that such relative Rota-Baxter operators are morphisms between certain Doi-Hopf modules.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [306] [Energy dissipation in linear viscoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05465)
*Juan Luis Gonzalez-Santander,Francesco Mainardi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在线性粘弹性框架下系统比较了Becker、Lomnitz和Lambert模型的比耗散Q^{-1}(ω)，推导了Becker与Lomnitz模型在ω→0⁺和ω→+∞时的渐近解析公式，并分别给出了广义Becker模型（ν为有理数时）和扩展Jeffreys–Lomnitz模型（两种α取值范围）的比耗散闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在粘弹性研究中，不同本构模型（如Becker、Lomnitz、Lambert等）的比耗散行为对材料衰减特性建模至关重要，但现有结果多依赖数值或特殊形式，缺乏统一的渐近分析和闭式解析表达式，阻碍了模型间的严格对比与应用选择。

Method: 通过线性粘弹性理论对各模型的比耗散Q^{-1}(ω)进行比较研究；采用图形化分析ω→0⁺与ω→+∞时各模型的渐近一致行为；运用解析推导获得Becker和Lomnitz模型的渐近公式；利用参数ν的有理性假设，推导广义Becker模型比耗散的闭式表达式；针对扩展Jeffreys–Lomnitz模型，分别在α∈(0,1]与α∈(-∞,1]两种区间上推导出两个新的闭式表达式。

Result: 获得了三个主要成果：(1) Becker与Lomnitz模型在ω→0⁺和ω→+∞时的渐近解析公式，且高频极限与Maxwell模型的比耗散一致；(2) 广义Becker模型在ν∈(0,1]为有理数时的闭式表达式Q_ν^{-1}(ω)，ν→0退化为Maxwell模型，ν=1恢复原始Becker模型；(3) 扩展Jeffreys–Lomnitz模型的两个新闭式表达式Q_α^{-1}(ω)，分别适用于α∈(0,1]和α∈(-∞,1]，α→0时恢复Lomnitz模型耗散，α=1时恢复Maxwell模型耗散，且高频渐近行为与Maxwell模型一致。

Conclusion: 模型间的比耗散在低频和高频极限下均表现出与Maxwell模型一致的行为，表明这些模型在高频渐近意义上具有等价性；所导出的闭式公式为广义Becker模型和扩展Jeffreys–Lomnitz模型在不同参数范围内提供了精确、可解析处理的比耗散表示，便于实际工程与地球物理应用中的模型选择与参数识别。

Abstract: We compare the specific dissipation $Q^{-1}\left( ω\right) $ for the Becker, Lomnitz, and Lambert models in linear viscoelasticity. Graphically, the specific dissipation of these models behaves similarly as $ω\rightarrow 0^{+}$ and $ω\rightarrow +\infty$. Furthermore, we obtain analytic formulas for the asymptotic behavior of $Q^{-1}\left( ω\right) $ in the Becker and Lomnitz models as $ω\rightarrow 0^{+}$ and $ω\rightarrow +\infty$, where the latter limit coincides with the specific dissipation of the Maxwell model. We also derive a novel closed-form expression for the specific dissipation in the generalized Becker model, $Q_{ν}^{-1}\left( ω\right) $, when the parameter $ν\in \left( 0,1\right] $ is rational. When $ν\rightarrow 0$, we recover the specific dissipation of the Maxwell model, whereas when $ν=1$, we recover that of the original Becker model. In addition, we derive two new closed-form expressions for the specific dissipation for the extended Jeffreys--Lomnitz model $Q_{α}^{-1}\left( ω\right) $, the first being valid for $α\in \left( 0,1\right] $, and the second for $α\in \left( -\infty ,1\right] $. When $α\rightarrow 0$, we recover the specific dissipation of the Lomnitz model, whereas when $α=1$, we recover that of the Maxwell model. Finally, we conclude that the asymptotic behavior of $Q_{α}^{-1}\left( ω\right) $ as $ω\rightarrow +\infty $ coincides with the specific dissipation of the Maxwell model. Keywords:

</details>


### [307] [Big Crunch on Julia and Anti-Julia Sets in the Integrable Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07308)
*Masatomi Iizawa,Satoru Saito,Yasuhito Narita,Yasuyuki Maeda,Hiromitsu Harada,Akira Shudo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"反Julia集"和"前Julia集/前反Julia集"的概念，通过引入实变形参数a的复映射，揭示可积与不可积系统之间的Julia集结构转化机制，特别描述了a→0极限下Julia集与反Julia集发生"大挤压"式碰撞湮灭的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Julia集定义为混沌系统中排斥周期点的闭包，但为何可积系统中不出现该结构？这一问题尚未被系统解答。本文旨在通过建立"反Julia集"等新概念来理解可积系统中Julia集缺席的根本原因及其过渡机制。

Method: 作者定义反Julia集为周期方程发散点的闭包，以及前Julia集和前反Julia集（取闭包前的集合）。通过引入实变形参数a构造一个在可积与不可积动力学之间插值的复映射，从解析角度（如渐近分析）和数值可视化两个层面研究集合的结构与演化，特别关注前反Julia集在可积极限下的渐近行为。
