Result: 当0<a≤1/2时，Julia集与反Julia集在复平面上重合，但前Julia集与前反Julia集完全不相交；在可积极限a→0处，这两个对偶结构发生临界碰撞并湮灭（类似宇宙学中的大挤压）；当1/2<a<1时，前反Julia集的边界包含Julia集。作者对这些现象进行了解析刻画，并提供数值可视化支持。

Conclusion: 通过对Julia集与反Julia集之间对偶关系的研究，本文揭示了可积极限下Julia结构消失的机制——即"大挤压"式碰撞湮灭过程，为理解混沌与可积系统之间的过渡提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The Julia set is defined by the closure of repelling periodic points in chaotic systems. Why does this structure not appear in integrable systems? In this paper, we address this question by demonstrating the existence of the closure of divergences of the periodic equations, which we designate as the "anti-Julia set." We also call the sets before taking the closures the pre-Julia and pre-anti-Julia sets, respectively. We illustrate the transition mechanism by considering a complex map that interpolates between integrable and non-integrable dynamics, by introducing a real deformation parameter $a$. For $0<a\le 1/2$, we show that the Julia set and the anti-Julia set coincide in the complex plane, although the pre-Julia and pre-anti-Julia sets remain completely disjoint. At the integrable limit $a\to 0$, these two dual structures undergo a critical collision and subsequent annihilation, reminiscent of a cosmological Big Crunch. When $1/2<a<1$, on the other hand, the boundary of the pre-anti-Julia set includes the Julia set. We analytically characterize these phenomena, focusing in particular on the asymptotic behavior of the pre-anti-Julia set as it approaches the integrable limit, and provide numerical visualizations that elucidate the underlying mechanisms of this Big Crunch phenomenon.

</details>


### [308] [Information Loss under Gauge Reduction of Discrete Electromagnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05473)
*Jean-Pierre Magnot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Whitney离散化电磁学中规范约化下的相对熵。在辅助势空间中，高斯分布的相对熵可精确分解为物理边缘分布的相对熵与非负的规范条件贡献之和，且物理散度等于所有高斯辅助扩展的最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散电磁学中，规范自由度（gauge degrees of freedom）不代表物理状态，但在数值计算和统计推断中却可能被包含在辅助势空间中。需要理解规范约化后信息论量（如相对熵）如何变化，以区分物理信息与规范伪信息，从而避免将规范变量赋予错误的物理意义。

Method: 利用Whitney离散化下的离散Hodge分解将辅助势空间分离为精确（exact，规范）、调和（harmonic）与余精确（coexact，物理）三个子空间。对辅助势空间上的高斯分布，通过Schur补、条件均值及物理-规范相关性推导相对熵的精确分解公式，并分析Fisher信息在无穷小层面的类似分解。通过一个两变量模型进行说明。

Result: 相对熵可精确分解为物理边缘相对熵与一个非负的条件规范贡献之和；物理相对熵是辅助相对熵在所有高斯扩展上的最小值；该条件规范贡献可通过Schur补和相关性显式表达；Fisher信息具有类似的无穷小分解；两变量模型展示了统计上不同的辅助势可对应相同的物理统计。

Conclusion: \n提供了离散规范约化的信息几何解释，确立了辅助空间与物理空间信息量之间的精确关系，明确了规范自由度对相对熵的非负贡献，为离散电磁学中的统计推断与信息度量提供了数学基础，且不给被消除的规范变量赋予物理意义。

Abstract: We study relative entropy under gauge reduction in Whitney-discretized electromagnetism. The discrete Hodge decomposition separates exact gauge directions from harmonic and coexact physical degrees of freedom. For Gaussian distributions on the auxiliary potential space, relative entropy splits exactly into the relative entropy of the physical marginals and a nonnegative conditional gauge contribution. We express this contribution through Schur complements, conditional means, and physical--gauge correlations, and characterize equality between auxiliary and physical relative entropies. The physical divergence is also shown to be the minimum over all Gaussian auxiliary extensions. At the infinitesimal level, Fisher information admits an analogous decomposition. A two-variable model illustrates how statistically distinct auxiliary potentials may represent identical physical statistics. The construction provides an information-geometric interpretation of discrete gauge reduction without assigning physical significance to the eliminated gauge variables.

</details>


### [309] [Distance Energies and Negative Type of Flat Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05622)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究平坦环面T上的距离能量泛函I_α(μ)的全局最大化问题。作者证明了切割轨迹的定量Fourier特征：对于每个Voronoi面及α>0，存在沿面法线接近的对偶格频率，使得ρ_L^α的Fourier系数为正，并给出由该面决定的显式主导渐近。由此推出Haar测度不是任何正距离幂的局部极大值点，且二维以上的平坦环面具有上确界负型和广义圆度为零。随后作者解决了正交矩形环面和正六边形环面两类平坦环面的全局最大化问题，揭示了α=2处的相变现象及不同torus上最优测度的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究距离能量泛函在平坦环面上的最大化问题，理解最优测度结构如何依赖于参数α与环面几何。该问题与负型（negative type）理论、广义圆度（generalized roundness）、势理论及最优传输等领域密切相关，此前对于环面上这类极值问题的定量刻画尚不完整。

Method: 采用Fourier分析方法：将距离能量泛函通过其Fourier系数在频域中表达。对于每个Voronoi facet，构造沿其对偶法线方向逼近的frequency序列，并推导Fourier系数的正性与显式主导渐近项。利用该Fourier signature证明Haar测度非局部极大点及负型/圆度结果。对两类特殊环面（正交矩形环面与正六边形环面），通过对偶子群结构与coupling分析，显式刻画全局极大测度的形式。

Result: (1) 证明了对任意Voronoi facet和α>0存在对偶格频率序列使Fourier系数为正，且给出由facet决定的主导渐近。(2) Haar测度对任意α>0都不是光滑密度中的局部极大值点。(3) 维数≥2的平坦环面具有supremal negative type以及generalized roundness为零。(4) 正交矩形环面上，1≤α<2时极大值为两阶挠子群上平移的均匀测度；α=2时该子群平移上的所有平衡耦合均极值；α>2时仅为等权直径偶。(5) 正六边形环面上，α≥1时极大值恰为三阶特殊循环子群平移上的均匀测度。

Conclusion: 本文建立了环面距离能量Fourier系数与Voronoi结构之间的精确联系，给出了距离能量最大化问题的全局解。结果揭示了能量最大化测度在α=2处发生从"扩散到子群上coupling"到"集中于直径偶"的相变，并展示了不同格结构对最优测度形式的影响。这些结论深化了环面上负型与广义圆度理论的理解。

Abstract: Let \(T_Λ=\mathbb R^d/Λ\), \(d\geq2\), be a flat torus with quotient metric \(ρ_Λ\), and consider the distance energies \(I_α(μ)=\iint ρ_Λ(x,y)^α\,dμ(x)\,dμ(y)\) of Borel probability measures \(μ\). We prove a quantitative Fourier signature of the cut locus: for every Voronoi facet and every \(α>0\), there is a sequence of dual-lattice frequencies approaching the facet normal along which the Fourier coefficients of \(ρ_Λ^α\) are positive, with an explicit leading asymptotic determined by the facet. As direct consequences, Haar measure is not a local maximizer for any positive distance power, even among smooth densities, and every flat torus of dimension at least two has supremal negative type and generalized roundness zero. We then solve the global maximization problem for two classes of flat tori. On an orthogonal rectangular torus, the maximizers undergo a transition at \(α=2\): for \(1\leqα<2\) they are the translated uniform measures on the two-torsion subgroup; at \(α=2\) all balanced couplings on translates of that subgroup are extremal; and for \(α>2\) only equally weighted diametral pairs remain. On the regular hexagonal torus, the maximizers are precisely the uniform measures on translates of a distinguished cyclic subgroup of order three for every \(α\geq1\).

</details>


### [310] [An Algebraic Proof of Gauge-Fixing Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05872)
*Jiuhe Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限维非阿贝尔规范场论模型中，利用BRST形式主义和微扰理论，通过代数方法证明了微扰渐近展开的规范固定无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在规范场论中，物理可观测量不应依赖于人为选择的规范固定条件，但这一性质在非阿贝尔理论中缺乏严格的代数证明，尤其是在微扰展开的层面上。

Method: 采用BRST形式主义建立代数框架，结合微扰理论，证明在合适的横截性条件下，可观测量的归一化期望值在满足假设的规范固定条件的光滑形变下保持不变。

Result: 建立了主要定理：在适当横截性条件下，可观测量的归一化期望值不受保持这些假设的规范固定条件光滑形变的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为非阿贝尔规范理论中微扰渐近展开的规范固定无关性提供了严格的代数证明，填补了该性质在严格证明层面的空白。

Abstract: This paper provides an algebraic proof of the gauge-fixing independence of perturbative asymptotic expansions in a finite-dimensional model of non-Abelian gauge theory. We develop an algebraic framework using the BRST formalism and perturbation theory. Our main theorem shows that under appropriate transversality conditions, normalized expectation values of observables remain unchanged under smooth deformations of the gauge-fixing conditions that preserve these assumptions.

</details>


### [311] [Renormalization Group on Wiener Space: Spectral Theory and Universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06183)
*André L. P. Considera,Alexei A. Mailybaev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Wiener空间上建立了严格的RG形式化框架，定义了作用于连续路径上概率测度的RG算子R，其不动点为Wiener测度。通过Hida微积分（白噪声分析）实现了R在Brown不动点附近的广义谱理论，发现了特征向量族{U_n}（特征值为λ_n=2^{1-n/2}），并由此揭示了Donsker不变原理中更深层次的第二层普适性——不仅收敛到Brownian极限是普适的，其全部前导修正层级也是普适的。该框架还可形式化推广至Gibbs型微扰，恢复标准的相关/边缘/无关分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Wilson重整化群框架依赖于精确/严格的函数空间谱理论，但Brown运动路径测度的RG不动点附近的线性化算子其特征向量过于奇异，无法作为路径空间上的通常测度来实化。这阻碍了将物理RG方法严格化地应用于随机过程和概率极限定理。作者旨在建立严格的RG形式化，克服特征向量的奇异性困难，从而揭示Donsker定理背后的深层结构。

Method: (1) 在Wiener路径空间上定义RG算子R，将粗粒化/重标度操作形式化为作用于概率测度的映射。(2) 在不动点(Wiener测度)处线性化R，得到算子L。(3) 由于L的特征向量作为普通测度过奇异，引入Hida分布（白噪声分析广义泛函）框架进行广义谱理论分析。(4) 特征向量在弱意义下满足LU=λU，实现为Hida分布。(5) 通过Wick多项式构造显式特征向量族U_n，并建立谱隙与对角集中性质。(6) 对Donsker不变原理的修正项进行层级分析，并形式化讨论Gibbs微扰情形。

Result: 获得了L的广义谱结构：谱隙以及特征向量族的对角集中性质；显式识别出特征对(λ_n=2^{1-n/2}, U_n)，其中U_n是白噪声Wick幂的积分，严格构造为Hida分布。在此基础上证明了Donsker不变原理具有第二层普适性：随机游走向Brownian极限的逐步修正（展开的各级项）由特征对(λ_n, U_n)控制，因而同样是普适的，不依赖于具体的随机游走细节。在Gibbs型微扰情形中，形式化推导恢复了基于幂次计数的相关/边缘/无关分类。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Wiener空间上严格的RG谱理论，借助Hida分布解决了特征向量奇异性的根本困难，将物理重整化群的观念（不动点、相关算子谱分析）严格地落实到了概率路径空间。所揭示的Donsker不变原理的第二层普适性（修正层级的普适性）是一个重要发现，表明随机游走的Brownian极限不仅是零阶普适的，其全部非平凡修正也是普适的。该框架同时为连接概率论与量子场论RG方法提供了新途径。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous renormalization group (RG) formalism on Wiener space. We introduce an RG operator $\mathcal R$ acting on probability measures over continuous paths, whose fixed point is the Wiener measure. We linearize $\mathcal{R}$ around the Brownian fixed point and analyze the spectral structure of the resulting operator $\mathscr{L}$. Its eigenvectors are too singular to be realized as honest measures on path space, and we therefore develop a generalized spectral theory within the framework of white noise analysis (Hida calculus). The eigenvectors of $\mathscr{L}$ are realized as Hida distributions, satisfying $\mathscr{L} U=λU$ in the weak sense. This analysis yields structural results such as a spectral gap and a diagonal-concentration property of the eigenvectors. Moreover, we identify a family $\{\mathfrak{U}_n\}_{n \geq 0}$ of eigenvectors with eigenvalues $λ_n = 2^{1-n/2}$, consisting of Wick polynomials of white noise formally expressed as $\mathfrak{U}_n = \int_0^1 {:}\dot W(t)^n{:}\, dt$, and rigorously constructed as Hida distributions. We then use this spectral structure to uncover a finer, second layer of universality in the Donsker invariance principle: not only is the convergence of random walks towards the Brownian scaling limit universal, but the entire hierarchy of leading corrections to the Brownian limit is universal as well, governed by the eigenpairs $(λ_n, \mathfrak{U}_n)$. We also show, at a formal level, that the framework developed here extends to Gibbs-type perturbations of the Brownian fixed point, in the spirit of self-interacting quantum field theories. In particular, we recover the standard irrelevant/marginal/relevant classification, usually obtained in the physics literature by power-counting, solely from the spectral analysis of $\mathscr{L}$.

</details>


### [312] [Condensation and Collapse in the Mean-Field Limit of Rotating 2D Bose Gases with Two-Body and Three-Body Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06466)
*Deke Li,Yuan Li,Qingxuan Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维旋转谐振子阱中N个相互吸引玻色子系统的基态性质。两体相互作用吸引且标度为N^{2α}U(N^αx)（0<α<1/12），三体作用排斥且标度为N^{4β}W(N^βx,N^βy)（0<β<1/24）。在平均场极限下，系统由旋转三次-五次非线性薛定谔（NLS）泛函描述。当两体耦合a趋于临界值a_*且三体耦合b趋于零时进入坍缩（collapse）区，NLS基态以Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式的最优解为普适轮廓发生爆破，能量满足E^{NLS}=(1-ζ/4+o(1))Q_pot ℓ_n²。多体理论严格证明了该有效描述：量子基态能量以相同渐近展开收敛于NLS能量，多体基态展现出向普适爆破轮廓的完全玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中的坍缩现象是量子多体物理的重要课题，特别是当吸引两体作用接近临界强度时系统会发生坍缩。此前已有研究在无三体作用或平衡状态下分析了该问题，但对同时存在吸引两体作用和排斥三体作用、且系统处于旋转状态下的坍缩区渐近行为尚未有严格的数学分析。本文旨在严格建立多体量子系统在坍缩区与NLS有效描述之间的一致性，为理解旋转BEC的普适爆破轮廓和能量渐近提供数学基础。

Method: 本文采用以下分析方法：（1）在平均场极限下，将多体哈密顿量的基态能量归约为旋转三次-五次NLS泛函的变分问题；（2）通过调节两体耦合a→a_*（临界值）和三体耦合b→0的相对速率，利用参数ζ≥0刻画坍缩区的不同子情形；（3）运用Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式的最优化理论确定爆破的普适轮廓；（4）通过严格的量子去平均场论证，结合能量上界（变分构造）和能量下界（相干态与局部密度估计），证明多体基态能量与NLS能量的渐近一致性以及完全玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚性质。

Result: 得到了坍缩区NLS基态的爆破轮廓由Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式最优解给出这一普适结果；推导了能量展开式E^{NLS}=(1-ζ/4+o(1))Q_pot ℓ_n²，其中系数ζ由a_n→a_*与b_n→0的相对速率决定。在参数范围0<α<1/12和0<β<1/24内严格证明了多体基态能量收敛于NLS能量并具有相同的坍缩区渐近展开，同时证明了多体基态表现出向该普适爆破轮廓的完全BEC凝聚。

Conclusion: 本文在严格的数学框架下完整刻画了旋转捕获阱中吸引玻色子在坍缩区的NLS有效理论，证明了三体排斥作用在防止完全坍缩并调控能量渐近行为中的关键作用，并确立了多体系统与平均场描述在该奇异的坍缩极限下的一致性。该工作为旋转BEC坍缩现象与三体效应提供了统一的变分与量子分析框架。

Abstract: We consider a system of $N$ interacting bosons in a rotating harmonic trap in $\mathbb{R}^2$, where the two-body interaction is attractive and scaled as $N^{2α}U(N^αx)$ with $0<α<1/12$, and the three-body interaction is repulsive and scaled as $N^{4β}W(N^βx,N^βy)$ with $0<β<1/24$. In the mean-field limit, the ground state energy is effectively described by a rotating cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger functional. We analyze the collapse regime where the two-body coupling $a$ approaches the critical value $a_*$ and the three-body coupling $b$ tends to zero. The NLS ground states blow up with a universal profile given by the optimizer of the Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality, and the energy satisfies $E^{\mathrm{NLS}} = (1-ζ/4+o(1))\mathcal{Q}{\rm{pot}}\ell_n^2$ with $ζ\geqslant0$ determined by the relative rates of $a_n\to a_*$ and $b_n\searrow0$. From the many-body theory, we rigorously justify this effective description: the quantum ground state energy converges to the NLS energy with the same asymptotic expansion in the collapse regime, and the many-body ground states exhibit complete Bose-Einstein condensation onto the universal blow-up profile.

</details>


### [313] [Novel computational formulas and relations for special numbers and functions: approach to analysis of Debye functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06620)
*Yilmaz Simsek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个与Debye函数相关的新特殊函数族，利用生成函数方法推导了涉及Debye函数、多重Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数与高阶Apostol-Bernoulli数之间的多种新公式与关系，并展示了该函数在负二项分布矩母函数、高阶Apostol型数、Frobenius-Euler数、Bernoulli数和Stirling数等之间的统一联系，同时给出了递推关系、Maclaurin级数展开和近似公式，以及在数学物理中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在构建一个统一框架，将Debye函数、多重Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数、Apostol型高阶数以及多种经典特殊函数和组合数联系在一起，并通过不同于解析数论的方法（生成函数法）证明新定理，填补这些函数之间缺乏系统统一表示的空白。

Method: 作者定义了与Debye函数相关的新特殊函数族，采用生成函数方法（generating function method）推导新公式，而非传统的解析数论证明方法。研究内容包括构造该函数的递推关系、Maclaurin级数展开以及近似公式，并揭示其在负二项分布矩母函数、高阶Apostol型数生成函数、Frobenius-Euler数和Stirling数等方面的统一性质。

Result: 推导出多种包含Debye函数的新公式与关系；建立了新函数与多重Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数及高阶Apostol-Bernoulli数的插值联系（在负整数处）；给出了该函数与矩母函数、多种生成函数之间的积分表示和特殊值关系；提供了递推关系、Maclaurin展开和近似公式。

Conclusion: 该论文通过引入新特殊函数族并采用生成函数法，将多个不同领域的经典数学对象（Debye函数、Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数、Apostol型数、Frobenius-Euler数、Bernoulli数、Stirling数等）统一联系起来，并给出了在数学物理中的应用说明与进一步展望。

Abstract: The first goal of this paper is to give a new family of special function related to the Debye functions. By using these function and generating function method, we derive many novel formulas and relations involving the Debye functions, multiple Hurwitz Lerch zeta function which interpolate the Apostol-Bernoulli numbers of higher order at negative integers. Proof method of theorems in this paper are different from those of theory of analytic numbers. The second goal of this paper is to show that special values and integral presentations of this function are closely related to unification of the Debye functions, the moment generating function for the negative binom distribution, the generating functions for the Apostol-type numbers of higher-order, and the Frobenius-Euler numbers, the Bernoulli numbers, and the Stirling numbers etc. Moreover, we give recurrence relations, Maclaurin's series expansion, and approximation formula for unification of the Debye functions. Finally, we give further remarks, observations and applications in mathematical physics on the results of this paper.

</details>


### [314] [Commutative Factorization of Nonlinear Second-Order Differential Equations: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06932)
*Gabriel Gonzalez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了可交换因式分解框架的推广，用于求解一类广泛的二阶非线性常微分方程，通过结合Riccati-Bernoulli方程与Bäcklund变换，实现对原方程的特定解和通解的系统性构造，并在经典力学及非线性振荡系统等多个物理和数学模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的可交换因式分解框架主要适用于特定类别的二阶非线性常微分方程，缺乏通用性。作者旨在扩展该框架，使其能够覆盖更广泛的非线性方程类别，并通过引入Riccati-Bernoulli方程与Bäcklund变换，为求解非线性微分方程提供更系统的解析工具。

Method: 作者推广了标准的可交换因式分解过程，将Riccati-Bernoulli方程和Bäcklund变换整合到分解框架中。该方法将二阶非线性常微分方程因式分解为可交换的一阶算子组合，从而为构造原方程的特定解和通解提供了系统化的路径。

Result: 通过若干具有物理和数学意义的非线性模型（包括经典力学方程和非线性振荡系统）的实例验证，证明了可交换因式分解作为求解非线性微分方程的有效解析工具，能够系统性地构造出原方程的特定解和通解。

Conclusion: 可交换因式分解经过推广后，结合Riccati-Bernoulli方程和Bäcklund变换，成为一种强有力的解析框架，可广泛应用于求解多样化的二阶非线性常微分方程，为非线性动力学和数学物理领域的问题提供了新的求解途径。

Abstract: This work presents a generalization of the commutative factorization framework for a broad class of second-order nonlinear ordinary differential equations. The main objective was to extend the commutative factorization procedure in order to use the Riccati-Bernoulli equation and the Bäcklund transformation. This provides a systematic route for constructing both particular and general solutions of the original second-order nonlinear equation. The theoretical development is illustrated through several nonlinear models of physical and mathematical interest, including equations arising in classical mechanics and nonlinear oscillatory systems. The results demonstrate that commutative factorization constitutes an effective analytical tool for solving nonlinear differential equations.

</details>


### [315] [A Note on Extended States on the Bethe Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06987)
*Vojkan Jaksic,Yoram Last,Simone Warzel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了弱无序Bethe格子上Anderson模型存在绝对连续谱的一个新证明，核心思路是建立Anderson型Hamilton量的循环性准则，将问题转化为证明两棵独立随机树的谱测度互不奇异，并通过Hellinger亲和度量加上谐波分析与紧致性论证完成证明，同时在$\mathbb{Z}^d$上给出了类似的定量循环性准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱无序Anderson模型在Bethe格子上绝对连续谱的存在性是一个经典问题，作者希望给出一个比已有证明更简洁、更具一般性的新论证路径，以深化对这一现象背后结构本质（循环性、谱测度非奇异性）的理解。

Method: 采用一般性的循环性准则框架，将绝对连续谱的存在性归结为两棵有根树独立副本谱测度互不奇异的问题；利用Hellinger亲和性检测两个谱测度是否互不奇异，并借助短谐波（调和函数）与紧致性论证获得弱无序区间的估计；此外在$d$维整数格情形下通过Poisson变换的$2\times2$矩阵谱测度的Schur补表述了定量的循环性准则。

Result: 成功给出了Bethe格子上弱无序Anderson模型绝对连续谱存在性的一个新证明，并获得了该问题所需的关键弱无序估计；同时在$\mathbb{Z}^d$上建立了对应的定量循环性准则。

Conclusion: 本文通过谱测度的Hellinger亲和性与循环性准则提供了一种简洁统一的途径来理解弱无序Anderson模型的绝对连续谱，为相关问题的进一步推广提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We give a new proof of the existence of absolutely continuous spectrum for the weakly disordered Anderson model on the Bethe lattice. The argument follows a general cyclicity criterion for Anderson-type Hamiltonians and reduces the problem to showing that the spectral measures of two independent copies of the rooted tree are not mutually singular. This is detected by their Hellinger affinity, and the required weak-disorder estimate follows from a short harmonic and compactness argument. We also formulate an analogous quantitative cyclicity criterion for the Anderson model on $\mathbb{Z}^d$, expressed through the Schur complement of the Poisson transform of a $2\times2$ matrix spectral measure.

</details>


### [316] [Renormalization group bounds on Araki relative entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07277)
*Edoardo D'Angelo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了一个多尺度 Bakry-Émery 判据，用于给出相对论量子场论中平衡态扰动下 Araki 相对熵的上界。作者利用随机正性将相对论玻色子场的伴随扰动与有质量欧几里得自由场的扰动对应起来，进而得到 Feynman-Kac 公式和 Araki 相对熵的几率表达式。通过将欧几里得自由场描述为抽象 Wiener 空间，推导出 Polchinski 方程，并扩展 Bauerschmidt-Bodineau-Dagallier 方法获得概率性相对熵上界，该上界可完全用算子代数语言表述。利用该判据，证明了紫外有限区域的洛伦兹有质量 Sine-Gordon 模型满足相对熵界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相对论量子场论（AQFT）框架下，计算平衡态受扰动后的 Araki 相对熵上界是一个重要但困难的问题。此前的工具（如 Bakry-Émery 判据）主要适用于经典概率或非相对论情形，缺乏直接适用于洛伦兹场论的相对熵界方法。本文旨在弥合欧几里得多尺度随机分析与相对论 QFT 之间的鸿沟，为谐振动产物类模型（特别是 Sine-Gordon）提供可验证的相对熵上界判据。

Method: 方法分为以下几步：(1) 利用随机正性将相对论玻色子场的伴随扰动映射为有质量欧几里得自由场的多项式扰动；(2) 通过 Feynman-Kac 公式将 Araki 相对熵转化为概率期望表达式；(3) 将欧几里得自由场实现为抽象 Wiener 空间以获得无穷维微分结构；(4) 在该框架下推导 Polchinski 型（重正化群流）方程以跟踪多尺度变化；(5) 扩展 Bauerschmidt-Bodineau-Dagallier 的精细分析技术，在尺度分解下控制各尺度贡献，最终得到概率性上界并将其翻译回算子代数语言，形成多尺度 Bakry-Émery 型判据。

Result: 获得了一个以算子代数术语完全表述的 Araki 相对熵上界判据，其形式类似于 Bakry-Émery 型标准但内置于多尺度/重正化群框架中。作为应用，证明了紫外有限区间的洛伦兹有质量 Sine-Gordon 模型满足该相对熵界，标志着这是首次在相对论 QFT 非微扰情形下验证此类熵不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Bakry-Émery 曲率下界思想推广到量子场论的多尺度背景中，建立了欧几里得概率方法与相对论量子场论之间新的桥梁。所得的相对熵判据不但适用于 Sine-Gordon 模型，也为其他相对论 QFT 平衡态在扰动下的稳定性以及信息论性质（如强次可加性的定量加强）研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: This paper derives a multiscale Bakry-Émery criterion for an upper bound on the Araki relative entropy of perturbations of an equilibrium state in relativistic QFT. Using stochastic positivity, affiliated perturbations of the relativistic bosonic field can be put in correspondence with perturbations of the massive Euclidean free field. This gives a Feynman-Kac formula, which in turns is used to derive a probabilistic expression for the Araki relative entropy. The description of the massive Euclidean free field as an abstract Wiener space provides the infinite-dimensional framework to derive a Polchinski equation. Extending the methods of Bauerschmidt, Bodineau, and Dagallier to this context yields a probabilistic bound on the Araki relative entropy, which can be formulated entirely in operator-algebraic terms. Using the criterion, it is shown that the Lorentzian massive Sine-Gordon model in the ultraviolet finite regime satisfies the relative entropy bound.

</details>


### [317] [Towards Heterogeneous Exascale CFD with a Single Fortran Code Base: OpenMP Target Acceleration of the High-Order Unstructured Solver UCNS3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07387)
*Panagiotis Tsoutsanis,Michal Uminski,Christopher Day,David Henty*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于OpenMP target的单一源代码方法，将UCNS3D（一种高阶非结构有限体积可压缩流求解器）现代化改造为CPU/GPU异构执行，完整移植了显式时间推进路径，无需分叉代码库。验证与性能测试表明GPU加速可带来2.71-4.05倍同节点加速和84-101%的强扩展效率，同时保持数值保真度至机器精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构exascale系统正改变计算流体力学格局，但将成熟的高阶求解器移植到加速器往往导致代码库碎片化并削弱可复现性。现有方法通常只移植孤立的内核，难以处理高阶非结构CFD中的不规则模板、复杂数据结构和大量临时存储。

Method: 采用单一源码的OpenMP target现代化方案，覆盖完整显式时间推进路径（高阶重构、梯度计算、无粘/粘性通量、边界处理、halo交换、解更新）。关键技术决策包括持久target数据区、扁平化运行时数组、编译期临时存储尺寸、显式局部代数、可target调用的例程以及带设备驻留缓冲区的GPU感知MPI。CPU路径与GPU卸载共享同一套数值公式和Fortran基础代码。

Result: 压缩Taylor-Green涡验证显示CPU与GPU耗散历史在LUMI上一致至机器精度，并与已发表参考数据吻合。ARCHER2 GPU平台上的单节点运行为可移植性提供了独立验证。重构后的CPU路径相对旧版快1.27-1.67倍，GPU卸载实现2.71-4.05倍同节点加速和84-101%强扩展效率。在Taylor-Green涡、LM1021音爆和NASA高升力CRM基准上进行了全节点端到端评估。

Conclusion: 基于标准的OpenMP能够在不牺牲数值保真度、CPU性能或软件可持续性的前提下，为生产规模的高阶非结构CFD提供可移植的异构加速，证明了单一源代码策略对成熟科学计算代码库的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous exascale systems are reshaping computational fluid dynamics, yet rewriting mature high-order solvers for accelerators can fragment code bases and weaken reproducibility. We present a single-source OpenMP target modernisation of UCNS3D, a high-order unstructured finite-volume solver for compressible flows. The common CPU/GPU implementation preserves its numerical formulation, Fortran code base, and workflows. Rather than porting isolated kernels, we enable the complete explicit time-advancement path: high-order reconstruction, gradient evaluation, inviscid and viscous fluxes, boundary treatment, halo exchange, and solution update. The implementation uses persistent target data regions, flat run-time arrays, compile-time sizing of temporary storage, explicit local algebra, target-callable routines, and GPU-aware MPI with device-resident buffers. These choices address the irregular stencils, complex data structures, and substantial temporary storage of high-order unstructured CFD. Verification using the compressible Taylor-Green vortex shows CPU and GPU dissipation histories agreeing to machine precision on LUMI and with published reference data. A single-node run on the ARCHER2 GPU platform provides an independent portability check. End-to-end performance is evaluated on fully populated nodes using Taylor-Green vortex, LM1021 sonic-boom, and NASA high-lift CRM benchmarks. Relative to the previous production implementation, the refactored CPU path is 1.27-1.67 times faster, while GPU offload delivers same-node speed-ups of 2.71-4.05 and 84-101 percent strong-scaling efficiency. The results show that standards-based OpenMP provides portable, production-scale acceleration without sacrificing numerical fidelity, CPU performance, or software sustainability.

</details>


### [318] [Möbius Invariant Dimers and Miquel Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07485)
*Niklas C. Affolter,Luis Mühlhoff*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个在Möbius变换群PO(3,1)下不变的圆模式与二聚体模型之间的新对应关系，并证明Miquel动力学保持配分函数，t-嵌入的凸性保证面权重的正性。该对应还揭示了隐藏的PO(3,3)对称群，与t-嵌入的Lorentz提升相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知的圆模式与二聚体模型的对应关系仅在欧几里得变换下不变，而圆模式本身的自然对称群是Möbius变换群PO(3,1)。作者旨在建立一种在Möbius变换（更符合圆模式几何本质的对称性）下不变的对应关系，从而扩展该对应关系的适用范围。

Method: 建立圆模式与二聚体模型之间的新对应，使其在Möbius变换群PO(3,1)下不变；通过Miquel动力学研究配分函数的不变性；分析t-嵌入的凸性与面权重正性的关系；通过Lorentz提升揭示隐藏的PO(3,3)对称群；并对等角图（isoradial graphs）、Doyle螺线、单脐环面及等温圆模式等具体情形进行讨论。

Result: 新建立的圆模式-二聚体模型对应关系在PO(3,1)下不变；Miquel动力学保持配分函数；t-嵌入的凸性保证面权重的正性；对应关系具有隐藏的PO(3,3)对称群，与t-嵌入的Lorentz提升相关联；在等角图、Doyle螺线、单脐环面和等温圆模式等案例上得到验证和讨论。

Conclusion: 所建立的对应关系将圆模式与二聚体模型的联系推广到了Möbius变换群下，为这两个领域提供了更深刻的几何统一视角，并揭示了额外的对称结构PO(3,3)及多个具体应用场景。

Abstract: As is already known, there is a correspondence between circle patterns and the dimer model, which is invariant under Euclidean transformations. We establish a new correspondence that is invariant under the group of Möbius transformations PO(3,1), which is the natural symmetry group of circle patterns. We show that Miquel dynamics preserve the partition function, and that convexity of the t-embedding guarantees positivity of the face weights. We also show that the correspondence has a somewhat hidden symmetry group PO(3,3), which relates to the Lorentz lift of the t-embedding. Finally, we consider isoradial graphs, Doyle spirals, the once-punctured torus and isothermic circle patterns.

</details>


### [319] [Entropic repulsion to the middle layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07578)
*Max Mihailescu,Ron Peled*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究限制在有限值域{-S,...,S}上的∇φ高度函数（偶凸相互作用能W），证明在某些维度和温度条件下模型具有唯一Gibbs测度且关联指数衰减，同时展示另一类W在高维和特定温度下可具有多个Gibbs测度；还证明即使唯一性失效，每个Gibbs测度的磁化强度仍落在(-1/2,1/2)内，这蕴含该模型在任意维度、任意偶凸W和任意温度下的去局域化结论，并将方法推广到连续值和半整数值设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受限高度函数模型中熵排斥效应的作用，解决Bricmont–El Mellouki–Fröhlich（1986）遗留的开放问题，并推广此前仅对二次相互作用证明的连续区间取值唯一性结果。

Method: 采用非Pirogov–Sinai的新证明方法处理低温情形；对偶凸相互作用能W按其二阶导数是否非增进行分类分析；利用磁化强度限制在(-1/2,1/2)的核心控制来推导去局域化，并将类似论证推广到实数区间[-1,1]取值和半整数取值{-S+1/2,...,S-1/2}的设置。

Result: 证明：(a) d=2时所有温度下唯一Gibbs测度；(b) d≥3时对宽类具有非增二阶导数的W（含|x|^p, p∈[1,2]）在所有温度下唯一；(c) d≥3时在低温和高温区间唯一。相反，构造了一类偶凸W在高维和适当温度下具有多个Gibbs测度。证明每个Gibbs测度的磁化强度在(-1/2,1/2)内，从而得到去局域化。还证明[-1,1]连续取值总具唯一测度（此前仅对二次相互作用证明），半整数情况的磁化强度在(-1,1)内。

Conclusion: 在受限高度函数模型中，熵排斥导致的唯一性与多测度性取决于相互作用W的具体形式、维度和温度；磁化强度的一致有界性为去局域化和连续/半整数设置的扩展提供了统一框架，并为W(x)=x²的经典问题给出了完整答案。

Abstract: We consider $\nabla\varphi$ height functions with even and convex interaction energy $W$ on the lattice $\mathbb{Z}^d$, which are restricted to take values in the set $\{-S, \ldots, S\}$ for some integer $S \ge 1$. We study the effect of entropic repulsion, which tends to push the spin values to the middle layer.
  We prove that the model has a unique Gibbs measure, with exponential decay of correlations, in the cases: (a) Dimension $d=2$ at all temperatures. (b) Dimensions $d\ge3$ at all temperatures, for a wide class of $W$ with non-increasing second derivative, including the family $W(x)=|x|^p$ for $p\in[1,2]$. (c) Dimensions $d\ge 3$ at both low and high temperatures, $T\in (0,\frac{W(1)d}{4(\ln d+\ln 8)})\cup(d W(2S),\infty]$, with the normalization $W(0)=0$. At low temperatures, our proof provides an alternative to Pirogov--Sinai methods.
  Conversely, we exhibit a class of even and convex interaction energies $W$ which, in high dimensions and suitable temperature regimes, have multiple Gibbs measures.
  Though uniqueness may fail, we show that the magnetization of every Gibbs measure lies in $(-\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2})$. This implies the delocalization of the model restricted to take values in $\{0,1,\ldots\}$ (i.e., conditioned to lie above a floor) for all dimensions, any even and convex $W$, and all temperatures.
  Our methods extend to additional setups: We prove that height functions taking values in the real interval $[-1,1]$ always have a unique Gibbs measure, a result previously proved only for the quadratic interaction. For height functions taking values in $\{-S+\frac{1}{2}, \ldots, S-\frac{1}{2}\}$, $S \ge1 $ integer, we prove that the magnetization of every Gibbs measure lies in $(-1,1)$.
  The special case $W(x)=x^2$ of our results addresses questions left open in the work of Bricmont--El Mellouki--Fröhlich (1986).

</details>


### [320] [Classification of Conformally Covariant 2-Tensors from the Kulkarni--Nomizu Product in Dimension 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07750)
*Yury N. Berdinsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在四维空间中完整分类了所有自然、共形协变、共形重量为-2、微分阶数不超过4的对称(0,2)-张量，证明该空间恰好是2维的，由Bach张量（二阶）和Eastwood-Singer张量（四阶）张成。分类的关键代数核使用Lean 4和Mathlib进行了形式化验证，约束矩阵和散度自由条件则通过精确有理算术计算验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共形几何中，理解所有满足共形协变性质的自然张量是一个基本且重要的问题。Bach张量在广义相对论和共形几何中扮演重要角色，而Eastwood-Singer张量在四维中与之密切相关。此前该空间中是否存在其他张量尚不明确，本文旨在给出一个穷尽且严格的分类结果，并借助形式化证明手段确保代数推导的正确性。

Method: 作者系统地枚举了由度量、Schouten张量、协变导数以及Kulkarni-Nomizu乘积所能构建的所有对称(0,2)-张量，并施加共形协变性和共形重量-2的限制，同时将微分阶数限制在4以内。分类的代数核心通过Lean 4和Mathlib进行形式化验证；8×11约束矩阵以及散度自由条件则使用精确有理数算术进行计算验证。

Result: 在四维中，满足上述条件的自然、共形协变的对称(0,2)-张量空间恰好是2维的，由Bach张量（微分阶数2）和Eastwood-Singer张量（微分阶数4）线性张成。所有约束的代数验证通过Lean 4形式化证明完成，数值约束矩阵的验证采用精确有理算术。

Conclusion: 本文给出了四维共形几何中一个完整的张量分类定理，确认了该空间中不存在除Bach和Eastwood-Singer之外的独立张量。此外，论文将形式化验证方法引入几何分析领域，增强了结果的可靠性，并提供了英俄双语的论文版本。

Abstract: We classify all natural, conformally covariant, symmetric (0,2)-tensors of conformal weight -2 and differential order less than or equal to 4 in dimension 4, built from the metric, the Schouten tensor, covariant derivatives, and the Kulkarni-Nomizu product. We prove that the space of such tensors is exactly 2-dimensional, spanned by the Bach tensor (order 2) and the Eastwood-Singer tensor (order 4). The algebraic core is formally verified using Lean 4 with Mathlib. The 8x11 constraint matrix and divergence-free condition are verified computationally with exact rational arithmetic. Both English and Russian versions provided; Russian version available as ancillary.

</details>


### [321] [Equation of Motion for Open Thermodynamic Systems and Its Dynamical Equivalence with Mechanical and Electrical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07810)
*E Aydiner*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量纲约化将热力学第一定律从紧凑形式转化为开放热力学系统的显式运动方程，发现该动力学等价于受驱阻尼谐振子和受驱RLC电路，并建立了三种系统的拉格朗日表述，揭示其共同的普适动力学结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学第一定律形式高度紧凑，未显式揭示系统动力学，也没有提供对应的运动方程。虽然力学和电学系统可以显式写出运动方程，但从第一定律直接建立热力学系统的动力学表述尚未实现，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过量纲约化（dimensional reduction）将第一定律从热力学紧凑形式变换为开放热力学系统的显式运动方程；对导出的方程和有效系数进行详细的量纲分析以验证内部一致性；系统比较力学、电学和热力学系统；构造热力学系统的拉格朗日表述并建立与力学和电学系统的对应关系。

Result: 导出的运动方程显示热力学系统的动力学等价于受驱阻尼谐振子和受驱RLC电路。量纲分析证实了该表述的自洽性。尽管三种系统物理起源不同，但它们共享相同的普适动力学结构。进一步建立了热力学系统的拉格朗日表述，证实其与力学和电学系统的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该工作表明可以从热力学第一定律直接生成显式运动方程，并揭示了热力学、力学和电学系统之间共同的动力学框架，为跨学科系统的统一理解提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Changes in the internal energy of a thermodynamic system are fundamentally described by the first law of thermodynamics. However, the first law has a highly compact form that does not explicitly reveal the dynamics of the system or provide a corresponding equation of motion. While equations of motion can be written explicitly for mechanical and electrical systems, an analogous dynamical formulation for thermodynamic systems has, to our knowledge, not been established directly from the first law. In this work, we show that, through dimensional reduction, the first law can be transformed from its compact thermodynamic form into an explicit equation of motion for an open thermodynamic system. Remarkably, the resulting equation reveals that the dynamics of the thermodynamic system is equivalent to that of a driven damped harmonic oscillator and a driven RLC electrical circuit. Detailed dimensional analyses of the derived equations and effective coefficients demonstrate the internal consistency of the resulting formulation. We further perform a systematic comparison of the mechanical, electrical, and thermodynamic systems and show that, despite their distinct physical origins, they share the same universal dynamical structure. Finally, we construct a Lagrangian formulation of the thermodynamic system and establish its correspondence with the Lagrangian descriptions of the mechanical and electrical systems. These results demonstrate that an explicit equation of motion can be generated directly from the first law of thermodynamics and reveal a common dynamical framework underlying thermodynamic, mechanical, and electrical systems.

</details>


### [322] [Energy diffusion in a pinned harmonic chain with free boundary condition and interactions of increasing range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08138)
*Yipeng Lu,Wei Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究二维固定谐振子链中交互作用范围随系统尺寸发散且伴随保守旋转噪声的情形。在交互作用范围微观发散但宏观消失的联合标度下，证明了期望经验能量测度收敛于热方程的唯一测度值弱解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡统计物理中能量传输的宏观行为，特别是当粒子间相互作用范围与系统尺寸相关时，能量如何随时间演化并最终由热方程描述。

Method: 考虑二维固定谐振子链模型，引入随系统尺寸发散的相互作用范围以及保守旋转噪声，在宏观极限下建立交互作用范围的联合标度，通过分析期望经验能量测度的收敛性来证明其趋近热方程的弱解。

Result: 在微观发散而宏观消失的交互作用范围的联合标度条件下，期望经验能量测度收敛到热方程的唯一测度值弱解。

Conclusion: 该结果验证了在此类广义交互作用和谐振子链设置下，能量传输的宏观极限满足热方程，为从微观哈密顿动力系统推导宏观扩散行为提供了严格数学证明。

Abstract: We consider a finite two-dimensional pinned harmonic chain with an interaction range that diverges with the system size and with conservative rotational noise. Under a joint scaling in which the interaction range diverges microscopically but vanishes macroscopically, we prove that the expected em- pirical energy measures converge to the unique measure-valued weak solution of the heat equation.

</details>


### [323] [Asymptotics of Ground States for Helium-Like and Bosonic Atoms in a Two-Cluster Region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08257)
*Yukimi Goto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究N粒子原子Coulomb哈密顿量的正基态在双簇区域的渐近行为，证明了当粒子间距趋于无穷时，N粒子波函数与(N-1)粒子基态之间存在精确的分解关系，并给出了不同区域的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多粒子原子系统在粒子分离（双簇区域）时波函数的渐近结构，是量子力学多体问题中确定排斥势的精确渐近形式、证明束缚态稳定性以及构建有效理论的基石。

Method: 采用解析方法研究N粒子Coulomb哈密顿量的基态波函数，利用单粒子密度ρ与(N-1)粒子基态φ之间的精确关系，在双簇区域（即x_N趋于无穷，其余粒子受限区域）推导出ψ/√ρ与φ之间的渐近展开及统一的误差估计。通过选择不同的截断尺度（|x_N|^α与R0 ln|x_N|）获得对应的收敛阶。

Result: 对于0<α<1，当|x_N|→∞时，在|x_i|≤|x_N|^α区域内，ψ/√ρ(x_N) = φ + O(|x_N|^{-2+α} ln|x_N|)；在|x_i|≤R0 ln|x_N|区域内收敛速率为O(|x_N|^{-2}(ln|x_N|)^2)；平方L^2误差具有O(|x_N|^{-2})的界。这些结果在本征谱以下及阈值处均成立。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了N体Coulomb基态分解为(N-1)体基态与单粒子密度的精确渐近收敛结果，在不同区域给出了最优的收敛速率，并且涵盖了谱的下方和阈值两种情况，为多电子原子系统的渐近分析提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of positive ground states of $N$-particle atomic Coulomb Hamiltonians in a two-cluster region. For the $N$-particle ground state $ψ$, its one-particle density $ρ$, and $(N-1)$-particle ground state $φ$, we show that for $0<α<1$, as $|x_N|\to\infty$, $ψ(x_1,\dots,x_N)/\sqrt{ρ(x_N)}= φ(x_1,\dots,x_{N-1})+O(|x_N|^{-2+α} \ln |x_N|)$ uniformly in the region $|x_i|\le |x_N|^α$. In the region $|x_i|\le R_0\ln |x_N|$, the convergence rate is $O(|x_N|^{-2} (\ln |x_N|)^2)$. We also establish an $O(|x_N|^{-2})$ bound on the squared $L^2$ error. These results hold both below the essential spectrum and at threshold.

</details>


### [324] [Arithmetic triangular structures in the transfer-matrix of finite Kronig-Penney models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08523)
*Lidia Aceto,Pietro Antonio Grassi,Helmuth Robert Malonek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对有限Kronig-Penney模型中单胞转移矩阵的N次幂进行了完整的解析表征，利用第二类Chebyshev多项式推导出全局透射和反射振幅的闭式表达式，并揭示了多重量子散射过程、离散卷积模式与超复数组合结构之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Figueroa等人(2025)识别了转移矩阵特定元素及相关组合系数的出现，但尚未建立相应闭式表达的严格推导。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，提供完整的解析表征。

Method: 将单胞转移矩阵的N次幂用第二类Chebyshev多项式表示，从而获得全局透射振幅和反射振幅的显式闭式表达式。

Result: 获得了全局透射和反射振幅的显式闭式表达，并揭示了一种此前未被认识的、介于多重量子散射过程、离散卷积模式和超复数组合结构之间的结构性对应关系。

Conclusion: 该公式化表述首次建立了有限Kronig-Penney模型中多个散射过程与Chebyshev多项式表示之间的严格数学联系，为理解相关量子输运现象提供了完整的解析框架。

Abstract: This work provides a complete analytical characterization of the transfer- matrix structure associated with the finite Kronig-Penney model consisting of one-dimensional arrays of Dirac delta potentials recently introduced by Figueroa et al. (2025). Although their study identified the emergence of specific transfer-matrix entries and related combinatorial coefficients, a rig- orous derivation of the corresponding closed-form expressions has not yet been established. By expressing the N th power of the unit-cell transfer ma- trix in terms of Chebyshev polynomials of the second kind, we obtain explicit closed-form representations for the global transmission and reflection ampli- tudes. The proposed formulation reveals a previously unrecognized structural correspondence between multiple quantum scattering processes, discrete con- volutional patterns, and hypercomplex combinatorial structures.

</details>


### [325] [The phase coordinates transformation in Moyal noncommutative framework 2-dimensional harmonic oscillator and Painlevé second equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08525)
*Irfan Mahmood*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Moyal非对易形式主义的物理应用，通过引入新的变换将旧正则坐标映射到纯非对易相空间坐标，并构建了16分量的无迹反对称张量。该形式同时包含空间-空间和空间-动量非对易性（而非仅Heisenberg量子对易），并应用变换构造了2维谐振子的非对易类比，研究其超可积性及Painlevé第二方程，还应用于生成第一个Yablonskii–Vorobev多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Moyal非对易形式主义的物理应用，并对传统仅含动量-坐标非对易性的框架进行推广，纳入空间-空间与空间-动量的非对易性，从而更全面地理解非对易结构下的量子力学系统。

Method: 提出连接旧正则坐标与纯非对易相坐标的新变换，该变换与Moyal非对易括号一致，并构建16分量无迹反对称张量。基于此变换构造二维谐振子的非对易类比，分析其超可积性，并推导Painlevé第二方程的非对易形式，进而通过坐标变换生成第一个Yablonskii–Vorobev多项式。

Result: 所构造的变形Hamiltonian包含额外对称性，揭示了附加对称性与非对易结构之间的深层物理联系。Painlevé第二非对易坐标变换被有效应用于生成第一个Yablonskii–Vorobev多项式及Painlevé第二参数变换。

Conclusion: Moyal非对易形式结合新变换可系统性地推广量子力学系统（如谐振子）并揭示其超可积性与额外对称性，同时与Painlevé方程及Yablonskii–Vorobev多项式等数学结构建立了深层联系。

Abstract: This work aims to explore the physical application of Moyal noncom- mutative formalism with the the presentation of new transformations which connect the old canonical coordinates to purly noncommuting phase coordi- nates through the parameters describe the Moyal noncommutative deforma- tion. These transformations are consistent with the Moyal noncommunica- tive brackets and build sixteen components traceless anti-symmetric tensor. This formalism incorporates space-space and space-momentum noncommu- taivity rather then noncommutativity (Heisenberg quantum commutation ) only for the canonical coordinates. The transformations imply to construct noncommutative analogs of 2-dimensional harmonic oscillator with
  its superintegrability and Painlevé second equation. The associated deformed Hamiltonians additionally involve some extra symmetries and reveal a deep physical understanding of about the additional symmetries with noncommunicative structure. The Painlevé second noncommutative coordinates transformation are also efficiently applied to generate the first Yablonskii Vorobev polynomial with Painlevé second parameter transformation.

</details>


### [326] [On spatial decoherence in many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08910)
*Stefano Marcantoni,Marco Merkli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个与玻色子储库强耦合的多体量子系统动力学,证明在无穷耦合强度极限下系统经历瞬时空间退相干,并通过引入细粒度时间尺度τ=λt精确解析退相干过程,构造了具有可控误差估计的近似有效演化映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合量子系统与玻色子环境的相互作用动力学,特别是在无限耦合强度下空间退相干如何产生,以及如何在有限时间尺度上精确解析这一退相干过程。

Method: 考虑大耦合强度λ和短时间尺度t∝λ^{-α}(α≥0),在λ→∞极限下分析动力学。区分不同时间尺度:α>1时动力学平凡,0≤α<1时退相干瞬时发生,α=1时定义细粒度时间尺度τ=λt以精确解析退相干过程。构造具有可控误差估计的近似有效演化映射。

Result: 在细粒度时间尺度α=1下,退相干过程可以被精确解析。有效动力学通常是马尔可夫的,但不由动力学半群给出。严格描述了宏观量子物体在位置空间中的局域化现象。

Conclusion: 该工作为强耦合多体量子系统的空间退相干提供了严格数学描述,并给出了宏观量子对象位置局域化的物理应用,揭示了有效动力学具有马尔可夫性但并非动力学半群这一非平凡特征。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of a many-body quantum system strongly interacting with a bosonic reservoir. The coupling is given by a potential operator for the system and it is linear in the field. We show that in the limit of infinite coupling strength the system undergoes instantaneous spatial decoherence. To resolve the decoherence process in time we consider large coupling strengths $λ$ and short times scaling as $t\propto λ^{-α}$, with $α\ge0$, in the limit $λ\rightarrow\infty$. On short time scales $α>1$ the dynamics is trivial while on longer ones $0\leα<1$ the decoherence is instantaneous. We show that the decoherence process is resolved exactly for $α=1$, defining the fine-grained time scale $τ=λt$. We construct an approximate effective evolution map of the many-body system with controlled error estimates. Generically, the effective dynamics is markovian, but not given by a dynamical semigroup. As a physical application we give a rigorous description of the phenomenon of localization of macroscopic quantum objects in position space.

</details>


### [327] [The inverse problem of time-dependent density functional theory on the torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08984)
*Asbjørn Bækgaard Lauritsen,Mathieu Lewin,Jakob Oldenburg*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意足够规则的单粒子密度，存在唯一的外部势能使多体薛定谔方程的解具有该预设密度。该结果意味着可以用非相互作用电子精确重现相互作用系统的时间相关密度，这是含有时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）的核心。结果涵盖了库仑相互作用和由扩展原子核产生的密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）的基础假设——即Runge-Gross定理的严格性。该定理声称相互作用系统的时间相关密度可以由非相互作用系统精确重现，但此前缺乏对于库仑相互作用等物理情形的严格数学证明。

Method: 通过数学分析的方法，证明对于任意给定的足够规则的单粒子密度，多体薛定谔方程存在唯一的外部势解，从而建立密度到势的映射关系。证明涵盖库仑势和扩展原子核产生的密度等物理相关情形。

Result: 证明了在库仑相互作用和扩展原子核情形下，存在唯一外部势使多体薛定谔方程的解具有预定密度，从而为非相互作用系统重现相互作用系统的密度提供了严格的理论保证。

Conclusion: 该结果为TDDFT的数学基础提供了严格的证明，确认了含时密度泛函理论在物理相关情形（包括库仑相互作用）下的有效性。

Abstract: We prove that, for any given sufficiently regular one-particle density, there exists a unique external potential for which the solution to the many-body Schrödinger equation has this prescribed density. This implies in particular that one can exactly reproduce the time-dependent density of an interacting system using non-interacting electrons, which is at the heart of time-dependent density functional theory. Our result covers the Coulomb interaction and densities arising from extended nuclei.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [328] [Lee-Yang zeros of modulated XY spin chains with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction: zero-contour topology and quantum phase-diagram reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05469)
*Hong Jiang,Xiang-Ping Jiang,Yan-Chao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用的不均匀各向异性XY自旋链在复横场平面中的Lee-Yang零点（LYZ），揭示了其与量子相变和带折叠的拓扑对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lee-Yang零点在复杂量子多体系统中的拓扑演化规律，建立其与量子相变、带结构和相图重构之间的直接对应关系，为表征量子临界性和相特征提供新的理论工具。

Method: 系统研究均匀链、周期2/周期3调制链及Fibonacci准周期链（长度5和8），通过数值求解复横场平面中的Lee-Yang零点，并结合解析推导分析DM耦合和各向异性配对对能带折叠及零点位置的影响。

Result: 随DM耦合强度D增大，均匀链的复零点向实轴坍缩；周期链出现闭合零点轮廓的分叉或复零点的单次重现；准周期链则呈现复零点的反复湮灭与再生。解析推导表明这些多样性源于DM诱导的折叠带移位以及粒子-空穴带交叉处各向异性配对对零点的调制。

Conclusion: 零点的孤立接触点对应于离散量子临界场，连续实零点区间直接标识手性无能隙相。Lee-Yang零点拓扑能够区分不同特征相，可作为带折叠和相图重构的直观探针。

Abstract: We investigate the Lee--Yang zeros (LYZ) of inhomogeneous anisotropic XY spin chains with Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interactions in the complex transverse-field plane, focusing on their fundamental connection to quantum phase transitions. We systematically study uniform chains, period-2 and period-3 modulated chains, and Fibonacci quasiperiodic chains of lengths 5 and 8. As the DM coupling strength $D$ increases, the LYZ exhibit qualitatively distinct topological evolutions on the complex plane: the complex zeros of the uniform chain collapse toward the real axis; periodic chains feature either bifurcation of closed zero contours before all zeros become real or a single re-emergence of complex zeros; quasiperiodic chains exhibit repeated annihilation and revival of complex zeros. Analytical derivations demonstrate that this diverse behavior originates from DM-induced shifts of folded bands and the modulation of zero positions by anisotropic pairing at particle--hole band crossings. Our results establish a direct correspondence between LYZ topology and band deformation: isolated contact points of zeros with the real axis correspond to discrete quantum critical fields, while continuous real-zero intervals directly identify gapless chiral phases. Accordingly, beyond locating phase boundaries, LYZ can distinguish characteristic phases and serve as an intuitive probe for band folding and phase diagram restructuring.

</details>


### [329] [Quantum phase transitions and quantum-information characterization of a non-Hermitian XY chain with staggered Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05470)
*Hong Jiang,Xiang-Ping Jiang,Yan-Chao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用量子信息方法研究具有交错Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用的非厄米XY链的相图，通过交替局域自旋转动变换将其映射到标准无DM的XY链上，发现变换后的哈密顿量具有旋转-时间反演（RT）对称性。结果表明单比特纠缠只能探测LL-PM相变（即RT对称性恢复的例外边界），而量子失协（QD）和量子相干（QC）能同时识别两个相界，且不同方向的QC测量可捕获DM诱导的子格间相对转动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米量子系统（特别是含交错DM相互作用的XY链）的相图结构，并系统评估量子信息量在表征非厄米系统中量子相变的能力，以期找到能全面刻画该系统各相界的最优量子信息探针。

Method: 首先通过交替局域自旋-转动变换将含交错DM相互作用的非厄米XY链映射为标准的无DM的XY链，利用已知标准XY链相图结果结合关联函数分析构造系统的完整相图；随后系统比较单比特纠缠、量子失协（QD）和量子相干（QC）及其高阶导数在检测非厄米系统中各量子相变的表现，并对不同方向上的QC测量进行分析以区分DM效应。

Result: 单比特纠缠只能探测LL-PM相变（对应RT对称性恢复的例外边界）；量子失协和量子相干均能同时确定两个相界——不仅能定位例外边界，其二阶导数还能分辨RT破缺区域内的FM-LL转变；此外，不同方向的QC测量可捕捉DM诱导的子格间相对转动，从而区分交错DM链与具有相同有效参数的无DM链。

Conclusion: 量子失协与量子相干是非厄米含DM XY链中优于单比特纠缠的量子相变探针，能够全面刻画包括例外边界在内的各相界；基于方向分辨的量子相干测量还可揭示DM相互作用带来的对称性破缺特征，为区分不同DM构型的非厄米自旋链提供有效手段。

Abstract: We investigate the phase diagram of a non-Hermitian XY chain with staggered Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interactions using quantum-information methods. Through an alternating local-spin-rotation transformation, the model is mapped onto a standard DM-free XY chain, and the resulting transformed Hamiltonian exhibits rotation--time-reversal ($\RT$) symmetry. Combining correlation-function analysis with known phase results of the standard XY chain, we construct the phase diagram for the present system. We further adopt quantum-information-based quantities to systematically assess their performance in characterizing quantum phase transitions within this non-Hermitian system. Our results show that single-site entanglement can only detect the Luttinger-liquid (LL)--paramagnetic (PM) phase transition, which corresponds to the exceptional boundary across which $\RT$ symmetry is restored. In contrast, quantum discord (QD) and quantum coherence (QC) identify both phase boundaries: in addition to locating the exceptional boundary, their second-order derivatives resolve the ferromagnetic (FM)--LL transition inside the $\RT$-broken region. Moreover, measurements of QC along different directions capture the DM-induced relative rotation between the two sublattices, thereby distinguishing the staggered-DM chain from a zero-DM chain with identical effective parameters.

</details>


### [330] [Quantum information spreading via higher-order operator correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05472)
*M Süzen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过引入N-算子关联函数测量量子信息传播的新视角。作者借鉴宇宙学中N点关联函数的思想，将传统的双算子OTOC推广为N算子关联函数（NOC），从而克服仅选择两个算子分析量子信息传播的局限性。作者在两个可解析求解的玩具系统（纵向场中的单量子比特和XYZ双量子比特系统）中演示了这一概念，计算了有限温度下算子时间演化的热平均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的OTOC仅选取两个算子来分析量子信息传播，分析范围受限。作者希望引入广义关联函数，使更广泛的测量探针能够参与量子信息传播程度的度量，从而更全面地刻画量子信息传播的特征。

Method: 借鉴宇宙学中的N点关联函数思想，将标准的双算子OTOC推广为N算子关联函数（NOC）。在有限温度下计算算子时间演化的热平均值，并在两个解析可解的玩具系统中进行验证：单个处于纵向磁场中的量子比特，以及XYZ型双量子比特系统。

Result: NOC能够纳入比传统OTOC更大集合的测量探针来度量量子信息传播的程度，并成功地在两个玩具模型中得到演示，表明该框架可用于减少仅选择两个算子所带来的分析限制。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子力学中的广义算子关联函数提供了基础性介绍，提出NOC作为测量量子信息传播的新视角，展示了其在可解析模型中的可行性。

Abstract: We provide a perspective into measuring the quantum information spreading by introducing N-operator correlators. Leveraging the idea from N-point correlation function from cosmology, we formulate a generalized Out-of-Time-Ordered Correlator (OTOC), called N-Operator Correlator (NOC). Such formulation can reduce the effect of restricted analysis associated with selecting only two operators for OTOC. NOC can take into account a larger set of measurement probes in measuring the degree of quantum information spreading. We demonstrate these concepts with two analytically tractable toy systems with computing thermal averages of operator time evolutions at a finite temperature: a single qubit in longitudinal field and XYZ two-qubit system. Our perspective serves as a basic introduction to generalized operator correlators in quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [331] [Atmospheric Turbulence-Aware Optimization of Generalized Kennedy Receivers for Coherent-State BPSK Quantum Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05471)
*Md Ashraf Hossain Ifty,Rahul Bhadani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了湍流自由空间光通信中相干态BPSK信号的广义Kennedy接收机，并建立了遍历互信息框架以联合优化接收机位移和输入先验概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在湍流自由空间光信道中，BPSK相干态通信的Kennedy接收机性能受大气闪烁（Gamma-Gamma衰落）影响显著，需要针对衰落信道条件设计优化的接收策略。

Method: 提出一个基于遍历互信息的优化框架，在Gamma-Gamma衰落模型下对广义Kennedy接收机的两个关键参量——接收机位移和输入符号先验概率——进行联合优化。

Result: 该框架能够同时确定最优的接收机位移参数与非均匀输入先验概率，从而提升衰落信道下相干态BPSK通信的互信息性能。

Conclusion: 通过遍历互信息框架联合优化位移与先验概率，可为湍流自由空间光信道中的广义Kennedy接收机提供性能增益，验证了在衰落条件下自适应参数优化的必要性。

Abstract: We investigate generalized Kennedy receivers for coherent-state BPSK communication over turbulent free-space optical channels. An ergodic mutual-information framework is developed to jointly optimize the receiver displacement and input prior probabilities under Gamma--Gamma fading.

</details>


### [332] [Matrix Product Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05598)
*Gabriel Woolls,Shahin Jahanbani,Adarsh Pashikanti,Matthew T. Fishman,Joseph Tindall,Michael P. Zaletel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"矩阵乘积信念传播"（MP-BP），一种用于二维张量网络和图模型近似收缩的可控方法，相较于现有方法可产生平方级的误差缩减（在相似计算代价下，有效数字数量渐近加倍），并涵盖了Baxter角转移矩阵方法、边界矩阵乘积态、角转移矩阵重整化群以及信念传播等现有方法作为特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维张量网络和图模型的精确收缩是计算上困难的问题，现有近似方法（如信念传播）误差较大且收敛质量有限。作者旨在开发一种可控、可系统改进的近似收缩方法，能够显著提升精度（平方级误差降低），同时保持与现有主流算法（如CTMRG、边界MPS等）的联系和兼容性。

Method: MP-BP将信念传播推广为"消息"生活在二维表面上，并假设消息具有矩阵乘积分解（matrix-product factorization）。在无穷系统极限下，该方法等价于Baxter角转移矩阵方法的实用实现；对于反射对称网络，它与传统实现的边界MPS和CTMRG算法一致。在矩阵乘积秩为单位时，MP-BP退化为标准信念传播。方法进一步扩展用于期望值的计算，实现类似的平方级精度提升。

Result: 在相近的计算代价下，MP-BP相对于现有方法实现误差的二次方缩减，即渐近地使有效数字数量翻倍。作者在多种经典和量子二维张量网络问题上数值验证了方法明显改善的收敛性质，并展示了更高矩阵乘积秩带来的系统精度提升。

Conclusion: MP-BP提供了一条统一且可控的路径，将信念传播（秩1极限）、Baxter角转移矩阵方法、边界MPS和CTMRG（作为特定实现情形）连接在一个统一的框架内，通过增加矩阵乘积秩可系统控制近似精度，实现平方级的误差降低，为二维张量网络和图模型的高精度近似收缩提供了统一的实用方案。

Abstract: We introduce "matrix product belief propagation" (MP-BP) as a controlled method for the approximate contraction of two-dimensional tensor networks and graphical models, which generically leads to a quadratic reduction of errors relative to existing methods: at similar computational effort, the number of accurate digits is asymptotically doubled. For infinite systems, MP-BP can be understood as a practical implementation of Baxter's corner-transfer-matrix method; for reflection-symmetric networks, it further coincides with the boundary matrix-product-state and corner-transfer-matrix renormalization group algorithms as conventionally implemented. In the limit of unit matrix-product rank, MP-BP is equivalent to belief propagation, and more generally, can be understood as a generalization of BP in which the "messages" live on surfaces and are assumed to have a matrix-product factorization. We further extend the quadratic improvement to the computation of expectation values, and numerically demonstrate the qualitatively improved convergence for a variety of classical and quantum 2D tensor network problems.

</details>


### [333] [Can a Local Quantum-Mechanical Description of Physical Reality Be Considered Complete?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05478)
*J. W. Moffat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出，若要从可观测原则和操作性定义出发构建完整的量子场论，最自然的方式是基于非局域理论。在该诠释下，恢复量子力学的一粒子非相对论极限也应当是非局域的，并据此推导出非局域薛定谔方程。同时还证明了正则位置-动量对易关系和海森堡不确定性原理可以从平移协变性中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者回到哥本哈根学派的原始理念（海森堡、玻尔等人），强调一个完整的量子理论应基于可观测和操作可定义的内容来构建。他们认为量子场论的构建也应当遵循这一哲学，从而自然引出非局域性。

Method: 采用非局域可观测原理作为构建量子场论的基础，在此诠释框架下求取一粒子非相对论极限，推导出非局域薛定谔方程；并通过平移协变性证明正则对易关系和海森堡不确定性原理的自然涌现。

Result: 推导出了非局域薛定谔方程，并证明位置-动量对易子及海森堡不确定性原理可由平移协变性作为必然结果涌现，而非作为额外的公设引入。

Conclusion: 该结果支持了量子场论应基于非局域可观测原理构建的观点，且量子力学的非相对论极限本质上也应当是非局域的；位置-动量不确定性关系是平移协变性的自然推论。

Abstract: In a complete quantum theory, one would construct it based on what we can observe in a system and operationally define in our universe. This philosophy goes back to some of the original ideals of quantum theory defined by W. Heisenberg, N. Bohr, and others as part of the Copenhagen school of thought. We argue in this letter that that if one wishes to construct a complete quantum field theory, that it is most natural to construct it based on a nonlocal theory of observable principles. If this interpretation is valid, then the one-particle nonrelativistic limit that recovers quantum mechanics would as well be nonlocal. In this interpretation we derive the nonlocal Schrödinger equation. We will as well demonstrate that the canonical position--momentum commutator and the Heisenberg uncertainty principle emerge from translation covariance.

</details>


### [334] [Hawking Radiation in non-Hermitian Microscopic Analogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05632)
*Diego F. Munoz-Arboleda,Cristiane Morais Smith,Marcus Stålhammar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个微观开放量子系统——耦合马尔可夫库的费米子紧束缚链，在有效非厄米极限下模拟黑洞物理。Lindbladian的rapidity谱形成倾斜例外锥，定义了Painlevé-Gullstrand几何并分离出黑洞/白洞扇区；数值研究揭示稳态密度和流在视界处保留特征，频率分辨散射和非局域密度-密度关联等显示出模拟Hawking辐射信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用非厄米系统作为实现黑洞物理的便捷平台，希望从微观开放量子动力学出发，建立开放系统动力学、涌现非厄米几何与费米多体Hawking辐射探针之间的系统性联系。

Method: 构建费米子紧束缚链耦合Markovian库的二次Lindbladian；取有效非厄米极限得到具有非互易次近邻跳变的增益-损耗晶格模型；利用rapidity谱分析倾斜例外锥结构和有效几何；引入费米子Gaussian Nambu扩展，通过频率分辨散射、非局域密度-密度关联和反常Hawking伴子振幅构造Hawking对见证。

Result: rapidity谱形成倾斜例外锥，定义有效Painlevé-Gullstrand几何并分出黑洞/白洞扇区；稳态粒子密度和流在视界位置保留清晰特征；双正交流识别出射外区和内区通道；相关函数展现模拟Hawking辐射特征，频率分辨Hawking伴子关联满足费米子协方差正性约束。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了微观开放系统动力学、涌现非厄米几何与费米多体Hawking辐射探针之间的联系，为在量子模拟平台上实现和探测类比Hawking辐射提供了微观理论基础。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian systems have recently emerged as a convenient platform to realize black-hole physics. Here, we propose a microscopic open quantum system, namely a fermionic tight-binding chain coupled to Markovian reservoirs, which emulates black-hole properties. The resulting quadratic Lindbladian admits an effective non-Hermitian limit that produces a gain and loss lattice model with a non-reciprocal next-nearest-neighbor hopping. The rapidity spectrum of the full Lindbladian forms tilted exceptional cones that define an effective Painlevé-Gullstrand geometry and separates into black-hole and white-hole sectors, while steady-state particle densities and currents retain clear signatures at the horizon positions. We then analyze the effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian that arise from the microscopic quantum system. The biorthogonal flux identifies the outgoing exterior and interior channels. Finally, we introduce a fermionic Gaussian Nambu extension and formulate Hawking-pair witnesses through frequency-resolved scattering, nonlocal density-density correlations, and anomalous Hawking-partner amplitudes. The resulting correlations display analogue Hawking radiation signatures, while the frequency-resolved Hawking-partner correlations satisfy the fermionic covariance-positivity constraint. These results establish a connection between microscopic open-system dynamics, emergent non-Hermitian geometry, and fermionic many-body probes of analogue Hawking radiation.

</details>


### [335] [The I3322 quantum value is attained spatially but not in finite dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05555)
*Seth Douglas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子关联集合C_q(3,3;2,2)不是闭集——I_3322 Bell泛函的量子上确界S（约0.250875387）无法被任何有限维量子策略达到，但可被ℓ²(Z)⊗ℓ²(Z)上的空间策略达到。同时确定了维度复杂度D(ε)=Θ(log(1/ε))，核心证明经Lean 4机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Pal和Vertesi (2010)提出的猜想——I_3322 Bell泛函的量子上确界是否可由有限维策略达到——以及Dykema、Paulsen和Prakash提出的可得性（attainment）问题。此前只知道某些非闭合性结果，但尚未确定最小的两输出双部分场景中非闭合性的存在。

Method: 研究采用多重数学工具:（1）非达到性通过凹临界Bellman存储、精确有理端点排除证书、反射粘接和凸包定理证明——有限维迫使精确最大化的两个等号输运重合，其值上限为1/4 < S;（2）达到性通过将换位算子最大元的光谱测度在其两个响应输运的轨道上分解，得到具有继承可归一化性的ℓ² Jacobi特征向量;（3）维度复杂性通过构造性上界（D(ε)≤23.9010650·log(1/ε)）和证书链下界确定;（4）机器验证使用Lean 4。

Result: （i）证明了没有任何有限维量子策略（任意有限局部维度、纯态或混合态、投影或POVM测量）能达到S，证实了Pal和Vertesi的猜想;（ii）S在ℓ²(Z)⊗ℓ²(Z)上的空间策略中达到。由此得出C_q(3,3;2,2)不是闭集——这是按输入数量计最小的已知非闭合两输出双部分场景——且C_qs(3,3;2,2)\C_q(3,3;2,2)非空。此外确定维度复杂度D(ε)=Θ(log(1/ε))。S的认证窗口为(0.2508753845015185, 0.250875388108398]。

Conclusion: I_3322场景中量子关联集合的非闭合性已获严格证明，填补了Pal-Vertesi猜想和Dykema-Paulsen-Prakash可得性问题的空白。维度上界与下界匹配至常数因子，且关键证明由Lean 4机器检查保证形式正确性，代表了量子信息论中无穷维可达性与计算辅助证明交叉领域的重要进展。

Abstract: Let $S$ be the quantum supremum of the $I_{3322}$ Bell functional in the Collins-Gisin normalization (classical bound 0; two-qubit maximum exactly 1/4). From a certified window $S\in(0.2508753845015185,0.250875388108398]$ (independently and more tightly enclosed by Mghirbi's prior certificates) and a certified equality of the tensor-product and commuting-operator suprema, we prove: (i) no finite-dimensional quantum strategy attains $S$ -- any finite local dimensions, pure or mixed states, projective or POVM measurements -- proving the conjecture of Pal and Vertesi (2010); (ii) $S$ is attained by a spatial strategy on $\ell^2(\mathbb{Z})\otimes\ell^2(\mathbb{Z})$, the infinite-dimensional attainment those authors asserted, on an independent route. So $C_q(3,3;2,2)$ is not closed -- the smallest two-outcome bipartite scenario by input count where nonclosure is known -- and $C_{qs}(3,3;2,2)\setminus C_q(3,3;2,2)$ is nonempty, settling the attainment question raised by Dykema, Paulsen and Prakash. Nonattainment is proved via a concave critical Bellman storage, exact rational endpoint-exclusion certificates, reflection-gluing and a convex-envelope theorem: finiteness forces an exact maximizer's two equality transports to coincide, capping its value at $1/4<S$. Attainment is proved by disintegrating a commuting maximizer's spectral measure over the orbits of its two response transports, yielding an $\ell^2$ Jacobi eigenvector with inherited normalizability. We also determine the dimension complexity: with $S_d$ the optimum at local dimension $\le d$ and $D(ε)=\min\{d:S-S_d\leε\}$, $D(ε)=Θ(\log(1/ε))$ -- the upper half constructively, with $D(ε)\le 23.9010650\log(1/ε)$; the lower half in a certificate chain in the accompanying repository. Cores of both halves are machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [336] [Boundary-Cancelled Score-Hamiltonian Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05904)
*Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从超对称薛定谔算符对的角度证明，扩散采样可视为概率密度的虚时退火过程。作者发现，固定加噪漂移会强制决定反向分数项，且该对数力正是超对称Score哈密顿量的一侧虚时反绝热连接。分数学习误差由此转化为哈密顿扰动，并推导出调度原则：当前r个终端导数消失时，Morita–Nishimori边界消去可将残差Hellinger误差从T^{-2}抑制到T^{-2r-2}，直至分数或采样下限。二维学习分数测试等反向SDE基准实验验证了预测的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散采样的采样质量与时间调度密切相关，但现有理论缺乏从物理学视角统一解释分数学习与采样误差内部机制的工具。作者希望建立扩散采样与超对称量子力学之间的严格对应，从而为调度设计提供原则性指导。

Method: 构造前向/反向欧几里得薛定谔算符对，证明固定加噪漂移时反向分数项被唯一决定；识别对数力为超对称Score哈密顿量的一侧虚时反绝热连接，据此将分数学习误差重铸为哈密顿微扰，并通过Morita–Nishimori边界消去分析导出调度原则与误差阶数估计。

Result: 在满足前r个终端导数消失的调度条件下，残差Hellinger误差从标准T^{-2}量级提升至T^{-2r-2}量级；多个反向SDE基准（含二维学习分数测试）的实验结果与理论预测的误差衰减改进一致。

Conclusion: 本文建立了扩散采样与（超对称）薛定谔虚时动力学的严格联系，为分数学习中的调度设计提供了基于边界消去的新理论原理，显著提升高采样末端时间的收敛精度。

Abstract: Diffusion sampling can be viewed as imaginary-time annealing of probability densities. From a forward/backward Euclidean Schrödinger pair, we show that fixing the noising drift forces the reverse score term, and that the same logarithmic force is the one-sided imaginary-time counterdiabatic connection of a supersymmetric Score Hamiltonian. The correspondence turns score-learning error into a Hamiltonian perturbation and yields a schedule principle: if the first $r$ terminal derivatives vanish, Morita--Nishimori boundary cancellation suppresses the residual Hellinger error from $T^{-2}$ to $T^{-2r-2}$ until score or sampling floors are reached. Reverse-SDE benchmarks, including two-dimensional learned-score tests, confirm the predicted improvement.

</details>


### [337] [Unraveling-Dependent Metastability in Monitored Quantum Systems and Associative Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06056)
*Manali Malakar,Roberta Zambrini,Gian Luca Giorgi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，开放量子系统中的亚稳定性通常从Liouvillian谱分离推断（刻画无条件系综平均动力学），但这种诊断在单量子轨迹层面是不完整的。条件化的实现可以绕过、瞬时访问或操作性地保持亚稳态记忆，具体取决于监测通道和观测记录。作者在实现量子联想记忆的驱动耗散非线性振荡器中展示这些机制，并通过非厄米后选择动力学和随机跳跃轨迹对比，揭示了轨迹级亚稳定性需考虑受监测通道、测量记录与亚稳态流形三者之间的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，开放量子系统亚稳定性仅通过Liouvillian谱分离来判断，这只适用于系综平均的无条件动力学；但真实实验中常对系统进行连续监测并在单轨迹层面操作，谱分离无法预测条件化量子轨道的亚稳态行为。为填补这一理论与实验间的差距，作者希望理解亚稳定性在轨迹层面的实际表现及其与测量协议的关系。

Method: 以驱动-耗散非线性振荡器（实现量子联想记忆）为平台，通过：(1)比较Liouvillian谱、目标保真度和相空间分布来诊断无条件与条件化动力学；(2)分析在无探测事件后选择条件下获得的非厄米动力学，发现归一化会选择非厄米谱中最慢衰减的模而非所寻址的记忆分支；(3)分析随机跳跃轨迹，研究后测量更新与相干记忆结构兼容时能否长时间保持检索。

Result: 发现并非所有条件化实现都能复制无条件动力学的亚稳态行为：无探测事件的后选择产生非厄米动力学，支持亚稳态检索但长期命运不同——归一化选择最慢衰减模式而非目标记忆分支；而随机跳跃轨迹在后测量更新与相干记忆结构兼容的情况下，可持续延长检索时间。

Conclusion: 轨迹级亚稳定性不能仅由平均生成元（Liouvillian）决定，它取决于被监测通道、测量记录与亚稳态流形之间的兼容性。因此，设计基于轨迹的量子信息协议时，必须超越谱分析，同时考虑具体监测方案和条件化动力学。

Abstract: Metastability in open quantum systems is usually inferred from spectral separation in the Liouvillian, which governs unconditional, ensemble-averaged dynamics. We show that this diagnosis is incomplete at the level of individual quantum trajectories: conditioned realizations of the same unconditional dynamics can bypass, transiently access, or operationally preserve a metastable memory, depending on the monitored channel and the observed record. We demonstrate these mechanisms in a driven-dissipative nonlinear oscillator realizing a quantum associative memory, by comparing spectra, target fidelities, and phase-space distributions. The non-Hermitian dynamics obtained by post-selecting on the absence of detected events supports metastable retrieval, but with a distinct long-time fate: normalization selects the least-decaying mode of the non-Hermitian spectrum rather than the addressed memory branch. In contrast, stochastic jump trajectories can preserve retrieval over extended times when the post-measurement update remains compatible with the coherent memory structure. Thus trajectory-level metastability is not determined by the averaged generator alone; it requires compatibility between the monitored channel, the measurement record, and the metastable manifold.

</details>


### [338] [Computing 256-bit elliptic curve discrete logarithms in 26 days on a fault-tolerant trapped-ion quantum computer with 20,000 qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05625)
*Thomas Häner,Felix Tripier,Jacob Young,Michael Naehrig,Andrii Maksymov,Safwan Alam,Dmitri Maslov,Matthew Parrott,Yvette de Sereville,Jordan Sullivan,Mark Webster,Nicolas Delfosse,John Gamble,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Walk Cat拓扑离子阱量子计算架构，通过面向secp256k1椭圆曲线离散对数问题（ECDLP）的专门优化，结合Shor算法实现了该问题的可解性评估。优化后的逻辑量子电路使用约1450个量子比特和40×10^6个Toffoli门；通过快速CCZ幻态工厂、深度一CCZ态注入、CliNR协议加速及更高效的损耗纠正协议等多项技术，最终估算出使用19,397个物理量子比特可在约25.7天内以63%成功率求解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Walk Cat架构的一大优势是可针对具体应用直接扩展和优化。作者以此作为概念验证，针对区块链技术（如比特币）所依赖的secp256k1椭圆曲线上的256位ECDLP问题，探索该架构是否能在实用的资源和时间约束下破解该密码学难题。

Method: 作者从Schrottenloher的工作出发进行电路优化，给出逻辑级ECDLP电路（约1450个量子比特、40×10^6个Toffoli门），并严格下界保证逻辑级成功概率≥1−2^−128。关键技术包括：快速CCZ幻态工厂和深度一的CCZ态注入（将CCZ门执行时间缩短31倍）；利用非重叠的基于cat态的并行测量提升逻辑测量并行度；采用CliNR协议加速Clifford操作；改进损耗纠正协议减少量子比特开销；设计允许回收CliNR辅助量子比特的布局；根据电路峰值测量并行度配置可复用cat态资源。

Result: 综合所有优化，估算在Walk Cat架构的离子阱量子计算机上，使用约19,397个物理量子比特，可在约25.7天内以约63%的成功概率求解secp256k1上的ECDLP。

Conclusion: Walk Cat架构经过面向特定应用的优化后，展现出在实用时间尺度上破解secp256k1等区块链椭圆曲线密码的实际可行性，为该架构在密码分析等实际量子计算任务中的应用提供了有力证据。

Abstract: One of the strengths of our recently proposed Walking Cat Architecture for a trapped-ion quantum computer is that it is straightforward to extend and optimize for a specific application. As a proof-of-concept, here we present such optimizations for solving the $256$-bit elliptic curve discrete logarithm problem (ECDLP) on $\mathtt{secp256k1}$, which is the elliptic curve used by blockchain technologies such as Bitcoin, using Shor's algorithm. We optimize the circuits from Schrottenloher's recent work and arrive at a logical quantum circuit for solving the ECDLP using about $1450$ qubits and $40\cdot 10^6$ Toffoli gates, with a rigorous lower bound on the logical-level success probability that holds with confidence at least $1-2^{-128}$. Using our compilation toolchain with manual optimization of the logical layout and integrated routing, we produce estimates for the logical measurement depth and the required number of physical qubits by compiling all components to measurement schedules that obey the architectural constraints. A key ingredient is a fast CCZ magic-state factory and a depth-one CCZ state injection, reducing the execution time of CCZ gates by a factor of $31$. We increase the logical-measurement parallelism using non-overlapping cat-based measurements in parallel, and we leverage the recently proposed logical CliNR protocol to speed up Clifford operations. To reduce the qubit overhead, we introduce a more efficient loss correction protocol, design a layout that allows us to recycle the CliNR ancilla qubits, and provision reusable cat-state resources according to the circuit's peak measurement parallelism. All results and optimizations combined, we conclude that a trapped-ion quantum computer based on our architecture can solve the ECDLP on $\mathtt{secp256k1}$ in approximately 25.7 days using 19,397 physical qubits with an estimated success probability of $63\%$.

</details>


### [339] [Wigner Time: a data-oriented approach to experimental timeline creation for quantum science and technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06230)
*T. W. Clark,B. Sárközi,A. Dombi,Á. Kurkó,D. Nagy,A. Simon,D. Varga,P. Domokos,A. Vukics*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 介绍Wigner Time，一个用于原子、分子和光学物理实验的Python包，它将实验程序表示为数据（定时更新的表格），通过函数组合来构建，并引用实验中的命名时间点而非绝对时刻。硬件仅在最终转换步骤介入，使描述可读且与后端无关。该包已在两个冷原子实验中运行超过两年，将使手写实时程序的时间分辨率提升了五倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原子、分子和光学物理实验的精确多设备控制依赖专用实时系统，其程序基于全局时钟和绝对时间描述，导致不同阶段之间紧密耦合——修改一个阶段会影响所有后续阶段，使得程序难以重用、检查和迁移。作者希望找到一种更灵活、可组合的表达方式。

Method: 作者提出Wigner Time Python包，将实验过程表示为一组持续更新的数据表（timed updates），通过函数组合进行组装，引用实验中具名的时间点（named points）而非绝对时刻来指定时序关系。硬件驱动的细节仅在最终转换步骤中引入，从而保持描述的高层抽象和可移植性。

Result: Wigner Time已在两个冷原子实验装置上持续运行超过两年。相比被替代的手写实时程序，该方案将可实现的实验时间分辨率提升了五倍。

Conclusion: Wigner Time证明了将实验程序视为数据（基于具名时间点的组合式描述）可以显著提高时间分辨率、可读性和可复用性，且该方法适用于任何需要在多个设备间进行精确时序控制的领域。

Abstract: Precisely timed, multi-device control in atomic, molecular and optical physics is provided by specialized real-time systems, which impose their own terms on the description of the experiment: a program over a global clock, in which every stage boundary is an absolute time computed from everything preceding it. Such descriptions are tightly coupled -- changing one stage affects all later ones -- and are correspondingly hard to reuse, inspect, or move elsewhere. We introduce Wigner Time, a Python package in which the experimental procedure is instead represented as data: a table of timed updates, assembled by composing functions and referred to named points in the experiment rather than to absolute instants. Hardware enters only at a final conversion step, leaving the description readable and back-end agnostic. Wigner Time has run two cold-atom setups for more than two years, where replacing a hand-written real-time program improved the achievable temporal resolution five-fold; the design applies to any domain requiring precise multi-device timing.

</details>


### [340] [Effects of Trotter Error, Digitization Error, and Initial State Overlap on Tapered Quantum Phase Estimation for Minimum Eigenvalue Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06249)
*Elijah Pelofske,Stephan Eidenbenz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值仿真实现了基于最优锥形量子相位估计（tQPE）的完整量子电路，用于最小特征值计算。作者在4量子比特的海森堡量子磁体模型哈密顿量上，使用最高12阶Trotter化、最多l=10个相位量子比特和m=3个附加误差抑制量子比特进行了数值实验，系统研究了Trotter误差、有限采样、非理想初态、总演化时间和数字化误差等参数对tQPE性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相位估计（QPE）是许多量子算法（如量子化学、量子模拟）的核心子程序，其用于求最小特征值。然而标准QPE在实际实现中存在误差来源复杂、参数优化不明确等问题。作者希望系统评估最优锥形QPE（tQPE）在实际可实现的条件下（使用Trotter化实现哈密顿演化），其性能如何随算法参数变化，从而为实际应用提供指导。

Method: 作者数值实现了完整的最优锥形QPE量子电路，其中：1）哈密顿时间演化酉算符使用标准Trotter化实现（最高12阶）；2）锥形态通过优化的带限DPSS锥（近似带限带宽）构造；3）在4量子比特海森堡量子磁体模型上进行精确的经典数值电路模拟；4）系统改变Trotter误差、有限采样、初态重叠度、总演化时间和相位量子比特数等参数，评估最优相位采样率、绝对误差和Shannon熵等指标。

Result: 数值结果表明：i)稳态最优相位采样率由初态与基态的重叠度决定；ii)在相位采样概率和特征值采样误差率方面，tQPE对演化时间不表现强烈依赖性，除非Trotter误差很高；iii)与标准QPE类似，tQPE中的近似时间演化酉算符可能导致显著的振铃效应（ringing），从而产生非物理的特征值估计。

Conclusion: 锥形QPE虽然是一种有前景的最小特征值计算方案，但其在实际Trotter化实现下存在与非理想时间演化相关的振铃伪影问题。作者指出在应用中需要仔细选择参数以确保物理上可靠的特征值估计，且演化时间对tQPE性能的影响有限（除非Trotter误差显著）。

Abstract: We numerically implement full quantum circuits of optimal tapered Quantum Phase Estimation (in the form of an implementable approximate bandwidth-limited DPSS taper) for the purpose of minimum eigenvalue computation, where the Hamiltonian time-evolution unitary is implemented using standard Trotterization, and the taper state is constructed using an optimized bandwidth-limited taper. We illustrate, on representative quantum Hamiltonians, with tractable exact classical numerical quantum circuit simulations, how tapered QPE performs with respect to optimal phase sampling rate, absolute error, Shannon entropy of the optimal phase distribution, when the tQPE algorithm parameters are changed. Those algorithm parameters are Trotter error, finite sampling, imperfect initial states, total evolution time, and digitization error (number of phase qubits). The reported numerical experiments include up to $\ell=10$ phase qubits of precision with $m=3$ additional phase-register error suppression qubits, on a $4$-qubit Heisenberg quantum magnet model Hamiltonian, using up to 12th-order Trotterization. We numerically show i) the steady-state optimal phase sampling rate is determined by the initial state overlap with the ground-state, ii) with respect to phase sampling probability, or overall eigenvalue sampling error rate, there is no strong evolution time dependence for tQPE unless there is high Trotter error, iii) the approximated time-evolution unitaries in tQPE, like in standard QPE, can result in substantial ringing, which leads to non-physical eigenvalue estimates.

</details>


### [341] [$N$-dimensional discrete Fourier transform via bosonic Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05644)
*Edgar Barriga,Camila Muñoz,Alejandro Muñoz,Santiago Rojas-Rojas,Pablo Solano,Carla Hermann-Avigliano,Aldo Delgado*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用玻色子哈密顿量在多模波导中通过单级演化实现N维离散傅里叶变换（DFT），仅需在相互作用区域前后配置移相器，采用3D波导几何结构。通过图建模解析和数值求解了完整图与非完整图的耦合配置，并提出灵敏度判据筛选对制造误差稳健的波导布局。实现N维DFT的标度律为O(N log log N)，组装2520维DFT仅需2625个干涉仪，远少于Reck和Clements架构所需的约3×10^6个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光量子芯片中实现N维DFT依赖级联的马赫-曾德尔干涉仪（MZI）架构（Reck/Clements），干涉仪数量随N²增长，面积与损耗巨大。作者希望找到一种更紧凑、可扩展到更大维度DFT的架构方案。

Method: 将波导系统建模为图（边=耦合，顶点=波导），构造玻色子哈密顿量使单阶段的 multimode 演化直接实现DFT。对完整图（complete graphs）在N≤6给出解析解、N≤31给出数值解；对非完整图需不同传播常数，报告N≤8全部解、N=9部分探索和N=10的选定案例，并给出三条猜想指导N≥11的数值搜索。提出闭式灵敏度判据筛选可制造且对误差鲁棒的波导布局，并推导了实现N维DFT的渐近标度律。

Result: 给出了完整图和非完整图下的DFT哈密顿量解决方案，涵盖了N≤8、N=9和N=10的部分案例；克服了波导间距导致消逝耦合强度趋零的限制；发现了6维DFT缺失的非仿射参数；推导出标度律O(N log log N)，使2520维DFT仅需2625个干涉仪即可实现，相比Reck/Clements架构降低了三个数量级的器件数量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种单阶段多模演化实现DFT的新范式，通过图理论的系统探索和灵敏度筛选，实现了器件数量的大幅缩减，为大规模光量子信息处理提供了更紧凑、更可扩展的片上DFT实现方案。

Abstract: The discrete Fourier transform (DFT) underpins many classical algorithms and is a fundamental unitary operator for quantum information processing. Implementing the $N$-dimensional DFT in photonic integrated circuits (PICs) is limited by the cascades of Mach-Zehnder interferometers that current architectures require. Here we propose bosonic Hamiltonians that realize the $N$-dimensional DFT through a single stage of multimode evolution, complemented only by phase shifters before and after the interaction region, in a geometry suited to 3D waveguides. Modeling the system as a graph, where edges correspond to couplings and the vertices are the waveguides, we obtain analytical solutions for complete graphs up to $N=6$ and numerical solutions up to $N=31$. For non-complete graphs, different propagation constants are required in the Hamiltonian. We report all solutions for $N\leq 8$, partial exploration for $N=9$, and selected cases for $N=10$, together with three conjectures that guide the numerical search for $N \geq 11$. These configurations circumvent the vanishing evanescent coupling strength imposed by the waveguide separation, and a closed-form sensitivity criterion selects those that are admissible as a waveguide layout and least sensitive to fabrication error. We also uncover the missing non-affine parameters of the $6$-dimensional DFT, and show that the scaling law for implementing the $N$-dimensional DFT with our building blocks is $\mathcal{O}(N\log\log{N})$. This allows assembling the $2520$-dimensional DFT with only $2625$ interferometers, in contrast to the $\approx 3\times 10^6$ of Reck and Clements architectures.

</details>


### [342] [Algorithmic Randomness and Physical Typicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06268)
*Jeffrey A. Barrett,Eddy Keming Chen,Josiah Lopez-Wild*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用算法随机性理论（特别是Martin-Löf随机性）来精确刻画物理学中"典型性"的概念，并以Bohmian力学中的分布公设为例，展示了将典型物理状态定义为相对于可计算测度的Martin-Löf随机状态，可以将分布公设表述为一种统计约束定律，并通过玩具模型证明该约束能保证标准Born统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学中经常诉诸"典型性"（typicality）的概念，但"相对于某概率测度是典型的物理状态"这一表述的含义往往不清晰，进而使得诉诸典型性的物理定律（如统计定律）的断言内容含糊。作者希望借助算法随机性理论为物理典型性提供精确的数学刻画。

Method: 作者将算法随机性理论中的Martin-Löf随机性概念引入物理语境：一个物理状态相对于某个可计算测度是典型的，当且仅当它是Martin-Löf随机的。以此为基础，他们提出"算法Bohmian力学"（aBM），将Bohmian力学中的分布公设重新表述为一条统计约束定律，并用一个玩具模型论证该约束在可计算实验协议下能保证标准Born统计成立。

Result: 通过将典型性定义为相对于可计算测度的Martin-Löf随机性，作者成功地将Bohmian力学中的分布公设形式化为一条具有精确内容的统计约束定律；在玩具模型中，该约束保证了对任意可计算实验协议均能产生标准Born统计结果。

Conclusion: 算法Bohmian力学（aBM）表明，算法随机性理论可以为物理学中的统计定律提供精确的内容与哲学上清晰的基础，展示了算法随机性作为刻画物理典型性的有力工具。

Abstract: Appeals to typicality are common in physics, but it is often unclear what it means for a physical state to be typical relative to a probability measure, and correspondingly unclear what a law that appeals to typicality asserts. Here we consider how one might characterize physical typicality using ideas from the theory of algorithmic randomness. As a concrete example, we show how taking a physical state to be typical relative to a computable measure when it is Martin-Löf random allows one to formulate the distribution postulate in Bohmian mechanics as a statistical constraining law of the theory. Using a toy model, we show how this constraint guarantees the standard Born statistics for computable experimental protocols. Algorithmic Bohmian mechanics (aBM) thus illustrates how algorithmic randomness may be used to provide precise content to a statistical law.

</details>


### [343] [Ordered-Angle Coding for Exact Multiuser Unanimity Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05645)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"有序角编码"方案，用于串行量子架构中n个仅做变换的用户进行二元一致性（unanimity）测试。作者证明，在二元Bell读出下对所有一致输入均确定性的同轴脉冲必须满足α_j=q_jπ/(2n)。完全确定性一致脉冲族中，有限完美码字存在当且仅当n为2的幂；对于n=2^r，二元字(1,2,4,...,2^{r-1})精确且脉冲最少，仅需r=log₂n次试验，将O(n²log(1/ε))的最坏情况开销降为O(logn)。论文还分析了存在操作误差时的鲁棒性界，并指出该方案可作为编码多用户关系原语（可选原始会议密钥候选用途），而非可组合安全的会议密钥协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在串行量子架构（源自Loop-Back通信）中，需要高效地测试n个仅做变换的用户是否达成二元一致性。传统的最小角度重复测试方案在最坏情况下需要O(n²log(1/ε))次操作，效率低下。作者希望设计一种精确的编码方案，将测试复杂度降低到对数量级，同时保持对确定性一致结果的完整判定能力。

Method: 引入"有序角编码"（ordered-angle coding）：每个用户B_i在逻辑字上固定一个私有符号s_i=±1，并在第j次试验中应用旋转R(s_iα_j)。当w个用户选择负号时，串行合成得到R[(n-2w)α_j]。作者刻画了所有对两个一致输入（全+和全-）在二元Bell读出下均确定性的同轴脉冲族，证明其必须满足α_j=q_jπ/(2n)。在此基础上分析混合Hamming权重模仿一致签名的概率M_q(w)=∏cos²(πq_jw/n)，并研究完美字的存在性与构造。对n=2^r，构造二元字(1,2,4,...,2^{r-1})实现精确且脉冲最少的验证；对奇素数n，构造平衡统计字实现均匀的混合权重模仿概率2^{-(p-1)}。同时分析了实际操作误差R(s_iα_j+δ_ij)下的鲁棒性界。

Result: 1) 所有对一致输入确定性的同轴脉冲必须满足α_j=q_jπ/(2n)，构成完全确定性一致脉冲族；2) 该族中存在有限完美字当且仅当n为2的幂；3) 对n=2^r，二元字(1,2,4,...,2^{r-1})是精确且脉冲最少的，m_min=r=log₂n，将O(n²log(1/ε))负担降为O(logn)；4) 奇素数n可构造平衡统计字，混合权重模仿概率均匀为2^{-(p-1)}；5) 存在操作误差δ_ij时，理想拒绝位置被提升为sin²Δ_j，其中Δ_j=Σ_iδ_ij，给出显式鲁棒性界。

Conclusion: 该方案以编码多用户关系原语的形式呈现，Bell纠缠对于加性代数并非必需，但在诚实试验中可使旅行者保持局部最大混合，因此可选用作原始会议密钥候选。作者明确不将其定位为可组合安全的会议密钥协议，而是提供一种高效的二元一致性验证原语，在n为2的幂时实现指数级的复杂度改进。

Abstract: We introduce ordered-angle coding for binary-unanimity testing among $n$ transformation-only users in a serial quantum architecture inherited from Loop-Back communication. User $B_i$ fixes one private sign across a logical word and applies $R(s_iα_j)$ in trial $j$. If $w$ users choose the negative sign, serial composition gives $R[(n-2w)α_j]$. We show that every fixed same-axis pulse that is deterministic for both unanimous inputs under the binary Bell readout has $α_j=q_jπ/(2n)$ with integer $q_j$. For a nonadaptive word $\mathbf q=(q_1,\ldots,q_m)$, a mixed Hamming weight imitates the unanimous signature with probability \[ M_{\mathbf q}(w)=\prod_{j=1}^{m}\cos^2\!\left(\frac{πq_jw}{n}\right). \] Within this complete deterministic-unanimity pulse family, a finite perfect word exists if and only if $n$ is a power of two. For $n=2^r$, the dyadic word $(1,2,4,\ldots,2^{r-1})$ is exact and pulse-minimal with $m_{\min}=r=\log_2n$ trials. It replaces the $O(n^2\log(1/\varepsilon))$ worst-case burden of repeated smallest-angle tests by exact $O(\log n)$ verification. Odd primes instead admit balanced statistical words with uniform mixed-weight imitation $2^{-(p-1)}$.
  For actual operations $R(s_iα_j+δ_{ij})$, an ideal rejecting position is lifted to $\sin^2Δ_j$, where $Δ_j=\sum_iδ_{ij}$. This yields explicit robustness bounds with angular and binary-readout errors. Bell entanglement is not required for the additive algebra, but it keeps the traveler locally maximally mixed in every honest trial. We therefore present the result as a coded multiuser relation primitive with optional raw conference-key-candidate use, not as a composably secure conference-key protocol.

</details>


### [344] [Solution-phase fluorination of nanodiamond: near-surface NV$^-$ activation and spin relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06605)
*Szabolcs Czene,Olga Krafcsik,Nikoletta Jegenyés,Dávid Beke,László Péter,Vladimir Verkhovlyuk,Adam Gali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了两种溶液相氟化路线（XeF₂脱羧直接C-F键形成与Balz-Schiemann反应）用于稳定荧光纳米金刚石（FND）中带负电的氮空位中心（NV⁻）。两种方法均实现了高达~90%（平均）甚至接近100%（富氟区域）的NV⁻占比，并保留了数百微秒级的自旋-晶格弛豫时间，但均短于未处理原始材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米金刚石中的NV⁻中心是室温量子比特的重要平台，但其稳定性受表面官能团调控。表面氟化被认为能稳定NV⁻电荷态，但此前缺乏系统性的化学路线对比与定量评估，尤其对于该尺寸纳米金刚石的氟终端研究尚不充分。

Method: 设计了两种溶液相氟化方案：（1）以XeF₂为氟源，通过脱羧反应经自由基机制直接形成C-F键；（2）Balz-Schiemann反应，将表面氨基替换为氟。采用红外光谱、X射线光电子能谱、能量色散X射线、拉曼光谱和光致发光光谱对两种路线进行对比表征，并通过频域弛豫测量评估自旋-晶格弛豫时间。

Result: 两种路线均获得高NV⁻占比，平均约90%，氟富集区域接近100%，为同类尺寸表面终端的纳米金刚石中的最高报道值。频域弛豫测量显示氟化颗粒保持较长自旋-晶格弛豫时间（XeF₂路线733±56 μs，Balz-Schiemann路线712±20 μs），数值为商用HPHT纳米金刚石的数倍，但短于未处理材料的1173±123 μs。

Conclusion: 电荷态稳定性与自旋寿命无法同时改善：氟化激活了近表面NV⁻中心——这些中心最暴露于表面噪声（导致自旋寿命缩短），但同时也是弛豫传感中起主导作用的NV⁻。这揭示了氟化处理对FND性能的权衡效应，为量子传感应用中表面工程的优化提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Nanodiamonds hosting luminescent point defects, known as fluorescent nanodiamonds (FND), are a leading platform for quantum technology. The nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centre is the most intensively studied of these; its negatively charged state (NV$^-$) can serve as a qubit at room temperature, and its stability is governed by surface functional groups. We present two solution-phase fluorination routes for stabilising NV$^-$: direct C-F bond formation by decarboxylation with xenon difluoride via a radical mechanism, and the Balz-Schiemann reaction, which replaces surface amino groups with fluorine. The two routes were compared by infrared, X-ray photoelectron, energy-dispersive X-ray, Raman and photoluminescence spectroscopy. Both gave a high NV$^-$ fraction, up to $\sim$90% on average and approaching 100% in fluorine-rich regions, which to our knowledge is among the highest reported for surface-terminated nanodiamonds of this size and, in particular, for fluorine termination.
