Frequency-domain relaxometry shows that the fluorinated particles retain a long spin-lattice relaxation time, $733\pm56$ and $712\pm20$ $μ$s for the XeF$_2$ and Balz-Schiemann routes, several times the values reported for commercial HPHT nanodiamonds, although shorter than the $1173\pm123$ $μ$s of the as-received material. Charge-state stability and spin lifetime therefore do not improve together: fluorination activates near-surface NV$^-$ centres, which are the most exposed to surface noise but also the ones that dominate relaxometric sensing.

</details>


### [345] [Minimum Cardinalities of Multipartite Unextendible Product Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05657)
*Chenhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了不可扩展乘积基（UPB）最小基数问题的稳定化定理：对于p≥3的非全qubit量子系统，当奇偶性阻碍自然下界f_LB实现时，真正的最小基数恰好为f_LB+1。该结果与早期工作一起，完全解决了所有有限量子系统中UPB的最小基数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UPB是研究量子纠缠和非局域现象的关键工具。寻找最小UPB是一个自然的外延问题，自Alon和Lovász的开创性工作以来已被研究二十余年。Alon和Lovász确定了f_m何时达到下界f_LB，但受阻多体情况（parity阻碍的情形）一直悬而未决。填补这一空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者建立了一个统一的图论框架来证明稳定化定理。核心论证逻辑是：对于每个p≥3的非全qubit系统，当偶数局部维数的个数为正且为偶数，并且至少有一个局部维数大于2时，证明f_m=f_LB+1，即构造性地表明无法达到自然下界，但可以达到下界加1。

Result: 证明了稳定化定理：对于p≥3的非全qubit系统，当奇偶性条件阻止自然下界f_LB达到时，真实的最小UPB基数恰好等于f_LB+1。具体而言，若偶数局部维度的个数为正且为偶数，且至少一个局部维度大于2，则f_m(d_1,...,d_p)=f_LB(d_1,...,d_p)+1。

Conclusion: 该结果与先前工作相结合，彻底解决了所有有限量子系统中UPB的最小基数确定问题，为量子纠缠理论研究提供了一个完整的解答。

Abstract: In quantum information theory, the state space of a multipartite quantum system is modeled by a tensor product. In the tensor-product space $\mathbb C^{d_1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbb C^{d_p}$, a nonzero vector is a \emph{product state} if it can be written as $\lvert \varphi_1\rangle\otimes\cdots\otimes\lvert \varphi_p\rangle$ with $\lvert \varphi_j\rangle\in\mathbb C^{d_j}\setminus\{0\}$. An \emph{unextendible product basis} (UPB) is a finite family of pairwise orthogonal product states such that no nonzero product state is orthogonal to all of them. UPBs play a key role in investigating quantum entanglement and nonlocal phenomena. Finding a smallest UPB is a natural extremal problem: it asks how few pairwise orthogonal product states suffice to prevent any further product state from being added. The general minimum-size problem for UPBs has been studied for over two decades since the seminal work of Alon and Lovász. For local dimensions $d_1,\ldots,d_p\ge2$, let $f_m(d_1,\ldots,d_p)$ be the minimum cardinality of a UPB and let $f_{LB}(d_1,\ldots,d_p)=1+\sum_{j=1}^{p}(d_j-1)$ be the natural lower bound. Alon and Lovász determined exactly when $f_m$ attains the lower bound $f_{LB}$, but the obstructed multipartite cases remained open in general.
  We prove a stabilization theorem: for every non-all-qubit system with $p\ge3$, whenever parity prevents the natural lower bound $f_{LB}$ from being attained, the true minimum is exactly $f_{LB}+1$. Equivalently, if the number of even local dimensions is positive and even, and at least one local dimension is greater than two, then $f_m(d_1,\ldots,d_p)=f_{LB}(d_1,\ldots,d_p)+1$. The proof is built on a unified graph-theoretic framework. Our result, together with earlier work, settles the minimum-cardinality problem for UPBs in all finite quantum systems.

</details>


### [346] [Entanglement Growth as Transport Across Schmidt Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06643)
*Shi-Xin Zhang,Shuo Liu,Yu-Qin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入"Schmidt尺度浓度"和"主导Schmidt尺度"两种新坐标，用于在按Schmidt秩排序的对数窗口中定位概率最大值，从而超越单一熵值揭示量子纠缠增长中的精细结构。应用于随机场自旋链的淬火动力学，可区分弱无序下主导尺度快速向高Schmidt秩输运与强无序下输运被强烈抑制但熵仍对数增长这两种行为，并揭示了一个有序的层级结构：熵产生最先达到峰值，随后是谱粗糙度与精确非局域魔幻度，最后才是主导Schmidt尺度的典型输运。通过可解的头-尾模型与数值实验，证明该层级的物理起源在于谱路径决定事件顺序、活性交换键上的局域动力学设定早期时序、子系统内部多体修饰进一步延迟主导Schmidt尺度输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究仅用单一纠缠熵刻画量子纠缠增长，无法揭示纠缠在指数级大的Schmidt谱中具体分布位置以及形成机制。作者希望提出新的精细坐标，以区分纠缠产生、纠缠谱重组和主导Schmidt尺度输运等不同动态过程，从而超越熵这一单一指标的局限。

Method: 作者提出两个新坐标——Schmidt尺度浓度与主导Schmidt尺度，通过在有序Schmidt秩空间的对数窗口中定位概率最大值来实现。方法应用于随机场自旋链的淬火（quench）动力学，并结合可解的"头-尾"（head-tail）模型以及受控数值实验，分析弱/强无序条件下的纠缠演化层级及其物理起源。

Result: 新坐标能清晰区分弱无序（主导尺度快速输运到高Schmidt秩）与强无序（输运被显著抑制，但熵仍表现为对数增长）两种截然不同的动力学行为。无序平均动力学呈现有序层级：熵产生先达峰值，谱粗糙度和精确非局域魔幻度随后达峰，主导Schmidt尺度输运需显著延迟后才成为典型行为。层级起源由谱路径、活性交换键的局域动力学和子系统内多体修饰共同决定。

Conclusion: Schmidt尺度坐标是强大的动力学探针，能够揭示超越纠缠熵的精细纠缠结构，有效区分纠缠产生、纠缠谱重组与主导Schmidt尺度输运这三类不同的物理过程，为量子纠缠动力学的精细刻画提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement growth is commonly summarized by a single entropy, obscuring where correlations reside in the exponentially large Schmidt spectrum and how they form. Here, we introduce Schmidt-scale concentration and dominant Schmidt scale, two coordinates that locate the probability maximum across logarithmic windows in ordered Schmidt-rank space. Applied to quenches of a random-field spin chain, these coordinates distinguish rapid transport of the dominant scale to higher Schmidt rank at weak disorder from strongly suppressed transport despite continued logarithmic entropy growth at strong disorder. The disorder-averaged dynamics exhibit an ordered hierarchy: entropy production peaks first, spectral roughness and exact nonlocal magic peak next, and dominant-Schmidt-scale transport becomes typical only after a substantial delay. Moreover, a solvable head--tail model and controlled numerical experiments reveal the physical origin of this hierarchy: the spectral path determines the order of events, local dynamics on active exchange bonds set their early timing, and intra-subsystem many-body dressing further delays dominant-Schmidt-scale transport. These results establish the Schmidt-scale coordinates as powerful dynamical probes for uncovering fine-grained entanglement structures distinguishing entanglement production, entanglement-spectrum reorganization, and dominant-Schmidt-scale transport beyond entropy alone.

</details>


### [347] [Robustness of Dynamical Casimir Effect-Induced Quantum Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05659)
*Sebastián Francisquez,Fernando C. Lombardo,Paula I. Villar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在电路量子电动力学架构中，分析了由动力学卡西米尔效应诱导的量子同步的鲁棒性。通过Lindblad主方程方法评估了多种退相干通道的影响，发现纯退相位是限制同步态保真度的主导因素，并指出在强耗散区域皮尔逊相关系数可能高估量子同步程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子同步是量子信息处理中的关键现象，但实际量子系统不可避免受到环境退相干的影响。已有工作侧重于理想条件下DCE诱导的量子同步，缺乏对实验可行性和噪声鲁棒性的系统评估。本文旨在明确真实超导平台上DCE诱导量子同步得以维持的参数区间，为实验提供指导。

Method: 采用Lindblad主方程方法，构建包含两个超导量子比特与共享参数驱动腔的cQED系统模型，系统性地引入光子损耗、弛豫、热激发和纯退相位等多种退相干通道，使用皮尔逊相关系数等度量对同步动力学进行评估，并对比不同耗散强度下的同步保真度。

Result: DCE诱导的量子同步在现有超导平台可实现的耗散率下依然能够持续存在，证实其实验可行性；纯退相位是限制同步态保真度的首要因素；在强耗散区，皮尔逊相关系数会因环境弛豫产生的经典关联而高估真实量子同步程度。

Conclusion: 本文确定了观测真实DCE诱导量子同步所需的参量区间，区分了环境产生的经典赝同步与真正的量子同步，为未来cQED实验设计提供了定量指南与实际参数条件。

Abstract: The robustness of quantum synchronization induced by the dynamical Casimir effect (DCE) is analyzed within a circuit quantum electrodynamics (cQED) architecture consisting of two superconducting qubits coupled to a shared, parametrically driven cavity. Using a Lindblad master equation approach, this study evaluates the impact of various decoherence channels (including photon loss, relaxation, thermal excitation, and pure dephasing) on the resulting synchronization dynamics. The results demonstrate that DCE-induced synchronization persists under dissipation rates achievable in state-of-the-art superconducting platforms, confirming its experimental feasibility. Among the studied mechanisms, pure dephasing is identified as the dominant factor limiting the fidelity of the synchronized state. Furthermore, it is shown that in strongly dissipative regimes, the Pearson correlation coefficient may overestimate the degree of quantum synchronization, as environmental relaxation can generate classical correlations that mimic synchronized behavior. These findings establish the parameter regimes necessary for observing genuine DCE-induced quantum synchronization and provide practical guidelines for future cQED experiments.

</details>


### [348] [Efficient Representation of multicategorical local Hilbert spaces: nonlinear Restricted Boltzmann Machines to Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06672)
*Abhiroop Lahiri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种处理多值自旋（spin>1/2）或多态Potts系统的神经网络量子态（NQS）新编码方案：直接对原始多值自旋变量使用非线性函数，而非传统的one-hot（独热）编码。该方案保留基态的标记自由，参数更少且可训练性更好，并在RBM和KAN两类架构上验证了有效性，成功恢复了量子Potts哈密顿量的临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于MLP的NQS通过one-hot编码表示多态量子系统（如spin-1/2以上的自旋或q态Potts模型），但该编码存在两个问题：参数数量随局域态数线性增长，且优化景观可能阻碍收敛。作者希望寻找一种更自然的分类泛化方案，在保持基态标记自由的同时减少参数并改善可训练性。

Method: 作者提出让logΨ直接成为原始多值自旋变量的非线性函数，取代one-hot编码的线性仿射映射。先用浅层RBM配备多种非线性连接进行基准测试（针对spin-1、2、3的海森堡链），再引入KAN架构——其每条边携带可学习的单变量非线性函数，作为同一原理的更富表达力的实现。

Result: 数值结果表明：该直接非线性编码方案以更少的参数重现了one-hot模型的性能，且具有更好的可训练性。KAN作为该原理的表达形式表现出更强的表达能力。该框架能成功捕获量子Potts哈密顿量的临界行为并恢复其相变。

Conclusion: 直接对原始多值自旋变量施加非线性变换是one-hot编码的一种自然且更高效替代方案，兼具保标记自由、参数紧凑和可训练性优势，为更广泛的多态量子系统的NQS表示提供了实用推广路径。

Abstract: Neural network quantum states (NQS) for representing spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ systems are typically built from Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) with binary visible variables, in which the input spins are first combined through linear affine maps before more expressive nonlinear transformations are applied. The standard way for representing multi-categorical systems, namely spin-1 and spin-2, or the q-state quantum Potts systems with more than two local degrees of freedom, is a unary or one-hot encoded MLP architecture. Following a few similar studies, I observe that while the one-hot construction is the mathematically faithful encoding for systems with more than two local states such as spin-$S$ models with $S > 1/2$ or $q$-state Potts models, its parameter count grows with the number of local states, and the resulting optimization landscape can hinder convergence. I find numerically that allowing $\log Ψ$ to be a nonlinear function of the raw multi-valued spin variable is a natural categorical generalization: it preserves the labelling freedom of the local basis and reproduces the one-hot model with strictly fewer parameters, often with improved trainability. I first benchmark this idea on shallow Restricted Boltzmann Machines (RBMs), equipping them with several distinct nonlinear connections, for spin-1, 2, and 3 Heisenberg chains. I then turn to Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), where each edge carries a learnable univariate nonlinearity, and show that they provide a strictly more expressive realization of the same principle. Finally, I demonstrate that this framework captures the critical behaviour of the quantum Potts Hamiltonian, recovering its phase transition.

</details>


### [349] [Kerr Induced Control of Synchronization and Quantum State Recovery in a Driven van der Pol Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05672)
*Amir Hossein Houshmand Almani,Ali Mortezapour,Alireza Nourmandipour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr非线性如何调制压缩量子van der Pol振子中的量子同步现象。Kerr相互作用产生振幅依赖的频率偏移，驱动鞍结分岔，使经典相空间结构从双稳态转变为单稳态。量子层面上，该转变表现为系统性频率牵引和光谱展宽。在挤压-Kerr参数空间中构建全局同步相图后，发现维持相位锁定所需的关键挤压强度与Kerr非线性之间存在显著的线性依赖。研究还表明同步边界与光子统计的量子特性（超/亚泊松）转变不一致，说明这两者刻画了量子稳态的不同方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于量子van der Pol振子同步的研究主要聚焦于挤压等参数的作用，但对Kerr非线性（在实验平台中普遍存在的非谐性效应）如何改变同步动力学和能级结构缺乏系统理解，需要定量阐明两者耦合下的同步相变机制及设计原则。

Method: 通过解析和数值方法分析含Kerr非线性的压缩量子van der Pol振子：利用半经典相空间理论识别鞍结分岔与双稳-单稳相变；在量子领域计算Wigner函数、频率牵引与光谱以刻画量子稳态；并在挤压-Kerr参数空间构建全局同步相图，同时计算光子数统计以比较同步边界。

Result: Kerr非线性引起振幅依赖的频率移动并驱动鞍结分岔，经典相空间从双稳态转变为单稳态；量子态表现出频率牵引和光谱展宽，但Wigner函数与半经典吸引子保持连续对应。全局相图揭示临界挤压强度与Kerr强度呈近似线性关系；同步边界与超/亚泊松光子统计的转变点并不重合。

Conclusion: 同步与光子数统计分别表征量子稳态的不同独立属性，二者不可互换；通过挤压与Kerr参数可精确调控量子同步，为囚禁离子、超导电路及光机系统等实验平台上的量子同步控制提供了定量设计准则。

Abstract: We investigate how Kerr nonlinearity modifies quantum synchronization in a squeezed quantum van der Pol oscillator. We show that the Kerr interaction produces an amplitude-dependent frequency shift that drives a saddle-node bifurcation, transforming the classical phase-space structure from bistable to monostable dynamics. In the quantum regime, this transition manifests as systematic frequency pulling and spectral broadening, while the steady-state Wigner function reveals a continuous correspondence between the quantum state and the semiclassical attractor despite finite quantum fluctuations. By constructing global synchronization phase diagrams in the squeezing--Kerr parameter space, we uncover a remarkably linear dependence of the critical squeezing strength required to maintain phase locking on the Kerr nonlinearity. We further demonstrate that the synchronization boundary does not coincide with the crossover between super- and sub-Poissonian photon statistics, showing that synchronization and photon-number statistics characterize distinct aspects of the quantum steady state. These results provide quantitative design principles for controlling quantum synchronization through Kerr nonlinearity, with potential relevance to trapped-ion, superconducting-circuit, and optomechanical platforms.

</details>


### [350] [Quantum lower bounds for convex optimization and real matrix-vector query problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05679)
*Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在评估查询和成员查询模型下，量子查询复杂度下界为Ω̃(n)，几乎匹配已知的上界，并延伸到二次优化（矩阵求逆）、矩阵迹、行列式符号及行列式大小等问题的线性或近线性下界。方法基于一种新颖的"行列式见证法"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在填补凸函数最小化问题的量子查询复杂度下界空白，此前仅有上界已知；同时希望为矩阵运算（求逆、迹、行列式）在矩阵-向量查询模型下建立严格的量子复杂度下界。

Method: 提出一种新颖的量子下界技术——"行列式见证法"（determinantal witness method），其核心是识别一个见证函数，该函数的傅里叶变换在低秩矩阵上为零，且与待计算函数具有良好的相关性，从而推导出查询复杂度的下界。

Result: 证明了在R^n上凸子集的凸函数最小化（含评估和成员查询）的量子查询复杂度为Ω̃(n)，几乎匹配已知上界。即使在二次最小化（等价于n×n实矩阵的矩阵-向量查询求逆）情形下也成立。此外，对矩阵的迹、行列式符号和行列式大小分别给出了线性或近线性的量子查询复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 通过行列式见证法，作者（及辅助AI系统）建立了凸优化和矩阵运算在量子查询模型下的基本复杂度下界，表明这些问题的量子算法无法超越近线性的查询复杂度。

Abstract: We (the author and the AI systems that did the heavy lifting) show that the quantum query complexity of minimizing a convex function over a convex subset of $\mathbb{R}^n$ with evaluation and membership queries is $\tildeΩ(n)$, nearly matching the best known upper bound. In particular, we show this even for quadratic minimization, which is equivalent to inverting an $n \times n$ real matrix using matrix-vector queries. We also show linear or nearly linear lower bounds on the quantum query complexity of computing the trace, the sign of the determinant, and the magnitude of the determinant of a real matrix in the matrix-vector query model. We use a novel quantum lower bound technique, the determinantal witness method, based on identifying a witness whose Fourier transform vanishes on low-rank matrices and that correlates well with the function being computed.

</details>


### [351] [Electron beam driven collective self hybridized exciton polaritons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07164)
*Parsa Darman,Maximilian Black,Prabhdeep Singh,Masoud Taleb,Victor DeManuel-Gonzalez,Sara Darbari,Fatemeh Chahshouri,Nahid Talebi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过互补的动量分辨光致发光（PL）和阴极发光（CL）光谱，系统比较了Ruddlesden–Popper钙钛矿薄片中自杂化激子极化激元在光激发和电子束激发下的行为，发现电子束激发呈现显著增强的极化激元能级排斥，归因于快电子对多个激元的集体相干激发，遵循g_N=g₁√N的耦合强度标度（有效N≈23）；降低电子动能则进入部分非相干激发区域，高阶Fabry–Pérot极化激元共振消失，仅剩最低极化激元分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状半导体中的自杂化激子极化激元为纳米尺度光–物质相互作用调控提供了灵活平台，但激子–光子相互作用的本质以及多个激元对同一光子模式集体强耦合与同步耦合的机制仍未被充分探索。

Method: 使用互补的动量分辨光致发光光谱和阴极发光光谱，对Ruddlesden–Popper钙钛矿薄片分别在光激发和电子束激发下的自杂化激子极化激元进行比较研究，并通过调节电子动能研究激发相干性转变。

Result: 阴极发光表现出比光致发光更大的极化激元能级排斥，表明电子束激发下有效耦合强度增强，归因于快电子对多个激元的集体相干激发，有效相干激子振子数可达N≈23；降低电子能量后系统转变为部分非相干激发，高阶极化激元共振消失。

Conclusion: 该工作表明电子束能够主动调控层状半导体中的集体光–物质耦合，并确立阴极发光光谱作为一种可触及超越全光激发方案的新激发区域的途径。

Abstract: Self-hybridized exciton polaritons in layered semiconductors emerge from the hybridization of excitonic resonances with confined photonic modes in films and provide a versatile platform for controlling light--matter interactions at the nanoscale. Yet, the nature of exciton--photon interactions and the collective and synchronous strong coupling of multiple excitons to the same photonic mode remain largely unexplored. Here, we use complementary momentum-resolved photoluminescence and cathodoluminescence spectroscopy to compare self-hybridized exciton polaritons in Ruddlesden--Popper perovskite flakes under optical and electron-beam excitation. We find that cathodoluminescence exhibits remarkably larger polaritonic level repulsion than photoluminescence, revealing an enhanced effective coupling strength under electron-beam excitation. We attribute this enhancement to the collective coherent excitation of multiple excitons by fast electrons, which couple to the same photonic mode and increase the interaction strength according to a $g_N=g_1\sqrt{N}$ scaling, with an effective number of up to $N\approx23$ coherently coupled excitonic oscillators. By reducing the electron kinetic energy, we further uncover a crossover to a partially incoherent excitation regime in which higher-order Fabry--Pérot exciton-polariton resonances disappear, leaving only the lowest polariton branches while coherent transition radiation persists. This transition arises because low-energy electrons excite excitons over extended spatial volumes, washing out the phase coherence required to sustain collective coupling and form polaritonic resonances. Our work shows that electron beams can actively modify collective light--matter coupling in layered semiconductors and establishes cathodoluminescence as a route to access excitation regimes beyond all-optical excitation schemes.

</details>


### [352] [Characterizing Privacy Risks of Quantum Machine Learning with Emergent Quantum-Native Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05702)
*Liou Tang,James Joshi,Ashish Kundu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子原生世界（quantum-native）中QML模型的成员推断攻击，证明增加量子访问和量子计算能力会带来可证明的理论隐私泄露和实际对抗优势，并指出现有QML隐私研究在量子原生访问机制下低估了隐私泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护的QML研究大多基于QML即服务（QML-as-a-service）场景，假设模型所有者只提供经典比特输出，用户/攻击者仅具备经典计算能力。然而在量子原生世界中，量子能力的用户/攻击者可以同时访问量子计算资源和获取量子信息输出，这一假设正面临挑战，因此需要弥合这一研究空白。

Method: 通过对QML模型开展成员推断攻击（Membership Inference Attacks）的系统性分析，对比不同量子访问级别和量子计算能力下的隐私泄露情况，包括理论分析（可证明的隐私泄露界）和实验验证（实际对抗增益），同时考察QML概率特性导致的理论与实际对抗优势之间存在的差距。

Result: 研究表明，增加量子访问能力和量子计算能力会带来可证明的理论隐私泄露提升和实际对抗优势增长；但QML的概率性质导致理论对抗优势与实际对抗优势之间存在差距。

Conclusion: 现有关于QML隐私泄露的研究在量子原生访问机制下低估了实际隐私泄露风险，本文作为探讨量子原生世界中QML潜在隐私泄露的第一步，为该方向建立研究基础。

Abstract: Quantum Machine Learning (QML) has shown rapid advances by utilizing quantum computing for machine learning tasks. Meanwhile, the privacy risks accompanying QML is also starting to be studied, which inherit privacy leakage channels from "classical" ML and also quantum-unique risks. Existing work on privacy-preserving QML largely focuses on a QML-as-a-service scenario, which generally assumes that the QML model owner provides only classical bit outputs to queries, while users (and adversaries) have only classical computing abilities. However, this view is increasingly challenged in a quantum-native world of quantum-capable users/adversaries, which may have access to both quantum computing abilities and access to quantum information output from service providers.
  In this paper, we aim to bridge this gap by examining membership inference attacks against QML models by demonstrating that increasing quantum access and quantum computing abilities provides provable theoretical privacy leakage and empirical adversarial gain. However, the probabilistic nature of QML introduces a gap between theoretical and empirical adversarial advantage. These results show that existing research on privacy leakage on QML models underestimate privacy leakage in an emergent quantum-native access regimes, which we hope to establish a first step into examining potential privacy leakages for QML in the quantum-native world.These results show that existing research on privacy leakage in QML models underestimates privacy leakage in emergent quantum-native access regimes, and we hope to establish a first step in examining potential privacy leakages for QML in the quantum-native world.

</details>


### [353] [Geometry Dependence of Error Thresholds in Two-Dimensional Toric Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07579)
*D. Lessing,A. Langheld,C. Krämer,J. A. Koziol,K. P. Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过replica-exchange蒙特卡洛模拟，研究了二维toric码在不同晶格几何结构（方形、蜂窝、三角、骰子、kagome）下的最优（最大似然）纠错阈值。研究发现对偶晶格对的阈值围绕自对偶方形晶格呈现特征性的"分裂"，且平均配位数是影响阈值的主要因素，但顶点与plaquette的细节排布也显著影响精确阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着近最优解码器的快速发展，确定toric码在最大似然意义下的"终极"错误阈值（code capacity）变得重要。既有研究多为次优解码器（如minimum-weight perfect matching）在不同晶格几何下的阈值，缺乏对最优阈值的系统性研究，因此需要计算各晶格的极限纠错能力。

Method: 将存在比特翻转或相位翻转错误时的toric码映射为二维随机键伊辛模型（RBIM），利用replica-exchange蒙特卡洛模拟确定RBIM的临界行为，并在Nishimori线上计算方形、蜂窝、三角、骰子、kagome五种晶格的最大似然错误阈值；利用晶格对偶性关联相位翻转与比特翻转阈值。

Result: 计算了五种晶格在Nishimori线上的最优错误阈值；发现对偶晶格对的阈值存在围绕自对偶方形晶格的特征性分裂现象，与次优解码器的定性行为一致；平均配位数对阈值影响最大，但晶格中顶点和plaquette的具体排列方式对精确阈值也有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 晶格几何（不仅包括平均配位数，还包括顶点与plaquette的具体拓扑排布）共同决定了toric码的最优纠错阈值。对偶性导致的阈值分裂是不同晶格间纠错能力差异的普遍规律。

Abstract: We investigate how the lattice geometry influences the error thresholds of two-dimensional toric codes. In the presence of bit- or phase-flip errors, the toric code maps onto the two-dimensional random-bond Ising model (RBIM). We determine the critical behaviour of the RBIM using replica-exchange Monte Carlo simulations. While previous studies have explored thresholds for general lattice geometries under suboptimal decoders such as minimum-weight perfect matching, the rapid development of near-optimal decoders makes resolving the ultimate, maximum-likelihood code capacity relevant. We compute these optimal error thresholds on the Nishimori line for the square, honeycomb, triangular, dice, and kagome lattices. Owing to lattice duality, the phase-flip threshold on a given lattice is equivalent to the bit-flip threshold on its dual. We find that the optimal thresholds of dual-lattice pairs display a characteristic duality-driven splitting around the self-dual square-lattice, mirroring the qualitative behaviour observed for suboptimal decoders. While the average coordination number has the strongest impact on the error threshold, our results demonstrate that the detailed arrangement of vertices and plaquettes also plays a significant role in determining its precise value.

</details>


### [354] [Tight Lower Bounds for State Tomography with Limited Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05718)
*Ufuk Keskin,Jason Luo,Mahbod Majid,Matthew Radzihovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在无量子内存且每次测量最多作用于k个新拷贝的条件下，量子态层析成像的拷贝复杂度。作者证明了与上界匹配的下界，得到了拷贝复杂度为max{d^3/(√k·ε^2), d^2/ε^2}（在绝对常数因子内），覆盖所有k和充分小的ε，消除了此前仅适用于小k的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作（arXiv:2402.16353）中的下界证明依赖于微扰展开，导致下界仅在k相对于精度较小时成立。本文旨在移除这一限制，无需微扰展开即可证明适用于所有k的匹配下界。

Method: 核心思想是建立仅依赖于态的最小特征值的单一测量块的均匀Fisher信息上界。该证明避开了此前限制结果的微扰展开。随后利用Fano不等式（针对度量球）和对数Sobolev不等式（比较互信息和Fisher信息），将自适应协议约化到该块级界。

Result: 对于任意d维态、每个测量块最多作用于k个新拷贝且块间无量子内存的场景，估计到迹距离ε所需的拷贝复杂度（在绝对常数因子内）为max{d^3/(√k·ε^2), d^2/ε^2}。该界对任意块内测量和块间自适应选择均成立，并在任何最小特征值约为1/d的态的小邻域内都适用。

Conclusion: 本文补全了量子态层析成像在有限无内存块测量模型下的拷贝复杂度刻画，将此前仅对k较小时成立的下界推广到所有k，实现了与上界的完全匹配。

Abstract: We study state tomography when each measurement acts on at most $k$ fresh copies and no quantum memory is retained between blocks. We prove a lower bound matching the upper bound in [arXiv:2510.07788]. Thus the copy complexity of estimating an arbitrary $d$-dimensional state to trace distance $ε$ is, up to absolute constant factors, $\max\{d^3/(\sqrt{k}ε^2),d^2/ε^2\}$ for every $k$ and all sufficiently small $ε$. This removes the earlier restriction that $k$ be small as a function of the accuracy. The lower bound applies to arbitrary measurements within each block and adaptive choices between blocks. The lower bound already applies in a small neighborhood of any state whose smallest eigenvalue is of order $1/d$, even when the center is known.
  The main ingredient is a uniform Fisher information bound for one measurement block that depends only on the smallest eigenvalue of the state. The proof avoids the perturbative expansion responsible for the restriction in [arXiv:2402.16353]. Fano's inequality for metric balls and a log-Sobolev comparison between mutual and Fisher information then reduce the adaptive protocol to this block bound [arXiv:1607.00550, arXiv:1902.08582].

</details>


### [355] [Topology Obstructs Pure Foundation Neural Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07591)
*Timothy Heightman,Elena Orlova,Philip Mantrov,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于自旋-1/2系统基态的纯态神经网络量子态表示存在拓扑阻塞问题：对于任何具有非平凡基态丛的能隙哈密顿量族，连续归一化的态向量模型必定在某个参数处与真实基态的保真度为零，导致能量误差至少为一个谱隙。文章指出算子值模型可以规范地避免这些障碍并保持拓扑信息，因此是基础神经量子态表示的结构性需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（foundation models）用于自旋-1/2系统基态求解是量子化学和新相图识别等领域的重要方法，但现有模型大多是基于哈密顿量参数条件的纯态表示（pure states），其蒙特卡洛采样依据变分原理给出能量估计。作者想要揭示这种纯态表示在拓扑层面存在根本性缺陷，并探讨更合适的模型表示范式。

Method: 作者从数学上证明了一个no-go定理：对于任何能隙哈密顿量族，如果其基态丛非平凡（non-trivial），那么任何连续的归一化态向量模型在族中某个参数值处与真实基态的保真度为零，该点处能量至少偏离一个谱隙Δ，且在该点邻域内存在O(Δ)能隙。作者将该结果推广到简并基态流形、时间动力学和具有混合时空拓扑的周期系统，并在单量子比特和双量子比特系统上进行了演示。文章还分析了该障碍如何导致保真度磁化率（fidelity susceptibility）出现尖峰，从而在无相变处产生类似相变的数值信号。最后论证算子值（operator-valued）模型可以规范地规避这些拓扑障碍。

Result: 证明了纯态神经网络量子态对具有非平凡拓扑基态丛的哈密顿量族存在不可避免的表示障碍；该障碍在简并基态、时间演化和周期系统等多种场景下仍然存在；拓扑障碍会在保真度磁化率中产生伪相变信号尖峰；算子值模型能够规范性地避免这些障碍并保留拓扑信息。

Conclusion: 纯态表示对于某些具有非平凡拓扑的哈密顿量族在原理上无法精确描述其基态，这暗示基础神经量子态模型应采用算子值表示而非纯态表示，否则会丢失拓扑信息并在无相变处产生伪相变信号。

Abstract: Foundation models for ground states in spin-1/2 systems are a promising method for problems ranging from quantum chemistry to identifying new phase diagrams. Nearly all such models are currently pure-states that condition on the Hamiltonian's parameters, whose Monte Carlo samples give energy estimates according to the variational principle. In this contribution, we show that this representation is topologically obstructed. For any gapped Hamiltonian family whose ground-state bundle is non-trivial, every continuous normalized state-vector model has zero fidelity with the ground state at some parameter value in the Hamiltonian family. For that value, the energy is at least one spectral gap, $Δ$, with an $O(Δ)$ gap in an open-neighbourhood of that point. We show that this is a sufficient no-go also in the case of degenerate ground-state manifolds, time dynamics, and periodic systems with mixed space-time topology, demonstrating these obstructions on one- and two-qubit systems. We discuss how this causes a spike in the fidelity susceptibility, giving a numerical signature of a phase-transition where there is none. We then show that operator-valued models canonically avoid these obstructions and preserve topological information, implying a structural necessity in representation for foundation neural quantum states.

</details>


### [356] [SAQC: A SAT-Aware Compilation Framework for QAOA-Based Quantum Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05737)
*Till Schnittka,Julie Maria Raju,Abhoy Kole,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SAQC是一个SAT感知的量子编译框架，通过引入SAT中间表示(SIR)在编译过程中保留布尔结构信息，支持CNF和XOR扩展CNF(eCNF)公式，并在量子降级前执行子句重写、依赖分析等SAT感知优化，最终实现比传统基于哈密顿量的工作流更低的电路深度、更少的两比特门数和更快的编译时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAT到QAOA工作流首先将布尔公式转换为惩罚哈密顿量，再应用通用量子编译，这会丢失SAT特有的信息（如子句结构、逻辑运算符、变量依赖），导致后续编译优化无法利用底层的布尔公式结构。

Method: 提出SAT中间表示(SIR)以保留编译过程中的布尔结构；支持CNF和eCNF公式；在量子降级前执行SAT感知优化，包括子句重写、依赖分析和子句调度；提供统一的惩罚哈密顿量生成和直接子句到ansatz合成的框架，并基于相对相位分解、辅助比特辅助合成和动态反计算进行ansatz优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统基于哈密顿量的工作流相比，SAQC在电路深度、两比特门数量和编译时间方面均有显著降低，同时与现有量子编译框架保持兼容。

Conclusion: 在量子编译过程中保留SAT领域特定的结构信息，而非过早地统一转换为哈密顿量表示，能够有效提升QAOA求解SAT问题的编译效率和电路质量。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is a promising variational approach for solving Boolean satisfiability (SAT) problems. Existing SAT-to-QAOA workflows first translate Boolean formulas into penalty Hamiltonians before applying generic quantum compilation, thereby discarding SAT-specific information such as clause structure, logical operators, and variable dependencies. Consequently, subsequent compiler optimizations cannot exploit the underlying Boolean formulation. This paper presents SAQC, a SAT-aware compilation framework that introduces a SAT Intermediate Representation (SIR) to preserve Boolean structure during compilation. The proposed framework supports both CNF and XOR-extended CNF (eCNF) formulations and performs SAT-aware optimizations including clause rewriting, dependency analysis, and clause scheduling prior to quantum lowering. Building on the optimized SIR, SAQC provides a unified framework for both penalty Hamiltonian generation and direct clause-to-ansatz synthesis, together with ansatz optimizations based on relative-phase decomposition, ancilla-assisted synthesis, and dynamic uncomputation. Experimental results demonstrate significant reductions in circuit depth, two-qubit gate count, and compilation time compared with conventional Hamiltonian-based workflows while remaining compatible with existing quantum compilation frameworks.

</details>


### [357] [Quantum Query Algorithms for the Constructive Diagonal Ramsey Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05812)
*Cheng Xin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究构造性对角Ramsey问题，给出一个有界误差量子算法，在N-顶点图中用O(2^K K log(K/η))次边查询找到并验证齐性K-集（失败概率≤η）。在Ramsey尺度N=2^n下，算法用O(√N log N log(logN/η))次查询找到阶为⌊n/2⌋+1的齐性集，首次实现了Ramsey关系的次线性最坏情况算法。文中还给出Ω(N^{1/12})量子下界、Ω(N^{1-1/√2})随机化下界以及随机图上的多项式量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造性对角Ramsey问题要求通过边查询找到由Ramsey定理保证存在的齐性集，此前显式的经典递归需要O(N)次查询。作者目标是在量子计算模型下设计次线性查询算法，并研究其查询复杂度上下界。

Method: 算法在隐式候选集上运行构造性递归：每个集合用短的邻接约束合取表示，通过带上限的未知解量子搜索进行采样；同时设计尺度感知的浓度调度以平衡估计精度与深层集合采样成本。上界与碰撞归约得到的下界、随机Painter分析迁移的随机化下界互补；另有免估计的size-biased递归推广到任意固定边色数。

Result: 最坏情况下用O(√N log N log(logN/η))查询在N=2^n图中找到齐性集，优于经典O(N)；量子下界为Ω(N^{1/12})，随机化下界为Ω(N^{1-1/√2})；在G(N,1/2)随机图上贪心量子搜索仅用Õ(N^{1/4})查询，证明Ramsey搜索存在多项式的量子加速。

Conclusion: 该工作首次给出Ramsey关系的次线性最坏情况量子算法，并在查询复杂度上建立了上下界，同时证明了随机图上量子算法相对经典算法的多项式加速。作者认为量子计算在构造性组合问题上有本质优势。

Abstract: The constructive diagonal Ramsey problem asks, given adjacency-oracle access to an $N$-vertex graph, for a clique or independent set of the order guaranteed by Ramsey's theorem. We give a bounded-error quantum algorithm that, for every $K\ge2$ and $N\ge4^{K-1}$, finds and verifies a homogeneous $K$-set using $O\!\left(2^K K\log\frac Kη\right)$ edge queries with failure probability at most $η$. At the Ramsey scale $N=2^n$, this yields a homogeneous set of order $\lfloor n/2\rfloor+1$ using $O(\sqrt N\log N\log(\log N/η))$ queries, improving on the $O(N)$ queries of the explicit classical recursion and giving, to our knowledge, the first sublinear worst-case algorithm for the Ramsey relation. We also derive an $Ω(N^{1/12})$ quantum lower bound by a reduction from collision finding.
  The algorithm runs the constructive recursion over implicit candidate sets. Each set is represented by a short conjunction of adjacency constraints and sampled using capped unknown-solution quantum search, and a scale-aware concentration schedule balances estimation accuracy against the increasing cost of sampling deeper sets. We complement the upper bound with an $Ω(N^{1-1/\sqrt2})$ randomized lower bound, transported from the random-Painter analysis of online Ramsey numbers, which holds on the uniform distribution $G(N,1/2)$. On that distribution a greedy quantum search uses only $\widetilde O(N^{1/4})$ queries, giving a provable polynomial quantum speedup for Ramsey search on random graphs. We also give an estimation-free size-biased recursion and extend it to every fixed number of edge colours.

</details>


### [358] [Microwave-Free $^{13}$C Hyperpolarization of Diamond Particles Enabled by Magic Angle Spinning and NV Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07653)
*Rémi Blinder,Guzel Musabirova,Daehee Kim,Anshuman Nayak,Jason Twamley,Viateschlav N. Agafonov,Raiker Witter,Jörg Matysik,Fedor Jelezko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了在随机取向的纳米/微米金刚石颗粒中实现13C核超极化，通过结合光激发与魔角旋转（MAS），在无需微波辐射的条件下，于7.1 T和9.4 T磁场下分别实现了280倍（20%同位素富集）和411倍（天然丰度）的光诱导极化增强，稳态绝对极化度超过0.1%。该方案通过NV中心、P1和13C构成的三自旋团簇在能级反交叉（LAC）附近的动力学实现极化转移，且超过99.9%的NV取向均能参与极化传递过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微波驱动的动态核极化（DNP）技术复杂且成本高昂，作者希望利用金刚石NV色心的光泵浦特性，探索一种无需微波辐射的替代核超极化方案，为将来将极化从金刚石颗粒转移至外部核素、实现室温高灵敏度和高分辨率NMR铺路。

Method: 实验采用尺寸为0.2-2 μm的随机取向金刚石颗粒（天然13C丰度1.1%和20%富集），在7.1 T和9.4 T磁场下，将连续光照射与魔角旋转（MAS）结合，扫描MAS频率0-6 kHz，通过NV中心的光学极化结合三自旋团簇（NV-P1-13C）在能级反交叉附近的核自旋动力学实现13C极化。全部过程无需微波辐射。

Result: 在7.1 T下，20%富集样品达到280倍极化增强，天然丰度样品达到411倍增强；连续光照下稳态绝对13C极化度超过0.1%。该方案显著缓解了NV自旋哈密顿量各向异性的不利影响，使超过99.9%的NV取向可参与极化转移。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次证明了MAS结合光泵浦可在随机取向的金刚石微/纳米颗粒中实现高效的13C核超极化，即使在各向异性环境下也能使绝大多数NV取向参与极化转移，是向将金刚石颗粒极化转移至外部核素迈出的第一步，在室温高灵敏、高分辨NMR应用中具有前景。

Abstract: Nuclear hyperpolarization from optically pumped color centers in solids offers an alternative to conventional microwave-driven dynamic nuclear polarization (DNP). Diamond can host the nitrogen vacancy (NV) center, whose ground spin state can be readily polarized by light at room temperature, making diamond a candidate platform for nuclear hyperpolarization. We report $^{13}{\rm C}$ nuclear hyperpolarization in randomly oriented diamond particles with sizes ranging from 0.2 to 2 $μ$m, both at natural $^{13}{\rm C}$ abundance (1.1 %) and at 20 % isotopic enrichment, at magnetic fields of 7.1 T and 9.4 T. The protocol combines optical illumination with magic angle spinning (MAS) and does not require microwave irradiation. By investigating the nuclear polarization as a function of the MAS frequency between 0 and 6 kHz at the magnetic field of 7.1 T, we find maximum light-induced polarization enhancements of $280$-fold for the isotopically enriched sample and $411$-fold for the natural abundance sample. Under continuous illumination, steady-state absolute $^{13}{\rm C}$ polarization levels above 0.1 % are reached. A model involving optical pumping of NV centers and spin dynamics near level anticrossings (LACs) in three-spin clusters formed by NV, a substitutional nitrogen (P1) and $^{13}{\rm C}$ is used to describe these findings. The protocol strongly mitigates the effect of the anisotropy of the NV spin Hamiltonian, allowing more than $99.9\%$ of NV orientations to participate in the polarization transfer process. These results represent a first step toward transferring nuclear polarization from diamond particles to external nuclei, with potential applications in sensitive and high-resolution NMR at room temperature.

</details>


### [359] [Quantum Query Complexity of Persistence Statistics in Graph Zigzags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05813)
*Cheng Xin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从快照邻接位（snapshot-adjacency bits）估计锯齿形条形码（zigzag barcode）寿命标量汇总的查询复杂度。核心结果是一个精确恒等式：采样R个均匀时间，其最小和最大时间之间的广义秩的期望等于概率生成函数F_φ。基于此，作者在无谱隙、无同源态、无QRAM假设下，给出量子估计器（加法误差εn，Õ(√(m(K+n))/ε)次查询）和自适应量子变体；并给出两个最优性区间下量子Õ̃(n√m/ε)vs经典Θ(n²m)的匹配上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锯齿形持续同调（zigzag persistence）的条形码计算成本高昂，尤其在图上从快照数据构建时。作者希望绕开完整条形码计算，仅从快照邻接信息高效估计标量汇总（如总持续同调能量、均匀时间窗平均广义秩等），并探究量子计算能否在此问题上带来查询复杂度的优势。

Method: (1) 建立精确恒等式：F_φ = E[rank的最小-最大时间窗口]，将非线性条形码泛函转化为对随机采样时间的广义秩期望；(2) 对图而言，广义秩等于某交集图的电路秩（circuit rank），从而可由边数和分量数计算，无需构建条形码；(3) 基于此设计量子与经典蒙特卡洛估计器，并利用量子振幅估计加速；(4) 通过构造对抗性实例证明经典与量子的最坏情形匹配下界，包括对自适应算法的完全对抗下界。

Result: 量子估计器的查询复杂度为Õ(√(m(K+n))/ε)，经典为Õ(m·min{n²,(K+n)/ε²})；自适应量子变体具有相同的实例依赖性。在固定幂权重x^r（r≥2）及均匀窗口均值情形下，最坏情形复杂度为量子Õ(n√m/ε)与经典Θ(n²m)。在稀疏实例及显式分裂泄漏保证下（满足对数度的幂权重与二项权重，F_φ≤K），复杂度匹配为量子Õ(√(mK)/ε)与经典Õ(m·min{n²,K/ε²})。经典下界对抗全自适应算法，少于m个统计量无法确定正寿命直方图。

Conclusion: 作者在极弱的假设条件下实现了对锯齿形条形码标量泛函的高效量子与经典估计，建立了两个严格最优的复杂度区间，并阐明量子相对经典的二次加速在两种机制下达到。同时指出在有显式更新流（update stream）的场景下，近线性完整条形码算法已存在，说明快照访问与流式访问在计算能力上的根本差异。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of estimating scalar summaries of zigzag bar lifetimes from snapshot-adjacency bits.
  For graphs $G_1,\ldots,G_m$ on $n$ labeled vertices, let $\ell_b$ be the snapshot lifetime of a degree-one bar $b$ of the intersection zigzag. For a probability generating function $φ(x)=\mathbb{E}[x^R]$, the statistic $F_φ=\sum_bφ(\ell_b/m)$ includes normalized degree-$r$ total persistence and the mean generalized rank over a uniform time window. An exact identity underlies our algorithm: sample $R$ uniform times; the expected generalized rank between their minimum and maximum equals $F_φ$. For graphs that rank is the circuit rank of an intersection graph, so a nonlinear barcode functional becomes an average of edge and component counts, and no barcode is computed.
  Without spectral-gap, homology-state, or QRAM assumptions, this gives a quantum estimator with additive error $\varepsilon n$ and $\widetilde O(\sqrt{m(K+n)}/\varepsilon)$ queries when a bound $K\ge F_φ$ is supplied, against $\widetilde O(m\min\{n^2,(K+n)/\varepsilon^2\})$ classically, and an adaptive quantum variant with the same instance dependence. These estimators are optimal in two regimes. For every fixed power weight $x^r$, $r\ge2$, and for the uniform-window mean, the worst-case complexities are $\widetildeΘ(n\sqrt m/\varepsilon)$ quantum and $Θ(n^2m)$ classical. On sparse instances, under an explicit split-leakage promise met by power and binomial weights of logarithmic degree and the promise $F_φ\le K$, they are $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{mK}/\varepsilon)$ and $\widetildeΘ(m\min\{n^2,K/\varepsilon^2\})$. The classical lower bounds hold against fully adaptive algorithms, and fewer than $m$ such statistics cannot determine the positive-lifetime histogram. All bounds concern snapshot access; with an explicit update stream, near-linear full-barcode algorithms are known.

</details>


### [360] [Restricted typicality in non-equilibrium quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07832)
*Konrad Pawlik,Piotr Sierant,Jakub Zakrzewski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于由守恒荷输运主导、具有时间尺度分离的量子多体系统，仅通过系统慢模约束最大熵Scrooge系综，即可精确重构全局时间演化态的复杂非线性量子性质（如半链纠缠熵和计算基下参与熵），提出了"全局受限典型性"这一新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确表征一般量子多体系统的时间演化需要指数级增长的资源，而流体力学和统计力学虽然能预测局域可观测量期望值，却无法提供量子态非线性特征的任何信息。作者希望恢复这部分丢失的信息。

Method: 利用系统的时间尺度分离和守恒荷输运动力学，仅以慢模作为约束条件构建最大熵的Scrooge系综，用于重构时间演化纯态的复杂非线性量子性质。

Result: 仅用系统的慢模信息即可准确重构半链纠缠熵和计算基下的参与熵等非线性量子性质，验证了该方法在流体力学瓶颈系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将正则典型性范式推广，揭示出受流体力学瓶颈限制的系统在希尔伯特空间中一个动态受限子流形内表现为典型纯态，作者将此现象称为"全局受限典型性"。

Abstract: Characterizing the time evolution of generic quantum many-body systems is a fundamental challenge, as representing the exact state requires exponentially scaling computational resources. While hydrodynamics and statistical mechanics successfully simplify this task by predicting the expectation values of local observables, these macroscopic frameworks provide no information about nonlinear characteristics of the quantum state. In this work, we demonstrate that for systems exhibiting a timescale separation, with dynamics governed by the transport of conserved charges, this lost information can be systematically recovered. By constraining the maximum-entropy Scrooge ensemble solely by the system's slow modes, we accurately reconstruct complex, nonlinear quantum properties of the global time-evolved state, including the half-chain entanglement entropy and the participation entropy in the computational basis. Our results generalize the paradigm of canonical typicality, revealing that a hydrodynamically bottlenecked system behaves as a typical pure state within a dynamically restricted submanifold of the Hilbert space - a phenomenon we term global restricted typicality.

</details>


### [361] [Towards Continuous Profiling and Optimization of Quantum-Classical Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05814)
*Ayush Bansal,Owen Cochell,Santiago Núñez-Corrales,Marcos Frenkel,Seetharami Seelam,Apoorve Mohan,Tianyin Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出LLQM（Low-Level Quantum Machine），一个基于 profiling 驱动的元框架，用于量子-经典混合流水线。它通过细粒度任务分解和连续的资源/硬件状态监控，捕捉跨阶段资源依赖关系，并以QEM为例在IBM 156-qubit处理器上验证了运行时瓶颈暴露和硬件/保真度/负载感知优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子应用日益以多阶段量子-经典流水线形式执行，各阶段资源需求多样且受硬件噪声漂移影响而呈现随机行为，但现有工作流框架将其视为静态、孤立的组件，无法捕捉跨阶段资源依赖和量子-经典决策间的交互。

Method: LLQM将流水线分解为细粒度任务，持续剖析其CPU/GPU、内存、QPU和队列依赖，并结合实时硬件状态建立统一的运行时抽象。以QEM作为代表性流水线阶段，在IBM 156-qubit Heron r2处理器上对多达100 qubit电路和1e7个transpiled门进行评估。

Result: 持续剖析能够暴露运行时瓶颈，并实现硬件感知、保真度感知和工作负载感知的优化。

Conclusion: 统一的profiling驱动运行时抽象能够有效刻画量子-经典流水线中跨阶段资源依赖与噪声状态的影响，为量子经典混合工作流的动态优化提供了新的元框架思路。

Abstract: Quantum applications increasingly execute as multi-stage quantum-classical pipelines, interleaving QPU computation with classical stages like circuit generation, transpilation, layout mapping, quantum error mitigation (QEM), and post-processing. These stages have diverse resource requirements and exhibit stochastic behavior under drifting hardware noises, yet existing workflow frameworks treat them as static, isolated components. We present LLQM (Low-Level Quantum Machine), a profiling-driven meta-framework for quantum-classical pipelines. LLQM decomposes pipelines into fine-grained tasks and continuously profiles their CPU/GPU, memory, QPU, and queue dependencies alongside real-time hardware states. This unified runtime abstraction captures cross-stage resource dependencies and reveals how classical and quantum decisions interact, enabling characterization of their impact on fidelity and resource consumption. We evaluate LLQM using QEM as a representative pipeline stage, on IBM 156-qubit Heron r2 processors with circuits up to 100 qubits and 1e7 transpiled gates. Our results show that continuous profiling exposes runtime bottlenecks and enables hardware-, fidelity-, and workload-aware optimizations.

</details>


### [362] [Topologically Protected Edge States in One-Dimensional Quantum Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05823)
*Emily Maxey,Jacob Mansfield,Beth Thacker,Wade DeGottardi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有可变步长的新颖拓扑量子游走，能够实现更高的拓扑绕数并支持多个边缘态。通过传递矩阵方法分析拓扑保护边缘态的空间轮廓和自旋结构，理论预测与数值结果及Jackiw-Rebbi零模解高度吻合，为设计具有可控边缘态数目、空间轮廓和自旋结构的量子游走提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体具有对无序鲁棒的受保护边界态，使其在实际应用中极具吸引力。离散时间量子游走已能在多种实验平台实现拓扑非平凡相和边界态，但现有设计在调控边缘态数目、空间形态和自旋结构方面仍缺乏灵活性。作者旨在引入可变步长的拓扑量子游走类别，以提供对其拓扑分类的理论见解，并实现对拓扑边缘态的更精确控制。

Method: 作者定义了一类步长可变的拓扑量子游走，利用这些系统可访问更高的绕数并支持多个边缘态，同时考察了时间反演对称性破缺时拓扑保护从Z指标退化为Z2指标的情形。他们采用传递矩阵方法求解边缘态的空间轮廓和自旋结构，并将结果与数值计算及Dirac方程的Jackiw-Rebbi零模解进行了对比验证。

Result: 可变步长量子游走能实现更高的拓扑绕数和多个边缘态；传递矩阵方法给出的理论预测在边缘态空间分布和自旋结构方面与数值结果高度一致，并且在适用情况下与Jackiw-Rebbi零模解精确吻合。

Conclusion: 该分析为设计具有可控边缘态数目、空间轮廓和自旋结构的量子游走提供了系统性路线图，深化了对量子游走拓扑分类（包括时间反演对称性破缺时Z到Z2指标的约化）的理解。

Abstract: Topological insulators host protected boundary states that are robust to disorder, making them attractive for applications and motivating their realization in engineered systems. Discrete-time quantum walks, which have been implemented in a variety of experimental platforms, can exhibit topologically nontrivial phases and their associated boundary states. Here, we introduce a class of topological quantum walks with variable step lengths that offer theoretical insights into the topological classification of such walks. For example, these walks can access higher winding numbers and support multiple edge states. They also provide context for the reduction of topological protection from a $\mathbb{Z}$- to $\mathbb{Z}_2$-valued invariant when time-reversal symmetry is broken. The topologically protected edge states are investigated using a transfer-matrix approach that describes their spatial profiles and spin structures. These predictions are in excellent agreement with numerical results and, where applicable, with the Jackiw--Rebbi zero-mode solution of the Dirac equation. Taken together, our analysis provides a roadmap for designing quantum walks with control over the number, spatial profile, and spin structure of their topological edge states.

</details>


### [363] [On the sample complexity of the generalized hidden shift problem over arbitrary finite groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05828)
*Juntai Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将广义隐藏偏移问题推广到任意有限群上，并通过群论数据刻画了样本复杂度的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏偏移问题是量子计算与群论交叉领域中的核心问题之一，以往研究多集中在特定群（如循环群或阿贝尔群）上。该论文希望将问题推广到任意有限群，并系统性地用群论内在结构来刻画所需的样本复杂度，为理论研究和实际应用提供更一般的框架。

Method: 将广义隐藏偏移问题形式化定义在任意有限群上，并基于群的结构数据（如不可约表示、群阶、子群结构等群论不变量）推导样本复杂度的上界与下界。上界通过构造性算法或概率分析方法获得，下界则通过信息论或对抗性论证得出。

Result: 得到了广义隐藏偏移问题在任意有限群上的样本复杂度的上界和下界，二者均由群的代数结构数据刻画表达。

Conclusion: 有限群上的广义隐藏偏移问题样本复杂度可由群论数据进行精确刻画，上下界之间存在明确的群论解释，为理解该问题的计算复杂性提供了一般性结论。

Abstract: We formulate the generalized hidden shift problem over arbitrary finite groups and obtain upper and lower bounds for the sample complexity in terms of group theoretic data.

</details>


### [364] [Instantaneous-Frame Theory of Strongly Driven Parametric Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08348)
*Kentaro Kubo,Shinichi Inoue,Jushin Tei,Yinghao Ho,Yasunobu Nakamura,Hayato Goto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于瞬时本征态的参量双量子比特门理论框架。与传统在空闲本征基下描述参量门的方法不同，作者将参量门视为瞬时本征态之间的相干旋转，并利用非阿贝尔Berry联络来描述跃迁及有效ZZ耦合。该理论在多种可调耦合器架构（双transmon、电容分流双transmon、单transmon）中与精确数值模拟定量一致，即使在大振幅（快速门操作）区域、空闲框架失效的情况下依然保持准确，同时显著减少了对计算密集的精确模拟的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的参量双量子比特门理论建立在空闲本征基（idle eigenbasis）之上，但在快速门操作所需的大振幅区域，这些传统理论会失效。因此需要一种更自然、更准确的描述框架，能够在非微扰区域仍然给出定量正确的预测，以支持可扩展超导量子处理器的门实现。

Method: 作者提出将参量门描述为瞬时本征态（instantaneous eigenstates）之间的相干旋转，而非空闲本征态之间的跃迁。瞬时本征基为驱动动力学提供了一个自然的移动参考系（moving frame），其中非阿贝尔Berry联络控制了态之间的跃迁，并提供了交换耦合和有效ZZ耦合的紧致描述。作者将这一理论应用于三种代表性可调耦合器架构进行验证。

Result: 该瞬时框架理论在双transmon、电容分流双transmon和单transmon可调耦合器架构中均与精确数值模拟定量一致。即便在空闲框架描述失效的大振幅区域，该理论依然保持准确，并显著减少了对手动精确模拟计算的依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了超越传统空闲框架模型的参量门瞬时框架理论，为驱动量子动力学提供了新的视角。这一理论有望成为设计快速、高精度超导量子门的重要工具，推动可扩展超导量子处理器的实际应用。

Abstract: Parametric two-qubit gates using tunable couplers are a promising approach to scalable superconducting quantum processors. However, conventional theories formulated in the idle eigenbasis break down in the large-amplitude regime required for fast gate operations. Here, we show that parametric gates are more naturally and accurately described as coherent rotations between instantaneous eigenstates rather than idle eigenstates. This approach may appear counterintuitive, since instantaneous eigenbases are usually associated with adiabatic dynamics. However, they also form a natural moving frame for driven dynamics, where the non-Abelian Berry connection governs transitions and provides a compact description of the exchange and effective $ZZ$ couplings. We show that this theory achieves quantitative agreement with exact numerical simulations across representative tunable-coupler architectures, including double-transmon, capacitively shunted double-transmon, and single-transmon couplers. The description remains accurate even in regimes where idle-frame descriptions fail, while substantially reducing the reliance on computationally demanding exact simulations. These results establish an instantaneous-frame theory of parametric gates beyond conventional idle-frame models and provide a new perspective on driven quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [365] [High-Resolution Dynamical Eigenspectroscopy via Variational Trotter Compression on a Superconducting Qubit Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05838)
*Liyang Sui,Xingrui Liu,Yufan Li,Sainan Huai,Zhiwen Zong,Kunliang Bu,Xiaopei Yang,Wenyan Jin,Bowen Chen,Xutao Zhang,Jianlan Wu,Shengyu Zhang,Yi Yin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验演示了一种动态本征谱学协议，利用变分Trotter压缩(VTC)扩展相干演化时间，在9量子比特超导处理器上通过量子态层析和傅里叶分析提取H2分子和Fermi-Hubbard模型的精细能谱结构，所得本征能量与精确对角化结果的误差在2×10^-3以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含噪中等尺度量子(NISQ)设备上实现高分辨率本征谱学面临电路深度与相干时间之间的根本性权衡。经典谱学方法需要长时间的高保真幺正演化，而当前超导处理器的有限相干窗口限制了这一需求，因此需要一种实用方案来突破此限制。

Method: 1) 提出动态本征谱学协议，从时间依赖的存活振幅中提取精细能级结构；2) 引入变分Trotter压缩(VTC)技术扩展高保真幺正演化的持续时间，克服有限相干窗口；3) 在9量子比特超导处理器上，通过量子态层析重建时间域自相关信号；4) 结合多频拟合与傅里叶分析提取本征能量。

Result: 在不同键长的H2分子以及不同关联区间的Fermi-Hubbard模型上，提取的本征能量与精确对角化值一致，误差在2×10^-3以内，包括强关联区间中的近简并能级也能被成功分辨。该系统覆盖了从弱耦合到强耦合的不同体系。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了实验动态谱学作为鲁棒且通用的框架，能够同时在近量子硬件上模拟量子化学与强关联晶格系统，弥合弱耦合与强耦合区间的鸿沟，为NISQ设备上的高分辨率能谱研究提供了新的范式。

Abstract: The pursuit of high-resolution eigenspectroscopy on noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices is often hindered by the trade-off between circuit depth and coherence time. In this work, we introduce and experimentally demonstrate a dynamical eigenspectroscopy protocol that extracts fine-grained energy structures from time-dependent survival amplitudes. To overcome the finite coherence window of current superconducting processors, we employ Variational Trotter Compression (VTC) as a practical means to extend the duration of high-fidelity unitary evolution. Using a multi-connected 9-qubit superconducting processor, we reconstruct the time-domain autocorrelation signal via quantum state tomography for the H2 molecule at different bond lengths and for the Fermi-Hubbard model across different correlation regimes. Through multi-frequency fitting and Fourier analysis, the extracted eigenenergies agree with the exact-diagonalization values to within 2x10^-3, including the near-degenerate levels in the strongly interacting regime. Our results establish experimental dynamical spectroscopy as a robust and generalizable framework for simulating both quantum chemistry and strongly correlated lattice systems, bridging weak- and strong-coupling regimes on near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [366] [Non-Markovian dynamics of a qubit on the zigzag edge of photonic graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08382)
*Enrico Di Benedetto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析研究了量子比特耦合到光子蜂窝晶格之字形边缘的动力学。利用预解算子方法和精确格点格林函数，绘制了量子比特-光子束缚态谱和复极点，揭示了量子比特布居数的长时间标度定律。与耦合到石墨烯体态的量子比特不同，连接狄拉克点的零能平带抑制了长时间分支切口诱导的拖尾，转而驱动衰减的拉比振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子比特与非平庸光子环境（特别是具有拓扑/平带性质的晶格边缘）耦合时的非马尔可夫动力学，探索不同于体态耦合场景的新物理现象。

Method: 采用解析方法：预解算子框架结合精确格点格林函数，映射束缚态谱与复极点，从而推导量子比特布居数的长时间标度律。

Result: 发现与体态耦合不同，之字形边缘的零能平带连接狄拉克点，抑制了长时分支切口引起的幂律拖尾，转而产生衰减的拉比振荡。

Conclusion: 量子比特耦合到之字形边缘展现出独特的动力学特征（阻尼拉比振荡而非长时拖尾），可为量子仿真和量子信息处理提供潜在实验平台。

Abstract: We present an analytical study of the dynamics of a qubit coupled to the zigzag edge of a photonic honeycomb lattice. Leveraging a resolvent operator approach and exact lattice Green's functions, we map the spectrum of qubit-photon bound states and complex poles to uncover the long-time scaling laws of the qubit population. Unlike a qubit coupled to the bulk of graphene, the presence of a zero-energy flat band connecting the Dirac points suppresses long-time branch-cut-induced tails, instead driving damped Rabi oscillations. Potential experimental platforms are briefly discussed.

</details>


### [367] [Demailly's Conjecture in Quantum Language and Its Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05866)
*Trung Hoa Dinh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将复射影空间中的脂肪点插值问题与固定粒子数玻色子的暗态问题建立精确对应关系，并在相关算术问题上证明了一个有限尺度"无暗态放大定理"，该定理的渐近推论即Demailly猜想不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图在代数几何（脂肪点插值和符号幂的初始次数）与量子物理（玻色子暗态）之间建立精确的翻译字典，并通过算术方法（Frobenius放大与特征零回传）解决Demailly猜想这一长期未解的渐近不等式问题。这类跨领域对应既能提供直觉工具，又能将纯几何问题转化为量子哈密顿量的谱问题来研究。

Method: 论文首先利用酉Fischer–Bargmann实现建立字典：齐次度映射为粒子数、Veronese提升映射为玻色子对称乘积态、m阶消失映射为正交于所有低于m阶的相干喷泉激发。符号幂的初始次数即首个允许正约束哈密顿量出现暗态的粒子数，Waldschmidt常数即每次抑制阶的渐近粒子代价。随后证明了有限尺度无暗态放大定理：Frobenius在某个特征为正的良好纤维中放大满秩证书，通过非零极大子式将其回传至特征零，由此渐进得到Demailly猜想的结论。

Result: 论文的核心成果包括：建立了射影几何语句、量子重构表述和算术部分三者之间可独立阅读又精确对应统一的理论框架；证明了有限尺度下的无暗态放大定理，并通过Frobenius技巧从正特征回到特征零来获得满秩证书；作为渐近推论，解决了Demailly猜想中的不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作打通了代数几何、量子多体物理与算术代数几何三个领域的壁垒，使得脂肪点插值、Waldschmidt常数、暗态物理和Frobenius同态在同一框架下得以统一理解。论文的结构设计（三个模块可独立阅读）也颇具匠心，便于不同背景的研究者各取所需。

Abstract: We give an exact translation between fat-point interpolation in complex projective space and a dark-state problem for fixed-particle-number bosons. Under the unitary Fischer--Bargmann realization, homogeneous degree becomes particle number, the Veronese lift becomes a bosonic symmetric product state, and order-$m$ vanishing becomes orthogonality to all coherent jet excitations of orders below $m$. The initial degree of a symbolic power is therefore the first particle number admitting a dark state of a positive constraint Hamiltonian, and the Waldschmidt constant is the asymptotic particle cost per suppression order. After developing both sides of this dictionary from first principles, we prove a finite-scale no-dark-state amplification theorem. Frobenius amplifies the associated full-rank certificate in one good positive-characteristic fiber; a nonzero maximal minor returns it to characteristic zero. Its asymptotic consequence is Demailly's conjectured inequality. The paper is written so that the projective statement, the quantum reformulation, and the arithmetic part of the solution can be read independently and then identified exactly.

</details>


### [368] [Do Quantum AIs Dream in Paths? Path-Integral Slow Thinking through Grover Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05842)
*Xiansheng Cai,Xiu-Hao Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Grover量子增幅的"慢思考"训练框架，将推理建模为叠加态路径积分，通过精确验证器将轨迹划分为集体接受/拒绝分量并使其干涉。有限Grover演化在预放大成功率低于1的内部目标处最优，从而避免了经典RL中成功率单调推向1所导致的策略坍缩。在2x3滑动拼图上，量子训练达到0.95准确率（经典最强0.73），并在泛化集上大幅超越经典对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的RLHF/可验证奖励训练虽能让LLM学会"慢思考"，但同时诱发策略坍缩——概率集中到少数成功轨迹上，探索性多样性被侵蚀。作者希望探索量子人工智能能否以一种不同的方式实现慢思考，以保留路径多样性并避免策略坍缩。

Method: 将慢思考形式化为推理轨迹上的相干动力学——一个离散路径积分，其中动作序列在叠加中并存并在测量前重组。训练实现使用精确验证器将整体分解为集体接受和拒绝分量，在Grover振幅放大下相互干涉。关键设计是：目标不是让成功率趋于1，而是设定一个解析确定的内部训练目标（位于1以下的预放大成功率值），从而消除单调压向单位成功率的压力。通过精确态矢模拟2x3滑动拼图验证。

Result: 在2x3滑动拼图的32题训练集上，Grover训练单轮达到0.95准确率，经典最强对照仅0.73。在留出题上，量子训练保留的准确率约是经典训练的3.2和3.9倍（四轮、匹配电路调用条件下）。固定规模策略可支持的训练问题数也随放大预算比匹配的经典重复增长更快。

Conclusion: 建立了基于Grover路径积分慢思考实现：内部目标保留探索性路径多样性，而整体层面的干涉将该多样性转化为可验证的性能。量子方法在解决方案密集的基准上较经典方法更有效地保留泛化能力并避免策略坍缩。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards enables large language models to think slowly, but the same training can induce policy collapse: probability concentrates onto a few successful trajectories and exploratory diversity erodes. We ask whether quantum AI can realize slow thinking differently. We formulate slow thinking as coherent dynamics over reasoning trajectories, a discrete path integral in which action sequences coexist in superposition and recombine before measurement. In our trainable realization, an exact verifier partitions the ensemble into collective accepted and rejected components that interfere under Grover amplitude amplification. A finite Grover evolution is maximized when the pre-amplification success probability lies at an analytically determined value below one, so inference itself defines an interior training target and removes the monotonic pressure toward unit success. In exact statevector simulations of a 2x3 sliding puzzle, Grover training reaches accuracy 0.95 on a 32-question training set at one round, against 0.73 for the strongest classical control. On held-out questions specialization has a cost: an untrained uniform policy read out through the same amplification remains the strongest reference on this solution-dense benchmark, and quantum training preserves far more held-out accuracy than classical training - at four rounds with matched circuit applications the two quantum models reach 3.2 and 3.9 times the strongest classical controls. The number of training questions supported by fixed-size policies trained at each amplification budget also grows faster with the budget than with matched classical repetition. These results establish a Grover-based realization of path-integral slow thinking: the interior target preserves exploratory path diversity, and ensemble-level interference converts it into verified performance.

</details>


### [369] [Restart and first detection in a lackadaisical quantum walk with flat-band localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08973)
*Debraj Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维松懈离散时间量子行走（带自环权重ℓ）中的随机重启与尖锐重启动力学。无重启时系统存在一个平带（导致本征局域化）和两个色散带（支持弹道传播）。研究者比较了两种初始局域基准态：平带活跃态（有限平带重叠）和平带暗态（零平带重叠），系统性地分析了不同重启协议（几何随机重启、幂律重启、监测下尖锐重启）对动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重启对具有多带结构（含平带）的量子行走动力学的影响，特别是重启如何与平带导致的本征局域化相互作用，以及不同初始态（平带活跃vs平带暗态）在重启下的差异响应。

Method: 理论上分析了三种重启场景：（1）几何随机重启（每步概率q）：推导稳态均方位移的q⁻²标度，研究重启站点占据概率在q→0的渐近行为；（2）幂律重启（等待时间分布p_m∝m⁻ˢ）：分析稳态归一化占据分布和绝对空间矩的收敛条件；（3）尖锐重启的监测首探测：固定重初始间隔r下，推导两种基准态的首过探测时间特性。

Result: 几何随机重启下：稳态均方位移∝q⁻²；平带活跃态的重启点占据概率趋近无重启本征局域值，平带暗态则∝q·ln(1/q)趋于零。幂律重启下：仅在s>2时存在归一化稳态占据分布，s>p+2时p阶绝对空间矩有限；1<s≤2时，平带活跃态收敛到本征平带轮廓而暗态趋于零。尖锐重启下：平带活跃态的首次探测平均时间在中间自环权重处出现最小值，平带暗态则随ℓ→∞趋于弹道探测极限。

Conclusion: 重启对平带相关量子行走动力学的影响高度依赖于初始态是否与平带重叠。平带活跃态在各种重启协议下均趋向本征局域化轮廓，而平带暗态则在重启下表现出截然不同的标度行为（如q·ln(1/q)衰减、趋向弹道极限等），揭示了重启与平带物理之间丰富的相互作用。

Abstract: We study stochastic and sharp restart in a one-dimensional lackadaisical discrete-time quantum walk with self-loop weight $\ell$. In the absence of restart, the dynamics has a flat band responsible for intrinsic localization and two dispersive bands supporting ballistic propagation. We compare two initially localized benchmark states: a flat-band-active state with finite flat-band overlap and a flat-band-dark state with zero flat-band overlap. For geometric stochastic restart with per-step restart probability $q$, the stationary mean-squared displacement scales as $q^{-2}$ as $q\to0$. In the same limit, the restart-site occupation probability approaches the restart-free intrinsic localized value for the flat-band-active state, whereas for the flat-band-dark state it vanishes as $q\ln(1/q)$. For power-law restart, where $p_m\propto m^{-s}$ is the probability that the waiting time to the next restart is $m$ steps, a normalized stationary site-occupation distribution exists only for $s>2$, while the stationary absolute spatial moment of order $p$ is finite only for $s>p+2$. In the regime $1<s\leq2$, at every fixed lattice site, the flat-band-active occupation converges to the intrinsic flat-band profile, while the flat-band-dark occupation tends to zero. We also consider monitored first detection with sharp restart, in which the walk is reinitialized after a fixed number $r$ of consecutive unsuccessful measurements. For fixed $r$, the mean first-detected-passage time of the flat-band-active state exhibits a minimum at an intermediate self-loop weight, whereas the flat-band-dark state approaches a ballistic detection limit as $\ell\to\infty$.

</details>


### [370] [Quantum Message Passing Convergence and Vanishing Block-Error Probability for Random LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05857)
*Avijit Mandal,Christophe Piveteau,Joseph M. Renes,Henry D. Pfister*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个两阶段基于量子消息的置信传播（BPQM）解码器，用于对称q元纯态信道上的随机q元LDPC码（q为素数），并证明当码长N趋于无穷时，其系综平均块错误概率趋于零。对于d_v≥3的正则系综，保真度界在BPQM成功区域内给出均符号错误概率的双指数衰减。该结果可为基于Regev归约的量子算法及解码量子干涉测量（DQI）中BPQM解码步骤提供理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPQM是一种在纯态经典-量子信道上实现最优解码的量子算法，但其树因子图分析无法保证含环的LDPC Tanner图具有消失的块错误概率。因此需要证明BPQM在含环LDPC码上仍然有效，以支撑其在量子算法（如基于Regev归约的算法和DQI）中的实际应用。

Method: 构造两阶段BPQM解码器：(1) 对具有树邻域的坐标应用深度-ℓ的BPQM，选取ℓ=Θ(log log N)以控制解码错误（利用非交换联合界）；(2) 将剩余坐标视为擦除，利用码的最小距离性质完成擦除恢复。分析针对对称q元纯态信道上的随机q元LDPC码（q为素数），涵盖正则与有限支撑非正则系综。

Result: 证明了随机q元LDPC码系综的块错误概率随N→∞趋于零。对d_v≥3的正则系综，保真度界导出BPQM成功区域内均符号错误概率的双指数衰减。分析扩展至有限支撑非正则系综。

Conclusion: 该结果证明了BPQM可有效解码含环LDPC码，从而验证了BPQM在DQI和基于Regev归约的编码理论量子算法的解码步骤中的适用性，前提是码来自所分析的随机LDPC系综且诱导的无记忆对称纯态信道落在BPQM成功区域内。

Abstract: Belief propagation with quantum messages (BPQM) is a quantum algorithm that decodes classical codes transmitted over classical--quantum channels. It realizes optimal decoding on tree factor graphs over pure-state classical-quantum channels. However, this tree-based analysis does not ensure vanishing block-error probability for LDPC Tanner graphs with cycles. In this work, we construct a two-stage BPQM decoder for random $q$-ary LDPC codes over symmetric $q$-ary pure-state channels, where $q$ is prime, and prove that its ensemble-average block-error probability vanishes as the blocklength $N$ tends to infinity. For regular ensembles with $d_v\geq3$, fidelity bounds yield double-exponential decay of the average symbol-error probability throughout the BPQM success region. We apply depth-$\ell$ BPQM to coordinates with tree neighbourhoods and treat the remaining coordinates as erasures. With a suitable $\ell=Θ(\log\log N)$, a noncommutative union bound controls the BPQM decoding errors, while the minimum-distance property guarantees erasure recovery. We also extend the analysis to finite-support irregular ensembles. These results are relevant to quantum algorithms based on Regev's reduction, where coherent decoding uncomputes a codeword register. Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) uses a closely related Fourier-based framework that reduces sparse max-LINSAT optimization problems to LDPC decoding problems on pure-state channels. Our results justify the use of BPQM in the decoding step of DQI and of coding-theoretic algorithms based on Regev's reduction whenever the code is drawn from one of the random LDPC ensembles analyzed here and the induced memoryless symmetric pure-state channel lies in the BPQM success region.

</details>


### [371] [Measurement-Based Feedback of Open Quantum Systems: A Control-Theoretic Review and Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06111)
*Weichao Liang,Jing Zhang,Gaoyue Guo,Daoyi Dong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从控制理论视角系统综述了连续监测开放量子系统中基于测量的反馈控制，核心是围绕递归-收缩框架（Hamiltonian反馈消除非目标不变障碍，测量诱导动力学提供局部指数收缩），并讨论了包括可扩展估计、采样延迟、自适应、非马尔可夫动力学等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续监测开放量子系统中的测量反馈控制缺乏统一的控制理论视角，需要从不变性、估计、递归和收缩等概念出发，搭建测量基量子反馈与非线性随机控制之间的桥梁。

Method: 以有限维扩散型随机主方程描述的量子系统为分析对象，引入状态空间、不变子空间和量子非破坏（QND）结构；将量子滤波解释为非线性观测器动力学；综述开环渐近性、滤波器稳定性、状态反馈镇定、鲁棒性和降阶观测器控制，重点阐述递归-收缩框架：Hamiltonian反馈消除非目标不变障碍，测量诱导动力学实现局部指数收缩。

Result: 建立了测量-估计-反馈架构在不同量子平台上的普适性；识别了可扩展估计、采样与延迟、自适应、混合与非马尔可夫动力学、实际稳定性、最优控制和学习型设计等关键实现挑战与开放问题。

Conclusion: 该综述以不变性、估计、递归和收缩为组织线索，为测量基量子反馈与非线性随机控制提供了教学型桥梁，核心框架虽基于有限维扩散模型，但架构思路可推广至广泛的量子平台。

Abstract: This review develops a control-theoretic perspective on measurement-based feedback for continuously monitored open quantum systems, with the main analysis focused on finite-dimensional systems governed by diffusive stochastic master equations. We introduce the relevant state-space, invariant-subspace, and quantum non-demolition structures, interpret quantum filtering as nonlinear observer dynamics, and review open-loop asymptotics, filter stability, state-feedback stabilization, robustness, and reduced-order observer-based control. Particular emphasis is placed on a recurrence--contraction framework, in which Hamiltonian feedback removes non-target invariant obstructions while measurement-induced dynamics provide local exponential contraction. Although the detailed analysis is developed for finite-dimensional diffusive models, the underlying measurement--estimation--feedback architecture is relevant across a broad range of quantum platforms. We further discuss implementation challenges and open problems involving scalable estimation, sampling and delay, adaptation, hybrid and non-Markovian dynamics, practical stability, optimal control, and learning-based design. By organizing these developments around invariance, estimation, recurrence, and contraction, the review provides a tutorial bridge between measurement-based quantum feedback and nonlinear stochastic control.

</details>


### [372] [Modular commutator as a robust topological invariant and approximate Markovianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09019)
*Tai Hsuan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了模交换子（modular commutator）作为手性中心电荷探测器的鲁棒性，在有限维希尔伯特空间中证明近似局域量子马尔可夫条件可保证模交换子在拓扑保持形变下的渐近不变性，并结合迹范数连续性确立其在高斯局域酉路径连接的间隙量子相中的渐近不变性；同时给出非零模交换子所需条件互信息的下界，并将结论推广到霍尔电导估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往证明模交换子在形变下的不变性依赖于严格的局域量子马尔可夫条件，即盘中三连续条带A、B、C的条件互信息I(A:C|B)=0，但该条件同时也迫使探测子为零，导致无法判断在物理相关的近似马尔可夫态中模交换子是否依然鲁棒，存在理论张力。

Method: 对有限维希尔伯特空间，证明近似局域量子马尔可夫性质蕴含拓朴保持形变下模交换子变化的渐近消失；随后证明模交换子的迹范数连续性；将形变不变性与迹范数连续性结合得到模交换子在高斯局域酉路径连接的间隙量子相中的渐近不变性。此外，通过分析模哈密顿量驱动的有限时间动力学，在纠缠面积律条件下推导条件互信息不能快于指数衰减的定量下界。最后将结论推广至霍尔电导估计器。

Result: (1) 近似局域量子马尔可夫条件保证模交换子对拓朴保持形变渐近不变；(2) 模交换子在迹范数意义下具有连续性；(3) 在隙量子相中满足近似局域量子马尔可夫条件时模交换子渐近不变；(4) 若态满足纠缠面积律，非零模交换子要求跨条带的I(A:C|B)至少随B宽度指数衰减，将经典的严格no-go定理推广为定量有限界；(5) 结论同样适用于霍尔电导估计器。

Conclusion: 本文消除了严格局域量子马尔可夫条件导致的矛盾，在物理上更合理的近似马尔可夫情形下确立了模交换子的鲁棒性，并给出了其非零所需的定量纠缠条件，同时表明这些结果对霍尔电导估计器同样成立，加强了模交换子作为手性中心电荷和拓扑不变量探测器的理论根基。

Abstract: The modular commutator provides a bulk, local, single-wave-function probe of the chiral central charge $c_-$ for gapped ground states. Its invariance under deformations was previously established under a local quantum Markov condition---namely, the conditional mutual information $I(A:C|B)$ is zero for all tripartitions of a disk into three consecutive strips A, B, and C \cite{Modular-commutator-Gapped}. However, the local quantum Markov condition also forces the probe to vanish, leaving open whether modular commutator remains robust in physically relevant states where the Markov property holds only approximately. In this paper, we resolve this tension for finite-dimensional Hilbert spaces: the approximate local quantum Markov property implies the change of the modular commutator under topology-preserving deformations vanishes asymptotically. We next prove trace-norm continuity of the modular commutator. Combining deformation invariance with trace-norm continuity, we establish that the modular commutator remains asymptotically invariant within gapped quantum phases connected by quasi-local unitary paths that preserve the approximate local quantum Markov condition. Conversely, by analyzing finite-time dynamics generated by modular Hamiltonians, we derive a quantitative lower bound on the conditional mutual information required for a non-zero modular commutator: $I(A:C|B)$ across such strip tripartitions cannot decay faster than exponentially with the width of B given that the state satisfies entanglement area law, extending the exact no-go theorem of \cite{strict-J-2024} to a quantitative finite bound . Finally, we demonstrate that those conclusions apply equally to the Hall conductance estimator \cite{FanSahayVishwanath2023}.

</details>


### [373] [Quantifying Nonstabilizerness of Quantum Codes by Removing the Inert Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06177)
*Yuan Liu,Ke-Mi Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过结构观察解决了量子纠错码中非稳定化子资源（magic）量化困难的问题：Clifford扇区不含magic且可通过Clifford变换移除，剩余三阶对象的magic可通过精确计算的第四矩获得。由此推导出多个码族的闭式公式，包括Dicke态公式（将100量子比特从4^100项缩减为短二项式求和）、三次相位码边界（仅D4码饱和）及群乘法态的循环/零判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非稳定化子（magic）是实现通用量子计算的量子资源，出现在容错码和拓扑序中。然而其量化极为困难：标准度量需要对指数多个Pauli算符求和，且此前对量子码缺乏定量的magic理论。

Method: 核心结构性观察：Clifford扇区不含magic，可通过Clifford变换将其去除，留下一个三阶对象，其magic是可精确求值的第四矩。去除惰性背景后，得到多种码族的闭式表达式。

Result: 获得了三个主要结果：(1) 所有Dicke态的magic公式，将100量子比特情形从4^100项缩减为短二项式求和；(2) 三次相位码（包括扭曲量子double和非阿贝尔拓扑序）的magic上界，仅D4码饱和；(3) 群乘法态的循环/零判据。

Conclusion: 这些结果将广泛类别的量子码的非稳定化子计算化简为有限的经典计数问题，并从中识别出魔法最大的码，为容错量子计算和拓扑序中的magic量化提供了系统性的理论工具。

Abstract: Nonstabilizerness (magic) is the quantum resource that, together with stabilizer operations, makes universal quantum computation possible. It appears in fault-tolerant codes and topological order. However, quantifying nonstabilizerness is difficult. The standard measure sums over exponentially many Pauli operators, and for quantum codes no quantitative theory has been available. We resolve this by a structural observation: the Clifford sector carries no magic and can be removed by a Clifford transformation, leaving an order-three object whose magic is an exactly evaluable fourth moment. Removing this inert background yields closed forms for several code families: a formula for all Dicke states, reducing a 100-qubit case from $4^{100}$ terms to a short binomial sum; a bound for cubic-phase codes (twisted quantum doubles and non-Abelian topological order), saturated only by the $D_4$ code; and a cyclic/zero criterion for group multiplication states. Together these results reduce the computation of nonstabilizerness for a broad class of codes to finite classical counting problems, and identify the maximally magical codes among them.

</details>


### [374] [Reducing quantum and classical resources for quantum-centric supercomputing workloads on near-term hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05957)
*Maxence Grandadam,Vladyslav Bohun,Dikshant Dulal,Maciej Koch-Janusz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于采样的Krylov量子对角化（SKQD）在去极化噪声下的收敛保证，推导出暴露指数深度惩罚的射击预算估计，并在40量子比特的单杂质安德森模型上进行了实验验证。研究发现设备噪声破坏了无噪声理论预测的收敛性，但近似编译技术可以压缩Krylov时间演化电路，减少硬件噪声积累，恢复能量收敛并降低量子射击预算和经典子空间维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SKQD是一种量子中心超级计算工作流的范例，其收敛保证依赖于从Krylov态集合中以足够概率采样重要计算基组构型的假设。然而在实际含噪硬件上，该假设是否成立目前缺乏严格的理论分析和实验验证，因此需要深入分析噪声对SKQD收敛性的影响。

Method: 理论上，在去极化噪声模型下推导射击次数的资源估计，揭示指数深度惩罚；实验上，在一维单杂质安德森模型的20格点（40量子比特）实例上运行SKQD，并采用近似编译技术压缩Krylov时间演化电路以缓解硬件噪声。

Result: 实验表明设备噪声破坏了无噪声SKQD分析所预测的实际收敛，但经过电路压缩后，硬件噪声积累显著降低，恢复了预期的能量收敛，同时减少了量子射击预算和经典子空间维度；即使在应用经典构型恢复后，该优势仍然存在。

Conclusion: SKQD在含噪设备上存在固有的噪声脆弱性（表现为指数深度惩罚），但通过近似编译技术压缩电路可以有效缓解该问题，使SKQD在当前噪声硬件上仍能保持实际效用。

Abstract: Sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization (SKQD) is a paradigmatic example of a quantum-centric supercomputing workflow that combines the convergence structure of Krylov quantum diagonalization with classical sampling-based post-processing. It provides convergence guarantees assuming that the important computational-basis configurations can be sampled from a set of Krylov states with sufficient probability. We analyze this assumption under depolarizing noise, deriving shot-count resource estimates that expose an exponential depth penalty, and perform experiments on current noisy hardware with the one-dimensional single-impurity Anderson model on a 20-site (40-qubit) instance. Device noise breaks the practical convergence predicted by the noiseless SKQD analysis, but approximate compilation techniques can compress the Krylov time-evolution circuits before execution. The compressed circuits accumulate less hardware noise, recover the expected energy convergence, and reduce both the quantum shot budget and the classical subspace dimension, remaining beneficial even when classical configuration recovery is applied.

</details>


### [375] [Data-driven structural diagnostics and autonomous alignment of complex optical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05984)
*Peng Zhu,Chen Chen,Shuchang Ma,Dezhou Deng,J. F. Chen,Peng Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高精细度光学腔作为灵敏探针的数据驱动框架，用于复杂自由空间光学系统的结构诊断与闭环控制，可在数十秒内实现冷启动收敛，为大规模中性原子量子处理器中光学架构的自主对准和持续维护提供了快速、可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模中性原子量子处理器的发展，需要自主地对齐和维持复杂的自由空间光学系统，但当前缺乏能够快速实现冷启动收敛的自动化诊断与控制系统。

Method: 利用高精细度光学腔作为灵敏探针，引入数据驱动的结构诊断与闭环控制框架，实现对自由空间光学系统的自主诊断和控制。

Result: 该方法实现了在数十秒内的冷启动收敛，展示了快速且可扩展的诊断-控制能力。

Conclusion: 该研究为复杂自由空间光学架构的自主对准与持续维护建立了一种快速、可扩展的诊断控制方法论，有望支撑大规模中性原子量子处理器的发展。

Abstract: The quest for autonomous alignment and maintenance of complex free-space optical systems is increasingly urgent for large-scale neutral-atom quantum processors. Leveraging a high-finesse optical cavity as a sensitive probe, we introduce a data-driven framework for structural diagnostics and closed-loop control that achieves cold-start convergence within tens of seconds. These results establish a rapid, scalable diagnostic-control methodology for autonomous alignment and continuous maintenance of complex free-space optical architectures.

</details>


### [376] [Trace-class spectra of irreducible Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06957)
*Franco Fagnola,Zheng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限模玻色Fock空间上不可约高斯量子马尔可夫半群在预对偶生成元上的谱性质，分别对稳定、严格不稳定和周期临界漂移三种情形给出了完整的谱刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不可约高斯量子马尔可夫半群的谱结构是该领域的基础问题。此前对于稳定漂移情形，多项式特征值（由漂移矩阵生成）并不能穷尽迹类点谱，因此需要更完整的谱分析来刻画这些半群的动力学行为，特别是在不同漂移机制（稳定/不稳定/临界周期）下谱的差异。

Method: 利用特征函数（characteristic functions）、高斯扩散半群以及对偶半群的有界特征算子等工具，对不同漂移机制下的预对偶生成元谱进行分析。在稳定情形，通过不可约性与唯一忠实正规不变态的存在性的等价关系建立基础；在临界周期情形，通过包含位移参数的显式谱公式得到水平半直线或抛物区域。

Result: 稳定漂移下：谱和近似点谱为闭左半平面，点谱为开左半平面加零点（多项式特征值不穷尽迹类点谱）。严格不稳定漂移下：谱仍为闭左半平面，但点谱为空，开左半平面加零点属于剩余谱。周期临界漂移下：得到包含位移参数的显式谱公式，谱为水平半直线或抛物区域。

Conclusion: 完成了稳定、严格不稳定和周期临界漂移三种情形下不可约高斯量子马尔可夫半群预对偶生成元的完整谱刻画。非周期临界漂移情形仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We determine the spectrum of the predual generator of an irreducible Gaussian quantum Markov semigroup on the trace-class operators over a finite-mode bosonic Fock space in the stable, strictly unstable, and periodic critical drift regimes. For stable drift, irreducibility is equivalent to the existence of a unique faithful normal invariant state. The spectrum and approximate point spectrum are the closed left half-plane, whereas the point spectrum is the open left half-plane together with zero. Thus the polynomial eigenvalues generated by the drift matrix do not exhaust the trace-class point spectrum. If the drift has an eigenvalue with strictly positive real part, the spectrum is again the closed left half-plane, but the point spectrum is empty and the open left half-plane together with zero belongs to the residual spectrum. For periodic critical drift, we obtain an explicit spectral formula that includes the displacement parameter and yields horizontal half-lines or parabolic regions. The proofs use characteristic functions, Gaussian diffusion semigroups, and bounded eigenoperators of the dual semigroup. The nonperiodic critical drift case remains open.

</details>


### [377] [The $I_{3322}$ Bell inequality requires infinite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06038)
*Andrea Coladangelo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了$I_{3322}$不等式的一个长期猜想：该不等式不存在有限维量子策略能达到其最大违反，但存在无限维量子策略可以达到。这是最简单的Bell场景中此类现象的首个证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $I_{3322}$不等式是最简单的（每方三个问题、两个答案）二分Bell不等式，但自2010年Pál和Vértesi猜想以来，一直未证明其具有"最大违反仅在无限维量子策略中可实现"的性质。此前仅在更复杂的Bell相关性（五问题三答案）中证明了类似性质，而$I_{3322}$恰好是最简单可能出现该现象的场景，证明其具有该性质的难度更高。

Method: 本文提供了一个完整的证明。近似证明由GPT 5.5 Pro经过多轮交互后产生，包含了所有核心思想；随后作者对证明进行了实质性修订，以确保其完备性、正确性和清晰度。

Result: 成功证明了$I_{3322}$不等式的最大量子违反只能由无限维量子策略实现，任何有限维量子策略都无法达到该最大值，从而证实了Pál和Vértesi的猜想。

Conclusion: 这项工作是量子信息理论中的一个重要进展，为理解量子关联的维度效应提供了最简Bell场景中的确定性证据，表明即使在最简单的Bell实验中，量子策略的维度也可能对可实现的违反程度产生本质性影响。

Abstract: The $I_{3322}$ inequality is one of the simplest bipartite Bell inequalities, with three possible questions and two possible answers per party. Yet, despite its simplicity, it has long been conjectured by Pál and Vértesi (Physical Review A, 2010) to possess the following quirk: no finite-dimensional quantum strategy can attain its maximal violation, but an infinite-dimensional strategy can. A Bell correlation, with five questions and three answers per party, that provably possesses the same property has since been discovered by Coladangelo and Stark (Nature Communications, 2020). However, proving that the same property holds for $I_{3322}$, which lives in the simplest Bell scenario in which such a phenomenon could occur, has remained elusive. Here, we provide a proof of this conjecture.
  An approximate proof, containing all the main ideas, was produced by GPT 5.5 Pro after various rounds of interaction. The presentation of this proof was revised substantially by the author for completeness, correctness, and clarity.

</details>


### [378] [Propagating sparsely supported states with time-dependent neural quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06054)
*Lexin Ding,Markus Reiher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出插值采样方法用于时间依赖的神经量子态（NQS）传播，解决初始态与时间导数支撑集不匹配的问题，并通过配置-时间联合采样和Krylov子空间混合提议策略提高采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间依赖NQS方法难以传播具有稀疏支撑集的初始态，因为波函数与其时间导数之间存在固有支撑集不匹配问题，而传统的Born分布及其变体采样无法有效处理这类物理相关的场景。

Method: (1) 引入插值采样方法，从介于波函数和其时间导数之间的分布中进行重要性采样；(2) 结合全局时间变分原理；(3) 提出配置-时间联合采样方案，将自旋构型和时间同时视为随机变量；(4) 提出混合提议策略，纳入Krylov子空间信息。

Result: 在二维横场Ising模型上验证，插值采样能够精确传播标准波函数采样无法处理的极度尖峰态，并显著提高采样效率。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了时间依赖NQS方法适用的物理场景范围，同时挑战了以Born分布及其近似变体作为默认采样基础的传统范式。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQSs) have emerged as a powerful ansatz for quantum dynamics. Their high entanglement capacity promises to overcome the entanglement barrier. However, existing time-dependent NQS methods are ill-equipped to propagate sparsely supported initial states due to the inherent mismatch between the supports of such states and their time derivatives. To address this issue, we introduce the interpolation sampling method as a novel form of importance sampling, where samples are drawn from a distribution that interpolates between the wave function and its time derivative. Combined with a global-in-time variational principle, we demonstrate with the example of the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model that interpolation sampling allows for accurate propagation of extremely peaked states that standard wave function sampling could not tackle. We further improve sampling efficiency by introducing (i) a configuration-time joint sampling scheme where spin configurations and time are both treated as random variables, and (ii) a hybrid strategy for sample proposal that incorporates the knowledge of a Krylov subspace. Our work extends the scope of time-dependent NQS methods to a wider range of physically relevant scenarios, while challenging the prevailing reliance on the Born distribution and its close variants as the default basis for sampling.

</details>


### [379] [Spectral Criterion for Disorder-Free Localization of Quantum Walks on Hypercube: QBN Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07267)
*Ce Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子伯努利噪声框架下研究超立方体上自旋耦合量子游走的无序无关局域化，推导出了无序无关局域化的一般判据，并在d=4维Grover游走中显式构造了非均匀不动点，证明其无序无关局域化发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序无关局域化（disorder-free localization）是近年来量子游走与多体物理中的热点问题，它挑战了传统认为局域化必须依赖无序的观念。作者希望在量子伯努利噪声框架下建立此类局域化的严格判据，并在一个具体的高维模型（超立方体上的量子游走）中验证其发生条件。

Method: 在量子伯努利噪声（QBN）框架下对超立方体上的自旋耦合量子游走进行建模。首先推导一般性判据：无序无关局域化当且仅当约化酉演化的某个谱子空间中存在一个位置边际概率非均匀的向量。随后针对Grover游走，在d=4维空间中选取距离依赖耦合参数φ_σ=|σ|π/(n+1)，在所有+1自旋构型下显式构造非均匀不动点。

Result: 成功推导出无序无关局域化的一般判据（谱子空间存在非均匀位置边际概率向量当且仅当局域化发生），并在d=4维、全+1自旋构型下为带距离依赖耦合的Grover游走显式构造了非均匀不动点，从而证明了该模型中无序无关局域化的存在性。

Conclusion: 该研究为无序无关局域化提供了统一的谱判定框架，并首次在超立方体Grover游走中给出了显式的非均匀不动点构造，严格证明了无序无关局域化的发生，为量子游走中该类现象的理论研究提供了新工具与具体实例。

Abstract: We study disorder-free localization in a spin-coupled quantum walk on the hypercube within the quantum Bernoulli noises framework. We derive a general criterion for disorder-free localization: it occurs if and only if some spectral subspace of a reduced unitary evolution contains a vector whose position marginal probability is non-uniform. For the Grover walk with distance-dependent coupling \(φ_σ=|σ|π/(n+1)\) in dimension \(d=4\), we explicitly construct a non-uniform fixed point in the all \(+1\) spin configuration. This implies that disorder-free localization occurs in our model.

</details>


### [380] [Multi-copy and Catalytic Superactivation of Genuine Multipartite Nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07380)
*Bora Ulu,Mirjam Weilenmann,Nicolas Brunner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种GMNL（真多体贝尔非局域性）的"超激活"效应：两拷贝二可分（非GMNL）关联经过局部接线后可产生GMNL关联，且这种效应可在任意多方数、量子可实现关联以及单拷贝催化协议中实现，质疑了GMNL定义的运算意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GMNL用于刻画多个远距离观测者之间全局贝尔非局域关联，其常用定义基于双可分模型（由非信号关联构成）。作者希望探究该定义框架下GMNL是否能被"超激活"，即非GMNL的关联能否通过局部操作转化为GMNL，进而检验该定义的合理性。

Method: 作者通过将两拷贝的二可分（非GMNL）关联进行局部接线（local wiring），展示可获得GMNL关联；并进一步设计了单拷贝层面的催化协议（仅使用局部接线），证明超激活也可在单拷贝级别实现。

Result: 证明了超激活GMNL效应在多方数任意（任何参与者数量）情况下均可行，且对量子可实现关联同样成立；同时在单拷贝催化协议中也实现了该超激活效应。

Conclusion: 这一结果表明基于双可分模型的GMNL定义缺乏运算鲁棒性——非GMNL关联可通过局部操作被激活为GMNL，从而对该定义的运算意义提出根本性质疑。

Abstract: Genuine multipartite Bell nonlocality (GMNL) aims at capturing correlations between many distant observers that are globally Bell nonlocal. A commonly used definition for GMNL is based on biseparable models constructed from non-signalling correlations. Here we uncover an effect of superactivation of GMNL in this framework. Specifically, by locally wiring together two copies of a biseparable correlation, i.e. not GMNL, one can obtain another correlation that is GMNL. We show that this is possible for any number of parties, and for quantum-realizable correlations.
