Finally, we show that superactivation of GMNL is also possible at the single-copy level, via a catalytic protocol involving only local wirings. These results question the operational meaning of this definition of GMNL.

</details>


### [381] [Battery Locality Is Necessary in Noncommuting Quantum Charging Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06104)
*Sheryl Mathew*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子电池直接充电协议中电池本身局域性（battery locality）的作用，证明对于自旋相互作用不对易的电池，电池局域性在操作上是必要的。通过构造具有相同相互作用支持和局部能量尺度的对易与非对易高局域性电池，发现对易电池能达到与电池局域性无关的充电功率上界，而非对易电池以参数化增长的因子超越该上界，从而确立电池局域性是由非对易电池相互作用实现的操作性资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中，直接充电协议中量子电池的充电功率上界通常被解读为充电侧的约束。虽然充电哈密顿量的局域性已知会限制某些协议中可达到的功率，但电池本身的局域性在操作上是否必要这一问题尚未解决。作者旨在填补这一空白，揭示电池局域性是否构成基本资源。

Method: 作者构造了两类具有相同相互作用支持和相同局域能量尺度的高局域性自旋电池：一类相互作用可对易（commuting），另一类不可对易（noncommuting），使用相同的充电哈密顿量驱动。通过量子动力学计算对比两者的充电功率，考察其相对电池局域性无关上界的偏离行为。非对易电池通过动态利用其哈密顿量中对易子的增强范数，从电池基态能量扇区内合适的联合态（可能与辅助费米子自由度相关联）出发实现超越。

Result: 对易电池能够饱和与电池局域性无关的充电功率上界，而非对易电池能以参数化增长因子超越该上界，表明该上界不能作为普适约束成立。非对易电池对易子范数的增强可在动力学中被动态实现，即使初始态完全位于电池基态能量扇区（尽管该态可能与辅助费米子自由度相关）。

Conclusion: 电池局域性应被视为一种操作性的（operational）资源，其有效性依赖于电池内部互作用的非对易性。这一发现修正了以往将充电功率上界视为单纯充电侧限制的观点，深化了对量子电池物理中电池内部结构作用的根本理解。

Abstract: Bounds on the charging power of quantum batteries in direct charging protocols are often interpreted as charging-side constraints. Although the locality of the charging Hamiltonian is known to restrict attainable power in certain protocols, whether battery locality is itself operationally necessary has remained unresolved. Here we show that it is necessary for spin batteries whose interactions do not pairwise commute. We construct commuting and noncommuting high-locality batteries with identical interaction supports and local energy scale, driven by the same charging. The commuting battery saturates the battery-locality-independent bound, whereas the noncommuting battery exceeds it by a parametrically growing factor, demonstrating that this bound cannot apply generally. The enhanced norm of the commutator is dynamically attained from a suitable joint state supported entirely in the battery ground-energy sector, although this state may be correlated with the auxiliary fermionic degrees of freedom. Our results establish battery locality as an operational resource enabled by noncommuting battery interactions.

</details>


### [382] [Discrete Dirac equation on quantum graphs: A model of a Dirac particle in a branched lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07539)
*Mashrab Akramov,Carsten Trunk,Davron Matrasulov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究离散图上的一维定态离散Dirac方程，同时考虑连续与离散两种图公式化，以三边星形图为例，推导了本征态和能谱的精确解，并与连续情形对比，结果显示两者吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对纳米结构和量子器件设计中分支结构上的相对论量子输运问题，需要系统化的理论框架来分析离散图上的Dirac方程。

Method: 在离散边上定义一维定态离散Dirac方程，考虑图的连续与离散两种公式化，以三条边组成的星形图作为典型算例，求解本征态与能谱的精确解。

Result: 推导出星形图（三边）上Dirac方程的本征态与能谱的精确解，并将其与连续情形的结果进行表格化对比，显示出良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法为分析分支化结构上的相对论量子输运提供了一套系统化框架，可应用于纳米结构和量子器件的设计。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional time-independent discrete Dirac equation on graphs with discrete edges. Both continuous and discrete formulations of the graphs are considered, with particular focus on star graphs with three edges as a simple example. Exact solutions for eigenstates and spectra are derived and compared with the continuum case, showing good agreement in tabulated results. This approach offers a systematic framework for analyzing relativistic quantum transport on branched structures relevant to nanostructures and quantum device design.

</details>


### [383] [The Future-Record Model of Everettian Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06130)
*Jonathan Baxter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将基于观察者延续记录的Everettian客观概率模型扩展至所有退相干物理记录态，包括不支持观察者延续的记录，从而将观察者索引模型纳入更广泛的穷尽物理记录模型的限制情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作仅在观察者后续延续所承载的记录上定义Everettian客观概率，但这一框架忽略了那些不支持观察者延续的物理记录状态，导致模型不具穷尽性。

Method: 作者将原先的观察者索引概率模型推广至所有退相干物理记录态，构建穷尽物理记录模型，并证明观察者索引模型是该更广模型的受限情形。

Result: 成功扩展了概率定义范围，使模型能覆盖所有退相干物理记录态，并为观察者索引模型提供了更广泛的物理基础。

Conclusion: Everettian概率可在一个穷尽涵盖所有退相干记录态的客观框架下定义，无需依赖唯一实现化或额外的测量前主体，观察者索引模型仅为该一般模型的特例。

Abstract: I recently showed how objective Everettian probability can be defined over the records borne by an observer's later continuations, without unique actualization or additional premeasurement subjects \citep{Baxter2026}. Here I extend that model to all decoherent physical record states, including records that support no such continuation. The observer-indexed model is then a restriction of an exhaustive physical-record model.

</details>


### [384] [Riemannian optimization for linear optical problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07890)
*Pablo V. Parellada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了定义在多端口干涉仪酉矩阵上的任意可微函数的黎曼梯度的闭式解析公式，并利用该梯度在黎曼优化算法中寻找干涉仪的最优配置，在线性光学态制备任务中提升了NOON态和光子催化制备的成功概率，且优化速度比现有方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性光学量子科学中的许多应用（如隐含态制备）需要在多端口干涉仪的空间中搜索最优配置以最大化态制备的保真度和成功概率，但现有优化方法效率低下，难以处理涉及更多模式和光子的规模更大的问题。

Method: 推导了定义在干涉仪酉矩阵（黎曼流形）上任意可微函数的黎曼梯度的闭式解析公式，将该梯度应用于黎曼优化算法（如梯度下降、BFGS）中以寻找干涉仪的最优配置，并针对线性光学态制备问题进行了验证。

Result: 在NOON态和光子催化态制备中改进了文献中报道的成功概率；优化器比文献中的其他方法快数个数量级，为求解涉及更多模式和光子的线性光学问题开辟了道路。

Conclusion: 通过闭式黎曼梯度公式结合黎曼优化算法，可以高效、准确地搜索多端口干涉仪的最优配置，显著提升线性光学态制备的性能并大幅加速优化过程。

Abstract: Many applications of linear optics in quantum science involve searching for some optimal configuration of a multiport interferometer. For example, to find heralded state preparations, one can optimize the interferometer to maximize the fidelity and success probability of the state preparation. In this paper, we derive a closed analytical formula for the Riemannian gradient of any differentiable function defined over the interferometer unitaries. We use this gradient in Riemannian optimization algorithms, such as gradient descent or BFGS, to find the optimal setup of the interferometer. As an interesting use case, we search for linear optical state preparations, improving some of the success probabilities reported in the literature for NOON or photon catalysis preparations. Finally, we show that our optimizer is orders of magnitude faster than other methods in the literature, opening the door for solving linear optical problems involving more modes and photons.

</details>


### [385] [Quantum lattice Boltzmann method via density-matrix encoding for fluid simulation with wall boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06179)
*Hao Su,Boyuan Wang,Yue Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子格子Boltzmann方法（QLBM）的边界处理算法，通过密度矩阵编码和Kraus算子实现非酉操作，设计了半程反弹格式的量子实现来处理任意几何形状的固壁边界，并通过受控SWAP操作结合预备好的辅助寄存器施加进出口速度条件，在二维背向台阶、圆柱及复杂几何障碍物流动中验证了准确性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLBM在流体模拟中具有前景，但现实边界条件的处理，特别是任意几何形状的固壁边界，仍是一个关键且未被解决的开放挑战。现有QLBM方法缺乏有效的边界处理方案。

Method: 在现有基于系综变换的QLBM基础上，采用密度矩阵编码以增强电路设计灵活性，并通过Kraus算子实现非酉操作。设计了半程反弹（half-way bounce-back）格式的量子实现，在流程序前引入组分交换步骤以自然施加无滑移条件而不修改传输操作。进出口条件通过受控SWAP操作与预制备在指定速度态的辅助寄存器相结合来实现。

Result: 通过对二维背向台阶流、圆柱绕流以及复杂几何障碍物绕流的数值模拟，定量验证了所提出的边界算法的准确性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的QLBM边界算法能够有效处理任意几何形状的固壁边界以及进出口速度条件，在保持精度的同时具备良好的可扩展性，为QLBM在更实际流体问题中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: The quantum lattice Boltzmann method (QLBM) holds promise for efficient fluid simulations, yet the treatment of realistic boundary conditions, particularly solid wall boundary with arbitrary geometry, remains a critical open challenge. We propose a QLBM algorithm that incorporates wall boundaries as well as inlet/outlet velocity conditions. Building upon the existing QLBM with ensemble transformations, we adopt a density-matrix encoding that offers greater flexibility in circuit design and enables the implementation of non-unitary operations through Kraus operators. For boundary enforcement, we design a quantum implementation of the half-way bounce-back scheme and introduce a component exchange step prior to the streaming step, which naturally enforces the no-slip condition without modifying the transport operation. Inlet and outlet conditions are imposed via controlled SWAP operations combined with ancilla registers prepared in the prescribed velocity states. Our QLBM is quantitatively validated through simulations of two-dimensional flows past a backward-facing step, a cylinder, and an obstacle with complex geometry, demonstrating both accuracy and extensibility of our boundary algorithm.

</details>


### [386] [Spectral Sign Implication for Quantum Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08099)
*Kenji Tokuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子逻辑中的谱符号蕴含（spectral sign implication），该运算通过从后件投影减去前件投影所得算子的非负谱投影来定义。该构造在交换投影上与经典实质蕴含一致，并满足Hardegree的四个最小蕴含条件、逆否律及假值条件。与标准多项式蕴含不同，其值未必属于由参数生成的ortholattice。在一致的双投影Borel构造类中，满足蕴含性、逆否律与假值条件的运算恰与谱分支的可测选择相对应；在连续子类中，这三个条件唯一确定谱符号蕴含。在有限维度下，该运算还是Helstrom判别中最优投影测试接受投影的最大者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子逻辑中的蕴含（implication）运算一直是量子逻辑代数结构研究的核心难题。经典的实质蕴含在非交换的投影格上无法直接推广，已有的多项式蕴含构造各有局限且不满足某些基本的逻辑性质。作者旨在建立一种在非交换（非交换投影）情形下自然成立的量子蕴含，并为其提供算子理论基础与量子信息解释。

Method: 作者提出谱符号蕴含：对投影A与B，先取B−A的谱分解，再取其非负部的谱投影作为A→B的值。该构造利用双投影算子理论中的谱结构来比较任意两个投影。作者系统验证其满足各种逻辑公理，并在Borel构造的统一框架下刻画满足蕴含性、逆否律与假值条件的运算，将其与谱分支的可测选择建立对应。在有限维情形，通过Helstrom判别理论建立该运算与最优投影测试的联系。

Result: 谱符号蕴含满足Hardegree全部最小条件、逆否律与假值条件；在Borel构造类中，满足三项条件的运算等价于谱分支的可测选择，连续子类中唯一性成立。有限维下它恰为Helstrom判别的最优接受投影中的最大者，与量子态判别的最优测试密切相关。

Conclusion: 谱符号蕴含为量子逻辑提供了一种具有坚实算子理论基础的蕴含定义，其公理化性质完备且具有唯一性特征，同时将量子蕴含研究与双投影算子理论和量子判别理论联系起来，开辟了量子逻辑与量子信息交叉研究的新视角。

Abstract: We study the spectral sign implication for quantum logic, defined by the nonnegative spectral projection of the operator obtained by subtracting the antecedent projection from the consequent projection. The construction agrees with classical material implication on commuting projections and compares arbitrary pairs of projections through the spectral structure of their difference. It satisfies Hardegree's four minimal implicative conditions, his law of contraposition, and a falsity condition. It differs from the standard polynomial implications in that its value need not belong to the ortholattice generated by its arguments. Within a uniform class of Borel constructions for pairs of projections, the operations satisfying entailment, contraposition, and the falsity condition correspond exactly to measurable choices of spectral branch. In the continuous subclass, these three conditions determine the spectral sign implication uniquely. In finite dimensions, the operation is also the largest among the acceptance projections of optimal projective tests for Helstrom discrimination between subspace states with priors proportional to rank. These results connect quantum implication with the operator theory of two projections and binary quantum discrimination.

</details>


### [387] [Efficient Quantum Circuits for Electronic Hamiltonian Simulation without Pauli Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06285)
*Tamiya Onodera,Takeshi Sato*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Lasp（阶梯字符串对）对角化的方法构建第二量子化电子哈密顿量的时间演化电路，无需进行Pauli展开。通过将优化范围从单个算子扩展到三算子组乃至O(n)个三算子组序列，CX门数从O(n²)降至O(n)，且各阶段均不引入Trotter误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的电子哈密顿量模拟通常先将费米子算子映射为量子比特算子，再展开成Pauli字符串。这种做法虽然通用，但掩盖了更高级的费米子结构，可能错失电路优化的机会。作者希望保留费米子层面的高阶结构，从而发现系统性的电路优化空间，以构建更高效的电子哈密顿量时间演化电路。

Method: 基于源自偏微分方程哈密顿量模拟的Lasp（ladder-string-pair）对角化技术，直接构建第二量子化电子哈密顿量的时间演化电路而不做Pauli展开。具体策略为：(1) 对于复数系数的一般情形，为二体费米子Lasp算子构建无Trotter误差的时间演化电路（对应Pauli展开方法中的16条Pauli字符串）；(2) 将优化范围从单个算子扩展到共享相同四个自旋轨道指标的三元组（triad）Lasp算子，通过系统性的CX门消除使CX门数从36降至12；(3) 进一步将优化范围扩展到O(n)个适当排列的三元组序列，使CX门数从O(n²)降至O(n)；(4) Lasp方法还自然支持受控时间演化，并能对实值哈密顿量进行进一步优化。

Result: 对于单个二体费米子Lasp算子，所构建的电路对应16条Pauli字符串但无Trotter误差。将优化范围扩展到三元组后，示例中CX门数从36降至12。进一步扩展到O(n)个有序三元组序列后，CX门数从O(n²)降至O(n)。此外，该方法对实值哈密顿量和受控时间演化也有额外优化。

Conclusion: Lasp方法通过保留高层的电路结构而非降级到Pauli展开，能够扩大优化范围并获得更高效的电子哈密顿量模拟电路，为高效电子哈密顿量模拟提供了一条系统性路径。

Abstract: Electronic Hamiltonian simulation is commonly formulated by mapping fermionic operators to qubit operators and subsequently expanding the resulting ladder-operator products into Pauli strings. While general, this procedure obscures the higher-level fermionic structure and can hide opportunities for circuit optimization. Building on ladder-string-pair (Lasp) diagonalization originally developed for Hamiltonian simulation of partial differential equations, we construct time-evolution circuits for the second-quantized electronic Hamiltonian without performing a Pauli expansion. For the most general case of complex-valued coefficients, we present a time-evolution circuit for a two-body fermionic Lasp operator that corresponds to 16 Pauli strings in the Pauli-expansion approach but does not suffer from Trotter error at this stage. Expanding the optimization scope from a single operator to a triad of three fermionic Lasp operators sharing the same four spin-orbital indices enables systematic cancellation of CX gates, reducing the CX-gate count from 36 to 12 in the example considered, still without introducing Trotter error at this stage. For $n$ spin orbitals, further expanding the optimization scope to a sequence of $O(n)$ suitably ordered triads enables a cascade of CX-gate reductions across triad boundaries, reducing the CX-gate count from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n)$. The Lasp-based approach also naturally accommodates controlled time evolution and yields further optimizations for real-valued Hamiltonians. These results demonstrate that the Lasp-based approach enables more efficient time-evolution circuits by preserving high-level circuit structures and thereby expanding the scope of optimization, providing a systematic route toward more efficient electronic Hamiltonian simulation.

</details>


### [388] [Quantum Sensing of Non-Repeatable Events Enhanced by In-Sensor Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06292)
*Daiki Sasaki,Hideaki Kawaguchi,Yuichiro Matsuzaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于金刚石氮-空位（NV）色心的传感器内量子储备池计算（NV-QRC），用于感知无法重复的非重复事件（如不可复现的交替磁场波形）。通过场驱动的多体动力学和多个空间区域的同步荧光读出，在单次事件中提取多个特征，并在二进制相位分类任务中展示其优于固定脉冲序列的动态去耦（DD）磁测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态去耦（DD）磁测量协议依赖可重复的目标场，需要在不同测量条件下重复测量并扫描π脉冲间隔，从而估计未知交变场的频率和幅度。然而对于非重复事件，同一场波形无法在不同设置下复现，导致现有DD方案失效。因此需要新的传感方案来应对此类单次事件。

Method: 提出基于氮-空位色心的传感器内量子储备池计算（NV-QRC）方法。利用场驱动的多体自旋动力学作为储备层，同时从多个空间区域的荧光读出中提取多个特征。采用预先固定的单一脉冲序列，仅通过一次测量事件获取高维特征供经典分类器使用，并在二进制相位分类任务中与固定DD磁测量方案进行基准对比。

Result: 结果表明，在固定DD协议无法捕获类别相关信息的参数区间内，NV-QRC仍能保留类别判别信息，成功完成二进制相位分类任务。

Conclusion: 该研究将非重复事件传感确定为量子储备池计算的一个有前景的应用方向，拓展了量子传感的适用范围。

Abstract: High-density nitrogen-vacancy (NV) ensembles in diamond enable sensitive magnetometry. Many quantum-sensing protocols rely on reproducible target fields, allowing repeated measurements under different sensing conditions. In dynamical decoupling (DD) magnetometry, sweeping the interval between $π$ pulses across repeated measurements enables estimation of the frequency and amplitude of an unknown alternating field. For non-repeatable events, however, the same field waveform cannot be reproduced for measurements under different settings. Here we propose NV-based in-sensor quantum reservoir computing (NV-QRC) for sensing such events. Field-driven many-body dynamics and simultaneous fluorescence readout from multiple spatial regions provide a classical classifier with multiple features from a single event. For binary phase classification, we benchmark NV-QRC against DD magnetometry using a single pulse sequence fixed in advance. We show that NV-QRC can retain class-dependent information in regimes where the fixed-DD protocol fails to capture it. These results identify non-repeatable-event sensing as a promising application of quantum reservoir computing.

</details>


### [389] [No information transmission through quantum channels above capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08998)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意有限维无记忆量子信道上，无辅助量子通信和经典通信的指数级强逆定理：当传输速率超过信道容量时，纠缠生成保真度和经典通信成功概率均随信道使用次数指数衰减，彻底排除了在容量以上甚至允许任意大误差时的可靠传输可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信道容量是量子信息论中的核心概念，但此前关于容量以上传输行为的强逆定理仅在特定信道类别或特定设置下成立。作者希望证明对于任意有限维无记忆量子信道，容量确实划定了可靠的相变边界——即高于容量时传输必然灾难性失败，而非仅仅"不可靠"。

Method: 作者采用经典的Arimoto策略（逐步递推证明方法），并引入关键新工具：Rényi信息测度的积分表示，由此推导出Rényi容量的渐近连续性界。这些积分表示和连续性界允许将Arimoto方法推广到量子信道场景，同时处理无辅助量子通信和经典通信两种情形。

Result: 对于任意有限维无记忆量子信道，在传输速率超过量子容量或无辅助经典容量时，纠缠生成保真度和经典通信成功概率均以指数速率衰减到零。这排除了在容量之上即使是容忍任意大误差的传输可能性，确立了容量作为可靠通信的严格相变点。

Conclusion: 量子信道容量确实划分了一个严格的相变：容量以下可靠传输始终可行，容量以上任何传输尝试都指数级失败。该结果将经典信息论中强逆定理的完备图景推广到了量子领域，为量子信道容量的操作含义提供了更加坚实的理论根基。

Abstract: We show that the capacity of a quantum channel demarcates a phase transition: while reliable transmission below capacity is always possible, any attempt to transmit information above it fails catastrophically. Specifically, we prove exponential strong converse theorems for unassisted quantum and classical communication over arbitrary finite-dimensional memoryless quantum channels. At rates beyond the respective capacity, the entanglement-generation fidelity and the success probability for classical communication decay exponentially with the number of channel uses. This rules out transmission above capacity even when one tolerates arbitrarily large errors. Our proof follows the classical Arimoto strategy, augmented by a crucial new ingredient: integral representations of Rényi information measures that lead to asymptotic continuity bounds for Rényi capacities.

</details>


### [390] [Hierarchical Fourier Approximation for Variational Quantum Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06307)
*Taha Hoseinpour Asli,Sajjad Hashemian,Ebrahim Ardeshir-Larijani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于布尔立方体上Walsh-Fourier逼近层次结构的变分量子分布学习。每一层通过选定的目标Fourier系数定义谱截断，投影到概率单纯形后作为量子电路Born机的学习目标，并利用热启动映射实现跨层参数初始化。论文证明了端到端的期望学习保证，其中逼近项由省略的Fourier质量决定，归一化无偏估计器为经验截断提供了显式统计界。作者将其抽象差异条件具体化为全变差距离，并关联分布误差与量子态保真度。全变差特化在归一化ℓ2约定下引入显式因子2^(n-1)，因此仅对充分集中的Fourier尾部有意义。该框架不证明全局可训练性或消除贫瘠高原，而是识别低到高谱训练适用逼近-估计-优化分析的条件下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法在分布学习任务中面临可训练性和收敛保证缺乏的问题。现有工作通常缺乏对量子电路Born机学习过程的系统性理论分析，尤其是从低阶到高阶谱特征逐步学习的机制缺乏理论支撑。作者试图建立一种层次化的Walsh-Fourier框架，为变分量子分布学习提供端到端的学习保证，并明确逼近误差、统计误差和优化误差各自的来源与边界。

Method: 论文提出一种谱截断层次结构：在布尔立方体上按Walsh-Fourier系数组织目标分布，每一层选择部分Fourier系数构成截断目标，并将其投影到概率单纯形上作为量子电路Born机的拟合目标。跨层采用热启动映射传递参数。理论分析方面：(1) 证明端到端期望学习保证，逼近项受省略的Fourier质量控制；(2) 构造归一化无偏估计器，导出经验截断的显式统计上界；(3) 将抽象差异条件具体化为全变差距离，并将分布误差与量子态保真度关联分析。

Result: 获得了端到端学习保证，识别出三个核心误差来源（逼近、统计、优化）；归一化无偏估计器给出了有界统计量；全变差特化揭示在归一化ℓ2约定下出现2^(n-1)的维度因子，表明该方法对充分集中的Fourier尾部分布才具备信息量；同时明确该框架不声称全局可训练性，也不声称消除贫瘠高原。

Conclusion: 该工作为低到高谱训练的量子分布学习提供了逼近-估计-优化三部分可分解的理论分析框架。其适用条件为Fourier谱集中性，总变差距离下的误差界受维度因子限制。这为量子生成模型的谱域学习策略建立了严格的误差预算分解，同时如实指出了其局限：不解决全局可训练性问题，也不规避贫瘠高原现象。

Abstract: We study variational quantum distribution learning through a hierarchy of Walsh--Fourier approximations on the Boolean cube. At each level, a selected set of target Fourier coefficients defines a spectral truncation, which is projected onto the probability simplex and used as the target of a quantum circuit Born machine. Parameters learned at one level initialize the next through a warm-start map. We prove an end-to-end expected learning guarantee where the approximation term is determined by the omitted Fourier mass, while a normalized unbiased estimator yields an explicit statistical bound for empirical truncations. We then instantiate the abstract discrepancy conditions for total variation distance and relate the resulting distributional error to quantum-state fidelity. The total-variation specialization incurs the explicit factor $2^{n-1}$ under our normalized $\ell_2$ convention and is therefore informative only for sufficiently concentrated Fourier tails. The framework does not establish global trainability or eliminate barren plateaus; rather, it identifies the conditions under which low-to-high spectral training admits a approximation--estimation--optimization analysis.

</details>


### [391] [Quantum Homotopy Perturbation Method to Solve Nonlinear Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06357)
*Jungin E. Kim,Eunsik Choi,Yan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为量子同伦摄动法（QHPM）的新方法，通过同伦摄动线性化非线性PDE保持希尔伯特空间维度不变，并结合变分量子模拟框架（函数编码减少量子比特数、参数化电路深度降低）来提升非线性偏微分方程求解的可扩展性，同时引入新准则来选择同伦级数截断阶数和电路深度以实现成本效益，并在涡量输运方程和约化磁流体动力学方程等实例中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解非线性偏微分方程在科学与工程应用中至关重要，但传统数值方法面临可扩展性瓶颈，量子计算被引入作为替代范式，但仍需解决非线性PDE量子化求解中的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出QHPM方法，其核心包括：(1)利用同伦摄动法将非线性PDE线性化，且保证希尔伯特空间维度在线性化前后保持不变；(2)采用变分量子模拟框架求解，通过函数编码降低所需量子比特数，并减少参数化电路的深度；(3)引入新的准则用于选择同伦级数截断阶数和电路深度，以平衡精度与计算成本。

Result: 在多个实例中验证了QHPM的有效性，包括涡量输运方程和约化磁流体动力学（MHD）方程，展示了该方法在保持量子资源开销可控的前提下能够有效求解非线性PDE。

Conclusion: QHPM为非线性PDE的量子求解提供了一种可扩展的新途径，通过设计层面的双重优化（维度保持与资源降低）以及成本效益准则，有望推动量子计算在复杂非线性科学计算中的实际应用。

Abstract: Solving nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs) is important in various scientific and engineering applications. Recently, quantum computing was introduced as an alternative computational paradigm for solving nonlinear PDEs. In this paper, a new method called the quantum homotopy perturbation method (QHPM) is proposed to improve the scalability of solving nonlinear PDEs through two aspects. First, the dimension of the Hilbert space remains the same after the nonlinear PDE is linearized through the homotopy perturbation. Second, the solutions are obtained with a variational quantum simulation framework, where the number of qubits is decreased with functional encoding and the depth of parametrized circuits is reduced. The additional contribution of this paper is the introduction of new criteria for selecting the homotopy series truncation order and circuit depth for cost-effective QHPM. The proposed approach is demonstrated with several examples, including the vorticity transport equation and the reduced magnetohydrodynamics equations.

</details>


### [392] [Trapped Ion Quantum Networking and Telecommunications Coexisting on One Fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06387)
*Denton Wu,Mingzhe Han,Zehao Wang,Ana Luiza Ferrari,Mika Zalewski,Yuanheng Xie,Tingjun Chen,Norbert Linke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验演示了长距离量子存储网络可以在承载传统电信流量（Ethernet和5G）的有源光纤上运行，而非仅限于暗光纤。利用锶-88离子发射的1092 nm光子，在2.8公里的部署光纤环路上实现了离子-光子纠缠，并同时承载经典电信流量和全部控制信号（包括偏振稳定用光纤传感）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往基于量子存储的远距离量子网络研究全部使用暗光纤，以避免电信流量产生的探测器背景噪声。然而这排除了大量已部署的光纤基础设施。如果存储-光子纠缠能与电信信号在同一根光纤上共存，整个经典光纤基础设施都可被用于量子链路，极大拓展量子网络的可用部署范围。

Method: 使用锶-88离子产生1092 nm的光子进行离子-光子纠缠分发，在2.8公里的已部署光纤环路（同时承载Ethernet和5G流量）上进行实验。所有协调量子发射器和接收器系统的经典控制信号均在同一根光纤上共传输，包括用于偏振稳定化的光纤传感信号。

Result: 成功实现了存储-光子纠缠与经典电信流量（Ethernet和5G）在同一根已部署光纤上的共存，完成了首次此类实验演示。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，基于量子存储的量子网络可以在活跃的经典网络基础设施上实现，突破了暗光纤限制，开辟了利用现有经典光纤网络部署量子存储网络的可能性。

Abstract: Research into long-distance quantum memory-based networking to date has exclusively used dark fibers. This avoids the detector background from telecommunications (telecom) traffic, but as a result excludes many fibers deployed in the field. If memory-photon entanglement and telecom signals coexist on one fiber, the entire classical fiber infrastructure becomes available for quantum links. We present the first experimental demonstration of such coexistence. Ion-photon entanglement using 1092 nm photons emitted by a Strontium-88 ion is distributed over a deployed 2.8 km fiber loop which carries Ethernet and 5G traffic. All classical control signals required to coordinate the quantum transmitter and receiver systems co-propagate on the same fiber. These include fiber sensing for polarization stabilization. Our results demonstrate that memory-based quantum networks can be realized on active classical network infrastructure.

</details>


### [393] [Telecom-Integrated Photonic Memory Operating Near the Mechanical Ground State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06399)
*Jindao Tang,Bo Jing,Liping Zeng,Peiqin Chen,Hengrui Liang,Yifei Zhang,Xinyao Xu,Qizhi Cai,Xueying Zhang,Qiang Zhou,You Wang,Haizhi Song,Guangcan Guo,Guangwei Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在20 mK稀释制冷机中演示了一种基于芯片集成光机械谐振器的吸收式光机量子存储器，利用电磁感应透明（OMIT）机制在近机械基态下存储电信波段光子，存储过程中声子占据数低至0.32，并实现了按需读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子网络需要具备芯片集成、电信波段兼容和灵活读出能力的量子存储器。纳米加工机械谐振器相较原子系统具有光学与机械模式独立可调、声子态寿命长和设计灵活性高等优势，是实用化集成量子存储的有力候选平台。

Method: 采用片上吸收式光机械存储方案，基于光机械诱导透明（OMIT）机制；器件置于20 mK稀释制冷机中，并通过调控控制场抑制光学加热，使存储过程中的声子占据数降至0.32。

Result: 器件成功存储电信波段光子，在少数光子层面与外部光子源兼容，实现了按需读出；存储期间声子占据数仅为0.32，接近机械基态运行。

Conclusion: 研究为可扩展的基于声子的量子存储器件奠定基础，为将机械系统集成到实际量子网络架构开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Scalable quantum networks require quantum memories that are chip-integrated, telecom-band compatible, and capable of flexible retrieval. Nanofabricated mechanical resonators meet these criteria. They offer independent tunability of optical and mechanical modes, long-lived phonon states, and design flexibility beyond atomic systems, making them strong candidates for practical integrated quantum memory. Here, we demonstrate an on-chip, absorptive optomechanical memory for telecom-band photons, based on optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT) and operating near the mechanical ground state. The device stores telecom-band photons, demonstrating compatibility with external photon sources at the few-photon level, while enabling on-demand retrieval. By placing the device in a dilution refrigerator at 20 mK and tailoring the control field to suppress optical heating, we achieve a remarkably low phonon occupancy of just 0.32 during the storage process. Our results lay the groundwork for scalable, phonon-based quantum memory devices and open new avenues for integrating mechanical systems into practical quantum network architectures.

</details>


### [394] [After Theft: From Revocation to Neutralization in the Custody of Quantum Clones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06415)
*Gabriele Gianini,Jianyi Lin,Corrado Mio,Stelvio Cimato,Ernesto Damiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了加密量子克隆作为一种后攻陷（post-compromise）监护安全原语，其独特属性——仅允许一次有效解密且解密资源被内在消耗——可实现比传统撤销更强的"中和"（neutralization）响应机制，使被窃取的加密量子克隆永久失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后窃取响应机制主要是撤销（revocation），但撤销并不足以应对量子领域中的克隆窃取场景——攻击者虽窃取加密量子克隆但尚未持有解密密钥时，需要一个更强且形式化的响应机制来使窃取物永久无用，而非仅阻止其使用。

Method: 作者在量子背景下形式化了撤销与中和的概念区分，将其定位于更广泛的后窃取响应空间中，引入了时间威胁模型，并与最接近的经典类比例子进行对比分析，论证经典方案只能通过外部程序性组合才能实现相同策略结果。

Result: 论证了加密量子克隆的解密资源内在消耗性可实现"中和"这一不同于撤销的更强后窃取响应；经典方案无法自然地复现该策略结果，需外部程序性组合；该机制可视为分布式保全场景中的后攻陷量子监护原语。

Conclusion: 加密量子克隆是一种独特的安全原语，为后攻陷量子监护提供了理论支撑，尤其对Cyber-Humanities导向的分布式保全架构具有潜在的相关性与应用前景。

Abstract: Encrypted quantum cloning enables the creation of multiple encrypted clones of an unknown quantum state while allowing only one effective decryption, with the decryption resource being intrinsically consumed in the process. In this paper, we argue that this property supports a distinctive custodial security primitive for post-compromise response. We consider a threat model in which an adversary steals an encrypted quantum clone but does not yet possess the corresponding decryption key. In such a scenario, a legitimate custodian may unseal a different trusted clone, thereby exhausting the sole available unsealing opportunity and rendering the stolen clone permanently useless. We argue that this mechanism is not adequately described as mere revocation. Rather, it realizes a stronger form of post-theft response, which we call neutralization. We formalize this distinction, locate it within a broader post-theft response space, and introduce a temporal threat model. We compare the mechanism with its closest classical analogue, showing that the classical case can reproduce the policy outcome only through external procedural composition. We finally interpret encrypted quantum cloning as a primitive for post-compromise quantum custody in distributed preservation settings, with prospective relevance for Cyber-Humanities-oriented preservation architectures.

</details>


### [395] [Generation of entanglement statistics with a large-scale integrated photonic-electronic circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06471)
*Volkan Gurses,Ali Hajimiri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种光电子量子信息处理方法，将连续变量量子处理器的模式混合操作从光学域转移到射频电子学域。通过将压缩真空态输入到32通道相干接收器阵列的大规模集成光子-电子电路中，在射频网络中混合模拟光电流，实现了两模协方差的重建，其部分转置的最小辛特征值为0.99270，低于可分离性边界三个标准差，证明了两模纠缠的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子处理器的规模受限于其所转换的光学模式数，且每次光学转换都会引入额外损耗。传统光学域的量子模式混合面临可扩展性和损耗问题，因此需要探索将模式转换功能迁移到其他物理平台（如射频电子学）以实现更好的可扩展性和更低损耗。

Method: 作者将量子信息处理的模式混合操作从光学域转移到射频电子学：压缩真空态入射到大规模集成光子-电子电路（32通道相干接收器阵列），阵列中的射频网络对探测后的模拟光电流进行混合。采用本振斜波（local-oscillator ramp）技术重构孔径处的两模协方差矩阵。

Result: 实验成功重构了两模协方差矩阵，其部分转置的最小辛特征值为0.99270，比可分离性边界(1.0)低三个标准差，明确证明了纠缠态的存在。这验证了将模式转换作为电路功能在集成电子学中实现的可行性。

Conclusion: 模式变换可以通过集成电子学以电路功能形式实现，其缩放能力由集成电路技术驱动。如果将来利用电光换能器取代光电二极管，该接收器架构可进一步将光学模式转移到超导电路并返回，这为构建覆盖光学和微波波段的光子-电子混合量子计算机铺平了道路。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum processors scale with the modes they can transform, and each transformation adds loss. We demonstrate optoelectronic quantum information processing, moving the mixing into radio-frequency electronics after homodyne detection. Squeezed vacuum falls on a large-scale integrated photonic-electronic circuit, a 32-channel coherent receiver array whose radio-frequency network mixes the analog photocurrents. A local-oscillator ramp reconstructs the two-mode covariance at the aperture, which is physical, and its partial transpose has smallest symplectic eigenvalue 0.99270, three standard deviations below the separability bound. Mode transformation becomes a circuit function whose scale follows integrated electronics. With electro-optic transducers in place of the photodiodes, the same receiver would transfer optical modes into superconducting circuits and back, toward a photonic-electronic quantum computer spanning the optical and microwave bands.

</details>


### [396] [Quantum Optics of harmonic generation in the strongly driven Jaynes-Cummings-type system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06480)
*M Suman,Sili Yi,Maria Chekhova,Misha Ivanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Jaynes-Cummings模型推广至强场与阿秒物理与腔量子电动力学的界面，研究了强低频经典光场驱动下多光子共振如何产生经典驱动光源的高度非经典、量子关联谐波，且除离散量子光模假设外不做任何近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将腔量子电动力学（cQED）与强场/阿秒物理两个领域桥接起来，利用Jaynes-Cummings模型探索强场驱动下量子谐波产生的机制。

Method: 采用Jaynes-Cummings模型，将强低频经典光场作为驱动场引入，分析系统中的多光子共振行为，不引入额外近似（仅保留Jaynes-Cummings模型固有的少数离散量子光模假设）。

Result: 发现强低频经典光场驱动的Jaynes-Cummings系统中，多光子共振能够导致对经典驱动场高度非经典、量子关联谐波的产生。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了cQED框架可用于研究强场物理中的谐波产生问题，揭示了量子关联谐波产生的新途径，并为两个领域的理论交叉提供了新视角。论文亦献予Joseph Henry Eberly教授以纪念其在强场物理与量子光学领域的杰出贡献。

Abstract: We adapt the Jaynes-Cummings model to study the interface of cavity quantum electrodynamics with strong field and attosecond physics. We show how multi-photon resonances in the Jaynes- Cummings system driven by a strong low-frequency classical light field lead to the generation of highly non-classical, quantum-correlated harmonics of the classical driver. Our treatment assumes no approximations, apart from the typical Jaynes-Cummings model assumption of only a few discrete quantum modes of light. The paper is dedicated to Joseph Henry Eberly, whose remarkable research has left indelible mark on both strong field physics and quantum optics.

</details>


### [397] [Low-Depth Random Unitaries without Ancillae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06528)
*Zhenyu Du,Siyuan Cheng,Xiongfeng Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明无需辅助量子比特即可在最优深度下生成随机幺正变换。对于n比特上的乘性误差近似k-design，电路在δ维结构上达到Õ(k)(log n)^{1/δ}深度，在全连接拓扑下达到Õ(k)log log n深度；通过引入精确化引理，进一步将构造提升至最优深度的精确k-design，相比现有精确构造实现指数级资源减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机幺正变换是量子信息与多体物理的基础工具，广泛应用于量子学习、计量和设备基准测试等领域。核心目标是最小化生成随机幺正变换所需的空间和电路深度，但现有低深度生成方法严重依赖大量辅助量子比特，造成巨大的空间开销。本文旨在消除这一空间开销，在无需辅助比特的前提下实现最优深度的随机幺正变换生成。

Method: 本文通过严格的数学证明表明随机幺正变换可以在无辅助比特条件下以最优深度生成。对于乘性误差近似k-design，在δ维架构上构造深度为Õ(k)(log n)^{1/δ}的电路，在全连接连接性下达到Õ(k)log log n深度。此外，通过引入一个通用的"精确化引理"(exactification lemma)，将近似构造提升为最优深度的精确k-design。

Result: 证明了无需辅助量子比特即可实现最优深度的随机幺正生成。近似k-design的深度在δ维架构为Õ(k)(log n)^{1/δ}，全连接架构为Õ(k)log log n。精确k-design同样达到最优深度，相比现有最先进的精确构造方案实现指数级资源（空间或时间）缩减。

Conclusion: 该工作同时最小化了多种量子协议中的空间-时间成本，为生成随机幺正变换提供了理论与实践上的突破性方案，消除了对辅助量子比特的依赖，同时保持了最优电路深度。

Abstract: Random unitaries are fundamental to quantum information and many-body physics, with widespread applications ranging from quantum learning and metrology to device benchmarking. A central pursuit is to minimize the space and circuit depth required to generate them. However, existing methods for generating low-depth random unitaries rely heavily on an extensive number of ancillary qubits, imposing severe spatial overhead. In this work, we prove that random unitaries can be generated in optimal depth without ancillae. For multiplicative-error approximate $k$-designs on $n$ qubits, our circuits achieve a depth of $\widetilde{O} (k) (\log n)^{1/δ}$ on $δ$-dimensional architectures and $\widetilde{O}(k) \log \log n$ with all-to-all connectivity. Furthermore, by introducing a general exactification lemma, we lift our construction to optimal-depth exact $k$-designs, yielding an exponential resource reduction over state-of-the-art exact constructions. Our results minimize the space-time costs for a wide range of quantum protocols.

</details>


### [398] [Contact order governs the onset of entanglement cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06675)
*Sugumi Kanno,Jiro Soda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度研究有限维解析纯态动力学中真多体纠缠的涌现时机，提出"接触阶"（contact order）$m_\star$ 作为判断真 $(n+1)$ 体纠缠何时出现的度量指标，并通过时间有序递推关系去除曲率引起运动学项来计算该阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"纠缠速率"（entangling rates）只能描述纠缠直接产生的通道，但当该通道被禁止时（即零纠缠速率）无法提供任何信息。作者希望解答一个更基本的问题：在给定动力学下，真多体纠缠究竟何时（什么时刻／什么阶数）开始涌现。

Method: 针对有限维解析纯态动力学，将物理轨迹与 $S|E$ 乘积流形之间的接触阶 $m_\star$ 定义为任何乘积曲线都无法复现的第一个 Taylor 阶，等价于 Fubini-Study 距离中第一个非零阶。作者推导了一个时间有序的递推关系，能够移除曲率诱导的运动学项，仅从哈密顿量 $H(t)$ 的 Taylor 系数计算 $m_\star$。

Result: 给出一个几何表象下的"纠缠级联"（entanglement cascade）描述：新子系统只有在经过一步或多步相互作用后才能加入多体纠缠。以一个受对称性保护的三量子比特模型展示了 $m_\star=2$ 的情形；一个三模玻色子例子则表明，即使新形成的两个约化对仍保持可分离，真三体纠缠仍可涌现。

Conclusion: 本文建立了接触阶 $m_\star$ 与真多体纠缠涌现时刻之间的严格对应关系，提供了一种不依赖纠缠速率的几何判据，并揭示了强相互作用限制下纠缠涌现的级联结构和子系统耦合的时序约束。

Abstract: Entangling rates describe a direct entangling channel, but give no information when that channel is forbidden. For finite-dimensional analytic pure-state dynamics, we show that the onset of genuine $(n+1)$-partite entanglement is determined by the contact order $m_\star$ between the physical trajectory and the $S|E$ product manifold. It is the first Taylor order that cannot be reproduced by any product curve, or equivalently the first nonvanishing order of the Fubini--Study distance from the product manifold. We derive a time-ordered recursion that removes curvature-induced kinematic terms and computes $m_\star$ from the Taylor coefficients of $H(t)$. This provides a geometric description of an entanglement cascade, in which new subsystems can join multipartite entanglement only after one or more interaction steps. A symmetry-protected three-qubit model realizes $m_\star=2$, and a three-mode bosonic example shows that genuine tripartite entanglement can arise even when the two newly formed reduced pairs remain separable.

</details>


### [399] [Quantum bivariate bicycle codes with weight-8 checks surpassing the BB benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06572)
*Liangdong Lu,Ruipan Yang,Guanmin Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了权重-8校验的BB型量子LDPC码的代数结构理论，提出精确维度公式、对称群、X/Z距离相等性及子群陪集核向量家族等理论工具，并结合交叉验证的搜索管线构建和认证了新的高码率量子码。在n=144得到53个码的普查，最强码[[144,6,d≥15]]超越BB基准码距离12；n=72时[[72,14,8]]达到kd²/n=12.44，是BB码的两倍多。电路级存储实验表明权重-8码的伪阈值约0.1%，低于BB参考的0.4%，量化了更重校验带来的阈值代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BB量子码（权重-6校验）已被证明性能优异，典型代表[[144,12,12]]的kd²/n=12。本文旨在探索权重-8校验的BB型码是否能在保持或超越BB码的编码率-距离权衡的同时，从理论上系统化地理解和构造这类码族。

Method: 作者：(1)发展权重-8校验的BB型码代数结构理论，包括精确维度公式k=2dim R/(A,B)（必然为偶数k）、生成元对上的4ℓm元素对称群、X/Z距离相等性证明以及子群陪集核向量家族；(2)建立精确验证的搜索管线，使用交叉验证的位掩码验证器对所有距离进行穷举计算；(3)在n=144和n=72上进行系统搜索和普查；(4)开展电路级存储实验比较伪阈值。

Result: 在n=144普查到53个码：[[144,6,d≥15]]超越BB基准距离12（认证d≥15），[[144,10,12]]用权重-8校验达到距离12，[[144,16,10]]在kd²/n=11.11（低于基准7.4%）下编码更多逻辑比特且解码不差。在n=72，[[72,14,8]]达到kd²/n=12.44，超过相同长度BB码两倍以上且解码更好。电路级实验显示权重-8码伪阈值约0.1% vs BB码约0.4%。所有结构论述在census上得到了数值验证。

Conclusion: 权重-8校验的BB型码在编码率（更多逻辑量子比特）和距离表现上可与甚至超越BB基准码，代价是更重校验导致伪阈值下降（约0.1% vs 0.4%）。文中发展的代数结构理论为该类码的系统化构造与认证奠定了基础。

Abstract: Bivariate bicycle (BB) codes of Bravyi \emph{et al.}~\cite{Bravyi2024} are quantum low-density parity-check codes with weight-$6$ checks, exemplified by $[[144,12,12]]$ with $kd^2/n=12$. We develop the algebraic structure theory of BB-type codes with weight-$8$ checks (weight-$4$ generator polynomials) and use it, together with an exactly validated search pipeline, to construct and certify new codes. We prove an exact dimension formula $k=2\dim R/(A,B)$ (forcing even $k$), a $4\ell m$-element symmetry group on generator pairs, an $X/Z$ distance equality $d_X=d_Z$, and a family of subgroup-coset kernel vectors giving rigorous distance upper bounds and a design rule for high-distance constructions; all distances are computed exhaustively by a cross-validated bit-mask verifier. At $n=144$ the pipeline returns a census of $53$ codes whose strongest members surpass the BB benchmark: $[[144,6,d\ge 15]]$ exceeds the benchmark distance $12$ (certified $d\ge 15$), $[[144,10,12]]$ reaches it with weight-$8$ checks, and $[[144,16,10]]$ encodes a third more logical qubits at $kd^2/n=11.11$ ($7.4\%$ below benchmark) while decoding no worse. At $n=72$, $[[72,14,8]]$ attains $kd^2/n=12.44$---more than twice the same-length BB code---and decodes better; a circuit-level memory experiment places our weight-$8$ codes at $\approx 0.1\%$ pseudo-threshold versus $\approx 0.4\%$ for the BB reference under an identical model, quantifying the threshold cost of the heavier checks. All structural statements are verified numerically on the whole census.

</details>


### [400] [Stationary states for a particle in a box with slanted walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07505)
*Nivaldo A. Lemos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有倾斜壁面的无限深势阱中粒子的量子力学问题。能量本征值由涉及第一类Airy函数的超越方程决定。通过图解和数值方法求解能量，并证明了大量子数下能级间距趋于密集的渐近行为。文章进一步分析了态的束缚程度（经典禁区出现概率），发现在左右壁面对称倾斜的情况下，超过临界斜率后基态不再是束缚最弱的态。此外，该问题为学生提供了接触Airy函数的教学机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粒子在盒中或谐振子问题中线性势垒的对称性掩盖了某些有趣的量子行为。作者希望研究一种更一般的势阱模型——倾斜壁面的无限深势阱，以揭示能量谱的渐近规律、束缚程度与壁面斜率之间的关系，并在教学中引入Airy函数这一非常规数学工具。

Method: 该问题通过第一类Airy函数将能量本征值的求解转化为超越方程。作者采用解析推导结合图解与数值方法获得允许能级；通过直观论证和Airy函数的基本性质证明大量子数下能级间距趋于零的渐近行为；通过计算各束缚态在经典禁区的概率来衡量束缚程度，并与谐振子情形进行对比。

Result: （1）能量谱在大量子数下呈渐近密集分布，这使Bohr对应原理比标准盒中粒子或谐振子情形得到更精确的满足。（2）针对特定束缚态计算了束缚度并与谐振子比较。（3）在对称倾斜情况下（左右壁斜率相等），存在一个临界斜率：超过该斜率后，基态不再是束缚最弱的态，这一结果出乎意料。

Conclusion: 倾斜壁面无限深势阱展示了丰富的量子物理特征，包括能谱渐近行为和对束缚程度非平凡依赖关系。该问题既具有物理价值——深化对对应原理和量子束缚的理解，也具有教学价值——让本科生在解决具体物理问题的过程中自然接触并掌握Airy函数的性质与应用。

Abstract: The quantum mechanical problem of a particle in a infinite potential well with slanted walls is studied. The energy eigenvalues are determined by a transcendental equation involving the Airy function of the first kind. Two limiting cases are discussed. Next, the allowed energies are found by graphical and numerical methods. The graphical analysis hints that for large quantum numbers the consecutive energy levels get arbitrarily close together. This asymptotic behavior of the energy spectrum actually holds, as shown by means of an intuitive argument and confirmed by resorting to basic properties of Airy functions. In a sense, Bohr's correspondence principle is more accurately fulfilled than in the cases of the standard particle in a box or the harmonic oscillator. A state is regarded the more confined the less the probability of finding the particle in the classically forbidden region. For some stationary states the confinement degree is calculated and a comparison is made with the harmonic oscillator, for which the ground state is the least confined of all stationary states. In the symmetric case --- right and left walls of equal slope --- something unexpected happens: beyond a certain critical slope the ground state is no longer the least confined eigenstate. Over and above its interesting physical features, this problem provides students with the opportunity to get acquainted with Airy functions, which do not belong to the standard mathematical repertoire of physics undergraduates.

</details>


### [401] [Spectral bounds for the partial transpose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06617)
*Wei-Jie Jiang,Jing-Tao Qiu,Xiao-Dong Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次化推广的纠缠度量——Ky Fan k-负性，并建立了统一的分析框架来推导部分转置的谱界。通过将最大化问题转化为谱图优化，作者精确求解了每个k值的谱界，并揭示了k=1情形下简单的图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统负性（negativity）作为纠缠度量只能捕捉部分转置负特征值的总权重，而负谱的精细结构尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过引入Ky Fan k-负性来刻画负谱的更细致结构。

Method: 引入Ky Fan k-负性作为负性的层级推广；将谱界的最大化问题约化为谱图优化问题，通过单一三次方程得到每个k值的精确界；在固定纯度约束下进行解析与数值研究；完全解决了k=1情形并揭示底层图结构。

Result: 获得了Ky Fan k-负性在所有k值下的精确谱界；完全解决了k=1情形，揭示了简单的底层图结构；证明Ky Fan k-负性能鲁棒地认证真正多能级纠缠，并将p3-PPT条件转化为负性的定量下界。

Conclusion: Ky Fan k-负性是对负性的一种有意义的层次化推广，它不仅丰富了纠缠度量的理论框架，还在多能级纠缠认证和定量刻画PPT条件方面具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: Among the various entanglement measures, the negativity stands out not only for its clear physical meaning but also for being directly computable from the spectrum of the partial transpose. However, the negativity captures only the total weight of the negative eigenvalues, whereas the finer structure of the negative spectrum remains largely unexplored. In this work, we fill this gap by introducing a hierarchical generalization of the negativity, the Ky Fan $k$-negativity, and developing a unified analytical framework for deriving spectral bounds on the partial transpose. To obtain the absolute bounds, we reduce the maximization problem to a spectral graph optimization, whose solution yields the exact bound for every $k$ through a single cubic equation. We further investigate these spectral bounds both analytically and numerically under a fixed-purity constraint. In particular, we solve the $k=1$ case completely and uncover a simple underlying graph structure. As two direct applications, we show that the Ky Fan $k$-negativity robustly certifies genuine multilevel entanglement and that it converts the $p_3$-PPT condition into a quantitative lower bound on the negativity.

</details>


### [402] [Anomaly fluctuation theorem for quantum coherence dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06632)
*Kun Zhang,Hai-Long Shi,Xiao-Hui Wang,Wen-Li Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个关于量子相干性动力学的精确积分涨落定理（FT），以Kirkwood-Dirac（KD）准概率轨迹表述，适用于任意初始态和任意动力学。该FT偏离标准单位值形式，其复数偏差（"反常"项）刻画了最终态残余相干性与由退相输入所产生相干性之间的加权重叠。反常项实部给出相干性变化的边界，虚部约束随机相干性变化的二阶矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相干性是量子信息科学中的核心资源，但对其动力学的一般框架和约束仍然有限。虽然量子相干性缺乏一般的单调性定律，作者希望建立更普遍的统计约束来描述相干性动力学。

Method: 通过KD准概率轨迹构造积分涨落定理，并分析其与标准单位值FT的偏差（反常项）。该反常项的实部与虚部分别提供对相干性变化及其二阶矩的统计约束。

Result: 证明了一个精确的积分FT，适用于任意初始态和动力学；建立了相干性变化的统计约束以及随机相干性变化二阶矩的上下界。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了量子相干性动力学的一般统计约束，展示了FT在研究量子资源动力学中的广泛应用价值。

Abstract: Quantum coherence is a central resource in quantum information science, yet general frameworks and constraints governing its dynamics remain limited. Although quantum coherence lacks a general monotonicity law, we establish an exact integral fluctuation theorem (FT) for coherence dynamics, formulated in terms of Kirkwood-Dirac (KD) quasiprobability trajectories and valid for arbitrary initial states and dynamics. This integral FT deviates from the standard unit-valued form, and the complex deviation, termed the anomaly, quantifies a weighted overlap between the residual final-state coherence and the coherence generated from the dephased input. The real part of the anomaly yields bounds on coherence change, while its imaginary part constrains the second-order moment of the stochastic coherence change weighted by the imaginary parts of the KD quasiprobabilities. Our results establish general statistical constraints on coherence dynamics, demonstrating the utility and broad applicability of FTs for studying quantum-resource dynamics.

</details>


### [403] [Error Exponents of Probabilistic Quantum Resource Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06688)
*Xian Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了在近似资源非生成仪器下分析概率资源蒸馏条件误差指数的统一框架，将概率蒸馏与后选择复合量子假设检验相联系，获得了纠缠、相干性和魔术蒸馏在有限块长下的条件误差指数解析刻画及其零速率渐近表达式，并在代表性态族中得到闭式公式，揭示了后选择在量子资源蒸馏中的操作性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源理论中，概率（后选择）蒸馏与确定性蒸馏在条件误差指数上的差异尚未被系统理解，缺乏统一的框架来刻画各类资源理论下的概率蒸馏性能极限。

Method: 建立统一框架分析条件误差指数；通过将概率蒸馏映射为后选择复合量子假设检验问题，推导通用单发界；针对纠缠、相干性和魔术蒸馏在有限块长场景下进行解析刻画并推导零速率渐近表达式；对代表性态族化简为闭式公式。

Result: 获得了纠缠、相干性和魔术蒸馏在零速率区域的渐近条件误差指数；多个代表性态族得到显式闭式公式；与确定性蒸馏指数对比后发现后选择在某些情形下可严格改善条件错误的指数衰减率。

Conclusion: 后选择在量子资源蒸馏中具有操作优势，后选择复合假设检验可作为刻画概率资源处理任务的通用工具。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we establish a unified framework for analyzing the conditional error exponents of probabilistic resource distillation under approximately resource-nongenerating instruments. For generic quantum resource theories satisfying suitable structural conditions, we derive general one-shot bounds on the conditional distillation error exponents. By relating probabilistic distillation to postselected composite quantum hypothesis testing, we further obtain analytical characterizations of the conditional error exponents for entanglement, coherence, and magic distillation at finite blocklength, and derive their asymptotic expressions in the zero-rate regime. For several representative families of states in entanglement and magic resource theories, these characterizations reduce to explicit closed-form formulas. Comparing them with the corresponding deterministic distillation exponents, we identify regimes in which postselection yields a strict improvement in the exponential decay rate of the conditional error. Our results reveal an operational advantage of postselection in quantum resource distillation and establish postselected composite hypothesis testing as a general tool for characterizing probabilistic resource-processing tasks.

</details>


### [404] [Thermodynamic Performance of a Measurement-Driven Quantum Engine with a Two-Parameter $(p,q)$-Deformed Harmonic Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06695)
*Tunde Joseph Osunmusanmi,Berihu Teklu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于量子测量反作用的单浴量子引擎，其工作介质为双参数(p,q)-形变谐振子。通过形变修饰的非线性能谱，系统地分析了测量驱动量子循环中的能量转换，并数值展示了形变可以增强相对于未形变谐振子的测量诱导能量输入和提取功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用量子测量反作用力驱动量子引擎做功，并研究非线性形变谐振子是否能够提升测量诱导的功输出和能量转换效率，从而为量子热机工程提供新的自由度。

Method: 构建单浴测量驱动量子引擎模型：工作介质为(p,q)-形变谐振子；循环包含(1)准静态绝热改变参数(ω,p,q)、(2)对最终有效位置正交算子进行非选择性高斯测量、(3)反向绝热冲程、(4)与原始热浴热化。采用一致的第一定律约定推导测量热、绝热功、热化热与约化效率；通过跃迁矩阵描述测量冲程，并识别确保测量热非负的无源性和幺正性条件；在有限基组、谱序和引擎运行约束下进行数值扫描。

Result: 推导出完整的测量驱动循环热力学量表达式；建立了形变参数改变能隙的机制；数值结果表明在物理可行区域内（QM>0, Wcyc<0, QT<0, 0<η<1），形变可增强测量诱导能量输入并提取更多功；给出了无源性/幺正性判据以确保测量热的非负性。

Conclusion: (p,q)-形变谐振子的非线性能谱为测量驱动量子引擎的性能调控提供了额外参数自由度，可在满足物理约束的前提下增强引擎的功输出。需注意所报道效率为约化效率，未计入测量设备本身的能量成本。

Abstract: We address a measurement-driven, single-bath quantum engine that generates usable work using quantum measurement backaction. The working medium is a two-parameter $(p,q)$-deformed harmonic oscillator, whose nonlinear spectrum modifies the energy gaps sampled by a nonselective Gaussian measurement and the work exchanged during quasistatic adiabatic strokes. The cycle starts from a Gibbs state and consists of an adiabatic change of $(ω,p,q)$, measurement of the final effective position quadrature, a reverse adiabatic stroke, and thermalization with the original bath. Using a consistent first-law convention, we derive the measurement heat, adiabatic work contributions, thermalization heat, and reduced working-medium efficiency. We also express the measurement stroke via a transition matrix and identify passivity/unitality conditions that ensure nonnegative measurement heat. Numerical scans show that, within the finite-basis, spectral-ordering, and engine-operation checks used in this work, deformation can enhance measurement-induced energy input and extracted work relative to the undeformed oscillator. The physical engine regime is selected by $\QM>0$, $\Wcyc<0$, $\QT<0$, and $0<η<1$. The reported efficiency is reduced and excludes measurement-apparatus costs.

</details>


### [405] [Scale-Free Localization Morphing in Bilocally Coupled Hatano-Nelson Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06799)
*Chong Wang,Linhu Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个Hatano-Nelson链在局部耦合下的反常临界非厄米趋肤效应（CNHSE），发现在强链间耦合下临界行为仍然存在，其机制是强局部杂化产生高能杂质模并有效将低能希尔伯特空间碎片化为几何选择的扇区，其中一个扇区支持无标度趋肤态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为临界非厄米趋肤效应通常与弱耦合子系统相关，本文旨在探索超越弱耦合范式的临界非厄米行为，揭示强耦合体系中CNHSE的存在可能性及其底层机制。

Method: 考虑两个Hatano-Nelson链在相隔距离d的两个体点处进行局部耦合。通过分析强链间耦合下的现象，发现局部杂化会产生高能杂质模式，并在有效低能理论中实现希尔伯特空间的碎片化，形成几何选择的扇区分割。同时从计数角度分析外层扇区所选状态数与系统尺寸的关系。

Result: 临界行为在强链间耦合区域依然存在；强局部杂化导致高能杂质模并碎片化低能希尔伯特空间为几何选择扇区，其中一个扇区支持无标度趋肤态；外层扇区被选择的状态数目为2(L-d)，其中L为链长、d为耦合点间的包含距离。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了局部耦合诱导的碎片化作为实现临界非厄米行为的新途径，突破了弱耦合范式的限制，为强耦合体系中非厄米趋肤效应与临界现象的理解提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate an anomalous critical non-Hermitian skin effect (CNHSE) in two Hatano--Nelson chains coupled locally at two bulk sites separated by an inclusive distance $d$. Whereas conventional CNHSE is commonly associated with weakly coupled subsystems, the critical behavior in our system persists in the strong inter-chain-coupling regime. Strong local hybridization generates high-energy impurity modes and effectively fragments the low-energy Hilbert space into geometry-selected sectors, one of which supports scale-free skin states. Independently, the count of states selected in the outer sector follows $2(L-d)$ for the two-chain system. These results establish local-coupling-induced fragmentation as a route to critical non-Hermitian behavior beyond the weak-coupling paradigm.

</details>


### [406] [Hilbert space geometry change by a nonadiabatic electromagnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06800)
*Ivan Georgiev Koprinkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现量子系统在非绝热电磁场中希尔伯特空间几何发生变化，量子态从正交演化为非正交，并可通过时变Fubini-Study度量来描述，该动态度量与广义相对论中时空动态度量具有相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子系统在非绝热电磁场作用下希尔伯特空间几何的改变及其物理起源，探索量子力学与广义相对论在几何度量层面的深层联系。

Method: 基于量子态矩阵元，在三种物理情形（零外场/闭系统、绝热缓慢变化场、非绝热快速变化场）中分析量子态从正交到非正交的演化过程，并在非绝热情形下构造依赖于时间的Fubini-Study度量。

Result: 前两种情形（零场和绝热场）中量子态保持正交；非绝热场中量子态连续地跟随场参数变化而变为非正交。基于非绝热情形下量子态矩阵元可确定描述希尔伯特空间的时变Fubini-Study度量。

Conclusion: 非绝热电磁场诱导的希尔伯特空间内禀几何变化与时变度量密切相关，该动态度量在形式上与广义相对论中的时空动态度量相呼应，可能为量子-引力统一提供新的几何视角。

Abstract: A change in the geometry of the Hilbert space of a quantum system interacting with a nonadiabatic electromagnetic field is found. To identify its physical origin, the evolution of orthogonality to non-orthogonality of the states of the quantum system has been investigated based on the matrix elements of the quantum states in three main physical cases: a quantum system in zero external field (closed quantum system), a quantum system in slowly changing (adiabatic) field, and a quantum system in rapidly changing (nonadiabatic) field. The quantum states are orthogonal in the first two cases and change to non-orthogonal in a nonadiabatic field, following continuously the parameters of the field. A time-dependent Fubini-Study metric of the Hilbert space can be determined based on the matrix elements of the quantum states in the nonadiabatic case. Such a dynamical metric of the Hilbert space in quantum mechanics resembles the dynamical metric of spacetime in general relativity.

</details>


### [407] [Spectral Twisting in a Common Bosonic Reservoir: Fragility of Two-Qubit Dark-State Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06802)
*Fabio Borrelli,Giovanni Miano,Carlo Forestiere*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个量子比特与共同玻色子库相互作用中的"谱扭转"(spectral twisting)现象——当库的谱密度矩阵J(ω)的秩为一但亮/暗方向随频率旋转时，局域暗态不再全局受保护。作者通过非微扰层次运动方程计算，证明了谱扭转从谱交叉点处的单重态诱发泄漏，泄漏率遵循二次弱扭转标度律P_leak(t)∝[ω×τ(ω×)]²，并加速Werner态并发度的衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化的共同库环境中，两量子比特系统可能存在"局域暗态"——即在每个频率处都有一个暗耦合方向。但当谱密度矩阵的本征方向随频率变化（谱扭转）时，这些仅在每一频率局部成立的暗态可能发生泄漏，破坏退相干自由子空间。理解这一效应对于暗态工程、相关噪声谱学和退相干自由编码至关重要。

Method: 作者将两量子比特与共同玻色子库的相互作用由谱密度矩阵J(ω)表征，定义谱扭转并用量子几何中的Fubini–Study速度τ(ω)量化亮谱投影的变化率。为区分扭转效应与耦合不对称、热吸收、逆旋转过程及哈密顿量对称破缺，他们比较了旋转波动力学与未扭转的非对称库，并考虑量子比特失谐。采用非微扰的层次运动方程(HEOM)对失配的Drude–Lorentz谱进行精确数值计算，对双量子比特动力学及其暗态保护、单态泄漏和Werner态并发度进行定量分析。

Result: 数值结果表明，谱扭转导致原本在频域上局部为暗的单重态发生泄漏；扭转还使最优保护态发生偏移，并加速Werner态并发度的衰减。在固定观测时间下，弱扭转区的泄漏满足精确的二次标度律P_leak(t)∝[ω×τ(ω×)]²，其中ω×为J₁₁和J₂₂的谱交叉频率。

Conclusion: 谱扭转是独立于耦合不对称、热吸收和逆旋转过程的独特失相机制，它对暗态的鲁棒性和纠缠保持具有显著影响。此发现为结构化库中的暗态工程提供了新的设计原则，对相关噪声谱学和退相干自由编码的实验实现具有指导意义。

Abstract: The interaction of two qubits with a common bosonic reservoir is characterized by the spectral-density matrix $\mathbf{J}(ω)$, whose diagonal entries $J_{11}(ω)$ and $J_{22}(ω)$ describe the local spectra, while the off-diagonal entries encode cross-correlations. For a maximally correlated reservoir, $\mathbf{J}(ω)$ has rank one and therefore a \textit{locally} dark coupling direction at each frequency. If $J_{22}(ω)/J_{11}(ω)$ varies with frequency, however, the bright and dark directions rotate and $\ker\mathbf{J}(ω)$ is frequency dependent. We call this \textit{spectral twisting} and quantify it through the Fubini--Study speed $τ(ω)$ of the bright spectral projector. We investigate how spectral twisting affects coupled two-qubit dynamics and dark-state protection. We quantify protection loss by the survival \textit{leakage} $P_{\mathrm{leak}}(t)$, which can become finite for states that are dark only locally in frequency. By comparing rotating-wave dynamics and untwisted asymmetric reservoirs, together with analyzing qubit detuning, we distinguish twisting from coupling asymmetry, thermal absorption, counter-rotating processes, and Hamiltonian symmetry breaking. For mismatched Drude--Lorentz spectra, our nonperturbative hierarchical-equations-of-motion calculations show that twisting induces leakage from the singlet, which is locally dark at the spectral crossing $ω_\times$ defined by $J_{11}(ω_\times)=J_{22}(ω_\times)$. Twisting also shifts the optimally protected state and accelerates the decay of Werner-state concurrence. At fixed observation time, we find the quadratic weak-twisting scaling $P_{\mathrm{leak}}(t)\propto[ω_\timesτ(ω_\times)]^2$. These results may guide dark-state engineering in structured reservoirs, with implications for correlated-noise spectroscopy and decoherence-free encodings.

</details>


### [408] [Quantum-Enhanced Learning Framework for Intelligent and AI-Native 6G Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06814)
*Muhammad Bilal Akram Dastagir,Rakesh Saini,Omer Tariq,Saif Al-Kuwari,Shahid Mumtaz,Anwer Al-Dulaimi,Ahmed Farouk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Quantumer，一种混合TinyML-量子框架，用于资源受限边缘设备上的入侵检测。它通过两阶段迁移学习（量子预训练Quantumer-Q到经典微调Quantumer-C），利用轻量Transformer架构集成多尺度膨胀卷积和缩放点积注意力。量子电路仅在离线预训练阶段作为非线性嵌入教师，部署时完全经典化，在Edge-IIoTset、TON IoT和WUSTL-IIoT-2021三个数据集上实现了紧凑参数（105.86K）、低延迟（16.84ms on Raspberry Pi 4）的强检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G无线系统与TinyML的融合推动了边缘网络端侧智能的需求，但面临超低延迟、严格能量预算和紧计算约束。轻量深度模型能高效提取局部模式却无法捕捉全局依赖，而注意力机制虽能捕捉全局依赖却代价高昂。现有方法难以在极低资源条件下兼顾局部与全局特征提取及检测性能。

Method: 1) 提出Quantumer混合框架，在轻量Transformer架构中集成多尺度膨胀卷积与缩放点积注意力；2) 采用两阶段迁移学习流水线：量子预训练（Quantumer-Q）→经典微调（Quantumer-C）；3) 设计QuantiblentLayer四比特变分量子电路，以可训练旋转和循环纠缠操作将紧凑流量表征映射为基于测量的Hilbert空间特征，仅用于离线预训练作为非线性嵌入教师；4) 部署前移除量子电路，保留完全经典推理模型，无运行时量子执行开销。

Result: 在Edge-IIoTset、TON IoT和WUSTL-IIoT-2021数据集上，Quantumer-Q达到竞争性紧凑模型性能：105.86K参数、0.4038 MB内存、0.5525 MB模型大小、5.5646 MFLOPs；INT8量化后Raspberry Pi 4部署获得16.8413 ms延迟与0.6493 MB占用。

Conclusion: 训练时量子辅助表示学习（而非运行时量子计算）能够为紧凑型边缘可部署入侵检测系统提供有效支持，Quantumer框架在极低计算与内存开销下实现了强检测性能，验证了量子-经典混合训练策略在资源受限边缘场景中的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: The recent convergence of 6G wireless systems and Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) has driven the need for on-device intelligence in edge networks, where ultra-low latency, stringent energy budgets, and tight compute constraints demand novel architectures. Lightweight deep models efficiently extract local patterns but fail to capture global dependencies, while attention mechanisms do so at the expense of energy and computational cost. To bridge this gap, we introduce Quantumer, a hybrid TinyML--quantum framework that integrates multi-scale dilated convolutions and scaled dot-product attention within a lightweight transformer architecture, employing a two-stage transfer learning pipeline from Quantum Pre-Training (Quantumer-Q) to Classical Fine-Tuning (Quantumer-C). We also present QuantiblentLayer, a four-qubit variational circuit that maps compact traffic representations into measurement-based Hilbert-space features using trainable rotations and cyclic entangling operations. The circuit is used only during offline pre-training as a nonlinear embedding teacher and is removed before Quantumer-C deployment, leaving a fully classical inference model without runtime quantum execution. By transferring these quantum-assisted embeddings into an energy-efficient, lightweight transformer, Quantumer achieves strong detection performance with minimal compute and memory overhead on resource-constrained edge devices. The intrusion detection system (IDS) is used as a case study and evaluated on the Edge-IIoTset, TON IoT, and WUSTL-IIoT-2021 datasets. Quantumer-Q achieves competitive compact-model performance with 105.86K parameters, 0.4038 MB memory usage, 0.5525 MB model size, and 5.5646 MFLOPs; the INT8 Raspberry Pi 4 deployment obtains 16.8413 ms latency with a 0.6493 MB footprint. These results support training-time quantum-assisted representation learning for compact edge-deployable IDS.

</details>


### [409] [Kinematic Control and Dephasing Dynamics of Quantum Resources in $e^{+}e^{-}\toτ^{+}τ^{-}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08555)
*Elhabib Jaloum,Omar Bachain,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了$e^{+}e^{-}$湮灭中产生的$τ^{+}τ^{-}$自旋对所携带的多种量子资源（Bell非定域性、可导引性、纠缠形成度与相干性），揭示其共同运动学起源，并研究在相关退相干通道下环境记忆效应对量子资源存活的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能对撞机中产生的$τ$轻子对携带丰富的自旋量子关联，理解这些量子资源（非定域性、纠缠、可导引性、相干性）的产生机制与演化规律，对于量子信息基础研究与高能物理实验（如Belle-II）中的量子效应探测具有重要意义。

Method: 首先将$τ^{+}τ^{-}$的自旋态表示为依赖于速度与角度的混合态（可分离部分与最大纠缠态的混合），从而统一解释Bell非定域性、可导引性、纠缠形成度与相干性随产生角度的变化规律。随后引入现象学相关退相干通道，通过对比马尔可夫与非马尔可夫（环境记忆）演化，研究量子资源的生存与动态行为。

Result: 研究发现各量子资源均受产生角控制，在横向发射（$\vartheta=π/2$）时最大。自旋态的速度-角度依赖表示在物理产生域内将阈值弱关联态（$\sqrt{s}=2m_τ$）与超相对论极限下的Bell态连续连接起来。相关退相干通道中，环境记忆效应导致量子资源出现"坍塌-复兴"（collapse-revival）振荡行为，而这些在单调的马尔可夫演化中完全不存在。

Conclusion: 该分析将产生自旋关联的运动学机制与决定资源后续存活的噪声（退相干）特性明确分离，揭示了高能$τ$对系统中量子资源产生的统一物理图像及其在环境噪声下的动力学行为。

Abstract: We characterize several quantum resources carried by the spins of the $τ^{+}τ^{-}$ pair produced in $e^{+}e^{-}$ annihilation. At the Belle-II energy, $\sqrt{s}=10.579\,\mathrm{GeV}$, Bell nonlocality, steerability, entanglement of formation, and coherence are governed by the production angle and are largest for transverse emission, $\vartheta=π/2$. Their common kinematic origin is exposed by expressing the spin state as a velocity and angle-dependent mixture of a separable contribution and a maximally entangled component. Within the physical production domain, this representation connects the weakly correlated threshold state at $\sqrt{s}=2m_τ$ to the Bell-state limit approached at ultrarelativistic energies. We then propagate the two-spin state through a phenomenological correlated-dephasing channel to determine how environmental memory and inter-channel classical correlations affect the available resources. Memory effects generate collapses and revivals that are absent from the monotonic Markovian evolution. The analysis therefore separates the kinematic mechanism that creates the spin correlations from the noise properties that control their subsequent survival.

</details>


### [410] [Coherence-Mediated Boundary Control of the Liouvillian Gap in Nonreciprocal Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06981)
*K. L. Li,M. H. Yu,X. Z. Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究单粒子Lindblad格点模型中非互易非相干跳跃与可调相干边界链的耦合效应。该相干边界链在不改变耗散生成元的前提下，通过群体-相干-群体反馈机制调控Liouvillian间隙。周期参考系统给出了精确的固定动量约化和标量本征方程，而开放有限格点（一维和二维）的精确对角化表明相干边界链可增强或抑制间隙，且可观测弛豫尺度可能偏离1/Δ_L。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开放量子系统中边界条件如何影响Liouvillian间隙及弛豫动力学，特别是相干边界调控能否在不直接改变对角群体生成元的情况下，通过群体-相干耦合反馈机制调控系统弛豫速率，为设计可控开放量子系统提供理论依据。

Method: 将非互易非相干跳跃与相干边界链结合，构建单粒子Lindblad格点模型。对周期系统利用平移对称性进行精确的固定动量约化，推导标量本征方程并提取流体力学漂移和相干修正扩散。对开放有限格点采用精确对角化和稀疏近零本征求解器，在一维和二维中进行系统研究。

Result: 相干边界链通过群体-相干-群体反馈（哈密顿量将群体耦合到阻尼相干，消除相干后产生频率依赖的自能）调控Liouvillian间隙：在一维中可增强或抑制间隙，在二维中产生几何依赖的响应。周期系统提供了精确解析基准；同时发现当间隙族在所选扰动和可观测量中可见性弱时，观测弛豫尺度可偏离1/Δ_L。

Conclusion: 相干边界链提供了一种不直接触碰耗散结构而调控开放量子系统弛豫的新机制；周期构造为验证有限尺寸模拟提供了精确解析基准，体现了同一相干介导机制在不同几何中控制开放边界间隙和可观测弛豫的普适性。

Abstract: We study a single-particle Lindblad lattice in which nonreciprocal incoherent hopping is combined with a tunable coherent boundary link. The link changes the Hamiltonian boundary condition while the incoherent jump rates are kept fixed, so it does not directly alter the diagonal population generator. Its effect on the Liouvillian gap comes from population-coherence-population feedback: the Hamiltonian couples populations to damped coherences, and eliminating those coherences produces a frequency-dependent self-energy for the slow population branch. For the periodic reference system, where translation symmetry is imposed on both the Hamiltonian and the dissipator, we derive an exact fixed-momentum reduction, a scalar secular equation for the population-connected branch, and the associated hydrodynamic drift and coherence-corrected diffusion. For open finite lattices, exact diagonalization and sparse near-zero eigensolvers show that the coherent boundary link can enhance or suppress the gap in one dimension and produce geometry-dependent responses in two dimensions. We also show that an observed relaxation scale can differ from $1/Δ_{\mathcal L}$ when the gap family has weak visibility in the chosen perturbation and observable. The periodic construction gives exact analytical benchmarks, while finite-size simulations in one and two dimensions demonstrate how the same coherence-mediated mechanism controls open-boundary gaps and observable relaxation.

</details>


### [411] [A Single Fixed Shallow Circuit for Classical Shadows of Arbitrary n-Qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07032)
*Yu Wang,Xiuwu Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个固定的浅层量子分析器，用于替代经典影子协议中逐次采样的随机测量设置。该分析器通过将未知系统与新鲜的n量子比特保真寄存器进行并行Bell读出，每次拷贝仅产生一个2n比特记录，但实现了一个秩一的极小信息完全测量（minimal informationally complete measurement），用单一固定设置取代了传统完整重构所需的3^n个局域Pauli设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经典影子协议依赖随机化测量设置的重用，虽然单个设置的深度可能很浅，但在不同设置之间切换会引入控制、校准和重新配置的额外开销，这些成本超出了传统度量指标（如样本复杂度）的范畴。作者希望将这些逐次分担的测量设置随机性和控制复杂度编译到一个固定的可重用分析器中，以消除设置切换带来的开销。

Method: 构造一个固定的浅层量子电路，该电路由两部分组成：(1) 一个新鲜制备的n量子比特保真寄存器A，其制备使用n-1个任意两比特门，无额外工作比特，在全连接拓扑下具有对数深度；(2) 未知系统S与A之间的并行Bell读出层，随后接局域Hadamard和读出。该电路实现了秩一的极小信息完全量子测量，其Pauli对角的框架具有解析逆。还提出了测量辅助制备方案：heralded路线使用n+1个额外量子比特实现O(1)自适应量子深度，而确定性完成方案使用O(n log n)的辅助比特。

Result: 该固定分析器保留了最小d^2个结果（d=2^n），对固定Hermitian可观测量，Haar平均的条件方差具有维度无关的系数，而态均匀系数则维度相关。对于n≥3，最坏情况的Pauli方差在轴向扇区保持有界，在混合扇区按Θ(d)缩放。

Conclusion: 测量设置随机性和控制复杂度可以部分地从逐次采样的动态开销中剥离出来，编译到固定的可重用分析器硬件中，从而在保持信息完全性和良好方差性质的前提下大幅简化经典影子协议的实验实现。

Abstract: Classical shadows extract quantum properties from reusable classical records, typically obtained through randomized measurement settings. Although individual settings may be shallow, switching among them introduces control, calibration, and reconfiguration costs beyond conventional metrics. Here we construct, for every $n$, a single fixed shallow quantum analyzer whose Born outcomes replace externally sampled settings as labels for reusable shadow snapshots. The analyzer combines a freshly prepared $n$-qubit fiducial register $A$ with parallel Bell readout of $A$ and the unknown system $S$, producing one $2n$-bit record per copy. The same circuit realizes a rank-one minimal informationally complete measurement: one fixed setting replaces the $3^n$ local-Pauli settings conventionally used for complete reconstruction while retaining the minimum $d^2$ outcomes with $d=2^n$. Its Pauli-diagonal frame admits an analytic inverse. For fixed Hermitian observables, the Haar-averaged conditional variance has a dimension-independent coefficient, whereas the state-uniform coefficient is dimension dependent. For $n\ge3$, worst-state Pauli variances remain bounded in the axial sector and scale as $Θ(d)$ in the mixed sector. Fiducial preparation before system contact uses $n-1$ arbitrary two-qubit gates, no work qubits beyond $A$, and logarithmic depth under all-to-all connectivity. The unknown system undergoes one parallel system-ancilla entangling layer followed by local Hadamards and readout. Measurement-assisted preparation achieves $O(1)$ adaptive quantum depth using $n+1$ extra qubits in the heralded route, or $O(n\log n)$ auxiliaries for deterministic completion. These tradeoffs show that part of the measurement-setting randomness and control complexity normally supplied shot by shot can instead be compiled into a fixed reusable analyzer.

</details>


### [412] [Capability-Gated Conformance Testing of Quantum Error-Correction Decoder Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07035)
*Jiachen Shen,Hui Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需oracle的量子纠错解码器"合规契约"测试方法，通过两种检查（返回修正必须解释caller索引空间中的症候群；同一实例在不同编号表述下解码，比较修正权重）来验证解码器是否符合其公开声明的能力。对五个公开库的九种配置进行测试，发现文档仅能回答54个能力问题中的4个，而调用者最依赖的"有界距离正确性"直接声明率为0.0%（尽管其假设在62.1%的情形下成立）。编号方式的改变甚至会让某个求解器产生加重26%的修正，该修正最多每两万次shots才达到一次逻辑类。此外发现传统评估会漏掉保持逻辑奇偶性的损坏，而基于caller权重的累加能捕获所有注入的错误。全部639个证书以bundles形式提供，独立验证器可从第一性原理重新推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错解码器的评估被单一指标主导（采样错误、解码、统计错误逻辑可观测数），缺乏对其他关键性质的系统性验证。本文旨在回答"除了标准评估外还能检查什么"，并建立不依赖oracle的合规检验方法。

Method: 提出两种无需oracle的合规契约检查：(1) 返回的修正必须能在caller的索引空间中解释症候群；(2) 将同一个实例以两种仅涉及记账方式差异的表述交给解码器，若两种可行修正权重不同，则权重更大者必然不是最小权重修正。验证结论以各库自身的公开声明为准门控。

Result: 对五个公共库的九种配置测试得到三种结果：文档仅能回答54个能力问题中的4个；有界距离正确性（调用者最依赖的性质）直接声明率为0.0%，但其前提条件在62.1%情形下成立；编号方式导致的修正敏感性真实但浅层——某求解器在不同编号下产生加重26%的修正，该修正最多每两万次shoot出现一次到达逻辑类的情况。传统评估漏掉保持逻辑奇偶性的损坏，而一个按caller权重求和的方法捕获了所有注入的损坏。全部639个证书作为bundles提供，可被独立验证器从第一性原理重新推导。

Conclusion: 标准解码器评估存在盲区，会漏掉不破坏逻辑奇偶性的损坏。本文提出的合规契约和基于caller权重的检查方法提供了不依赖oracle的补充验证手段，能发现库文档未能兑现的保证以及解码器对不同编号表述的敏感性。研究揭示文档承诺与实际能力之间存在显著差距，需要更系统的合规验证体系。

Abstract: A quantum error correction decoder is a library other people's results depend on, judged in one dominant way. Sample errors, decode, and count wrong logical observables. We ask what else can be checked there. Our conformance contract needs no oracle. One check asks that a returned correction explain the syndrome in the caller's index space. The other hands a decoder one instance under two presentations differing only in bookkeeping, where two feasible corrections of different weight prove the heavier is not minimum-weight. Verdicts are gated on what each library declares, so a firing contradicts a published guarantee. Nine configurations from five public libraries give three results. Documentation answers 4 of 54 capability questions. Bounded-distance correctness, the property callers most depend on, has a direct declaration yield of 0.0%, though its hypotheses hold in 62.1% of cases. Presentation sensitivity is real but shallow. One solver moved to a 26% heavier correction under a different numbering, which reaches the logical class at most once in twenty thousand shots. Established evaluation misses corruptions that preserve logical parity, while one summation over the caller's weights catches every one we injected. All 639 certificates ship as bundles a standalone verifier re-derives from first principles.

</details>


### [413] [2-Fold Forrelation is in QAC$^0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07060)
*Francisca Vasconcelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在逆多对数承诺间隙下，2倍Forrelation问题可由多项式规模的QAC⁰电路以有界误差解决。与标准基于预言机的Forrelation算法不同，这些电路以与AC⁰电路相同的形式显式接收输入。在恒定间隙下，这给出了QAC⁰与AC⁰之间自然的承诺问题分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算复杂类QAC⁰与经典AC⁰之间的分离是量子计算理论中的重要问题。Forrelation问题此前已知可作为两者分离的候选工具，但标准算法依赖预言机模型，缺乏显式输入的构造，因此需要找到一种能直接处理显式输入的QAC⁰电路构造方法。

Method: 构造多项式规模的QAC⁰电路，直接接收显式输入（与AC⁰电路相同的编码形式）来求解2-fold Forrelation问题。通过将承诺间隙放宽到逆多对数（inverse-polylogarithmic）范围，利用更宽松的间隙条件允许更高效的深度恒定量子电路实现。

Result: 在逆多对数承诺间隙下，多项式规模QAC⁰电路能以有界误差求解2-fold Forrelation；在恒定间隙条件下，该结果导出了QAC⁰与AC⁰之间的一个自然的承诺问题分离。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了QAC⁰超越AC⁰的显式证据，且避免了预言机模型的限制，暗示量子浅层电路在特定承诺问题上具有经典浅层电路无法匹敌的计算能力。

Abstract: We show that 2-fold Forrelation with inverse-polylogarithmic promise gap can be solved, with bounded error, by polynomial-size QAC$^0$ circuits. Unlike the standard oracle-based Forrelation algorithm, our circuits receive the input explicitly, in the same form as the AC$^0$ circuits against which Forrelation is known to be hard. At constant gap, this yields a natural promise-problem separation between QAC$^0$ and AC$^0$.

</details>


### [414] [Single-tone drive-enhanced CROT gate for bosonic quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07076)
*Shushen Qin,Long D. H. My,Adrian Copetudo,Amon M. Kasper,Yvonne Y. Gao,Hui Khoon Ng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在电路QED架构中直接实现两模受控旋转（CROT）门的方法，通过同时驱动一个与两个微波腔耦合的transmon来增强腔间有效非线性，从而获得具有code-agnostic通用性和良好错误传播特性的CROOT门，避免了以往级联imperfect primitive操作导致特性丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色子纠错（尤其是旋转对称玻色子码如猫码）需要CROT门作为关键组件。虽然CROT原则上可由现有玻色子物理原语组合而成，但级联不完美的原语操作会丢失其理想的code-agnostic通用性和错误传播特性，而以往的直接实现又依赖于特定码的特征，缺乏通用性。

Method: 在电路QED架构中，通过同时驱动一个与两个微波腔耦合的transmon，可控地增强腔间的有效非线性，从而直接工程化实现CROT门所需的两模相互作用。仅使用单一驱动频率即可获得足够好的on-off比率，同时最小化模式畸变。提供简单解析公式识别可行工作区间，并用精确数值方法进一步验证。

Result: 仅用单一驱动频率即可实现足够高的on-off比率以支持门操作，同时最小化模式畸变；解析公式与精确数值结果一致；在涉及同一电路中不同RSB码的纠错示例中展示了CROT方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 该CROT门为通用玻色子信息处理提供了直接可用的两模门，兼具code-agnostic的普适性和良好的错误传播特性，扩展了可用的直接两模门工具集。

Abstract: Bosonic error correction provides a hardware-efficient route to protected qubits for accurate quantum information processing. A critical component for error correction of rotation-symmetric bosonic (RSB) codes, like the well-known cat codes, is the two-mode controlled-rotation (CROT) operation. The CROT gate is useful because of its code-agnostic generality and its error-propagation properties. While the CROT gate can in principle be composed from existing bosonic physical primitives, the desirable properties are typically lost when composed as a sequence of imperfect primitive operations. Past work has focused on direct implementations that rely on features of specific codes. Here, we present a direct route to CROT within the circuit-QED architecture that retains its code-agnostic and error-propagation characteristics. By driving a transmon simultaneously coupled to two microwave cavities, we controllably enhance the effective nonlinearities between the microwave cavities and thus engineer the necessary two-mode interaction underlying the CROT gate. Using only a single drive frequency, we can achieve an on-off ratio sufficient for gate implementation, while minimizing mode distortions. We provide simple analytical formulas that permit the identification of potential working regimes, further refined by exact numerics, and illustrate the efficacy of our CROT approach in an error-correction example involving different RSB codes within the same circuit. Our CROT gate adds to the arsenal of direct two-mode gates available for general bosonic information processing.

</details>


### [415] [Ancilla-assisted nondestructive discrimination of distributed GHZ-class states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07084)
*Abdul Q. Batin,Suman Chand,Rajiuddin Sk,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的辅助量子比特（ancilla-assisted）协议，用于在分布式多方之间实现n量子比特GHZ类态的严格非破坏性判别。通过多体GHZ辅助态和局域幺正相互作用，系统GHZ态的计算模式和相对相位信息可相干映射到两个辅助寄存器，同时保持系统态不变。作者推导了三/四量子比特的判别规则并推广至任意n，在退极化和振幅阻尼噪声下获得成功概率的解析表达式及临界噪声阈值，并在IBM Qiskit平台上验证了实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非破坏性量子态判别是分布式量子信息处理中的基本原语，需要识别共享多体纠缠资源而不消耗它们。现有方法在处理多体GHZ类态的严格非破坏性判别时缺乏可扩展性和系统性方案，这是推动本工作的核心动机。

Method: 采用多体GHZ辅助态作为辅助寄存器，结合局域幺正相互作用，将系统GHZ态的计算模式和相对相位信息相干映射到两个独立的辅助寄存器，同时确保系统态保持不变。作者显式推导了三、四量子比特GHZ类态的判别规则，并发展出适用于任意n的系统性扩展方法。此外，在辅助系统受退极化和振幅阻尼噪声影响的情况下进行理论分析，获得成功概率的解析表达式和临界噪声阈值，并使用IBM Qiskit平台进行实验验证。

Result: 成功展示了可扩展的n量子比特GHZ类态严格非破坏性判别协议；获得了三、四量子比特的显式判别规则及任意n的系统扩展；在退极化和振幅阻尼噪声下推导出成功概率的解析表达式和关键噪声阈值；通过IBM Qiskit验证了协议的实验可行性并复现了预期的辅助测量特征。

Conclusion: 本框架为多体GHZ类态的严格非破坏性判别提供了可扩展的解决方案，并提出了将辅助GHZ资源重新解释为退相干探针的视角，可能为分布式量子系统中的量子退相干传感开辟新的联系和应用方向。

Abstract: Nondestructive quantum state discrimination is a fundamental primitive in distributed quantum information processing, where shared multipartite entangled resources need to be identified without being consumed. In this work, we present a scalable ancilla-assisted protocol for the strict nondestructive discrimination of $n$-qubit GHZ-class states distributed among distant parties. By employing multipartite GHZ ancillary states and local unitary interactions, we show that the computational-pattern and relative-phase information of the system GHZ state can be coherently mapped onto two ancillary registers while leaving the system state unchanged. We explicitly derive the discrimination rules for three- and four-qubit GHZ-class states and develop a systematic extension to arbitrary $n$. We further investigate the discrimination protocol in the presence of depolarizing and amplitude-damping noise in the ancillary system and obtain analytical expressions for the success probability and the corresponding critical noise thresholds. The protocol is also implemented using the IBM Qiskit platform to demonstrate its experimental feasibility and reproduce the expected ancillary measurement signatures. The proposed framework provides a scalable approach to the strict nondestructive discrimination of multipartite GHZ-class states and suggests a broader interpretation of the ancillary GHZ resources as probes of decoherence, opening a possible connection to quantum decoherence sensing in distributed quantum systems.

</details>


### [416] [QCxSimulation: Scatter-Aware X-Ray Projection Radiography via Discrete-Time Quantum Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07089)
*Anja Heim,Theobald Fuchs,Thomas Lang,Dimitri Prjamkov,Kilian Dremel,Stefan Kasperl,Christoph Heinzl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间量子游走的算法来模拟X射线投影放射成像中的光子传输，通过在单一量子态中编码所有允许的光子路径，利用叠加原理同时传播所有散射历史（包括光电吸收、康普顿散射和瑞利散射），实现对多种成像模态的灵活读取，并验证了其与经典参考模拟的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典光子传输模拟（如蒙特卡洛方法）在包含真实X射线散射物理时计算成本极高，因为需要采样大量不同的散射路径，这限制了参数空间（如束能量）的广泛探索。量子计算的叠加特性为加速高维问题求解提供了潜在可能。

Method: 提出离散时间量子游走算法模拟X射线光子在异质体中的传输，将光子路径编码到量子态中，通过叠加原理并行传播所有散射路径，涵盖光电吸收、高阶康普顿散射和瑞利散射等物理过程，并支持提取主图像或特定散射阶次的图像。

Result: 与经典参考模拟的定量对比表明，该量子游走算法在基础物理模型的限制范围内能够准确重现放射投影图像。结果证明量子电路能够产生准确的放射图像。

Conclusion: 随着量子硬件规模扩展，该算法有望在大规模、含散射的虚拟成像研究中超越经典蒙特卡洛方法，为X射线传输模拟提供新的高效途径。

Abstract: X-ray projection radiography is a non-invasive imaging technique used in medical diagnostics and industrial inspection. The simulation of X-ray projections is commonly used to optimise acquisition protocols and improve image quality before performing costly scans. Classical photon transport simulations that include realistic X-ray scattering physics are computationally expensive because they require the sampling of a large number of distinct scattering paths. This limits the practical exploration of parameter spaces such as beam energy. Quantum computing offers the potential to solve high-dimensional problems faster by making use of quantum properties such as superposition. This work introduces a discrete-time quantum walk algorithm that simulates the transport of X-ray photons through heterogeneous volumes. It approximates the physics of X-ray projection radiography, including processes such as photoelectric absorption and higher-order scattering, including Compton and Rayleigh scattering. The quantum walk encodes all admissible photon paths into a single quantum state, enabling all scattering histories to be propagated simultaneously via the superposition principle. This quantum state representation enables flexible readout of various imaging modalities, including the primary, i.e., unscattered, image, or images exclusively containing Rayleigh and Compton scattering of specified orders. A quantitative comparison with classically computed reference simulations shows that the proposed quantum walk accurately reproduces radiographic projections, given the limitations of the underlying physical model. These results indicate that quantum circuits for X-ray transport can produce accurate radiographic images and imply that, as quantum hardware scales up, these algorithms could outperform classical Monte Carlo-based approaches in large-scale, scatter-aware virtual imaging studies.

</details>


### [417] [Complexity Amplification from Compression in Quantum Random Access Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07090)
*Stuart Hadfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子随机访问优化（QRAO）在Pauli关联编码（PCE）框架下的计算复杂度，证明其最优能量决策问题分别对NP、StoQMA和QMA完全，且这些硬度结果直接适用于Qiskit Optimization 0.6.0实际编译器实现，揭示了量子压缩带来的最坏情况复杂度障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子压缩编码旨在克服硬件限制，通过将多个经典变量映射到更少量子比特上的非交换可观测量来扩展量子计算可处理的问题规模。然而，作者想要弄清楚这种压缩是否会在最坏情况下放大问题的计算复杂度。尽管经典松弛方法（如MaxCut的SDP松弛）以解决方案质量换取计算效率，但基于压缩的量子松弛可能适得其反地增加问题的复杂度，这是需要严格分析的核心动机。

Method: 作者将QRAO置于PCE框架下形式化研究，该框架将每个量子比特的三个二进制变量分别赋给Pauli X、Y、Z可观测量。他们基于规定的封装（packing）选择来证明：任意非负权重成对Pauli耦合（在已知平移和缩放下）可压缩为加权MaxCut实例。通过保持逆多项式复杂度间隙的归约（无需gadget或辅助量子比特），他们分别在单轴、双轴/三轴对齐设置下刻画了NP完全和QMA完全（及二分限制下的StoQMA）的复杂度结果，并直接验证了Qiskit Optimization 0.7.0中的实际编译器实现。

Result: 主要结果为：（1）QRAO最优能量承诺问题在一根对齐轴上为NP完全；（2）使用两根或三根正对齐Pauli轴时一般为QMA完全，而二分限制下的问题属于StoQMA；（3）NP、StoQMA和QMA完全问题的归约均保持逆多项式承诺间隙；（4）这些硬度结果直接适用于当前Qiskit Optimization 0.7.0中的QRAO编译器实现，表明并非人为构造的封装规则所致。

Conclusion: 量子压缩编码在最坏情况下会带来根本性的复杂度障碍——QRAO在不同封装设置下分别产生NP完全、StoQMA完全和QMA完全的能源决策问题，且这些结果在实际Qiskit编译器中成立。作者谨慎地指出：这些结果只关乎最坏情况，不涉及典型案例或使用该编码的算法管线的性能与可训练性，为实践者提供了明确的复杂度警告而非对量子压缩方法的全盘否定。

Abstract: Compressed quantum encodings aim to overcome hardware limitations towards tackling challenging problems at scale, with many classical variables mapped onto noncommuting observables of fewer qubits. Classically, relaxations such as the semidefinite program formulation of MaxCut trade solution quality for computational efficiency. By contrast, quantum relaxations based on compression can amplify the worst-case complexity of the problem being solved. We study quantum random access optimization (QRAO), a special case of the Pauli correlation encoding (PCE) framework that assigns up to three binary variables to the Pauli $X$, $Y$, and $Z$ observables of each qubit, with the packing choices determining the compressed Hamiltonian to be optimized. We identify explicit QRAO optimal energy promise problems complete for NP, StoqMA, and QMA, with inverse-polynomial promise gaps for the latter two. Our problem reductions preserve inverse-polynomial promise gaps without requiring gadgets or ancillas. For any prescribed packing, we show that weighted MaxCut instances compress, up to a known shift and rescaling, to arbitrary nonnegative-weight pairwise Pauli couplings allowed by the packing. For QRAO, using one aligned axis gives an NP-complete energy problem. Using two or three positive aligned Pauli axes generally gives QMA-complete problems, while their bipartite restrictions lie in StoqMA.
  We show that this computational hardness survives compilation and is practically relevant. Notably, this result applies directly to the current QRAO compiler implementation in Qiskit Optimization 0.7.0, confirming our hardness results are not artifacts of artificial or contrived packing rules. Altogether our results identify worst-case complexity barriers arising from quantum compression, while making no broad claims about typical cases or the performance and trainability of algorithm pipelines that use it.

</details>
